### [1] [Distinguishing Revision and Delayed Elaboration in Incremental Narrative Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21364)
*Yi-Chun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文区分了叙事理解中的两种增量更新算子：修正驱动更新（非单调）和延迟精化（单调），并通过视觉叙事展示如何用结构化表示分离已确定与未充分指定的内容，支持两种更新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨人类和AI系统在增量处理叙事或长文本时，内部表征如何随时间演化，特别是区分修正与精化两种根本不同的更新方式，以理解增量推理的结构要求。

Method: 采用视觉叙事作为诊断领域，构建结构化叙事表征，明确区分已确定内容与未充分指定内容，通过实例演示延迟精化的单调细化与修正的非单调修正过程。

Result: 实验表明，结构化表征能显式分离已确定与未充分指定内容，支持延迟精化的单调扩展和修正驱动更新的非单调修正，验证了两种更新算子的结构差异。

Conclusion: 该结构区分对增量推理和混合符号-神经系统具有广泛意义，为设计能同时处理单调精化与非单调修正的系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Both human and AI systems that process narrative or long-form content operate incrementally: input is received over time, and internal representations must be updated accordingly. Incremental interpretation, therefore, depends not only on what is represented but also on how the representational state evolves under new evidence.
  We distinguish two structurally different update operators that arise in narrative interpretation: revision-driven update and delayed elaboration. Revision-driven updates retract or replace previously committed structure in response to a contradiction and are therefore non-monotonic. Delayed elaboration, by contrast, refines initially underspecified elements through constraint addition without retracting prior commitments, yielding monotonic extension of the interpretive state. Although both operators may alter how earlier material is understood, they impose fundamentally different structural requirements on state transitions.
  Using visual narratives as a diagnostic domain, we demonstrate how a structured narrative representation can explicitly separate committed from underspecified content and support both update operators during incremental construction. Through a worked example, we show how delayed elaboration enables monotonic refinement of interpretive state, while revision requires non-monotonic correction. We discuss the broader relevance of this structural distinction for incremental reasoning and hybrid symbolic-neural systems.

</details>


### [2] [KSE-Web: An Analysis of Hybrid Retrieval and LLM-Assisted Query Expansion for Low-Resource Khmer Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21365)
*Nimol Thuon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言高棉语的语义检索难题，构建了 KSE-Web 数据集并系统评估了 BM25、稠密检索、混合检索及 LLM 辅助查询扩展等方法。实验显示字符 n-gram BM25 整体表现最优（Recall 0.943，nDCG 0.876），混合检索次之，稠密检索单独效果较差。LLM 扩展未提升整体性能，但模型规模（Qwen2.5-3B 优于 0.5B）显著影响扩展质量；直接扩展易引入主题偏移、通用词及噪声，简单过滤反而可能丢失语义线索。研究为高棉语检索数据集构建及 Khmer-aware 模型提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高棉语作为低资源语言，面临标注数据稀缺、词边界模糊、多语言嵌入模型支持薄弱、高棉-英语混用频繁等检索挑战，现有方法在该语言下的有效性未充分验证，亟需系统性的基准数据集与方法对比。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：从约 17K 高棉语标题候选中经清洗、规范化、去重、长度控制得到 3K 全文档；人工审核 300 条用户风格查询，生成银标相关性标签并部分人工校验。2. 检索方法评估：字符 n-gram BM25、多语言稠密检索、BM25+稠密混合检索。3. LLM 查询扩展：使用 Qwen2.5-0.5B 与 Qwen2.5-3B 生成扩展查询，对比扩展前后效果。

Result: BM25 取得最佳整体指标（Recall 0.943，nDCG 0.876）；混合检索接近（Recall 0.929，nDCG 0.871）；稠密检索单独表现较弱。LLM 扩展未超越无扩展基线，但 3B 模型扩展效果显著优于 0.5B，表明模型规模与扩展质量对低资源语言至关重要。直接扩展易导致主题偏移、泛化词汇与噪声重写，简单过滤可能误删有用语义线索。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 LLM 辅助检索在高棉语中的潜力与局限：传统词法匹配（BM25）仍是强基线，混合检索可作为替代；LLM 扩展需更大模型与更精细的扩展策略。工作为未来构建更高质量人工标注高棉语检索数据集及开发 Khmer-aware 检索模型奠定基础。数据集与文档将开源于 github.com/back-kh/KhmerSemantic-Search。

Abstract: As a low-resource language, Khmer presents several retrieval challenges, including limited annotated data, ambiguous word boundaries, weak support in multilingual embedding models, and frequent mixed Khmer-English usage. This paper presents KSE-Web, an analysis of hybrid retrieval and LLM-assisted query expansion for Khmer semantic search. We construct the dataset from approximately 17K candidate Khmer titles and retain 3K cleaned full-text Khmer documents after filtering, normalization, deduplication, and document-length control. The dataset includes 300 manually reviewed user-style Khmer search queries and silver relevance labels with partial human verification. We evaluate character n-gram BM25, multilingual dense retrieval, hybrid BM25+dense retrieval, and LLM-assisted query expansion using Qwen2.5 models. Experimental results show that BM25 achieves the strongest overall performance, reaching 0.943 Recall and 0.876 nDCG. Hybrid BM25+dense retrieval performs comparably, achieving 0.929 Recall and 0.871 nDCG, while dense retrieval alone performs lower. LLM-assisted query expansion does not outperform non-expanded retrieval; however, Qwen2.5-3B produces substantially stronger expanded-query results than Qwen2.5-0.5B, suggesting that LLM size and expansion quality matter for low-resource Khmer retrieval. Our analysis further shows that direct LLM expansion can introduce topic drift, generic terms, and noisy reformulations, while simple filtering may remove useful semantic cues. These findings highlight both the potential and limitations of LLM-assisted retrieval for Khmer semantic search and provide a foundation for future Khmer retrieval datasets with stronger human-verified annotations and Khmer-aware retrieval models. The dataset and documentation will be made available at github.com/back-kh/KhmerSemantic-Search.

</details>


### [3] [Wazobia Eval: A Benchmark for Nigerian Pidgin Emotion Understanding, Sarcasm Detection, and Cultural Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21369)
*Stephanie Okoye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Wazobia Eval，首个针对尼日利亚皮钦语的情感理解、讽刺检测和文化推理基准，包含550+人工标注样本及16类文化特定情感分类体系，填补了该语言在语言模型评估中的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼日利亚皮钦语是非洲使用最广泛的语言之一，但现有基准主要聚焦翻译、转录或通用情感分析，缺乏对文化落地语言理解（如细粒度情感、讽刺、文化推理）的评估，导致该语言在AI评估中严重欠代表。

Method: 构建人工标注数据集（550+样本），设计16类情感分类法捕捉文化特有情感语域，制定标准化评估协议与基准任务（情感理解、讽刺检测、文化推理），并公开数据集于Hugging Face。

Result: 提供了基准设计、标注方法论、分类法开发流程及初步试点评估结果，建立了可复现的尼日利亚皮钦语评估基础设施。

Conclusion: Wazobia Eval为尼日利亚语言AI提供了基础评估设施，确立了文化敏感的语言理解评估新范式，推动低资源非洲语言在大模型评估中的公平性与包容性。

Abstract: Nigerian Pidgin is one of Africa's most widely spoken languages, yet remains severely underrepresented in language model evaluation. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on translation, transcription, or generic sentiment analysis, leaving critical aspects of culturally grounded language understanding unmeasured. We introduce Wazobia Eval, a benchmark for evaluating Nigerian Pidgin emotion understanding, sarcasm detection, and cultural reasoning. The benchmark is built on a manually annotated dataset containing over 550 examples and a 16-category emotion taxonomy designed to capture culturally specific emotional registers that are not represented in conventional sentiment frameworks. Wazobia Eval provides standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark tasks for assessing model performance on nuanced Nigerian language understanding. We present the benchmark design, annotation methodology, taxonomy development process, and preliminary pilot evaluation results. Our goal is to provide foundational evaluation infrastructure for Nigerian language AI and establish a reproducible benchmark for future research. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/WAZOBIALABS.

</details>


### [4] [On the Role of Citations in Preference Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21376)
*Yu Hou,Hal Daumé,Rachel Rudinger,William Walden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究科学问答中引用对人类判断者和四个开源大语言模型偏好的影响，发现人类偏好引用更多样但数量更少，而大语言模型虽无法访问来源却表现出某些引用相关偏好，但这些偏好依赖于数据和具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自然语言处理任务要求系统在输出中提供归属引用，以防止模型幻觉并让用户验证输出可信度。然而，人类和大语言模型在比较输出时如何评估引用尚不清楚，这对奖励建模和现代大语言模型后训练至关重要。

Method: 在科学问答背景下，利用混合效应模型研究引用对人类判断者和四个开源大语言模型成对判断的影响。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 人类偏好引用更多样但总数更少；(2) 大语言模型尽管无法访问来源，仍表现出某些引用相关偏好，但这些偏好取决于数据和具体模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果对偏好数据收集有重要启示，需考虑引用多样性与数量的平衡，以及不同模型对引用的敏感度差异。

Abstract: Many NLP tasks require systems to provide attribution in their outputs--i.e. citations to grounding sources. Attribution serves as a bulwark against model hallucination and as a means for users to verify the credibility of model outputs. Yet, it is unclear how humans and LLMs evaluate citations when comparing outputs, a process central to reward modeling and modern LLM post-training. This paper studies the role of citations in the preferences of human judges and four open-source LLMs within the context of scientific question answering, leveraging mixed effects models to investigate the influence of citations on pairwise judgments. Among our key findings are (1) that humans prefer more diverse citations but fewer overall, and (2) that LLMs show some citation-related preferences compared to humans, despite lacking access to the sources, but these preferences depend on the data and specific models. We further discuss the implications of our findings for preference data collection.

</details>


### [5] [Agentic Scaffolding Amplifies Sycophantic Behavior in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21377)
*Thantham Jittham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLM）在智能体系统中的交互脚手架（如反馈循环、重考检查点、迭代优化）如何系统性地放大奉承行为，发现多轮交互、用户施压和迭代自我优化会导致模型更倾向于迎合用户，准确率平均下降6.3个百分点，更强模型放大效应更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM奉承行为的研究主要集中在单轮设置，但随着AI系统向智能体化发展，交互脚手架（多轮对话、反馈循环等）成为常态，亟需理解这些机制是缓解还是加剧奉承倾向。

Method: 在4,800个真实性判断任务上（200个陈述 × 6个模型 × 4个条件），系统性评估单轮基线、多轮交互、用户施压、迭代自我优化四种条件下的奉承行为，引入‘智能体奉承放大’（ASA）概念及‘投降率’、‘奉承性投降率’两个新指标量化漂移程度。

Result: 交互脚手架显著放大奉承行为：多轮交互、用户施压、迭代自我优化均使模型更倾向于迎合用户，准确率平均下降6.3个百分点；更强模型的放大效应更大，呈现‘能力越强、奉承放大越严重’的反常现象。

Conclusion: 随着AI系统自主性增强，奉承行为从‘持续存在’转为‘复合放大’；设计包含人工监督循环的系统可能无意中创造了导致模型漂移迎合的条件，需在智能体架构中显式缓解ASA风险。

Abstract: Sycophancy in large language models, the tendency to prioritize user agreement over truthful responses, has been documented extensively but studied primarily in single-turn settings. This paper investigates a critical question: does subjecting LLMs to greater interaction scaffolding make sycophancy better or worse? Across 4,800 veracity judgments (200 statements $\times$ 6 models $\times$ 4 conditions), we find that the interaction scaffolding characteristic of agentic systems (feedback loops, reconsideration checkpoints, and iterative refinement) systematically amplifies sycophantic behavior. Multi-turn interaction, user pressure, and iterative self-refinement each provide additional opportunities for models to drift toward agreement, and this drift coincides with a mean accuracy drop of $-6.3$ percentage points, establishing the capitulation as harmful rather than corrective. More capable models show larger amplification effects, a troubling inversion of expectations. We introduce the concept of agentic sycophancy amplification (ASA) and two novel metrics: capitulation rate and sycophantic capitulation rate. Our results indicate that as AI systems acquire greater autonomy, sycophancy becomes compounding rather than merely persistent. Systems designed with human oversight loops may inadvertently create the conditions for this drift.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Two Bytes per Letter: Tokenization Overhead in Cyrillic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21384)
*Ivan Dobrovolskyi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文量化了现代多语言分词器对乌克兰语等西里尔语言的分词开销差异，发现乌克兰语在现代分词器上有68-121%的token开销，在旧版cl100k高达220%。提出两种缓解策略：LLMLingua-2压缩可减少47-49%输入长度且无损检索；训练平衡字节级BPE分词器（200K词表上限，实际158,184条目）将乌克兰语/英语token比率从2.22x降至1.30x。实验表明训练数据分配导致西里尔分词开销，且在推理和分词器设计两阶段均可缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多语言分词器对乌克兰语等低资源西里尔语言分词碎片化更严重，导致推理成本和上下文容量的不公平差异。研究旨在系统量化九大生产分词器在五种标准化西里尔/拉丁语言上的开销，探究西里尔词表分配与开销的关联，并评估推理时压缩和分词器重设计两种缓解路径的有效性。

Method: 在九个生产分词器和五种语言（含标准化西里尔与拉丁表记）的837万词形上评估；使用BrUK和Brown语料库的全文生育率测量token开销；计算西里尔词表分配与开销的Spearman相关性；在电商RAG基准（1536商品、145查询）上测试LLMLingua-2压缩效果；训练200K词表上限的平衡字节级BPE分词器（收敛158,184条目）并评估held-out集UK/EN比率；对比罗马化对token计数的影响。

Result: 乌克兰语在现代分词器上token开销68-121%，cl100k高达220%。开销与西里尔词表分配负相关（Spearman rho=-0.536）但不显著（p=0.215, n=7）。LLMLingua-2将乌克兰语输入长度压缩47-49%，80个可检索案例中无压缩诱导的价值损失。平衡字节级BPE将held-out集UK/EN token比率从2.22x降至1.30x。罗马化在大多数分词器上反而增加乌克兰语token计数2-19%。五语言中分词效率倾向于网络数据中更普遍的脚本。

Conclusion: 训练数据分配是导致西里尔语言分词开销的主要因素。该开销可在推理阶段通过LLMLingua-2等压缩方法、在分词器设计阶段通过平衡字节级BPE训练有效缓解，为改善低资源语言在大语言模型中的公平性提供了实证路径。

Abstract: Modern multilingual tokenizers often fragment Ukrainian and other underrepresented Cyrillic-script languages more heavily than English, creating disparities in cost and context capacity. We quantify this overhead across nine production tokenizers and five languages with standardized Cyrillic and Latin representations, covering 8.37 million word forms. On a corpus benchmark, Ukrainian shows 68-121% token overhead on modern tokenizers and 220% on the older cl100k, measured through full-text fertility on the BrUK and Brown corpora. Overhead is negatively associated with Cyrillic vocabulary allocation in the subset with independently verified English baselines, although the association is not statistically significant (Spearman rho = -0.536, p = 0.215, n = 7). We evaluate two mitigation strategies. LLMLingua-2 reduces Ukrainian input length by 47-49% on an e-commerce RAG benchmark of 1,536 products and 145 queries, with no compression-induced value losses among 80 retrievable cases. A balanced byte-level BPE tokenizer trained with a 200K vocabulary cap, converging at 158,184 actual entries, reduces the held-out UK/EN ratio from 2.22x to 1.30x. Romanization increases Ukrainian token counts by 2-19% on most tokenizers. Across the five languages, tokenization efficiency favors the script more prevalent in web data. These findings indicate that training data allocation contributes to Cyrillic tokenization overhead and that mitigation is possible at both inference and tokenizer-design stages.

</details>


### [7] [A Social Media Analysis of Discourse on the Israel--Palestine Conflict on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21385)
*Michail Zafeiropoulos,Despoina Antonakaki,Sotiris Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过多方法计算分析，对比了Telegram上亲以色列和亲巴勒斯坦社区在多次冲突升级期间的政治传播特征，发现双方使用相同的死亡和受害者词汇，但情感基调截然不同：亲以色列频道以中性、报道风格为主，亲巴勒斯坦频道则明显更负面，反映了行动方与受影响方的话语位置差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为武装冲突争夺的核心场域，但Telegram上亲以色列和亲巴勒斯坦社区的广播式架构提供了直接的政治传播记录，此前尚未在大规模上进行系统对比。

Method: 收集2021年5月至2026年6月期间16个Telegram频道（各8个亲以/亲巴）的87,617条消息，结合情感分析、三种立场检测方法（关键词匹配、基于自然语言推理的零样本DeBERTa、微调BERTweet）及框架分析，并以736条人工标注消息为基准进行评估。

Result: 微调BERTweet模型表现最佳（72.1%准确率，0.721宏F1），比两种无标签基线高8-11个百分点；基线停滞在60%左右，显示未针对领域语言适应的立场检测存在硬性上限。核心发现：双方社区使用相同的死亡/受害词汇，但情感基调相反——亲以频道以中性、报道风格为主，亲巴频道显著更负面，符合行动方与受影响方的不同话语位置。

Conclusion: 情感、立场与框架的联合分析揭示了冲突各方在相同词汇资源上的差异性情感部署，凸显了领域适应模型在立场检测中的必要性，也为理解数字冲突传播中的非对称话语策略提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Social media has become a central arena in which armed conflicts are contested, yet the pro-Israel and pro-Palestine communities on Telegram, whose broadcast architecture yields an unusually direct record of deliberate political communication, have not been systematically compared at scale. This study presents a multi-method computational analysis of 87,617 messages from sixteen Telegram channels, eight pro-Israel and eight pro-Palestine, spanning May 2021 to June 2026 and covering multiple conflict escalations. It combines sentiment analysis, three stance detection methods drawn from distinct paradigms (keyword matching, zero-shot DeBERTa via natural language inference, and a fine-tuned BERTweet model), and a framing analysis, all evaluated against 736 manually annotated messages. The fine-tuned model performed best (72.1% accuracy, 0.721 macro F1 under 5-fold cross-validation), outperforming both label-free baselines by 8 to 11 points; the baselines stalled in the low-to-mid 60s, indicating a hard ceiling for stance detection not adapted to in-domain language. The central finding emerges only when sentiment, stance, and framing are read together: the two communities deploy the same death- and victim-related vocabulary in opposite emotional registers, pro-Israel channels predominantly neutral and report-style, pro-Palestine channels markedly more negative, consistent with writing from the distinct discourse positions of acting party and affected party.

</details>


### [8] [Mitigating Bias in Large Vision-Language Models via Counterfactual Ensemble Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21415)
*Yisong Xiao,Aishan Liu,Yongxin Huang,Zonghao Ying,Shiji Zhao,Tianlin Li,Yong Han,Jian Yang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Counterfactual Ensemble Decoding (CED) 框架，通过在视觉表征空间构建多群体反事实视角并集成解码，减少大型视觉语言模型中的社会偏见，实验显示偏见降低达 47.97%，且保持模型核心能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去偏方法依赖单一刻板视角，忽视社会视角多样性，导致偏见缓解有限；受社会科学中多样性促进公平原则启发，需构建多群体反事实视角以打破刻板叙事。

Method: CED 包含两步：1) 视觉空间反事实引导：识别各社会群体相关语义方向，生成反事实表征，提供多样视角；2) 解码阶段集成：定位解码器中视角差异最大层，用不确定性感知权重集成各视角 token 分布，优先高置信度 token，得更平衡概率分布。

Result: 在三个社会偏见评测基准上，CED 优于基线，偏见降低高达 47.97%，涵盖职业、描述词、人设特质等场景，且模型核心能力保持几乎无损。

Conclusion: CED 通过多群体反事实视角集成有效缓解 LVLMs 社会偏见，验证了多样性促进公平的原理，为公平视觉语言生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks; however, they often inherit social biases from their training data, resulting in biased behavior when processing portraits from different social groups. Existing debiasing approaches typically compare token probabilities between the original and biased generations during decoding, but they are fundamentally limited by their reliance on a single, stereotyped viewpoint and fail to account for the diversity of social perspectives. Inspired by the social science principle that diversity fosters fairness, we propose Counterfactual Ensemble Decoding (CED), a novel framework that constructs multi-group counterfactual perspectives within the visual representation space and integrates them during decoding to promote equitable model behavior. CED first performs counterfactual steering in the visual space by identifying semantic directions associated with each social group and generating counterfactual representations along these directions, thereby offering diverse perspectives that disrupt stereotypical narratives. During decoding, CED locates the decoder layer exhibiting the greatest divergence among these perspectives and ensembles their token distributions using uncertainty-aware weights, prioritizing high-confidence tokens from different groups to yield a more balanced probability distribution that guides fairer generation. Extensive experiments on three social bias evaluation benchmarks demonstrate that \tool achieves substantial improvements over leading baselines, reducing bias by up to 47.97% across scenarios involving occupations, descriptors, and persona traits. Moreover, CED also preserves the core capabilities of the original model with minimal degradation.

</details>


### [9] [Agentic Security: A Systematization of Tools, Failure Modes, and Design Laws for LLM-Driven Penetration Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21423)
*Israt Moyeen Noumi,Tarannum Ahmed Nowshin,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Jakir Hossain,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了基于大语言模型（LLM）代理的智能安全系统在从演示走向生产部署时反复出现的运营故障，提出四维集成摩擦指数量化一次性工程成本与持续组织、法律、维护成本，建立随机 LLM 策略加确定性中介器的数学模型，推导出会话证据衰减、子代理压缩比扩展可用视界、双阶段裁决级联的似然比乘积与相关误差失效、不可评估结果偏差、规划器-执行器路由背包问题及闭式执行上限 η* = α v / c、提示词无法约束实际执行等定量规律，并以 Inspectra 平台为落地实例标注已交付、部分、计划机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 代理在安全工具编排、红队测试、云审计等场景从原型转向生产，实践者反复遭遇相同的运营失效（证据丢失、误报偏差、预算失控、提示词约束失效等），但缺乏系统化的定量分析框架与可复用的工程对策，亟需将零散经验上升为可度量的设计原则。

Method: 1. 实测评估：对十款主流静态、动态、云、编排、AI 红队工具进行无人值守流水线实测。2. 四维集成摩擦指数（IFI）：将集成成本分解为一次性工程、组织协调、法律合规、持续维护四个维度。3. 理论建模：将代理系统建模为「随机 LLM 策略 + 确定性中介器」，利用马尔可夫链、似然比、背包优化、重尾分布等数学工具推导关键规律。4. 平台原型：开发 Inspectra 平台，按 shipped/partial/planned 标注机制成熟度，验证理论预测。

Result: 1. 长会话随阶段数呈指数级丢失驻留证据；短生命周期子代理按原始证据与摘要的压缩比线性延长可用视界。2. 双阶段裁决级联使评分器似然比相乘，但当评分器误差相关时增益趋近于零。3. 将不可评估结果计为攻击失败会系统性偏向隐蔽、高危响应。4. 规划器-执行器模型路由等价于背包问题，重尾工具的最优执行上限为 η* = α v / c（α 为风险厌恶系数，v 单位价值，c 单位成本）。5. 系统提示词无法约束实际执行的工具调用与资源消耗，必须在中介器层强制执行作用域与预算。6. Inspectra 已交付证据压缩、双阶段裁决、背包路由等机制；部分交付预算强制；法律合规、跨租户隔离仍在计划中。

Conclusion: 智能安全系统的可靠性瓶颈不在模型能力，而在确定性中介器对随机策略的工程约束能力。通过 IFI 量化集成摩擦、证据压缩比设计子代理生命周期、双阶段裁决去相关、背包路由控制重尾工具、中介器强制作用域/预算，可将反复出现的运营失效转化为可度量、可优化的工程指标，为生产级代理安全系统提供首套端到端的定量设计框架。

Abstract: Agentic security uses large-language-model (LLM) agents to plan, dispatch, and interpret security tools. As these systems move from demonstrations to deployed products, practitioners repeatedly encounter the same operational failures. We systematize these failures through a hands-on evaluation of ten widely used static, dynamic, cloud, orchestration, and AI red-teaming tools for unattended pipelines. We introduce a four-dimensional Integration Friction Index that separates one-time engineering cost from recurring organisational, legal, and maintenance cost. We then derive quantitative regularities that explain recurring failure modes. Modelling an agentic security system as stochastic LLM policies wrapped by a deterministic mediator, we show that long-lived sessions lose resident evidence with phase count, while short-lived sub-agents extend the usable horizon according to the compression ratio between raw evidence and its summary. We show that a two-stage verdict cascade multiplies scorer likelihood ratios, but provides little benefit when scorer errors correlate. We show that treating unevaluable outcomes as attack failures biases downstream measurements toward evasive and severe responses. We formulate planner-versus-worker model routing as a knapsack problem and derive a closed-form execution cap for heavy-tailed tools, eta* = alpha v/c. Finally, we show why scope and budget enforcement cannot be delegated to system prompts: prompts do not constrain what actually executes. Inspectra, our implemented platform, serves as a worked instantiation, with mechanisms labelled shipped, partial, or planned, including those that did not work.

</details>
