Abstract: Due to the selection of their training data, large language models (LLMs) perform best on standard-language inputs from languages using the Latin alphabet with large speaker populations, while disadvantaging other language varieties. Nevertheless, they can also be a versatile tool for preserving precisely such endangered languages. But do they also possess the necessary creativity and capacity for abstraction to decode phonetically encoded language the same way humans do?

</details>


### [11] [Forgotten in Weights, Recovered by Tools: Agentic Tool Unlearning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21544)
*Baicheng Chen,Zheyuan Liu,Jingyu Zhang,Kaize Ding,Ningshan Ma,Yue Huang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Agentic Tool Unlearning (ATU)框架，解决工具增强智能体中遗忘目标通过工具调用恢复的问题，通过两阶段方法实现参数化遗忘与轨迹级强化学习相结合，在保持正常工具使用的同时抑制目标追踪行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法仅抑制参数化记忆，但在工具增强智能体部署中，遗忘目标仍可通过工具调用（如网络搜索、检索、数据库查询）恢复，这种工具介导的恢复导致遗忘失效，需研究智能体工具遗忘以同时减少参数化回忆和工具介导恢复。

Method: 提出ATU两阶段框架：第一阶段应用参数化知识遗忘抑制直接回忆；第二阶段在模拟工具增强环境中进行轨迹级强化学习，惩罚目标追踪工具行为和最终答案泄露。

Result: 在RWKU和MUSE基准上，跨不同LLM架构实验表明ATU在目标遗忘与保留效用之间取得更好平衡，使遗忘在工具增强智能体部署下更鲁棒。

Conclusion: ATU通过参数化遗忘与工具感知强化学习相结合，有效解决工具介导恢复问题，为工具增强智能体场景下的LLM遗忘提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as tool-augmented agents, where responses can depend on tool calls and external observations rather than model parameters alone. This creates an evaluation mismatch for LLM unlearning: previous unlearning methods may suppress direct parametric recall, but an agent can still recover the same forget target through tools such as web search, retrieval, or database lookup. We identify this failure mode as tool-mediated recovery and study agentic tool unlearning, which aims to reduce both parametric recall and tool-mediated recovery while preserving normal tool use for retained knowledge. To address this challenge, we propose Agentic Tool Unlearning (ATU), a two-stage framework. The first stage applies parametric knowledge unlearning to suppress direct recall, while the second stage performs trajectory-level reinforcement learning in simulated tool-augmented environments to penalize target-seeking tool behavior and final-answer leakage. Experiments on RWKU and MUSE across different LLM architectures show that ATU achieves a better balance between target forgetting and retained utility, making unlearning more robust under tool-augmented agent deployment.

</details>


### [12] [Automating Multi-Hop RAG Evaluation via TRIAD: From Context Extraction to Validated Dataset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21558)
*Lorenz Brehme,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAD，一种三阶段自动化数据集生成方法，用于创建领域特定的问答数据集以评估RAG系统性能，解决了现有数据集（如HotpotQA）无法直接迁移到领域特定场景的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）的进步和RAG系统在工业界的采用，迫切需要领域特定的问答数据集来评估RAG系统在专有数据上的表现。现有数据集（如HotpotQA）基于维基百科知识，无法直接迁移到领域特定场景；全面评估RAG系统质量需要多跳查询和不可回答问题。

Method: TRIAD采用三阶段自动化流程：第一阶段为RAG系统的领域特定知识库生成问答（QA）对；第二阶段通过验证器在反馈循环中检查每个QA对；第三阶段为QA对扩展带有相关性标注的上下文文档，用于下游评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，生成的数据集在不同RAG设置下表现出与MuSiQue和HotpotQA相似的性能趋势，人工验证表明生成的问题适合评估领域特定RAG系统。代码和验证结果已开源。

Conclusion: TRIAD提供了一种有效的自动化方法生成领域特定评估数据集，能够捕捉多跳推理和不可回答问题等关键评估维度，为RAG系统在专有领域的部署和评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLMs and the adoption of RAG systems in industry have created a need for domain-specific question-answer datasets that can assess RAG performance on proprietary data. Existing datasets, such as HotpotQA, challenge current RAG systems on Wikipedia-based knowledge, but they cannot be transferred directly to domain-specific settings. A comprehensive evaluation of RAG system quality requires both multi-hop queries and unanswerable questions. This paper introduces TRIAD, a three-stage automated dataset generation approach. First, it generates question--answer (QA) pairs for the domain-specific knowledge base of a RAG system. Second, a validator checks each QA-pair in a feedback loop. Third, the QA pairs are extended with relevance-labeled context documents for downstream evaluation. We evaluate this approach against the established MuSiQue and HotpotQA datasets. The results show that the generated dataset exhibits similar performance trends across different RAG setups, while human validation indicates that the questions are suitable for evaluating a domain-specific RAG system. The code used to generate the dataset and all validation results are available in our GitHub repository(https://github.com/lorenzbrehme/triad).

</details>


### [13] [Evidence-State Reliability Under Controlled Degradation: Parser-Validity Divergence in a Multi-Stage LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21559)
*Naimur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出证据状态可靠性（ESR）作为多阶段LLM流水线中间证据质量的评估层，独立于解析器有效性（结构一致性）。实验表明：在证据退化条件下，结构一致性可能改善，但证据敏感的阶段成功率却显著下降；且检测退化证据与从退化中恢复是两码事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段LLM流水线在下游证据变得不完整、压缩或冲突时，仍可能保持结构有效。现有评估缺乏专门针对中间证据是否充分完整、有依据、内部一致且可用于阶段功能的指标。

Method: 使用GLM-5.2在60个消毒基础案例上，针对四种证据条件（干净、有损压缩、部分丢失、噪声冲突）通过决策、审计、升级三个阶段进行评估。共设计720次计划调用，保留713次消毒后的执行行。

Result: 九组匹配的退化减干净条件-阶段对比中，所有操作阶段成功率估计均为负，95%自举区间均低于零；所有九个解析器有效性点估计均为正（三个部分丢失区间含零）。在解析器有效的退化审计输出中，退化检测率在各退化条件下均为1.0，但虚假保证率非零；在解析器有效的退化升级输出中，恢复率在每个退化条件下均为0.0。

Conclusion: 评估流水线存在有界的可靠性层分歧：在相同受控干预下，结构一致性可定向改善，而证据敏感的阶段成功率却恶化。同时区分了退化证据的检测与恢复。结论仅限于评估的模型配置、流水线设计、选定消毒案例、评分程序和单次规模化运行。

Abstract: Multi-stage LLM pipelines can remain structurally valid even when evidence available to downstream stages becomes incomplete, compressed, or conflicting. This paper introduces and operationalizes Evidence-State Reliability (ESR), an evaluation layer concerned with whether intermediate evidence remains sufficiently complete, grounded, internally consistent, and usable for a stage's assigned function. ESR is evaluated separately from parser validity, which measures structural conformance.
  We evaluate the framework using GLM-5.2 on 60 sanitized base cases under four evidence conditions: clean, compressed-lossy, partial-dropout, and noisy-conflicting. Each condition was processed through decision, audit, and escalation stages. The design comprised 720 planned and ledgered calls, with 713 retained, sanitized execution rows.
  Across nine matched degraded-minus-clean condition-stage comparisons, all operational stage-success estimates were negative, and all 95% bootstrap intervals remained below zero. All nine parser-validity point estimates were positive, although the three partial-dropout intervals included zero. Among parser-valid degraded audit outputs, degradation detection was 1.0 in each degraded condition, while false-assurance rates remained non-zero; among parser-valid degraded escalation outputs, recovery was 0.0 in every degraded condition.
  The results show a bounded reliability-layer divergence in the evaluated pipeline: structural conformance can improve directionally while evidence-sensitive stage success deteriorates under the same controlled intervention. They also separate detection of degraded evidence from recovery. The conclusions are limited to the evaluated model configuration, pipeline design, selected sanitized cases, scoring procedure, and single scaled run.

</details>


### [14] [Can LLMs Truly Forget? Revealing Unlearning Gaps Through Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21606)
*Ayush Gupta,Hima Varshini Surisetty,Sreevidya Bollineni,Varad Ingale,Tuhina Tripathi,Abhishek Lalwani,Somya Chatterjee,Sadid Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Attack Success Rate (ASR)指标评估机器遗忘的对抗鲁棒性，发现标准指标下表现良好的微调方法在对抗提示下仍大量泄露目标信息，ASR与人工事实评估高度一致，强调必须引入对抗压力测试作为遗忘评估的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘基准主要在干净、非对抗查询下评估，无法确认看似遗忘的信息是否仍可通过策略性提示恢复，存在评估盲区。

Method: 在TOFU数据集上使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct统一评估基于提示和基于微调的遗忘方法，随后对标准指标表现强的方法进行对抗鲁棒性评估。引入ASR这一LLM-as-judge指标，衡量泄露分数超过0.2的对抗响应比例，跨八个攻击套件测试信息恢复情况，并进行人工审计验证ASR与人类事实判断的一致性。

Result: 多个微调方法Forget Quality超过0.91，但ASR在72.8%-84.3%之间，接近无保护基础模型的87.5%；干净多语言重述仅产生2.95%泄露。人工审计显示ASR二值决策与人工事实评估在10个案例中有7个一致，表明ASR提供了有用但不完美的行为可恢复性信号。

Conclusion: 仅凭标准指标的强性能不足以确立遗忘后的鲁棒性，对抗压力测试应作为遗忘评估的互补核心组件。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of targeted training data from a model while preserving its remaining capabilities, but evaluating whether such information has truly become inaccessible remains challenging. Existing benchmarks primarily assess unlearning under clean, non-adversarial queries, leaving open whether information that appears forgotten can still be recovered through strategic prompting. We address this gap through a unified evaluation of prompt-based and fine-tuning-based unlearning methods on TOFU using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, followed by an adversarial robustness evaluation of methods that perform strongly under standard metrics. We introduce Attack Success Rate (ASR), an LLM-as-judge metric that measures the fraction of adversarial responses whose leakage score exceeds $0.2$, and evaluate recovery across eight attack suites. Our results reveal a substantial gap between clean-query forgetting and adversarial robustness. Although several fine-tuning-based methods achieve Forget Quality above $0.91$, targeted information remains recoverable with ASRs between $72.8\%$ and $84.3\%$, close to the $87.5\%$ ASR of the unprotected base model. In contrast, clean multilingual reformulations yield only $2.95\%$ measured leakage. A manual audit further finds agreement between binary ASR decisions and human factual assessments in seven of ten cases, indicating that ASR provides a useful, though imperfect, signal of behavioral recoverability. These findings show that strong standard-metric performance alone is insufficient to establish robustness after unlearning and motivate adversarial stress-testing as a complementary component of unlearning evaluation.

</details>


### [15] [Mitigating Database Leakage in RAG Systems with Keyword-Grounded Fact Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21656)
*Ziliang Zhang,Yubo Zhu,Wei Tong,Jingyu Hua,Zijian Wang,Yuan Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出KFS-RAG，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）系统中提示词注入攻击的防御方法，通过关键词引导的事实重写来替换原始检索上下文，有效降低数据库泄露风险且保持回答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统易受提示词注入攻击，导致检索器或生成器泄露敏感数据库内容，现有防御手段不足，亟需一种实用且有效的缓解方案。

Method: 首先利用注意力回溯结合因果扰动机制从检索上下文中识别少量关键影响关键词；随后引导辅助大语言模型基于这些关键词从检索段落中生成紧凑的关键词落地事实；最后用这些经过筛选的事实替换原始上下文，使生成器在净化后的证据上运行。

Result: 实验表明，KFS-RAG在注入攻击下显著降低数据库泄露风险，同时保持回答的准确性与相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建安全可信的RAG系统提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for combining large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge sources. However, RAG systems remain vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, which may mislead the retriever or generator to expose sensitive database contents. To address this issue, we propose KFS-RAG, a defense that mitigates information leakage by reformulating the retrieved context. Specifically, our method first identifies a small set of influential keywords from the retrieved context via an attention rollout plus a causal perturbation mechanism. These keywords are then used to guide an auxiliary LLM to generate a compact set of keyword-grounded facts from the retrieved passages. Finally, the original context is substituted with these curated facts, ensuring that the generator operates on sanitized evidence rather than the raw retrieved text. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that KFS-RAG significantly reduces the risk of database leakage under injection attacks while maintaining response accuracy and relevance. This work highlights a practical pathway toward building secure and trustworthy RAG systems.

</details>


### [16] [LëtzCross: A Cross-Lingual Page-Level Benchmark for Multimodal Retrieval over Luxembourgish Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21714)
*Omar El Bachyr,Fred Philippy,Laura Maria Bernardy,Saad Ezzini,Jacques Klein,Tegawende Bissyande*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LëtzCross基准，用于评估跨语言页面级检索在卢森堡语PDF文档上的表现，对比了基于OCR的纯文本检索器与ColPali风格的页面图像检索器，并探索了单语和多语微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的页面图像检索器（如ColPali）在视觉丰富文档检索上表现优异，但在跨语言、低资源场景下的行为知之甚少，缺乏针对卢森堡语等低资源语言的基准和系统性评估。

Method: 构建LëtzCross基准：包含文本导向和视觉落地的QA对，查询语言覆盖英语、法语、德语和卢森堡语，文档页面以图像形式索引。对比OCR纯文本检索器与页面图像检索器，并进行单语（英语/法语/德语/卢森堡语）及多语微调实验。

Result: 页面图像检索器在所有查询语言上均优于纯文本检索器。微调具有跨语言迁移能力：单语微调中，法语微调在卢森堡语查询上平均表现最佳；多语微调包含卢森堡语时效果最强，显著提升卢森堡语查询的检索性能。

Conclusion: 在跨语言、低资源的PDF检索场景中，页面图像检索器优于传统OCR文本检索器；多语微调（特别是包含目标低资源语言）能有效提升检索性能，为低资源语言的RAG系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Recent page-image retrievers such as ColPali have improved retrieval over visually rich documents, yet little is known about how they behave in cross-lingual, low-resource settings. We introduce LëtzCross, a benchmark for cross-lingual page-level retrieval over Luxembourgish PDF documents, with document pages indexed as images and queries provided in English, French, German, and Luxembourgish. The benchmark combines text-focused QA pairs with visually grounded QA pairs, covering both textual and visual retrieval needs in PDF-based RAG. We use LëtzCross to compare OCR-based text-only retrievers with ColPali-style page-image retrievers and find that the latter perform better across query languages in this system-level comparison. We also examine single-language and multilingual fine-tuning. Fine-tuning transfers across query languages, with French yielding the highest mean performance on Luxembourgish queries among the single-language settings. In the multilingual setting, including Luxembourgish gives the strongest results and substantially improves retrieval for Luxembourgish queries.

</details>


### [17] [FCPRAG: Fusion-Controller Parametric Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Stable Multi-Passage LoRA Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21750)
*Jinchang Zhu,Jindong Li,Yi Ding,Xiaojian Nie,Rong Fu,Shuangyong Song,Haowei He,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FCPRAG（融合控制参数化RAG框架），通过轻量级控制器实现检索条件下的样本级适配器融合，解决多段落检索时证据融合的瓶颈问题，在多个QA基准上显著提升F1分数并降低调优成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化RAG（PRAG）通过段落专用LoRA适配器注入检索证据，减少了对长上下文提示的依赖。然而，当为同一查询检索多个段落时，证据级融合成为瓶颈：等权重融合会放大弱证据或冲突证据，而将检索信号转化为融合权重往往需要脆弱的全局调优。此外，单一数据集级温度在异方差检索不确定性下表现次优。

Method: FCPRAG引入轻量级控制器，预测每段落融合分数及样本级校准信号（混合门控和自适应温度），实现检索条件下的样本级适配器融合。训练采用合并感知监督，利用多适配器合并中每个适配器的边际贡献，仅需训练数据。控制器使融合在检索信号信息量大时保持选择性，在不确定性下保持保守性。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、PopQA、ComplexWebQuestions四个数据集及三种LLM骨干上，FCPRAG持续优于标准RAG和参数化RAG基线，F1提升高达4.65%（2WikiMultiHopQA）和7.55%（CWQ），同时降低调优成本，提升检索扰动下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FCPRAG通过样本级自适应融合控制有效解决了多段落检索中的证据融合难题，实现了选择性与保守性的动态平衡，在多跳问答任务上取得显著性能提升，且具备更低的调优开销和更强的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Parametric retrieval-augmented generation (PRAG) injects retrieved evidence into a large language model (LLM) through passage-specific LoRA adapters, reducing reliance on long in-context prompts. When multiple passages are retrieved for the same query, however, evidence-level fusion becomes a bottleneck: equal-weight merging can amplify weak or conflicting evidence, and translating retrieval signals into fusion weights often requires fragile global tuning. We propose FCPRAG, a fusion-controlled parametric RAG framework that adds a lightweight controller for retrieval-conditioned, sample-level adapter fusion. The controller predicts per-passage fusion scores together with sample-level calibration signals, including a mixing gate and an adaptive temperature, enabling fusion that stays selective under informative retrieval signals and conservative under uncertainty. FCPRAG is trained with merge-aware supervision derived from each adapter's marginal contribution within a multi-adapter merge, using training data only. We further show that a single dataset-level temperature is suboptimal under heteroscedastic retrieval uncertainty, motivating sample-level adaptation. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, PopQA, and ComplexWebQuestions (CWQ) across three LLM backbones show that FCPRAG consistently improves F1 over standard RAG and parametric RAG baselines, with gains of up to 4.65% on 2WikiMultiHopQA and 7.55% on CWQ, while also reducing tuning cost and improving robustness under retrieval perturbations.

</details>


### [18] [Evaluation Awareness in Language Models: Representation, Verbalization, and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21766)
*Farzaneh Heidari,Amin Memarian,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了语言模型的‘评估感知’现象，即模型能推断出自己正在被评估并据此调整行为。通过探测六个模型的激活空间、输出token的口头化表达以及操控因果效应，发现评估感知在所有模型的残差流中均可线性解码（AUROC≥0.7），但内部表征与口头化表达仅部分对齐；操控探测方向能改变口头化分数。在Olmo模型训练阶段对比中，评估感知在基础模型中已存在，经监督微调放大，之后保持稳定；而操控效应随训练阶段逐渐增强。结果揭示评估需考虑内部表征、口头化输出与操控效应之间的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能力与安全基准假设模型在测试时的行为能反映其部署时行为，但若模型察觉被评估并据此调整响应，该假设可能失效。这种‘评估感知’已在前沿与开放权重模型中观察到，但缺乏系统性研究。本文旨在全面探究评估感知在模型内部表征、口头化输出及操控响应中的表现及演化规律。

Method: 选取四个模型家族、三种规模共六个语言模型，采用三个指标：(i) 线性探测残差流中‘被评估’状态的可解码性；(ii) 使用LLM-as-judge评估输出token中评估感知的口头化程度；(iii) 沿探测方向操控模型以观察因果效应。对开放检查点Olmo模型，进一步在各训练阶段（基础模型、监督微调各阶段、后续阶段）重复上述测量。

Result: 1. 所有模型的残差流中均可线性解码评估感知（最佳AUROC≥0.7）。2. 内部表征与口头化表达仅部分对齐：相关性与互信息在某些设置下非零，但随模型、层、读出方式差异巨大。3. 沿探测方向操控能显著改变口头化评分。4. Olmo检查点对比显示：评估感知在基础模型已存在，经监督微调放大，之后保持稳定；操控效应则随训练阶段逐渐增强。

Conclusion: 评估感知在模型内部普遍存在且可线性提取，但其内部表征、口头化输出与操控响应三者之间存在显著脱节。这提示未来的模型评估必须综合考量内部表征、外显输出及操控效应的差异，避免仅依赖单一维度的测试结果。

Abstract: Both capability and safety benchmarks rest upon the assumption that the behavior of language models undergoing a test is informative about their behavior in deployment. This assumption can fail, should models infer that they are being evaluated and condition their response on such context. This hypothesis, termed ``evaluation awareness'', has been observed in frontier and open-weight language models alike. We provide a systematic study of this phenomenon, by probing for it across six language models (from four families and three sizes) and three metrics. More precisely, we examine whether (i) being under evaluation is linearly represented within the models' activations space, (ii) it is verbalized in their output tokens (as scored by an LLM-as-judge), and (iii) steering causally affects their behavior. For the open-checkpoint Olmo models, we further test these measures at every training stage. In doing so, we report that evaluation awareness is linearly decodable from the residual streams of every model (best AUROC $\geq 0.7$). By contrast, these representations align only in part with verbalization: their correlations and mutual information are nonzero in some settings, yet vary substantially across models, layers, and readout choices. Nevertheless, steering along probe-derived directions can shift the verbalization scores. Finally, a comparison across the Olmo checkpoints reveals that evaluation awareness is already present within base models, becomes amplified throughout the stages of supervised fine-tuning, and remains stable thereafter---unlike the effects of steering, that grow more pronounced at every successive training stage. These results show the need for evaluations to account for the disjunction between what models represent internally, what they verbalize, and their steering.

</details>


### [19] [No One Model Catches Every Harm: Benchmarking Content Moderation Across Safety Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21775)
*Afshin Orojlooyjadid,Hitesh Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对53个大语言模型在11个数据集（分为四大类）上的安全能力进行了最全面的系统评估，发现大型前沿模型在某些类别上领先，但在其他类别上却显著落后于较小的专业模型，且所有模型家族在真实对话安全方面仍 largely unsolved，挑战了“规模越大越安全”的假设，并为模型选择提供了结构化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在实际部署中仍易生成有害内容（如对抗性越狱、隐性仇恨言论），现有的专业内容审核模型和通用大模型作为安全层，但针对不同类型有害内容哪个模型最适用尚无定论，亟需系统性、大规模的评估来指导模型选择。

Method: 系统测试53个模型在11个数据集（组织为四大类别）上的表现，采用prompt-only和prompt-response两种评估设置，涵盖对抗性越狱、隐性仇恨等多种风险类型，进行全面对比分析。

Result: 关键盲点：大型前沿模型在某一类别领先，却在其他类别显著落后于较小的专业模型；真实世界的对话安全在所有模型家族中仍 largely unsolved；规模并不单向保证安全性。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了“模型规模越大越安全”的假设，揭示了当前安全评估的盲区，并为社区提供了基于任务类型的结构化模型选择框架，推动更明智的安全模型部署决策。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, yet they remain vulnerable to generating harmful content. From adversarial jailbreaks that bypass safety filters to implicit hate that evades detection, the range of risks these models pose continues to grow. While both specialized content moderators and general-purpose LLMs are being used as safety layers, the question of which model is best suited for which type of harmful content remains unanswered. We present the most comprehensive evaluation of LLM safety capabilities to date, systematically testing \textbf{53} models across \textbf{11} datasets that we organize into four distinct categories. Our evaluation under both prompt-only and prompt-response settings uncovers critical blind spots: large frontier models that lead on one category fall significantly behind smaller, specialized alternatives on others, and real-world conversational safety remains largely unsolved across all model families. These findings challenge the assumption that scale alone ensures safety, and provide the community with a structured framework for informed model selection.

</details>


### [20] [Lexical Coupling in GUI Element Grounding: Sentence Embeddings Track Labels across Mobile and Web](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21794)
*Qijia Chen,Giulio Jacucci*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出GUI接地评估中基于嵌入相似度的方法常混淆可见标签恢复与语义接地，实验表明词法基线仍具竞争力，编码器增益有限，建议报告词法基线、标签类型分层及可部署融合诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GUI接地评估常将指令-元素嵌入相似度高视为语义接地证据，但可能被可见标签恢复混淆，需澄清评估中的混淆因素。

Method: 在三个移动和Web基准上，将每个动作视为同屏排序任务，对比五个现成单向量编码器与词法基线，分析编码器top-1命中率与词法排名、候选池大小、标签类型的关系。

Result: 词法基线在top-1上仍具竞争力，标签稀疏目标对纯文本方法较弱，编码器top-1命中可由词法排名等预测，编码器虽能恢复部分词法漏报，但可部署融合增益远小于目标感知oracle增益。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的评估易混淆可见标签恢复与语义GUI接地，应报告词法基线、标签类型分层和可部署融合诊断，并开源分析脚本与去文本面板。

Abstract: GUI grounding evaluations that expose UI elements as text metadata often treat high instruction-element embedding similarity as evidence of semantic grounding. Across three mobile and web benchmarks, we show that this interpretation is frequently confounded by visible-label recovery. Lexical baselines remain competitive at top-1, label-poor targets remain weak for text-only methods, and encoder top-1 hits are predictable from lexical rank, candidate-pool size, and label type. We evaluate each action as a same-screen ranking task, comparing five off-the-shelf single-vector encoders with lexical baselines. Encoders recover some lexical misses, but deployable fusion gains are much smaller than target-aware oracle gains. These findings show that embedding-based evaluations can conflate visible-label recovery with semantic GUI grounding. Embedding-based evaluations should therefore report lexical baselines, label-type stratification, and deployable-fusion diagnostics. Our released repository provides analysis scripts and detexted per-step panels: https://github.com/qijia123/lexical-coupling-release.

</details>


### [21] [More Computational Resources Do Not Ensure Higher Scholarly Impact: Evidence from Leading NLP Conference Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21806)
*Shuai Chen,Tong Bao,Jitong Peng,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了 2020-2025 年 ACL、EMNLP、NAACL 主会 13,921 篇论文的 GPU 使用情况与学术影响力的关系，发现 GPU 资源高度集中于少数论文，但仅能微弱解释引用和奖项差异，GPU 数量比硬件代际更稳健地预测影响力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP 研究日益依赖计算资源，但报告的 GPU 能力与学术影响力的对应程度尚不明确，需要系统量化二者关系。

Method: 从全文提取 GPU 型号和数量，将每篇论文最大配置标准化为可比的硬件能力指标，并关联引用、奖项、主题及机构元数据进行统计建模分析。

Result: GPU 报告率上升但仍不完整，能力提升主要来自新一代硬件和中规模多卡配置；前 20% 高 GPU 论文占据 83.9%-89.9% 总算力，却仅获 27%-32% 引用和 20%-33% 奖项；十倍算力增益仅使主题-年引用百分位提升 3.52 个百分点，R² 仅增 0.0042；GPU 数量对引用和奖项的正向关联比硬件代际更稳健。

Conclusion: 报告的 GPU 资源与学术影响力存在正相关，但单独作为解释变量的效力极低，无法充分解释研究影响力的差异。

Abstract: Computational resources are increasingly central to NLP research, but how closely reported GPU capability aligns with scholarly impact remains unclear. We analyze 13,921 ACL, EMNLP, and NAACL main-conference papers published between 2020 and 2025, using GPU resources as our operational measure of computational resources. From full texts, we extract GPU models and counts, standardize each paper's largest reported configuration into a comparable hardware-capability measure, and link these data to citation, award, topic, and institutional metadata. GPU reporting became more common but remained incomplete, while reported capability increased mainly through newer hardware generations and medium-scale multi-GPU configurations. Resource concentration substantially exceeded impact concentration: the annual top 20% of GPU-quantifiable papers accounted for 83.9%-89.9% of reported GPU capability, but only 27%-32% of citations and 20%-33% of paper awards. In adjusted models, a tenfold increase in aggregate reported GPU capability was associated with a 3.52-percentage-point increase in within-NLP topic-year citation percentile, but increased model R^2 by only 0.0042. GPU count showed more consistent positive associations with citation and award outcomes than newer hardware generation. Overall, reported GPU resources are associated with scholarly impact but provide little standalone explanation of research influence.

</details>


### [22] [MCite-RL: Towards Reliable Multimodal RAG via Citation-enhanced Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21808)
*Suifeng Zhao,Zida Liu,Xinyu Lei,Lei Sun,Jun Gao,Sujian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MCite-RL，一种基于代理强化学习的框架，通过迭代检索、推理和递归裁剪实现动态视觉引用，并结合过程级和结果级奖励联合优化答案准确性和来源可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG中的视觉引用对于MLLMs的可追溯性和可验证性至关重要，但现有RAG和SFT方法难以实现鲁棒的跨模态推理，导致引用不精确或引用与生成答案脱节。

Method: MCite-RL包含Agentic Refinement模块，采用迭代检索、推理和递归裁剪逐步缩小搜索空间，将引用转化为动态证据驱动推理过程；引入Citation-enhanced Reward机制，在强化学习范式中整合过程级和结果级反馈，联合优化答案准确性和来源可追溯性。

Result: 在Wiki-VISA、FinRAGBench-V、MMLongBench-Doc等基准上进行大量实验，证明MCite-RL有效实现了引用精度和答案质量的联合优化。

Conclusion: MCite-RL通过代理细化模块和引用增强奖励机制，解决了多模态RAG中视觉引用不精确和解耦问题，显著提升了引用精度和答案质量。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) with visual citation is crucial for ensuring the traceability and verifiability of MLLMs. However, current RAG and SFT-based methods struggle to achieve robust cross-modal reasoning, causing imprecise visual citations or decoupling between the citation and the generated answers. To address these limitations, we propose MCite-RL, a citation-enhanced agentic reinforcement learning framework designed for reliable multimodal RAG. MCite-RL introduces an Agentic Refinement module for visual citation that employs iterative retrieval, reasoning, and recursive cropping to progressively narrow the search space, transforming citation into a dynamic, evidence-driven reasoning process rather than a static step. Furthermore, we incorporate a Citation-enhanced Reward mechanism that integrates both process-level and outcome-level feedback within a reinforcement learning paradigm to jointly optimize answer accuracy and source traceability. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as Wiki-VISA, FinRAGBench-V, and MMLongBench-Doc demonstrate that MCite-RL effectively achieves the joint optimization of citation precision and answer quality.

</details>


### [23] [Convergence in Science, Divergence in Religion: Calibrated Framing Differences Across Wikipedia's Language Editions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21821)
*Hung-Hsuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出基线校准距离测量维基百科多语言版本中同一概念的框架差异，发现宗教领域差异最大、科技领域最小，政治概念中审查和难民分歧大而民主人权一致性高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注语言版本间的覆盖缺口，缺乏对同一概念在不同语言版本中框架差异的量化度量，需要一种能消除语言对编码器对齐偏差的跨语言框架距离指标。

Method: 选取150个Wikidata锚定概念，覆盖20个语言版本、4个领域及校准集，使用LaBSE、multilingual MPNet、CMLM三种多语言编码器获取嵌入，计算原始嵌入距离，再减去校准概念（化学元素、数字、颜色等跨文化指称稳定概念）在该语言对上的平均距离，得到基线校准距离。

Result: 校准后距离显著降低语言对特有的对齐差异及语系模式；三种编码器一致显示科学条目比校准条目更对齐，宗教领域距离最大、科技领域最小；概念级排序跨编码器高度一致；宗教在LaBSE下显著高于校准基线；政治领域中审查、难民等概念分歧集中，民主、人权等概念对齐度最高。

Conclusion: 基线校准距离有效量化了跨语言框架差异，揭示了领域层面和概念层面的系统性偏移模式，为跨语言知识一致性分析提供了可复用的基准和工具。

Abstract: When Wikipedia's language editions describe the same concept, how differently do they frame it? Prior work measures coverage gaps between editions; we measure framing distance for matched concepts. We analyze 2,799 valid articles from 3,000 possible concept-language observations, spanning 150 Wikidata-anchored concepts, 20 language editions, 4 domains, and a calibration set. Raw embedding distances reflect both content differences and how well the encoder aligns each language pair. Even among calibration concepts with stable cross-cultural denotations (e.g., chemical elements, numbers, colors), the largest language-pair mean distance is 3.6 times the smallest, and distances are typically smaller within language families. We define a baseline-adjusted distance (calibrated distance): the distance between two language versions of a concept minus the mean distance for calibration concepts in the same language pair. This adjustment substantially reduces pair-specific alignment differences and the language-family pattern. Across three multilingual encoders (LaBSE, multilingual MPNet, and CMLM), scientific articles align more closely than calibration articles, and all three rank religion first and science/technology last. Concept-level rankings are highly consistent across encoders (Spearman rho=0.75-0.79 for MPNet and CMLM relative to LaBSE). Religion lies significantly above the calibration baseline under LaBSE. Within politics, divergence concentrates on concepts such as censorship and refugee, while democracy and human rights are among the most aligned. Code, data, and per-language-pair calibration baselines are released.\footnote{https://github.com/hhchen1105/cross-linqual-concept}

</details>


### [24] [Do Large Language Models Perform Well on Comprehending Poetic Logic in Modern Chinese Poetry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21827)
*Tian Lan,Shanshan Wang,Zehua Duo,Jiang Li,Guanglai Gao,Derek F. Wong,Xiangdong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Peony基准，专门评估大语言模型对现代汉诗“诗意逻辑”的理解能力，实验表明当前主流模型在此任务上存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在通用NLP任务上取得显著进展，但对文学文本特别是现代汉诗的理解仍未被充分探索。现代汉诗具有独特的“诗意逻辑”，需要超越表层语义的整体性推理，而现有评估范式忽略了这一关键维度。

Method: 构建Peony基准，将诗意逻辑定义为跨三个层级（段、行、意象）的四个任务；选取六个主流大语言模型，在非思考与思考两种配置下进行系统评估与分析。

Result: 实验结果揭示了当前大语言模型在理解现代汉诗诗意逻辑方面的局限性，同时验证了Peony基准的有效性与必要性。

Conclusion: Peony填补了评估大语言模型诗意逻辑理解能力的空白，为后续相关研究提供了标准化基准与数据；数据与代码将公开以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved significant progress across a wide range of natural language processing (NLP) tasks, yet their ability to understand literary texts, particularly modern Chinese poetry, remains largely unexplored. The unique literary characteristics of modern Chinese poetry necessitate a distinct form of reasoning for effective comprehension. Unlike conventional texts that convey clear information, the unique "poetic logic" of modern Chinese poetry requires a holistic reasoning approach that goes beyond superficial semantic analysis to be understood. However, current evaluation paradigms largely ignore this critical dimension. To address this gap, we propose Peony, the first benchmark specifically designed for evaluating the poetic logic of modern Chinese poetry. We define poetic logic as four tasks across three levels, namely stanza, line, and imagery, and systematically evaluate and analyze six mainstream LLMs based on Peony. We evaluate these models under both non-thinking and thinking configurations. The experimental results reveal the limitations of current LLMs in understanding the poetic logic of modern Chinese poetry and validate the effectiveness and necessity of Peony. Our data and code will be available.

</details>


### [25] [Training a Knowledge Base: Supervised Structure Learning for Agent-Curated Document Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21829)
*Yu Pan,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将知识库视为非参数模型的方法，通过监督训练的策展代理根据问答对动态编辑知识库，实现了在两个无污染基准上的性能提升，且仅使用少量链接即可匹配现有方法的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成将文档库视为冻结输入，离线构建结构无监督且均匀索引整个语料库，缺乏针对问题所需结构的信号。

Method: 将知识库作为非参数模型，使用策展代理在监督问题上对当前存储进行回答，展示金标准答案后编辑存储，存储持久化并用于测试集评估；在KBGym和PhantomWiki两个基准上，通过四组重叠度递减的问题组探究泛化能力。

Result: 策展存储的优势随重叠度增长：无键共享时持平，共享两键时F1提升0.176，在训练问题上减少25%动作且F1提升0.294（两基准均显著）；以1913个链接匹配HippoRAG的196112链接增益，每覆盖语料库点的动作节省1.5倍、准确率增益2.1倍；准确率随索引覆盖语料库比例稳步上升。

Conclusion: 通过监督训练动态编辑知识库可高效提升检索增强生成性能，扩大训练问题覆盖范围能增强泛化能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats the document store as a frozen input, and the offline pipelines that do build structure over it build it unsupervised -- a whole corpus indexed at uniform effort, with no signal about which structure a question will need. We instead treat the knowledge base as a non-parametric model trained on (question, answer) pairs: a curator agent answers a supervised question against the current store, is shown the gold answer, then edits the store. The store carries forward, and we evaluate the curated store with a test set, on two contamination-free benchmarks: KBGym, a fictional-universe generator we release, and PhantomWiki. Generalization is probed with four question groups of decreasing overlap with the training set: the trained questions themselves, and unseen questions sharing both of their keys with training, one key, or neither. The curated store's advantage grows with overlap -- from parity where no key was shared, through +0.176 F1 where both keys were, to 25% fewer actions at +0.294 F1 on the trained questions, the one cell significant on both benchmarks -- while matching HippoRAG's gains with 1,913 links against its 196,112: per point of corpus covered, 1.5x the action saving and 2.1x the accuracy gain. Accuracy rises steadily with the share of the corpus the indexes cover, so training on more questions widens coverage, and with it the generalization.

</details>


### [26] [GUI-Primitives: Diagnosing Spatial Reasoning Failures in Vision-Language GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21832)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-Primitives基准，包含994个对比指令对，覆盖七种GUI空间关系，用于评估视觉语言模型将关系语言绑定到正确界面元素的能力。实验显示19个模型严格准确率最高仅32%，主要失败源于候选区域定位而非关系理解。标记候选区域可大幅提升准确率35-57个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法隔离模型是否将关系语言正确绑定到目标元素，缺乏对空间关系理解的系统性评估。

Method: 构建对比指令对基准，保持截图和锚点固定，仅改变关系表达，使正确目标在两个指定候选间切换。五位标注员验证子集一致性。测试19个视觉语言模型，采用非约束坐标预测，按落入候选区域分类评估。

Result: 模型严格点框准确率≤32%，60-92%预测落在两候选区域之外。条件落在候选区域内时，水平/垂直位置、邻近度、列表序数目标选择率0.82-0.90，但包含和遮挡关系不显著优于0.50。基准准确率与ScreenSpot-Pro相关性Spearman ρ=+0.74。标记候选区域可提升选择准确率35-57个百分点。

Conclusion: GUI-Primitives揭示当前模型主要瓶颈在于候选区域定位而非空间关系理解，为计算机使用代理的GUI交互能力评估提供了细粒度诊断工具。

Abstract: Computer-use agents ground natural-language instructions in screenshots to locate interface elements, yet existing benchmarks do not isolate whether models bind relational language to the correct element. We introduce GUI-Primitives, a 994-item benchmark of contrastive instruction pairs over seven spatial relations in graphical user interfaces (left/right, above/below, containment, alignment, proximity, list ordinal, occlusion). Each pair holds the screenshot and anchor fixed while changing the relation expression, so the correct target moves between two designated candidates. Five annotators validate a 196-item subset ($κ= 0.94$ well-formedness; $κ= 0.79$ target selection). Nineteen vision-language models reach at most $32\%$ strict point-in-box accuracy. Because models emit unconstrained coordinates, we classify each prediction by the candidate region it falls within. Predictions fall outside both candidates on $60-92\%$ of items. Conditional on falling within a candidate region, target selection reaches 0.82-0.90 for horizontal position, vertical position, proximity, and list ordinal, but does not differ significantly from 0.50 for containment and occlusion: most failures reflect candidate localization rather than relation understanding. Across ten models, benchmark accuracy correlates with ScreenSpot-Pro accuracy (Spearman $ρ= +0.74$), an exploratory association at this sample size. Marking the two designated candidates raises selection accuracy by 35--57 percentage points, an oracle diagnostic that supplies the candidate set rather than a deployable method. We release the benchmark, predictions, and code.

</details>


### [27] [RAG Collapse: LLM Responses Collapse When Retrieved Documents Are Self-Authored](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22118)
*Gregory Druck,Ethan Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现当LLM基于检索增强生成（RAG）系统检索自己生成的内容时，会发生类似模型崩溃的现象，称为“RAG崩溃”。实验表明79.6%的模拟以崩溃告终，甚至单个自我生成的参考文献就能触发崩溃，因为LLM对自身内容存在不成比例的引用偏好，且该偏好在控制参考质量后依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容在互联网中占比上升，LLM训练或检索时可能大量接触自身生成的内容，形成自我强化回路。先前工作已证明递归训练自身输出会导致模型崩溃，本文旨在探究RAG场景下LLM检索自身生成内容是否会导致类似崩溃。

Method: 设计三种模拟AI系统检索自身生成参考文献的实验范式，采用三个模型家族，使用1,019个信息检索提示词，共进行1,528次模拟，累计超过一百万次LLM API调用，系统评估RAG崩溃的发生率及触发条件。

Result: 79.6%（1,216/1,528）的模拟以崩溃结束。惊人地发现，即使仅有一个自我生成的参考文献也能触发崩溃，因为LLM表现出对自身内容的显著引用偏好，且该自我偏好在控制参考文献质量后依然持续存在。

Conclusion: RAG系统在检索LLM自身生成的内容时面临崩溃风险，这种自我偏好导致的崩溃不依赖于内容质量，提示在部署RAG系统时需警惕并设计机制缓解LLM对自身生成内容的过度依赖。

Abstract: LLM responses are based on the internet (via training or RAG), and AI is now used to generate a significant amount of content online (Paredes et al., 2026), creating the potential for a self-reinforcing feedback loop. Prior work has shown that when LLMs are recursively trained on their own output, they experience model collapse (Shumailov et al., 2024): responses become less diverse, and eventually no longer resemble the original training data. In this paper, we show that a similar collapse occurs if LLM-based AI systems retrieve references they authored using a search tool. We call this RAG collapse. We conduct extensive experiments with three types of simulations of AI systems retrieving references they generated, using three model families, and 1,019 information-seeking prompts, totaling 1,528 simulations and over one million LLM API calls, and find that 79.6% (1,216/1,528) of simulations end in collapse. Surprisingly, even a single self-authored reference can trigger collapse because the LLM disproportionately cites its own content. This self-bias persists even after controlling for reference quality.

</details>


### [28] [PUMA: A Polish Benchmark for Culturally Grounded Multimodal Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21853)
*Sławomir Dadas,Michał Perełkiewicz,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Bartłomiej Jaworski,Izabela Woźniakowska*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PUMA（波兰统一多模态评估）基准，包含900个人工设计任务，专门评估多模态模型在波兰语言和文化背景下的能力，涵盖文本、图像、音频及视觉丰富文档。大规模评估显示顶级商业模型在视觉问答表现优异，但多数模型在复杂音频和文档理解上表现不佳，揭示显著性能差距。作者开源评估框架以推动本地化多模态AI研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型评估主要集中在英语环境，缺乏针对非英语语言和文化背景的综合评测基准，无法全面反映模型在特定文化语境下的多模态处理能力。

Method: 构建PUMA基准：设计900个手工任务，覆盖文化理解与实际技能，涉及文本、图像、音频及视觉丰富文档四大模态。对前沿商业模型、开放权重模型及专用小模型进行系统评估，量化各模态及任务类型的性能表现。

Result: 顶级商业模型在视觉问答任务上取得高分，但绝大多数模型在复杂音频理解和文档理解任务上表现挣扎，不同模型类别间存在显著性能鸿沟，揭示了当前多模态模型在非英语文化语境下的局限性。

Conclusion: PUMA基准填补了波兰语言文化背景下多模态评估的空白，实验结果强调了本地化多模态基准的必要性。开源评估框架将促进针对特定语言文化的多模态AI研究与发展。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly moving beyond text processing, adding support for other modalities such as images and audio. While text understanding and generation have been extensively studied, multimodal data processing capabilities, particularly in the context of cultures and languages other than English, have not yet been evaluated comprehensively. In this paper, we propose PUMA (Polish Unified Multimodal Assessment), a novel benchmark of 900 hand-crafted tasks designed to probe the limits of multimodal models in the Polish cultural and linguistic context. The dataset evaluates both cultural understanding and practical skill in processing text, images, audio, and visually rich documents. Our extensive evaluation of frontier commercial models, open-weights models, and specialized smaller systems highlights a significant performance gap. While top commercial models achieve high scores in visual question answering, most models struggle with complex audio or document understanding. We open-source our evaluation framework to advance localized multimodal AI research.

</details>


### [29] [HiDiffTIR: Hierarchical Difficulty-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21863)
*Yucan Guo,Xiaohan Wang,Miao Su,Saiping Guan,Zhongni Hou,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Xiaolong Jin,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiDiffTIR 是一种分层难度感知的策略优化框架，用于多轮工具集成推理，通过在轨迹和步骤层面进行难度感知的信用分配，无需额外监督即可提升工具调用准确性和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的工具集成推理方法通常采用统一的轨迹级优势估计，同等对待所有正确的工具调用，忽略了不同轨迹和推理步骤的难度差异和学习价值，导致学习信号不够精确，难以区分简单和复杂的工具使用模式。

Method: 提出 HiDiffTIR 框架，在轨迹层面和轮次层面进行难度感知的信用分配，利用标准 RL 采样得到的组级统计量计算难度权重，无需额外监督信号，实现细粒度的策略优化。

Result: 在三个工具使用基准上进行的大量实验表明，HiDiffTIR 相较于强基线持续提升多轮 TIR 性能和工具调用准确率。

Conclusion: 难度感知的信用分配对于工具集成 LLM 智能体的有效策略优化至关重要，HiDiffTIR 验证了这一思想的有效性。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) is a fundamental capability for LLM agents to solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools iteratively. Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become the dominant paradigm for enabling this capability. However, existing approaches typically assign uniform trajectory-level advantages and treat all correct tool calls equally, ignoring the varying difficulty and learning value across trajectories and reasoning steps. This can lead to imprecise learning signals that do not adequately distinguish between trivial and challenging tool-use patterns. To address this limitation, we propose HiDiffTIR, a Hierarchical Difficulty-aware policy optimization framework for multi-turn TIR. HiDiffTIR performs difficulty-aware credit assignment at both trajectory and turn levels, enabling the policy to focus on more informative trajectories and harder reasoning steps. Notably, this fine-grained optimization is achieved without additional supervision, relying solely on group-level statistics derived from standard RL rollouts. Extensive experiments on three tool-using benchmarks demonstrate that HiDiffTIR consistently improves multi-turn TIR performance and tool invocation accuracy over strong RL baselines, highlighting the necessity of difficulty-aware credit assignment for effective policy optimization in tool-integrated LLM agents.

</details>


### [30] [Enrich-Retrieve-Rank: Scaling Capability Discovery Beyond In-Context Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22695)
*Nazib Sorathiya,Daniel Zhang,Bardiya Akhbari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种离线丰富元数据和在线检索-重排流水线，替代基于上下文的路由，以解决大规模MATS组件（模型、智能体、工具、技能）能力发现随规模增长性能急剧下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体生态系统包含数千个MATS组件，发现机制依赖上下文路由：将注册表（名称、提示、描述）放入提示词，由LLM选择候选并调用，失败则重试。随着注册表快速增长，这种模式性能严重退化，亟需可扩展的发现方案。

Method: 将能力发现重构为注册表上的搜索：1) 离线丰富步骤，将稀疏元数据转化为可搜索的画像；2) 在线检索-重排流水线，在不调用任何候选组件的情况下返回排序后的候选短名单。使用固定配置（相同的丰富、检索器、评分器权重）跨智能体、工具、技能注册表通用。

Result: 从N=10到7,278能力，上下文路由的Match@1从0.85暴跌至0.12；检索-重排流水线下降较缓和（0.81到0.39），因重排器在检索找到正确能力后仍能以0.70-0.87的概率将其排第一。Nova Micro扫描显示交叉点约N=500。全规模下流水线比Search&Pick高6.5个百分点，成本约减半；比Full-Ctx降低70倍成本。已在大规模多智能体平台作为默认能力发现层投入生产。

Conclusion: 检索-重排流水线显著优于基于上下文的路由基线，在大规模注册表下保持较高准确率并大幅降低成本，证明了将能力发现作为搜索问题的有效性，为大规模智能体生态系统提供了实用的生产级解决方案。

Abstract: Agent ecosystems now include thousands of MATS components (Models, Agents, Tools, and Skills), yet their discovery still relies on in-context routing. These systems read a registry (names, hints, or descriptions, as context budget permits), pick a candidate, invoke it, and retry on failure. This pattern degrades with scale, and registries are growing fast. We recast capability discovery as search over a registry by defining an offline enrichment step that turns sparse metadata into searchable profiles, and an online retrieve-then-rank pipeline that returns a ranked shortlist without invoking any candidates online. We show that from N=10 to 7,278 capabilities, in-context routing's top-1 accuracy (Match@1) collapses (0.85 to 0.12), while retrieve-then-rank degrades more gently (0.81 to 0.39) because its reranker still ranks the right capability first 0.70-0.87 of the time once retrieval finds it. In the Nova Micro sweep, the crossover is around N=500. We compare against two in-context baselines. Full-Ctx puts the whole registry in the prompt and asks the LLM to pick. Search&Pick gives the LLM a search tool to narrow candidates before it picks. At full scale the pipeline leads Search&Pick by 6.5 percentage points (pp) on Match@1 at about half the cost. It reduces cost 70x versus Full-Ctx. We use a fixed configuration (same enrichment, retriever, and scorer weights) across agent, tool, and skill registries. The pipeline runs in production as the default capability-discovery layer of a large-scale multi-agent platform.

</details>


### [31] [The Chase Is the Curriculum, the Capture Anchors the Credit: Pursuit-Evasion Self-Play for Zero-Data LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21871)
*Jing Yu,Shengchao Chen,Yiyun Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LURE框架，将零数据自我博弈重构为追逐-规避博弈，通过规避者在难度轴上定位任务、追捕者获得密集过程奖励，实现无需人工标注数据的强化学习推理训练，在多个推理环境和骨干模型上超越基线，且统一模型在OOD零样本泛化上表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习依赖大量人工策划任务集合；零数据自我博弈虽移除此依赖，但现有方法仅通过事后探测和拒绝筛选任务，未学习任务在难度轴上的最优定位，且仅使用稀疏终端奖励，导致训练效率低、泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出LURE（追逐-规避博弈框架）：1) 规避者（Evader）在各环境难度轴上定位任务，使其恰好处于追捕者能力边缘；2) 规避者采用捕获前沿奖励，峰值出现在追捕者恰好在一半轨迹中捕获时，将‘勉强可捕获’转化为学习到的定位策略；3) 追捕者（Planner-Executor）获得捕获锚定的密集过程奖励，将单调验证器进度与终端捕获联合组归一化，并引入轮次锚定KL散度约束保证协同演化稳定性。

Result: 在三个可验证推理环境和三个骨干模型族上，LURE在统一/专家设置下均优于先进基线；统一模型在三个任务族的九个保留基准上，OOD零样本准确率聚合表现超过所有训练基线。

Conclusion: LURE通过追逐-规避博弈机制，实现了无需人工数据的自适应难度课程学习和密集过程监督，显著提升了大语言模型推理能力的训练效率和零样本泛化性能，为零数据强化学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become the dominant recipe for improving large language model reasoning, yet it presumes large human-curated task collections. Zero-data self-play removes this dependency, but existing methods vet learnability only by probing candidates and rejecting post hoc, never learning where along an environment's difficulty axis to place a task, and credit the solver with sparse terminal rewards alone. We recast zero-data self-play as a pursuit-evasion game: in LURE, an LLM evader positions tasks along each environment's difficulty axis to stay one step ahead of a planner-executor pursuer that hunts it down through verifiable interaction. The evader is trained on a capture-frontier reward that peaks when the solver captures it on exactly half of its rollouts, turning barely catchable into a learned positioning strategy rather than a hand-tuned rejection band. The pursuer earns capture-anchored dense process credit, in which monotone verifier progress is group-normalized jointly with the terminal capture under a round-anchored KL that keeps the co-evolution stable. Across three verifiable reasoning environments and three backbone families, LURE outperforms advanced baselines under unified/specialist settings, while the unified model attains stronger aggregate OOD zero-shot accuracy than all trained baselines across nine held-out benchmarks from three task families.

</details>


### [32] [BanglaVeilGuard: Cross-Script Safety Benchmarking and Lightweight Guardrails for Bangla Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21880)
*Md. Rakibul Hassan,Muhammad Iqbal Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BanglaVeilGuard，一个面向孟加拉语的轻量级安全基准与提示词防护器，覆盖六种语言形式（标准、罗马化、Banglish、代码混合、噪声、方言），通过非破坏性多视图归一化与提示风险分类器在生成前拦截恶意输入，在多个目标模型上将攻击成功率从 93.8–100% 降至 6.3% 左右，不安全召回率达 88.5%，但方言与噪声良性提示存在过度拒绝问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以英语为中心或标准脚本的基准无法覆盖孟加拉语用户常用的跨脚本、多拼写、代码混合及方言变体，导致孟加拉语大模型安全评估严重缺位，亟需一种原生支持异构语言形式的基准与即插即用防护机制。

Method: 构建含 2,366 条质量过滤提示的 BanglaVeilGuard 基准（含 354 条举出评估集），涵盖不安全、安全、安全敏感三类请求；采用非破坏性多视图归一化将六种语言形式映射到统一表示，配合提示风险分类器与阈值化前生成门控，在不修改模型权重的情况下即时拦截高风险提示。

Result: 在 Claude Opus 4.8、BanglaLLama、TituLLM 等模型上，防护后攻击成功率从 93.8–100% 降至 6.3%；TigerLLM-1B 加装防护后准确率 78.2%、ASR 8.8%；不安全召回率 88.5%，显著高于纯提示词基线；主要代价是方言与噪声良性提示的过度拒绝，揭示了安全-有用性权衡前沿。

Conclusion: BanglaVeilGuard 证明了面向低资源、多脚本语言的原生安全基准与轻量级提示词防护可大幅降低攻击成功率，同时保持模型权重不变；后续工作需针对方言与噪声场景优化召回与拒绝平衡，推动孟加拉语大模型安全部署。

Abstract: Bangla large language model (LLM) safety is difficult to evaluate with English-centric or standard-script benchmarks because Bangla users routinely write across scripts, spellings, code-mixed forms, and regional registers. This paper presents BanglaVeilGuard, a compact Bangla-first safety benchmark and lightweight prompt guard for six language forms: standard Bangla, Romanized Bangla, Banglish, code-mixed Bangla--English, noisy Bangla, and dialectal Bangla. The benchmark contains 2,366 quality-filtered prompts and a held-out 354-prompt evaluation split spanning unsafe, safe, and safe-sensitive requests. BanglaVeilGuard uses non-destructive multi-view normalization with a prompt-risk classifier and thresholded pre-generation gate, allowing it to screen prompts for heterogeneous target models without changing their weights. Across target-model families, guarded runs reduce attack success under deterministic response scoring from 93.8--100.0\% to 6.3\% for Claude Opus 4.8, BanglaLLama, and TituLLM; TigerLLM-1B with BanglaVeilGuard achieves 78.2\% accuracy with 8.8\% ASR. The prompt guard also attains 88.5\% unsafe recall, substantially above the evaluated prompt-only guard baselines. The main remaining cost is over-refusal on dialectal and noisy benign prompts, revealing a concrete safety-helpfulness frontier for Bangla LLM deployment.

</details>


### [33] [Better Retrieval, Worse Robustness: How Multi-hop RAG Amplifies Upstream ASR Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22872)
*Zhenghua Bao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究语音应用中ASR错误对检索增强生成(RAG)的影响，实验发现实体图链接和迭代重述两种扩展虽提升绝对F1，但放大了ASR错误导致的性能下降，主要失效模式为查询实体损坏，轻量级缓解措施效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音应用将口语查询通过ASR转文本后送入检索模块，ASR错误作为固定上游约束进入流水线。现有RAG扩展（实体图链接、迭代重述）是否能吸收或放大这些错误尚未充分研究。

Method: 使用神经TTS合成四种英语口音，在三个多跳问答基准（HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA、MuSiQue）上评估四种RAG配置（含朴素密集检索、实体图链接、迭代重述及其组合），对比干净文本 oracle。

Result: 结构更丰富的配置在ASR输入下保持更高绝对F1，但组合扩展使清洁文本到最高WER口音的F1差距比朴素密集检索扩大36-67%。主导失效模式为查询实体损坏，占2WikiMultiHopQA上所有方法降级案例的87-96%。两种轻量级表面形式缓解措施无法消除大部分差距，表明下游检索结构放大了残留实体错误。

Conclusion: ASR错误中的实体损坏被下游RAG结构放大，现有扩展反而加剧误差传播，需从实体鲁棒性角度改进语音RAG系统。

Abstract: Speech-based applications pass spoken queries through automatic speech recognition (ASR) before any retrieval module, so ASR errors enter the pipeline as a fixed upstream constraint. We empirically test whether two extensions to standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), entity-graph linking and iterative reformulation, absorb or amplify these errors. Using four English accents synthesized through neural TTS, we evaluate four RAG configurations on three multi-hop QA benchmarks (HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA and MuSiQue) against a clean-text oracle. Although the structurally richer configurations generally retain higher absolute F1 under ASR input, both extensions amplify the error: the F1 gap from clean text to the highest-WER accent is 36-67% larger under their combination than under naive dense retrieval, on all three benchmarks. The dominant failure mode is corruption of one or more query entities, accounting for 87-96% of degradation cases on 2WikiMultiHopQA across all four methods. Two lightweight surface-form mitigations leave most of the gap intact, indicating that downstream retrieval structure amplifies remaining entity errors. We release code and data at https://github.com/Continuum-AI-Corp/spoken-multihop-rag .

</details>


### [34] [Modeling Claim Dependency Structure for Patent Litigation Prediction with Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21924)
*Takao Arai,Hiroyasu Inoue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ClaimGAT，一种基于图注意力网络的专利诉讼风险预测模型，通过独立编码每项权利要求、构建有向依赖图并利用注意力聚合，实现了 AUC-ROC 0.818 与前 10% 提升 4.89 倍的预测性能，并揭示高风险专利中结构选择与内容敏感性的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 BERT 的专利诉讼风险预测模型存在两大根本局限：平铺序列编码丢失了独立权利要求与从属权利要求间的依赖结构（该结构在法律上决定专利保护范围）；将整组权利要求喂入单一编码器会丢弃法律关键文本。因此，需要一种能保留权利要求依赖图结构并逐项编码的方法，利用授权时即可观测的信息实现早期风险识别。

Method: ClaimGAT 包含四个核心步骤：1) 使用预训练语言模型独立编码每条权利要求文本；2) 根据从属关系构建有向权利要求依赖图；3) 采用图注意力卷积层（GATConv）在图上传播信息，捕捉结构依赖；4) 通过注意力聚合模块对独立权利要求进行加权汇总，输出诉讼风险分数与逐项门控权重，支持事后结构分析。在 134 万份 USPTO 实用专利上进行六模型消融实验，验证各组件的独立贡献。

Result: ClaimGAT 仅使用授权时可观测信息，达到 AUC-ROC 0.818，在前 10% 高风险专利中实现 4.89 倍提升。消融实验表明：逐项编码、图连接、注意力机制与注意力聚合均提供独立且可叠加的预测增益。模型还揭示高风险专利中结构选择（图拓扑）与内容敏感性（文本特征）呈现分离趋势，符合防御性撰写策略。

Conclusion: ClaimGAT 通过显式建模权利要求依赖图并结合注意力聚合，显著提升了专利诉讼风险的早期预测精度，并提供可解释的逐项重要性权重。研究证实了结构与内容特征的互补价值，为专利组合管理、风险定价及防御性撰写策略分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Patent litigation imposes substantial costs on firms and distorts R&D incentives, making early risk identification a practically important task. While prior work has applied BERT-based models to patent claim text, two fundamental limitations remain: flat sequence encoding loses the dependency structure between independent and dependent claims that legally determines patent scope, and feeding the entire claim set to a single encoder discards legally critical text. A six-model ablation on 1.34 million USPTO utility patents confirms that per-claim encoding, graph connectivity, attention, and Attentional Aggregation each provide independent, additive predictive value. We propose ClaimGAT, a Graph Attention Network that encodes each claim independently, constructs a directed claim dependency graph, processes it with GATConv layers, and aggregates independent claims via Attentional Aggregation to yield both a litigation risk score and claim-level gate weights that enable post-hoc structural analysis. ClaimGAT achieves an AUC-ROC of 0.818 and a lift of 4.89x at the top 10%, using only information observable at the time of patent grant. It reveals a tendency in high-risk patents for structural selection and content sensitivity to diverge, a pattern consistent with defensive claim drafting.

</details>


### [35] [TSWAP: A Multilingual Retrieval-Augmented Thai Wellness Advisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22917)
*Pornthep Ukosaramig,Kobkrit Viriyayudhakorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TSWAP 是一个已部署的八语言对话式健康顾问，基于泰国传统医学和认证健康服务提供者的验证知识库，通过检索增强生成（RAG）实现落地。系统采用未修改的开放权重 LLM (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B)，结合混合稠密-稀疏检索器、交叉编码器重排序、首轮查询分类器强制工具检索、基于规则的安全层以及零样本翻译后检索策略，实现多语言服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决泰国传统医学与健康服务领域缺乏可靠、多语言、可落地的对话式 AI 系统的问题，同时验证 RAG 在专业医疗领域的有效性，并探索部署中量化、检索路由等工程挑战。

Method: 1. 使用约 3.06 万文本块的泰语索引，采用混合稠密-稀疏检索器配合交叉编码器重排序；2. 首轮查询分类器强制实体查找走工具检索；3. 基于规则的安全层限制医疗范围并接入泰国急救路由；4. 八语言零样本服务采用翻译后检索策略；5. 发布首个泰国传统医学/健康检索基准（50 问，Recall@5=0.88）、生产 QA 日志（259 例测试-重测通过率 91.1%）及 71 问无检索探针实验。

Result: 1. 基准测试 Recall@5 达 0.88；生产环境测试-重测一致性 91.1%。2. 消融实验显示：无安全提示时模型会生成完整用药剂量表并响应超范围请求；无知识库时无法给出可验证的服务提供者推荐。3. 发现两个可迁移的部署经验：英文校准的 4-bit AWQ 量化会破坏泰语声调标记；强制检索路由对可靠落地至关重要。

Conclusion: TSWAP 验证了在专业医疗领域通过 RAG 落地开放权重 LLM 的可行性，强调了安全层、知识库落地、强制检索路由以及量化对低资源语言影响的重要性，为多语言医疗 AI 部署提供了可复用的工程范式和评估基准。

Abstract: We present TSWAP, a deployed eight-language conversational wellness advisor grounded, via retrieval-augmented generation, in a verified knowledge base of Thai traditional medicine and certified wellness providers. An unmodified open-weight LLM (Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on vLLM) is grounded on a ~30.6K-chunk Thai index by a hybrid dense-sparse retriever with cross-encoder reranking; a first-turn query classifier forces tool-based retrieval for entity lookups; a rule-based safety layer enforces medical scope and Thai emergency routing; and all eight languages are served zero-shot with translate-then-retrieve. We release the first Thai traditional-medicine/wellness retrieval benchmark (50 questions with gold document IDs; Recall@5 = 0.88), production QA logs (91.1% test-retest pass over 259 cases), and a 71-question frontier no-retrieval probe showing what each grounding pillar contributes: without the safety prompt the backend model family produced a full drug-dosing schedule and complied with out-of-scope requests, and without the knowledge base it produced zero verifiable provider recommendations. We further report two transferable deployment findings: English-calibrated 4-bit AWQ quantization corrupts Thai tone marks, and forced-retrieval routing is necessary for reliable grounding.

</details>


### [36] [EDGE: Experience-Distillation for Guided Exploration in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21946)
*Can Xie,Yuyi Zhou,Wen Yang,Ziyi zhang,Siyao Song,Yingzhuo Deng,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 EDGE 框架，将检索到的历史经验作为训练时的临时脚手架，通过经验条件与无经验轨迹对比估计正增益，再用反向 KL 蒸馏内化到策略参数中，并配合共进化经验库动态更新，在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上比 GRPO 提升 8.3/12.5 个成功率点，且移除外部经验后仍保留 96% 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果的 RL（如 GRPO）虽能解决长程任务，但交互轨迹中可复用的探索模式在单次策略更新后即被丢弃；现有经验增强方法仅在推理时检索历史指导，未考虑策略能力的演进，且产生对外部检索的持久依赖。

Method: EDGE 将每组 rollout 分为经验条件轨迹与无经验轨迹，无需额外采样即可估计并仅保留正边际增益；随后在策略自身的经验支持集上用反向 KL 目标将诱导行为蒸馏回基础策略；同时维护一个共进化经验库，从新出现的失败模式合成指导并随策略演进剔除过时条目。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 基准上，7B 规模下 EDGE 相对 GRPO 成功率分别提升 8.3 与 12.5 个百分点；推理时移除外部经验后，仍保留 96.0% 的脚手架性能。

Conclusion: EDGE 通过训练时脚手架与蒸馏内化机制，有效将历史探索经验转化为策略参数化知识，消除推理期对外部检索的依赖，显著提升 LLM 智能体在复杂任务上的泛化与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with outcome-based objectives such as GRPO enables LLM-based agents to solve complex, long-horizon tasks, yet the reusable exploration patterns embedded in interaction trajectories are largely discarded after a single policy update. Existing experience-augmented approaches retrieve historical guidance at inference time, but they apply experiences without accounting for the policy's evolving capability and create persistent dependencies on external retrieval. We propose EDGE (Experience-Distillation for Guided Exploration), a framework that treats retrieved experiences as temporary training-time scaffolds and progressively internalizes their benefits into the parametric policy. Concretely, EDGE partitions each rollout group into experience-conditioned and experience-free trajectories to estimate and admit only positive marginal gains without extra sampling, then distills the induced behavior into the base policy via a reverse-KL objective on its own empirical support. A co-evolutionary experience bank further synthesizes guidance from emerging failure modes and prunes obsolete entries as the policy evolves. On ALFWorld and WebShop, EDGE improves over GRPO by 8.3 and 12.5 success-rate points at the 7B scale and retains 96.0% of its scaffolded performance when external experiences are removed at inference time. The code is available at https://github.com/xvolcano02/EDGE.

</details>


### [37] [Aligning Biomedical Texts and Knowledge Graphs: A Systematic Comparison of Lightweight Alignment Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23214)
*Artem Bisliouk,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana,Rita T. Sousa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，系统研究生物医学文本与知识图谱（KG）对齐的设计选择。冻结文本编码器和KG嵌入模型，仅学习轻量级投影头，通过对比学习在CTD-Align数据集上评估六个设计维度。实验表明，三元组组合方式和训练方向（共享检索空间）影响最大，而文本编码器和困难负样本采样影响较小。最佳策略是将文本投影到KG空间，使用拼接主语-谓语-宾语嵌入的线性投影头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学知识以非结构化文献和结构化知识图谱两种形式存在，二者对齐对于知识 grounding、证据检索和 KG 补全至关重要，但现有方法未显式对齐自由文本证据与 KG 三元组。需要系统研究对齐的设计选择，建立轻量、实用的基准。

Method: 构建 CTD-Align 数据集（22K+ 一对一三元组-文档对），链接比较毒物基因组数据库中的化学-基因交互到 PubMed 段落。冻结预训练文本编码器和 KG 嵌入模型，仅训练轻量级投影头，采用对比学习目标。系统探索六个设计维度：文本编码器、KG 嵌入模型、投影头、三元组组合、训练方向、困难负样本采样。在文档到三元组和三元组到文档两种检索设置下评估。

Result: 三元组组合方式和训练方向（即共享检索空间的方向）对对齐性能影响最大；文本编码器选择和困难负样本采样影响微乎其微。简单策略胜出：将文本投影到 KG 空间，使用拼接主语、谓语、宾语嵌入的线性投影头效果最好。

Conclusion: 轻量级对比对齐是连接生物医学文本与知识图谱的有效、实用基础。研究确立了关键设计原则，为后续知识 grounding、证据检索和 KG 补全任务提供了坚实基准。

Abstract: Biomedical knowledge exists in two complementary but distinct forms: unstructured scientific literature and structured knowledge graphs (KGs). Aligning them is essential for knowledge grounding, evidence retrieval, and KG completion, yet existing methods do not explicitly align free-text evidence with KG triples. We present a unified framework for systematically studying design choices for aligning biomedical text and KGs. With a text encoder and a KG embedding model both frozen, we learn only a lightweight projection between their spaces via a contrastive objective. This enables a fair comparison across six design dimensions: text encoder, KG embedding model, projection head, triple composition, training direction, and hard-negatives sampling. We construct CTD-Align, a corpus of over 22K one-to-one tripledocument pairs linking chemical-gene interactions from the Comparative Toxicogenomics Database to supporting PubMed passages. We evaluate alignment on it in two retrieval settings: document-to-triple and triple-to-document. We find that the triple composition and the training direction (i.e., shared retrieval space) have the greatest impact, whereas the text encoder and hard-negatives sampling matter little. Overall, simple choices win: projecting text into the KG space with a linear head over concatenated subject, predicate, and object embeddings performs best. These findings establish lightweight contrastive alignment as an effective, practical foundation for bridging biomedical text and KGs.

</details>

Conclusion: BULBUL 通过平衡语言多样性与标注质量，填补了多方言、口音感知阿拉伯语 ASR 资源的空白，为后续研究提供了高质量基准数据集。

Abstract: Arabic automatic speech recognition (ASR) faces unique challenges due to diglossia, extensive regional dialect variation, and limited speech resources. Existing speech datasets often focus on single dialects or large-scale broadcast/web data, leading to trade-offs between linguistic diversity and annotation quality. We present BULBUL, a multi-dialect Arabic ASR dataset collected from 275 speakers in 11 Arab countries. BULBUL includes structured dialect and sub-dialect coverage, as well as recordings of classical Arabic and modern standard Arabic spoken by participants in their native dialectal accents to support accent-aware modeling. The quality of the recordings was ensured through a two-level human verification process. We further benchmark a range of recent ASR systems, establishing strong baselines for modern dialectal and accented Arabic ASR.

</details>


### [39] [ToSCA: Leveraging Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning on Temporal and Strategic Abstractions of Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21969)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Qingqing Gu,Yue Zhao,Teng Chen,Yuqi Cao,Xiaokai Chen,Hongyan Li,Luo Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种双层分层强化学习框架用于对话代理，连接了此前的token级和utterance级RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类在日常交互和思维中具有多层次时间抽象（如概念感知和战略规划）的启发，旨在弥合token级和utterance级RL方法之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于双层MDP，token级响应解码以utterance级动作（显式文本策略）为条件。理论上使用DQN求解高层critic，PPO求解低层actor-critic。设计双粒度奖励机制，融合utterance级满意度分数、token级内在动机和K-L惩罚，缓解奖励稀疏并促进收敛。

Result: 在日常对话和情感支持对话实验中，该方法在策略确定和响应质量上均优于多功能基线。

Conclusion: 提出的双层分层RL框架结合双粒度奖励机制，有效整合了token级和utterance级RL，显著提升了对话代理的策略决策和生成质量。

Abstract: Humans have multiple levels of temporal abstractions on daily interaction and thinking, such as concept perception and strategic planning. Inspired by this nature, we propose a two-level hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework for conversational agents, bridging the gap between previous token-level or utterance-level RL methods. Developed on a two-level MDP, the token-level response decoding is conditioned on the utterance-level action, the explicit textual strategies. Based on theoretical derivation and efficiency consideration, we use DQN to solve the high-level critic and PPO to solve the low-level actor-critic. To further alleviate the reward sparsity and facilitate the convergence, we also design the dual-granularity reward mechanism, in which the utterance-level satisfaction score is integrated with token-level intrinsic motivation and K-L penalty. Experiments on both daily and emotional support conversations show that our method outperforms versatile baselines in strategy determination and response quality. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/AaronJi/ToSCA.

</details>


### [40] [The Emergence of Relevance Through Axiomatic Attention Patterns During LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23338)
*Matthew Perlman,Atharva Nijasure,James Allan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验和注意力分析，探究LoRA微调在RankLLaMA重排序任务中，注意力层的位置对性能的影响，发现中间网络区域的LoRA注意力更新贡献了超过一半的性能增益，且该区域的注意力变化与稀有词敏感性、查询-文档交互等IR公理特征高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA微调是将LLM适配到重排序任务的标准方法，但尚不清楚网络中哪里学习了任务特定的相关性行为，以及伴随该学习的注意力层面变化是什么。

Method: 采用消融实验和注意力分析，在RankLLaMA模型上，固定全网络MLP的LoRA微调，仅在注意力层的不同区域应用LoRA，观察性能变化；同时分析注意力模式与词汇匹配、稀有词敏感性、查询-文档交互等IR公理特征的相关性。

Result: 发现将LoRA注意力更新限制在紧凑的中间网络区域，即可恢复应用于所有注意力层性能增益的半数以上；省略该区域的注意力微调比其他区域更损害性能。性能影响最大的区域与微调后对IR公理特征（稀有词敏感性、查询-文档交互、组合特征）注意力增加的区域重叠，这些特征与排名性能增益高度相关。

Conclusion: 结果支持了一种可解释的相关性解释，说明相关性导向行为如何在LoRA微调中涌现，并指向改进重排序器适配策略的方向。

Abstract: LoRA fine-tuning is standard for adapting LLMs to reranking, but it remains unclear where in the network task-specific relevance behavior is learned and what attention-level changes accompany that learning. Through ablation and attention experiments, we identify where LoRA attention updates to RankLLaMA improve performance and whether those gains coincide with interpretable relevance-oriented attention patterns such as lexical matching, rarity sensitivity, and query-document interaction. We find that given LoRA fine-tuned MLPs throughout the network, restricting LoRA attention updates to a compact mid-network region is sufficient for recovering over half of the performance gained by applying LoRA to all attention layers, and that omitting attention fine-tuning in this region hurts performance more than elsewhere in the network. Additionally, we show that regions where applying LoRA affects performance the most overlap with regions where fine-tuning increased attention to axiomatic IR features. Rarity sensitivity, document-query interaction, and several compositional features are highly correlated with gains in ranking performance. Our results support an interpretable, correlational account of how relevance-oriented behavior emerges during LoRA fine-tuning and point toward improved strategies for adapting rerankers.

</details>

*Mohammed Q. Shormani,Yehia A. AlSohbani,Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用 MARBERT 模型和二元逻辑回归，分析了 15856 条含表情符号的阿拉伯口语推文，发现名词在语料中标准化频率最高，但动词密度对表情符号使用的影响最强（β=0.821, p=.001），结论指出表情符号与词汇/语用范畴的关联需结合计算、统计与语用混合方法解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在数字语用学框架下，阿拉伯口语社交媒体文本中表情符号使用与词汇/语用范畴（名词、动词、形容词、副词、疑问句、感叹句）的关联模式，填补计算语言学与语用学结合的研究空白。

Method: 1. 使用 Python 从 X 平台采集 15856 条含表情符号的阿拉伯口语帖子；2. 对文本分词并标准化为 4 个词汇范畴（名词、动词、形容词、副词）和 2 个语用/结构范畴（疑问句、感叹句）；3. 微调并优化 MARBERT 模型以识别哪个范畴在标准指标上得分更高、从而与表情符号使用相关；4. 采用二元逻辑回归检验哪个范畴与表情符号出现在统计上显著相关。

Result: 名词在语料中标准化频率占主导（M=0.675, SD=0.161），动词次之（M=0.083, SD=0.100）；然而动词密度对表情符号使用具有最强预测力（β=0.821, p=.001, 95% CI [0.332, 1.309]）。

Conclusion: 在 X 平台阿拉伯口语的数字语用学中，表情符号使用与词汇/语用范畴的关联可由计算、统计和语用混合方法解释，体现了机器学习、语言/词汇特征、语境表征与语用交际之间的相互作用。

Abstract: This study examines the performance of the state-of-the-art MARBERT model in identifying the lexical/pragmatic category associated with emoji use on X within a digital pragmatics approach (DPA). A net corpus of 15856 Colloquial Arabic (CA) posts containing emojis was collected from X using Python. The texts were tokenized and normalized into 4 lexical categories, namely noun_norm, verb_norm, adj_norm, and adverb_norm, and 2 pragmatic/structural categories, question_norm and exclamation_norm. MARBERT was finetuned and optimized to identify which category scores standard metrics more, hence associated with emoji use, while binary logistic regression was used to examine which category is statistically associated with emoji occurrence. Findings unveil that nouns dominate the corpus in normalized frequency (M = 0.675, SD = 0.161), followed by verbs (M = 0.083, SD = 0.100). However, verbs have the strongest influence of emoji use indicated by verb density (\b{eta} = 0.821, p = .001, 95% CI [0.332, 1.309]). The study concludes that in digital pragmatics of CA on X, emoji use association with lexical/pragmatic category can be explained by a hybrid approach of computational, statistical, and pragmatic methods, reflecting the interaction among machine learning, linguistic/lexical features, contextual representation, and pragmatic communication.

</details>


### [42] [Align, Unify, Suppress, Route: A Coherentist View of Transformer Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22034)
*Nura Aljaafari,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出连贯主义概率组合主义（CPC）框架，用对齐、统一、抑制、路由四种算子角色描述Transformer机制，跨15个模型（5大架构家族）验证：抑制、统一、路由的权重空间签名与激活级角色度量显著相关；抑制跨任务比统一更稳定；消融对齐头在10模型中降低下游抑制活动，但无冲突提示亦现类似效应，揭示一般上游依赖而非矛盾特异性耦合；显式矛盾在14模型中显著偏移层级相干性代理，扣除共享残差协方差后缺口方向全符合预测；基础与指令微调模型保持归纳头分数结构（r≥0.98），算子签名无一致向后层迁移。结果支持CPC作为跨架构共享词汇，但机制深度与几何表达仍具架构特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性虽已识别Transformer电路，但缺乏跨任务、跨架构描述其功能组合的共享词汇，难以系统比较不同模型的计算机制。

Method: 1. 提出CPC解释框架，植根连贯主义解释理论，定义四算子角色：对齐（识别候选关系）、统一（整合支持信息）、抑制（压制不兼容备选）、路由（传递选中信息至输出）。2. 在5大架构家族的15个模型上开展实验：① 计算权重空间签名与激活级角色度量的相关性；② 消融对齐头并对比随机头控制，测量下游抑制活动变化；③ 构造显式矛盾与无冲突提示，追踪层级相干性代理变化；④ 对比基础模型与指令微调变体的归纳头分数及算子签名层分布。

Result: ① 抑制、统一、路由的权重空间签名与激活级角色度量相关性均显著高于随机基线。② 抑制角色跨任务稳定性高于统一角色。③ 消融对齐头在10/15模型中降低下游抑制活动，但无冲突提示下同样出现，表明是通用上游依赖而非矛盾专用耦合。④ 显式矛盾在14/15模型中显著改变层级相干性代理；去除共享残差协方差后，所有模型的相干性缺口方向均符合理论预测。⑤ 基础与指令微调模型归纳头分数结构高度保持（r≥0.98），算子签名无一致性向后层迁移。

Conclusion: CPC提供了一套可跨架构比较Transformer机制的共享词汇（四算子角色），实证支持其描述能力；但算子在层深度分布与几何表达形式上仍呈现显著的架构特异性，提示通用框架与具体实现之间存在张力。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has identified transformer circuits, but lacks a shared vocabulary for describing how their functions compose across tasks and architectures. We introduce Coherentist Probabilistic Compositionalism (CPC), an interpretive framework that grounds transformer computation in coherentist theories of interpretation and describes it through four operator roles. Alignment identifies candidate relations, unification integrates supporting information, suppression reduces incompatible alternatives, and routing carries selected information to the output. Across 15 models from five architecture families, the suppression, unification, and routing weight-space signatures correlate with held-out activation-level role measures above random baselines. Suppression is more stable across tasks than unification. Ablating alignment heads reduces downstream suppressive activity beyond a random-head control in 10 models, but similar effects on no-conflict prompts indicate a general upstream dependency, not contradiction-specific coupling. Explicit contradictions significantly shift a layerwise coherence proxy in 14 models; after removing shared residual covariance, the gap has the predicted direction in every model. Base and instruction-tuned variants preserve induction-head score structure ($r{\geq}0.98$) without a consistent shift of operator signatures towards later layers. These results support CPC as a shared vocabulary for comparing transformer mechanisms while showing that their depth and geometric expression remain architecture-specific.

</details>


### [43] [Real-TurnTurk: A Multimodal Turkish Corpus for Turn-Taking Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22071)
*Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak,Fatma Nur Korkmaz,Bekir Berker Türker,Mustafa Sertaç Türkel,Alper Kaplan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对土耳其语自然对话中的轮流说话预测问题，构建了一个多模态双人交互数据集，并采用遗传算法优化可解释的混合AND-OR决策规则，融合视觉、声学和语言特征进行二分类预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽探索多模态和大语言模型用于话轮结束预测，但缺乏专门针对土耳其语轮流说话动态的自然对话语料库，限制了该语言下同步对话系统的建模能力。

Method: 构建包含同步前置视频、分轨音频（支持重叠语音归因）和时间对齐转录的多模态土耳其语数据集；将轮流说话预测建模为二分类任务；利用遗传算法优化从视觉、声学、语言特征中提取的混合AND-OR规则表示，以捕捉话轮转换前的多种线索组合。

Result: 提出了首个专注于土耳其语轮流说话动态的自然多模态对话数据集，并验证了基于遗传算法优化的可解释混合规则框架在轮流说话预测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为土耳其语同步对话系统的轮流说话建模提供了数据基础和可解释的预测方法，展示了多模态特征融合与进化算法优化规则在低资源语言对话分析中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Turn-taking is a basic organizational feature of human conversation and remains difficult to model in natural, synchronous dialog systems. While existing research has explored multimodal approaches and large language models for turn-ending prediction, there is a lack of naturalistic conversational corpora specifically addressing turn-taking dynamics in Turkish. This study introduces a multimodal Turkish conversational dataset of unscripted dyadic interactions, comprising synchronized front-facing video, per-speaker audio channels that allow overlapping speech to be attributed to individual speakers, and time-aligned transcriptions. Turn-taking prediction is formulated as a binary classification problem, and a Genetic Algorithm (GA) is employed to optimize interpretable decision rules derived from visual, acoustic, and linguistic features. A hybrid AND-OR rule representation is adopted in the proposed framework to represent the alternative cue combinations that precede a turn transition.

</details>


### [44] [Spine-Branch Coordination for Multi-agent Computer Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22077)
*Mian Zhang,Manasi Sharma,Sheng Zhang,Minglai Yang,Kejian Shi,Ying Liu,Zhiyu Zoey Chen,Daniel Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了脊干-分支协调框架，通过将任务分解为具有连续虚拟机状态的主脊干和可丢弃的并行分支，解决了多智能体计算机使用中的虚拟机状态无法合并的物理瓶颈，显著提升了成功率并降低了成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体计算机使用代理（CUA）在部署时面临关键物理瓶颈：两个虚拟机的状态无法合并。现有系统对此仅采取临时应对措施，未将其作为一等关注点处理。

Method: 提出脊干-分支协调框架，将任务分解为脊干-分支图：脊干承载主任务流并保持连续的虚拟机状态；分支任务并行执行以收集脊干所需信息。分支虚拟机在任务完成后即被丢弃，从而完全避免了虚拟机状态合并的需求。

Result: 在Odysseys基准的200个长时程任务上，跨三种CUA骨干网络实验表明，脊干-分支协调将成功率提升6.0%至16.5%，单任务成本降低34%至70%。

Conclusion: 显式建模虚拟机状态合并约束，能使多智能体计算机使用高效扩展。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) are increasingly deployed as multi-agent systems that decompose a task into multiple subtasks executed across parallel virtual machines (VMs). However, a critical physical bottleneck is that the state of two VMs cannot be merged. Previous systems handle this ad-hoc rather than treating it as a first-class concern. We propose Spine-Branch Coordination for multi-agent computer use, a framework that decomposes a task into a "spine-branch" graph, where the spine carries the main task flow with continuous VM state and branch tasks execute in parallel to collect information the spine needs to complete the task. Branch VMs are discarded once their tasks finish, so no VM merging ever occurs. Experiments show that on 200 long-horizon tasks from Odysseys and across three CUA backbones, Spine-Branch improves success rate over the baseline system by 6.0% to 16.5%, while reducing per-task cost by 34% to 70%, indicating that explicitly modeling VM-state merging constraint enables multi-agent computer use to scale efficiently.

</details>


### [45] [Semantic Reasoning Denoising: Correcting Language Model Reasoning with Semantic Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22090)
*Yujiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了语义推理去噪（SRD），一种针对自然语言推理轨迹的算子化马尔可夫去噪方法。SRD 使用可执行的错误算子来表示语义噪声，通过训练模型识别并反转这些算子，实现逐步的、局部化的推理轨迹修正，在多项基准测试上显著优于同骨干基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的推理轨迹虽流畅，但局部语义错误会传播导致错误结论；无约束的自我修正可能保留、放大或引入新错误。现有扩散语言模型虽提供迭代细化，但通常将噪声定义为 Token 掩码或替换，而非推理过程中的语义错误。因此需要一种能显式建模并修正推理语义错误的方法。

Method: SRD 定义可执行的错误算子（描述错误类型、位置、损坏与修复后的命题），组合算子构建逐级加噪状态。训练阶段，模型学习识别当前轨迹中的活跃语义噪声，并重构配对的相邻低噪声状态。推理阶段，采用噪声水平感知的去噪：反复预测逆算子并检查其适用性，每次执行的更新都使轨迹向稳定状态局部迈进。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码、知识和常识的六个域内基准上，SRD 使最强同骨干基线平均提升 3.2 分。在七个跨数据集迁移目标上，其表现与 Llama-3-8B-Instruct 竞争，并使最强 Qwen3-8B 基线平均提升 2.9 分。消融实验表明，结构化语义噪声预测和迭代算子执行是性能提升的核心。

Conclusion: SRD 通过将推理错误显式建模为可组合、可逆的语义算子，并采用马尔可夫式的逐步去噪范式，有效解决了推理轨迹中语义错误的传播与修正难题，在域内与跨域任务上均取得了稳健的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent reasoning traces whose local semantic errors propagate to an incorrect conclusion, while unconstrained self-correction may preserve, amplify, or introduce errors. Existing diffusion language models provide iterative refinement, but usually define noise as token masking or replacement rather than as errors in the reasoning process. We present Semantic Reasoning Denoising (SRD), an operatorized Markov denoising method for natural-language reasoning trajectories. SRD represents semantic noise with executable error operators that describe the error type, its location, and the corrupted and repaired propositions. Composing these operators constructs progressively noisier states. During training, the model learns to identify the semantic noise active in the current trajectory and to reconstruct the paired adjacent lower-noise state. During inference, noise-level-aware denoising repeatedly predicts an inverse operator and checks whether it is applicable, so each executed update makes a localized move toward a stable trajectory. Across six in-domain benchmarks spanning mathematics, code, knowledge, and commonsense, SRD improves the strongest same backbone baseline by 3.2 points on average. On seven cross-dataset transfer targets, it remains competitive with Llama-3-8B-Instruct and improves the strongest Qwen3-8B baseline average by 2.9 points. Analyses of noise sources, objectives, and denoising depth further show that structured semantic-noise prediction and iterative operator execution are central to the improvement.

</details>


### [46] [LLM assisted writing deserves empirical evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22124)
*Xuan Zhong Feng,Yi Lin,Yiye Zhang,Chunhua Weng,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了69,209篇健康信息学论文，发现LLM辅助写作与更聚焦的表达、更广泛的引用实践及更全球化的作者分布相关，但并不证明科学质量更高，建议以学术质量和问责制而非工具使用来评估稿件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助写作常被视为检测问题，引发对清晰度、诚信、公平性和评估的担忧，需要实证研究了解其对学术出版的实际影响。

Method: 对69,209篇健康信息学领域的学术论文进行大规模文献计量分析，考察LLM辅助写作与论文表达聚焦度、引用广度、作者地理分布等特征的关联。

Result: LLM辅助写作与更聚焦的论文表达、更广泛的引用实践、更全球化的作者分布显著相关；但这些模式不直接证明科学质量更高。

Conclusion: 应基于学术质量和问责制评估稿件，而非单纯依据工具使用情况；LLM辅助写作呈现出的积极模式支持这种评估范式的转变。

Abstract: LLM-assisted writing is often treated as a detection problem, as it raises questions about clarity, integrity, equity, and evaluation. An analysis of 69,209 Health Informatics papers links it to more focused presentation, broader citation practices, and more globally distributed authorship. These patterns do not prove better science, but they support evaluating manuscripts by scholarly quality and accountability rather than by tool use.

</details>


### [47] [SSE-Bio: A Structured Self-Evolving Agent with Agentic Retrieval Policy for Multi-Hop Biomedical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22132)
*Zhaohan Meng,Zaiqiao Meng,Siwei Liu,Hao Xu,Ke Yuan,Iadh Ounis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SSE-Bio是一种结构化自我进化智能体，通过代理检索策略实现多跳生物医学推理，在三个基准上超越现有基线，BioHopR上提升6.56个绝对点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体依赖静态检索流程或粗粒度提示重写，当推理程序需要更新时会导致指令漂移，限制了多跳生物医学问答的适应性和准确性。

Method: SSE-Bio维护结构化状态，通过可训练代理策略选择性检索知识三元组和先验模板，并通过细粒度模板编辑改进推理记忆。引入基于群体相对策略优化的代理训练策略，利用备选检索选择上的决策对比群组优化检索决策。

Result: 在三个生物医学多跳问答基准上，SSE-Bio持续优于现有基线，在BioHopR上比最强自我进化基线提升6.56个绝对百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化自我进化智能体配合代理检索策略能有效解决指令漂移问题，通过细粒度模板编辑和决策对比优化显著提升多跳生物医学推理性能。

Abstract: Biomedical multi-hop question answering (QA) requires models to connect evidence across intermediate entities such as diseases, drugs, proteins, and phenotypes. Existing agents typically rely on static retrieval workflows or coarse-grained prompt rewriting, which can lead to instruction drift when reasoning procedures need to be updated. We propose SSE-Bio, a structured self-evolving agent with an agentic retrieval policy for multi-hop biomedical reasoning. Instead of globally rewriting agent instructions, SSE-Bio maintains a structured state, selectively retrieves knowledge triplets and prior templates through a trainable proxy policy, and improves its reasoning memory through fine-grained template editing. To optimise retrieval decisions, we introduce a proxy-training strategy based on group relative policy optimization, where the proxy is improved through decision-contrastive groups over alternative retrieval choices. Experiments on three biomedical multi-hop QA benchmarks show that SSE-Bio consistently outperforms existing baselines, achieving an improvement of 6.56 absolute points over the strongest self-evolving baseline on BioHopR.

</details>


### [48] [Lexical Perturbations Disrupt LLM Reasoning: An Empirical Study of Attention Diversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22140)
*Jiaqian Zhu,Yang Zhang,Junhua Ding,Xiaowei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL
