TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型在面对真实词汇损坏（如键盘噪声、字符交换、填充插入）时的鲁棒性，发现字符级扰动显著降低多步推理准确率，而填充插入影响较小。归因于“注意力转移”：词汇损坏导致子词标记化碎片化，碎片在中后层吸引过量注意力。实验证实碎片化而非长度导致性能下降，且内容损坏与注意力分配耦合，单一修复无效，需同时恢复两者。解释了思维链、拼写纠错、自我修复等推理时策略失效的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理任务上表现强劲，但对真实词汇损坏的鲁棒性知之甚少。需要系统评估不同类型词汇扰动对推理能力的影响，并揭示其背后的机制。

Method: 在四个推理基准上评估四个开放权重指令微调模型和前沿模型，施加键盘噪声、字符交换、填充插入三种扰动。通过长度匹配控制实验分离碎片化效应，采用因子干预实验分离内容损坏与注意力分配两个通道，并测试思维链提示、拼写纠错、自我修复、更强修复模型等推理时策略。

Result: 字符级扰动大幅降低准确率，尤其在多步推理任务；填充插入影响微小。注意力转移机制：词汇损坏导致子词标记化碎片化，碎片在中后层吸引过量注意力。长度匹配控制确认碎片化而非长度驱动性能下降。因子干预显示内容损坏与注意力分配耦合：仅恢复注意力有害，仅恢复内容不足，仅同时恢复两者才能显著恢复性能。推理时策略（思维链、拼写纠错、自我修复、更强修复模型）因单通道修复而持续失效。

Conclusion: 词汇损坏通过子词碎片化引发注意力转移，内容损坏与注意力分配耦合导致推理崩溃。单一通道的推理时修复策略无法恢复性能，需同时修正内容与注意力分配。为提升模型鲁棒性提供了新的理论依据和改进方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong reasoning performance, but their robustness to realistic lexical corruption remains poorly understood. We evaluate four open-weight instruction-tuned models and frontier models across four reasoning benchmarks under keyboard noise, character swaps, and filler insertion. Character-level perturbations substantially degrade accuracy, especially on multi-step reasoning tasks, while filler insertion has little effect. We trace this asymmetry to Attention Diversion: lexical corruption fragments subword tokenization, and the resulting fragments attract disproportionate attention mass, concentrated in middle and final transformer layers. Length-matched controls confirm that fragmentation, not prompt length, drives the loss. A factorial intervention then shows why the damage is hard to undo: fragmentation corrupts token content and attention allocation together, and the two are coupled. Restoring clean attention while the content remains corrupted is actively harmful, restoring content alone is insufficient, and only restoring both recovers a substantial share of the gap. This coupling explains why inference-time strategies, including chain-of-thought prompting, spell-checking, self-repair, and stronger repair models, fail to consistently recover performance: each addresses one channel at a time. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Jiaqian-Janelle/Attention-Diversion

</details>


### [49] [The Collaboration Tax: How Much LLM Multi-Agent Systems Pay to Coordinate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22152)
*Weixiang Sun,Zehong Wang,Hong Huang,Colby Nelson,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文定义了“协作税”来量化双智能体协作相对于单智能体的性能损失，在32个任务上测试11个模型，发现协作税随模型能力单调下降，且由四阶段对话级联机制导致；提示词干预可部分弥补差距，异构智能体组合中协作税向更强智能体靠拢，验证了理论预测的超加性违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型多智能体系统广泛部署，但两个LLM协作相比单独行动损失多少性能尚不清楚，需要一种可度量、可预测且可部分缓解的框架来量化协作成本。

Method: 将协作税形式化为双玩家合作博弈中的团队去中心化损失，给出符号判定与最大超加性违背等价性命题；构建32个单智能体可解任务并按接地摩擦来源分组，在7家提供商的11个模型上测量；分析对话特征预测协作税，并设计针对四阶段级联的提示词干预实验。

Result: 协作税呈现两个无例外规律：跨所有模型的任务类别序贯序、随模型能力单调递减；近因非推理缺陷，而是四阶段对话级联（无依据主张、不查询伙伴、不整合双视角、不重新推导即接受答案）；对话特征可机械预测协作税，全阶段提示词干预显著缩小差距，不同类别瓶颈各异；异构组合中协作税向强智能体倾斜而非加性中点，实证验证了最大超加性违背。

Conclusion: LLM系统中的协作是一种可度量、可预测且部分可治理的成本，协作税框架为多智能体系统的设计与优化提供了理论与实证基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from large language models are deployed widely, yet how much performance is lost when two LLMs must coordinate rather than act alone remains unclear. We formulate the collaboration tax as the team-decentralisation loss of a two-player cooperative game with private information, with two propositions characterising its sign and its equivalence to a max-superadditivity violation. We operationalise this definition on 32 solo-tractable tasks grouped by source of grounding friction and measure it on 11 models from 7 providers. The tax is structured along two no-exception axes: a category ordering across every model and a monotonic decrease with capability. The proximate mechanism is not a reasoning deficit but a four-stage conversational cascade in which agents make ungrounded claims, fail to query the partner, skip integrating both views, and accept the answer without re-derivation. The tax is mechanically predictable from conversation features and partly tractable: a prompt intervention targeting all four stages closes a substantial fraction of the gap, with the dominant bottleneck differing across categories. In heterogeneous pairs the tax is pulled toward the stronger partner rather than the additive midpoint, empirically realising the max-superadditivity violation predicted by our framework. Together these results recast collaboration in LLM systems as a measurable, predictable, and partly tractable cost.

</details>


### [50] [How Agents Represent Humans: Human-Directed Stereotypes in an Open Agent Social Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22192)
*Huangchen Xu,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在Moltbook平台上对人类的刻板印象，提出四维评价框架（道德、友善、能力、自主）和二级子类型方案，发现能力评价主导，人类常被建构为认知、文化或具身主体；平台反馈模式更受曝光、作者可见性和内容选择影响，而非内外群排斥，提示智能体社会偏见应作为话语过程研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体在持久社交环境中部署，其生成的主张被发布、回复、记忆和复用，需要理解智能体如何将人类建构为社会范畴，以及这种建构在平台层面的传播机制。

Method: 在Moltbook平台上开展人类导向分析，引入四维评价标注框架（道德、友善、能力、自主）和二级子类型方案用于描述性“他者”归因；考察人类表征在人-智能体叙事语境和平台级传播中的呈现；辅助对比智能体内部社区反馈（行为宿主亲和性）。

Result: 能力评价在人类导向评价中占主导；大量“他者”归因将人类描述为认知、文化或具身主体；Moltbook反馈模式由曝光度、作者可见性和内容选择解释，而非人类在线社区常见的稳定内外群排斥。

Conclusion: 智能体社会中的偏见不应仅作为孤立模型输出研究，而应作为话语过程进行考察，关注其在持久社交环境中的动态建构与传播。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed in persistent social environments, where generated claims can be posted, replied to, remembered, and reused. We study human-directed stereotypes on Moltbook, an open agent-native social platform, asking how agents construct humans as a social category. For this human-target analysis, we introduce an annotation framework with four evaluative dimensions---morality, friendliness, competence, and autonomy---and a second-stage subtype scheme for descriptive \textit{other} attributions. We find that competence dominates human-directed evaluations, while many \textit{other} attributions describe humans as epistemic, cultural, or embodied subjects. We further examine how these human representations appear in human--agent narrative contexts and platform-level circulation. As an auxiliary comparison, we analyze agent-internal community feedback through behavioral host affinity. Rather than reproducing the stable insider--outsider rejection often observed in human online communities, Moltbook feedback patterns are better explained by exposure, author visibility, and content selection. These findings suggest that bias in agent societies should be studied not only as isolated model output, but also as a discourse process.

</details>


### [51] [Dual-Layer Agentic Memory with Fast Write Routing and Slow Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22215)
*Wenzhi Li,Dong Nie,Rui Lan,Tongtong Lyu,Peiyao Wang,Lingzi Hong,Weihang Pan,Boyuan Pan,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dual-Layer Agentic Memory (DLAM) 框架，受神经科学互补学习系统(CLS)理论启发，将记忆管理前置到写入阶段：通过小到大模型级联(1.7B/8B)进行成本感知的认知路由，将输入分为不写入、新写入、更新写入三类，剪枝高达 68% 冗余外部记忆；再经周期性参数固化(监督微调)将高价值外部记忆内化为模型参数，使路由器自适应抑制冗余写入。实验显示仅升级 <50% 输入却保留 >98% 下游 QA Exact Match，实现选择性外化与选择性内化的统一范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent 记忆系统将外部记忆视为单调增长的仓库，导致检索性能随时间退化且计算成本持续上升。核心难点不在于检索本身，而在于知识全生命周期管理：决策何时外化、更新、最终内化。

Method: 1. 认知路由：采用 1.7B→8B 小大模型级联，将输入分类为 non-write / write-new / write-update，最小化路由开销并过滤冗余记忆。2. 回写固化：周期性地通过监督微调将高价值外部记忆固化进模型参数，实现选择性内化。3. 自适应抑制：随着模型认知边界演进，路由器自动减少冗余写入。

Result: 级联路由剪枝 68% 冗余外部记忆，仅升级 <50% 输入，却保留 >98% 穷举保留基线的下游 QA Exact Match；周期性固化成功内化外部知识，路由器随模型能力演进自适应抑制冗余写入。

Conclusion: DLAM 确立了‘选择性外化 → 选择性内化’的统一 Agent 记忆范式，从写入端解决记忆膨胀与检索退化问题，代码与数据集将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents operate in dynamic environments where knowledge continuously evolves. Existing memory systems typically treat external memory as a monotonically growing repository, inevitably leading to retrieval degradation and increasing computational costs over time. We argue that the core challenge is not retrieval alone, but managing the knowledge lifecycle: deciding what to externalize, update, or ultimately internalize. Inspired by Complementary Learning Systems (CLS) theory in neuroscience, we propose Dual-Layer Agentic Memory, a framework that shifts memory management to the write phase through cost-aware epistemic routing and periodic parametric consolidation. Incoming information is categorized as non-write, write-new, or write-update, and routed through a small-to-large model cascade that minimizes routing overhead while filtering redundant memories. A subsequent write-back phase selectively consolidates high-value external memories into model parameters via supervised fine-tuning. Experiments demonstrate the dual efficiency of our approach: a 1.7B/8B cascade prunes up to 68% of redundant external memory while escalating fewer than 50% of inputs, yet retains over 98% of the downstream QA Exact Match (EM) achieved by an exhaustive retention baseline. We further show that periodic consolidation successfully internalizes external knowledge, allowing the router to adaptively suppress redundant writes as the model's epistemic boundaries evolve. Overall, our framework presents a unified paradigm for agent memory: selective externalization followed by selective internalization. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [52] [Grounded Normative Rule Generation with Structured Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22229)
*Fanqi Kong,Huaxiao Yin,Ruijie Zhang,Xiaoyuan Zhang,Yizhe Huang,Jian Gao,Shuo Chen,Song-Chun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Grounded Normative Rule Synthesis (GNRS)问题及GNRS-Search框架，利用MCMC优化五槽And-Or Graph，将操作结构与文本生成解耦，提升规则的可执行性与合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言生成和结构化输出基准主要关注表面流畅度或模式合规性，导致生成的规则在实际执行中因依赖不可用数据日志或范围错位而失效，缺乏操作层面的落地验证。

Method: 形式化为GNRS问题，提出GNRS-Search框架：采用MCMC采样优化离散五槽And-Or Graph（AOG），显式解耦中间操作结构与最终文本生成，将可执行性与文笔风格分离，支持在生成前定位规则失效。

Result: 在GNRS-Bench（116个目标，八大场景）和RealCharter-Bench（53个真实策略任务）上，平均评分从68.8%提升至81.0%，在可执行综合指标上排名第一；槽位干预实验证实提升源于稳健的操作逻辑而非修辞调优。

Conclusion: 将自动规则起草转化为可审查的搜索问题，为在受监管环境中部署可验证、合规就绪的个人智能体提供了基础范式。

Abstract: Normative rules like institutional charters and workplace policies must be both human-readable and operationally verifiable against actual environment records. However, current language generation and structured-output benchmarks primarily reward surface fluency or schema compliance, leaving operational grounding weakly tested. This creates a critical vulnerability where standard language models generate plausible-sounding policies that fail during enforcement because they rely on unavailable data logs or misaligned scopes. To address this challenge, we formalize the problem as Grounded Normative Rule Synthesis (GNRS) and introduce GNRS-Search, a framework that utilizes Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling to optimize a discrete, five-slot And-Or Graph (AOG). By explicitly decoupling intermediate operational structure from final prose generation, this method isolates executable feasibility from writing style and allows rule failures to be localized prior to surface realization. We evaluate our approach on GNRS-Bench, a benchmark spanning 116 controlled goals across eight scene families, and RealCharter-Bench, which evaluates transfer to 53 real-derived policy tasks with hidden source clauses. GNRS-Search raises average rubric quality from 68.8% to 81.0% and ranks first under a disclosed executable composite metric, while systematic slot interventions confirm that performance gains stem from robust operational logic rather than rhetorical tuning. Ultimately, by transforming automated rule drafting into an inspectable search problem, this work provides a foundational paradigm for deploying verifiable and compliance-ready personal agents within regulated environments.

</details>


### [53] [Whitewashing Hate, Smearing Harmless Content: Annotator-Style Rebuttal Attacks on LLM-Based Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22230)
*Junyu Lu,Kaiyuan Liu,Jingyi Kang,Deyi Ji,Hailong Zhang,Lanyun Zhu,Qi Zhu,Bo Xu,Liang Yang,Hongfei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨大语言模型（LLM）在仇恨言论审核中面对标注员风格反驳（白洗与抹黑）时的脆弱性，发现多轮交互显著降低审核性能，且不同模型对两类操纵方向存在稳定的不对称脆弱性；显式推理提示和防御指令能缓解但无法消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在仇恨言论审核中的广泛应用，人机协作流程中引入的标注员反馈可能被恶意利用：将仇恨内容伪装为正常（白洗）或将正常内容污名化为仇恨（抹黑）。现有研究缺乏对这种双向操纵下模型判断稳定性的系统性评估，亟需量化不同方向攻击的效果差异并探索防御措施。

Method: 提出“重判协议”，在直接反驳基础上引入决策边界扰动和对抗性理由，构建多轮交互场景；在多个主流LLM上使用两个仇恨言论数据集进行实验，对比白洗与抹黑两种操纵方向下的性能下降程度，并测试显式推理提示和防御指令的缓解效果。

Result: 实验表明：标注员风格反驳显著降低审核准确率，多轮设置下效果更强；不同模型表现出稳定的、模型特定的白洗与抹黑不对称脆弱性（即方向性漏洞模式）；显式推理提示和防御指令可减轻但无法完全消除性能下降。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人机协作审核流程中存在的方向性脆弱性，强调需要针对白洗与抹黑的差异化防护机制，并呼吁建立专门的反馈鲁棒性评估基准，以保障实际部署中的审核可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for hate speech moderation, often within human--AI workflows in which reviewers provide feedback before a final decision. Such feedback introduces two manipulation directions: whitewashing hateful content as normal and smearing normal content as hateful. This study examines the susceptibility of initially correct model judgments to annotator-style rebuttals and analyzes whether attack effectiveness differs across manipulation directions. We introduce a rejudge protocol that extends direct contradiction with decision-boundary perturbations and adversarial rationales. Experiments with multiple LLMs on two hate speech datasets show that annotator-style rebuttals substantially degrade moderation performance, with stronger effects in multi-turn settings. The results further reveal stable, model-specific asymmetries between whitewashing and smearing across attack configurations, indicating distinct directional vulnerability patterns. Explicit reasoning prompts and defensive instructions reduce these effects but do not eliminate them. These findings highlight the need for direction-aware safeguards and dedicated feedback-robustness evaluation in human--AI moderation workflows.

</details>


### [54] [Improving Few-Step Language Flows with Untied Self-Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22244)
*Bocheng Li,Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出流匹配语言模型在少步采样时生成质量急剧下降，根源是训练与推理时的 self-conditioning 不匹配。提出无需重训练的 Untied Self-Conditioning 采样器，通过冻结投影权重消除冗余方向、利用预测历史近似步平均预测，在 8 步采样下将 OpenWebText 困惑度从 531 降至 62（8.6×），Arena-Hard-Auto v2 偏好率 96%，ELF-B 困惑度从 71 降至 43，改进在 8–256 步均保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配语言模型支持并行细化所有 token 位置并可用采样步数换取延迟，但少步采样时生成质量显著下降。作者追溯到训练-推理不匹配：训练时 self-conditioning 输入直接从当前噪声状态计算，无中间求解器步骤；推理时求解器将上一预测折叠进潜变量，导致该预测再次作为显式 self-conditioning 输入，产生随步宽增长的冗余耦合，降低生成质量。

Method: 提出 Untied Self-Conditioning 采样器，无需重训练、每步一次评估。两部分修正：1) 利用冻结投影权重识别 self-conditioning 输入与潜变量冗余的方向并抑制；2) 从求解器积分结构推导出需要步平均预测，用预测历史近似，尺度由离线轨迹统计决定。

Result: 在 LangFlow 上 8 步采样：OpenWebText 生成困惑度从 531 降至 ~62（8.6 倍提升）；适配 Arena-Hard-Auto v2 协议下输出在 96% 成对比较中胜出。ELF-B 上困惑度从 71 降至 ~43。改进在 8 至 256 采样步范围内均成立。

Conclusion: 通过解耦训练-推理时的 self-conditioning 耦合，Untied Self-Conditioning 显著提升了流匹配语言模型在少步采样下的生成质量，且无需额外训练开销，为实用部署提供了高效采样方案。

Abstract: Flow-matching language models refine all token positions in parallel and can trade sampling steps for latency, yet generation quality still degrades sharply with few sampling steps. We trace a source of this degradation to a train--inference mismatch in previous-prediction self-conditioning: during training, the self-conditioning input is computed from the current noisy state with no intervening solver step; during sampling, the solver folds the previous prediction into the latent before that same prediction reappears as the explicit self-conditioning input. This coupling, absent during training, creates redundancy that grows with step width. We show that the mismatch degrades both the self-conditioning input and the solver update, and derive a correction for each from the model's own structure. From the frozen projection weights we identify directions along which the self-conditioning input is redundant with the latent and dampen them; from the solver's integration structure we derive that a step-average prediction is needed and approximate it from prediction history, with scale set by offline trajectory statistics. The resulting sampler, Untied Self-Conditioning, requires no retraining and uses one evaluation per step. At 8 sampling steps on LangFlow, it reduces OpenWebText generative perplexity from $531$ to~$62$ ($8.6\times$); under an adapted Arena-Hard-Auto~v2 protocol, its outputs are preferred in $96\%$ of pairwise comparisons. On ELF-B it reduces generative perplexity from $71$ to~$43$. Improvements hold from 8 to 256 sampling steps.

</details>


### [55] [Nürnberg NLP @ GermEval Shared Task 2026: Harmful Content Detection in German Social Media through Error-Independent LLM Voters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22246)
*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GermEval 2026 德国社交媒体有害内容检测共享任务，通过九模型集成（跨 LLM、训练方法、类别范围三正交轴）解决严重类别不平衡，在四子任务隐藏测试集上均获宏观 F1 第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国社交媒体有害内容造成现实危害，需自动检测；共享任务四子任务采用宏观 F1 评分，核心难点是严重类别不平衡——有害类别稀少且与主流类别表层语言相似，单一模型难以在宏观 F1 上取得高分。

Method: 基于“错误独立性”洞见，为每个子任务构建九投票者集成，跨越三个正交轴：大语言模型（LLM）、训练方法、类别范围；主要依据内部交叉验证选择模型，集成预测。

Result: 在隐藏测试集上宏观 F1 达到 89.56（C2A）、71.63（DBO）、54.84（VIO）、83.02（DEF），四子任务均排名第一。

Conclusion: 针对严重类别不平衡且类别间表层语言相似的检测任务，单一更强模型不如错误独立的多模型集成有效；跨正交维度的集成策略能显著提升宏观 F1，为同类任务提供通用范式。

Abstract: Harmful content in German social media does real-world damage, from calls to action to criminal defamation. The GermEval 2026 shared task scores its detection in four subtasks. The technical challenge is a severe class imbalance. The harmful classes are rare and share surface language with the dominant majority class, yet under macro-F1 they decide the score. The decisive lever is then not a stronger single model but error independence. This insight becomes a per-subtask nine-voter ensemble spanning three orthogonal axes: LLM, training method and class scope. Selected mainly on internal cross-validation, the system reaches macro-F1 of 89.56 (C2A), 71.63 (DBO), 54.84 (VIO) and 83.02 (DEF) on the hidden test set, placing first on all four subtasks.

</details>


### [56] [Length-Adaptive Decoding for Masked Diffusion Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22274)
*Yan Zhan,Mengkai Hou,Wanting Zhang,Zhijun Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）在机器翻译中的目标长度选择问题，提出了免训练的Entropy-Valley（EV）长度选择器。EV通过全掩码前向传递的平均预测熵对候选目标画布评分，选择骨干模型最易填补的画布。实验显示EV在En→Zh、Zh→En、En→De上分别恢复了参考目标长度带来的COMET-22增益的64.9%、65.3%、33.0%。专家评测支持En↔Zh充分性提升，且对比LLaMA-3-8B自回归模型，EV系统在En→Zh持平、在Zh→En领先。诊断表明去噪友好长度不必与参考长度一致，且目标长度的供给方式比揭示token顺序更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型用于机器翻译时，必须在去噪前确定目标序列长度（固定画布解码），而现有工作主要研究token解掩码顺序，对长度决策这一直接影响覆盖率和冗余的关键因素探讨不足。

Method: 提出Entropy-Valley（EV）免训练长度选择器：对候选目标长度画布进行全掩码前向传递，计算平均预测熵作为评分，选择熵最低（即模型最确信、最易填补）的画布长度作为目标长度。

Result: 在En→Zh、Zh→En、En→De三个方向上，EV相较于基于训练语料长度统计的基线，分别恢复了参考目标长度带来的COMET-22增益的64.9%、65.3%、33.0%。三位翻译专家评测支持En↔Zh充分性提升，Zh→En证据更强。对比同数据微调的LLaMA-3-8B自回归模型，EV系统En→Zh持平、Zh→En领先。Oracle长度诊断显示：目标长度的供给方式比token揭示顺序更重要。

Conclusion: 去噪友好的目标长度不一定等于参考长度；Entropy-Valley通过预测熵有效选择长度，显著提升掩码扩散翻译质量，且目标长度决策比解掩码顺序影响更大。

Abstract: Machine translation tests masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) because every source token must be rendered faithfully, while fixed canvas decoding must choose target length before denoising. Existing masked diffusion decoding work mainly studies token unmasking order, leaving this length decision under-explored despite its direct effect on coverage and redundancy. We introduce Entropy-Valley (EV), a training-free length selector that scores candidate target canvases by mean predictive entropy from all-mask forward passes and selects the canvas the backbone is most prepared to fill. Relative to a baseline using training corpus length statistics, EV recovers 64.9%, 65.3%, and 33.0% of the COMET-22 gain from reference target lengths on En$\to$Zh, Zh$\to$En, and En$\to$De. Our diagnostics show that denoising-friendly lengths need not match reference lengths. Evaluation by three translation experts supports the En$\leftrightarrow$Zh adequacy gains, with stronger evidence on Zh$\to$En. Compared with a LLaMA-3-8B autoregressive (AR) model trained on the same fine-tuning data, the EV system ties on En$\to$Zh and leads on Zh$\to$En; an oracle-length diagnostic further shows that, in this masked diffusion MT setting, deciding which tokens to reveal first matters less than how the target length is supplied.

</details>


### [57] [LLM Evaluation on Unseen Questions: Contextual Multidimensional IRT Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22295)
*Ergan Shang,Weijing Tang,Yinqiu He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合多维项目反应理论与问题上下文的模型化评估框架，用于预测大语言模型在未见问题上的表现。实验表明，在场景内评估中，引入问题嵌入能提升预测效果，多维潜在结构比单维替代方案更丰富地描述能力变化；但跨场景泛化仍是一个核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型评估需要在收集大量新标注前预测模型在新问题或任务上的表现。由于问题难度、场景和底层能力需求差异大，简单的回顾性平均可能混淆模型能力与项目特征。

Method: 采用多维项目反应理论模型结合问题上下文（问题嵌入）来建模，将LLM表示为潜在能力概况，利用问题内容告知项目特征，实现超越已观察项目的信息迁移。

Result: 在场景内评估中，引入问题嵌入优于无模型基线；多维潜在结构比单维替代方案提供更丰富的能力变化描述。但泛化能力不一定能转化为跨场景偏移下的可靠预测。

Conclusion: 感知上下文的心理测量建模是高效且可解释的LLM评估的有前景方向，同时跨场景泛化仍是核心开放挑战。

Abstract: Evaluation of large language models (LLMs) increasingly requires predicting how a model will perform on new questions or tasks before collecting large amounts of new annotations. This problem is challenging because question difficulty, scenario, and underlying capability demands can vary substantially. Simple retrospective averages may confound model ability with item characteristics. In this paper, we study a model-based evaluation framework that combines multidimensional item response theory model with question contexts to predict LLM performance on unseen questions. The framework represents LLMs through latent capability profiles while using question content to inform item characteristics, allowing information to transfer beyond previously observed items. Empirically, we find that for within-scenario evaluation, incorporating question embeddings improves prediction relative to model-free baselines, and that multidimensional latent structure provides a richer description of capability variation than unidimensional alternatives. At the same time, our results reveal an important limitation that the generalizability does not necessarily translate into reliable prediction under cross-scenario shift. These findings suggest that context-aware psychometric modeling is a promising direction for efficient and interpretable LLM evaluation, while also highlighting cross-scenario generalization as a central open challenge.

</details>


### [58] [Text-Anchored Semantic Perturbations for Transferable Jailbreak Attacks on Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22312)
*Wenyun Li,Guiping Cao,Xiangyuan Lan,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文本锚定语义扰动攻击（TA-SPA），一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的黑盒越狱框架。通过文本锚定语义因子分解（TASF）将跨模态语义因子与模态特有残差分离，并结合语义保持增强（SPA）多样化有害目标锚点，在文本锚定语义空间优化可迁移扰动，实现强攻击效果并迁移至商业MLLMs，且在主流防御下表现竞争力。研究表明表示层面的安全对齐比输入层面过滤更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在视觉-语言交互上取得显著进展，但其安全对齐仍易受越狱攻击。核心挑战在于文本空间学到的安全行为无法可靠迁移到融合的跨模态表示中，导致多模态输入可通过潜在语义线索被利用。现有攻击多聚焦输入层面，缺乏对跨模态语义表示的系统性扰动方法，亟需一种能在语义空间优化可迁移扰动的黑盒框架。

Method: 提出TA-SPA框架，包含两大核心组件：1) 文本锚定语义因子分解（TASF），鼓励将跨模态语义因子与模态特有残质分离，构建文本锚定的语义空间；2) 语义保持增强（SPA），在保持语义一致性的前提下多样化有害目标锚点。二者结合在黑盒设定下优化可迁移的语义扰动，无需模型内部参数即可实施攻击。

Result: 实验显示TA-SPA在多个MLLMs上攻击成功率显著优于基线，并能有效迁移至商业模型（如GPT-4V、Gemini等）。在代表性防御（如输入过滤、对抗训练）下仍保持竞争力。消融实验与探测分析验证了TASF的因子分解效果，但未声称完美解耦。结果支持从表示层面而非仅输入层面进行安全对齐的必要性。

Conclusion: 文本空间的安全对齐不足以保障多模态模型安全，跨模态融合表示中存在可被利用的语义漏洞。TA-SPA通过语义空间扰动揭示了这一本质问题，呼吁研究界转向表示层面的安全对齐机制，而非仅依赖输入级过滤。该工作为多模态模型安全评估与防御提供了新视角和基准。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in vision-language interaction, yet their safety alignment remains vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. A key challenge is that safety behavior learned in the textual space does not reliably transfer to fused cross-modal representations, leaving multimodal inputs exploitable through latent semantic cues. We propose Text-Anchored Semantic Perturbation Attack (TA-SPA), a black-box jailbreak framework that optimizes transferable perturbations in a text-anchored semantic space. TA-SPA integrates Text-Anchored Semantic Factorization (TASF), which encourages the separation of cross-modal semantic factors from modality-specific residuals, with Semantic-Preserving Augmentation (SPA), which diversifies harmful target anchors while preserving semantic consistency. Experiments show strong attack effectiveness and transfer to commercial MLLMs, with competitive performance under representative defenses. Additional controls and probing support the intended factorization without implying perfect disentanglement, motivating representation-level safety alignment beyond input-level filtering.

</details>


### [59] [Semantics or Structure? Auditing Text Sensitivity in Multimodal Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22321)
*Karthik Sridhar,Atharva Gupta,Nishant Pradhan,Murari Mandal,Dhruv Kumar,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控文本扰动实验，揭示了Time-MMD基准上多模态时间序列预测模型（Aurora、MM-TSFlib、TaTS）报告的性能提升并非源于文本语义内容，而是源于随文本共同提供的数值列；文本内容对预测性能影响极小（MSE变化<0.5%），并发布了可复用的诊断工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证多模态时间序列预测模型是否真正利用了自然语言文本的语义信息，还是仅依赖伴随文本的协变量（如数值列），从而澄清文本模态在冻结编码器架构中的实际作用。

Method: 采用受控文本扰动协议：将每行文本替换为空文本、常量文本、域内打乱文本、跨域文本；结合归因分析与Aurora文本通路探针，在Time-MMD基准上对三种架构（Aurora、MM-TSFlib、TaTS）进行消融实验，并对比移除协同数值列后的性能变化。

Result: 所有扰动条件下均方误差（MSE）均值变化均小于0.5%，文本语义对预测无显著贡献；移除随文本共同提供的数值列后，原文报告的性能提升消失，证实提升源于数值协变量而非文本内容。

Conclusion: 在Time-MMD基准及冻结编码器架构家族中，文本内容不是报告性能增益的有效信号；未来多模态基础模型在结构化数据上整合文本需重新评估，作者发布的扰动协议与评估工具可作为诊断基准。

Abstract: Multimodal time-series forecasting has emerged as a promising paradigm in which natural-language context is expected to improve predictive performance. Recent multimodal foundation models, including Aurora, as well as early- and late-fusion approaches such as MM-TSFlib and TaTS, report substantial gains over unimodal baselines on the Time-MMD benchmark, attributing these improvements to textual information. However, whether these models are actually sensitive to the semantic content of the text remains unverified. We address this question through controlled text perturbations, attribution analyses, and probes of Aurora's text pathway. On Time-MMD, swapping each row's text for any other real text (empty, constant, within-domain shuffled, or cross-domain) moves mean MSE by less than $0.5\%$ on all three architectures. The improvement reported in the literature is recovered when a co-shipped numeric column is removed without touching text. We conclude that, on this benchmark and within this family of frozen-encoder architectures, text content is not the operative signal behind the reported gains. To support future work on text integration in multimodal foundation models for structured data, we release our perturbation protocol and evaluation harness as a reusable diagnostic toolkit.

</details>


### [60] [Noise Floor Audit for Agent Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22331)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Xiyang Wu,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了3个原生工具调用端点在BFCL基准上的测量变异性，发现温度为0时重复运行高度确定性，但语义保持的提示扰动导致显著更大的变异性，且不同端点的失败模式差异巨大，边际准确率掩盖了稳定性和失败模式的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估原生工具调用端点在BFCL多工具和并行类别上的测量变异性，了解确定性运行与提示扰动下的稳定性差异，以及失败模式的分布特征。

Method: 使用匹配的AST评分，在温度0下对Groq端点和启用思考模式的Gemini端点进行重复运行测试，并引入语义保持的提示扰动，对比重复运行与扰动运行的配对标准差，分析ever-flip比例、运行相关性及失败模式分布。

Result: 重复运行几乎完全确定性：ever-flip比例分别为0.7%、2.0%、2.7%，平均运行相关性为0.997、0.966、0.961。提示扰动导致的中位配对标准差比重复运行大11到58倍。畸形输出失败占任务失败的30%、7%、<1%，显示失败模式随端点显著变化。

Conclusion: 边际准确率无法反映端点的稳定性差异和失败模式分布，工具调用评估需综合考虑测量变异性、提示鲁棒性及失败模式特征。

Abstract: We audit measurement variability for 3 native tool-calling endpoints across 2 providers on the official BFCL multiple and parallel categories, using matched AST grading. At temperature 0, reruns are nearly deterministic across Groq endpoints and a thinking-enabled Gemini setting: ever-flip fractions are 0.7%, 2.0%, and 2.7%, with mean run correlations of 0.997, 0.966, and 0.961. Semantics-preserving prompt perturbations create the larger floor on all endpoints, with median perturbation paired SDs 11x to 58x larger than rerun paired SDs. The failure character also shifts: malformed-output failures account for 30%, 7%, and <1% of task failures, so marginal accuracy hides not only stability but also failure mode.

</details>


### [61] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning via Sequential Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22332)
*Murat Dura,Serkan Öztürk,Selma Tekir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出序列激活修补框架，追踪链式思维（CoT）推理过程中注意力头的因果效应，发现分布式推理支持子电路，揭示了CoT条件下注意力头对推理轨迹维护、答案锚定、示例-目标分离和数值生成等机制的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在CoT提示下展现出强大的问题解决能力，但其内部机制尚不明确。现有的单token位置激活修补无法捕捉CoT推理在多token生成过程中时序分布的因果效应，亟需一种能够跨token位置追踪注意力头激活的方法。

Method: 引入序列激活修补框架，结合词性引导分析，跨token位置追踪CoT条件下的注意力头激活；提出序列多头修补评估分布式头集合的联合贡献，并引入跨问题和随机激活控制；通过针对性零消融实验验证识别头的功能重要性。

Result: 识别出的注意力头对成功生成答案至关重要，影响多个重叠机制：推理轨迹维护、答案锚定、示例-目标分离和数值生成。序列多头修补显示分布式头集合共同贡献于最终答案计算。

Conclusion: 研究提供了分布式推理支持子电路存在的证据，这些子电路与CoT条件下的计算相关联，揭示了CoT推理在模型内部的因果机制，为理解大模型推理能力的涌现提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable problem-solving capabilities when guided by Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting, yet the internal mechanisms underlying these improvements remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate where CoT-related causal effects emerge across the generated reasoning trajectory and which attention heads carry signals that contribute to final-answer computation. Because CoT reasoning unfolds over multiple generated tokens, standard activation patching at a single static token position is insufficient to characterize these temporally distributed effects. To address this limitation, we introduce a sequential activation patching framework that traces CoT-conditioned attention-head activations across token positions and aggregates their effects using Part-of-Speech-guided analysis. We further introduce Sequential Multi-Head Patching to evaluate the joint contribution of distributed head sets, together with cross-question and random activation controls. Targeted zero-ablation experiments show that the identified heads are functionally important for successful answer generation and affect several overlapping mechanisms, including reasoning-trajectory maintenance, answer anchoring, exemplar-target separation, and numerical generation. Overall, our results provide evidence for distributed reasoning-support sub-circuits associated with CoT-conditioned computation.

</details>


### [62] [Register Shifts Break LLM Safety: A Bengali Benchmark with Culturally Grounded Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22335)
*Naymul Islam,Nusrat Jahan Lia,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sabik Bin Sultan,Abdullah Khan Zehady*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对孟加拉语（全球第七大语言）LLM安全评估缺失的问题，构建了BanglaSafe基准（879个提示，17个危害类别，5种提示条件），评估18个前沿LLM发现：53.6%响应不安全/部分不安全，14.7%严格有害；写作风格（正式报纸调查 vs 随意消息）导致有害请求成功率相差17个百分点，且现有安全分类器在孟加拉语上失效率近半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语为全球第七大语言，但LLM安全评估长期以英语为中心，缺乏针对低资源语言的文化敏感型安全基准，导致模型在非英语场景下的安全风险未被充分揭示。

Method: 构建BanglaSafe基准：包含309个本土编写+570个专家审核的孟加拉语提示，覆盖17个文化接地危害类别，设计5种提示条件（语言、写作风格、权威框架等变体）。在18个前沿LLM上进行系统评估，并对比现有安全分类器在孟加拉语内容上的检测性能。

Result: 1) 整体不安全率高：53.6%响应不安全/部分不安全，14.7%含严格有害内容。2) 写作风格影响最大：同一有害请求以正式报纸调查风格呈现比随意消息风格成功率高17个百分点（无对抗工程）。3) 安全分类器失效：即使前沿模型也在近半孟加拉语案例上失败，现有分类器难以可靠评估孟加拉语内容。

Conclusion: 低资源语言安全评估亟需文化敏感型基准；写作风格而非语言切换是诱发有害输出的关键因素；现有安全分类器对非英语语言泛化能力不足，需开发多语言鲁棒的安全检测机制。

Abstract: Bengali is the seventh-most-spoken language globally, yet LLM safety evaluation remains overwhelmingly English-centric. We introduce BanglaSafe, a benchmark of 879 Bengali prompts combining 309 natively authored prompts with 570 expert-reviewed prompts, spanning 17 culturally grounded harm categories and five prompting conditions that vary language, writing style, and authority framing. Evaluating 18 frontier LLMs, we find that over half of all responses are unsafe or partially unsafe (53.6%) while 14.7% contains strictly harmful content, and that the strongest observed effect is not the switch from English to Bengali but the choice of writing style within Bengali: the same harmful request phrased as a formal newspaper investigation succeeds 17 percentage points more often than the same request phrased as a casual message, with no adversarial engineering involved. We further show that existing safety classifiers struggle to reliably evaluate Bengali content, with even frontier models failing on nearly half of all cases.

</details>


### [63] [When Not to Imitate: Boundary-Aware Skill Memory for Reliable Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22339)
*Zihan Lin,Zhenyu Chen,Jiawen Wei,Xiaohan Wang,Jie Cao,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了边界感知技能记忆（BASM），解决了仅从成功轨迹提取技能导致的“技能模仿陷阱”，即在相似但需不同工具的任务中，检索更多技能反而增加错误工具调用的置信度。BASM 为每个技能添加显式边界字段（适用条件、风险提示、避免规则、恢复说明），将技能从无条件动作模板转为状态条件指导，在三个智能体基准和四种模型规模上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我演化范式假设从成功轨迹提取的技能记忆能单调提升解决问题能力，但探测分析发现这会陷入技能模仿陷阱：对于类似过往成功但需不同工具的任务，检索更多技能反而使模型对错误工具调用更自信，程序技能使错误工具边界较无记忆基线提高 47%。

Method: 提出边界感知技能记忆（BASM），为每个技能增添四个显式边界字段：适用条件、风险提示、避免规则、恢复说明。这些字段使检索到的技能变为状态条件指导：条件满足时应用技能，不满足时抑制不适用工具调用，执行失败时给出针对性修复。

Result: 在 AppWorld、BFCL、AgentDojo 三个基准及四种模型规模上，BASM 持续优于成功蒸馏技能记忆基线：AppWorld 任务成功率最高提升 23.8%，BFCL 准确率最高提升 5.0%，AgentDojo 攻击成功率降低 4.6%，同时相对无记忆基线将 AppWorld 平均步数降低至多 6.6%。

Conclusion: BASM 通过显式边界字段将技能从无条件模板转为条件指导，有效避免了技能模仿陷阱，在多个基准和模型规模上显著提升任务成功率、准确率与安全性，并减少交互步数，证明了边界感知技能记忆对 LLM 智能体高效演化的关键作用。

Abstract: Extracting skills from past successes is critical for the efficient evolution of Large Language Model (LLM) agents. Prevailing agent self-evolution paradigms typically rely on a core assumption: equipping LLMs with skill memories derived from successful trajectories will monotonically improve their problem-solving capabilities. However, probe analyses reveal that extracting skills solely from successful trajectories traps the model in a \textbf{Skill Imitation Trap}. For tasks that resemble past successes but require different tools, retrieving more skills paradoxically increases the model's confidence in wrong tool calls---procedure skills raise the wrong-tool margin by $47\%$ over a memory-free baseline. To overcome this limitation, we propose \textbf{Boundary-Aware Skill Memory} (BASM), which augments each skill with explicit boundary fields---applicability conditions, risk cues, avoidance rules, and recovery notes. These fields transform each retrieved skill from an unconditional action template into state-conditioned guidance: the agent applies the skill when its conditions hold, suppresses inapplicable tool calls when they do not, and issues targeted repairs when execution fails. Across three agent benchmarks and four model scales, BASM consistently outperforms success-distilled skill-memory baselines: it improves task success rate by up to $23.8\%$ on AppWorld, accuracy by up to $5.0\%$ on BFCL, and reduces attack success rate by $4.6\%$ on AgentDojo, while simultaneously reducing average AppWorld steps by up to $6.6\%$ relative to the memory-free baseline.

</details>


### [64] [Context-Aware Cluster Decoding: Semantic Anchor-Driven Coherence in dMLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22367)
*Yikai Zhao,Qiyan Zhao,Jiaquan Zhang,Xiaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Yuan,Pengzhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文针对扩散多模态大语言模型（dMLLMs）长文本生成中的语义漂移和重复问题，识别出现有解码方法的两大结构性缺陷：基于置信度的评分忽略已解码邻居支持，以及块划分阻碍高就绪度语义锚点的访问。提出了无需训练的上下文感知聚类解码（CACD），通过softmax置信度与邻居邻近度的乘法组合评分，实现无块解码并引入架构感知校准，在三个dMLLMs和四个基准上显著提升生成质量并减少幻觉，尤其在长文本生成中增益更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决dMLLMs长文本生成质量随长度增长而下降的问题，核心痛点是现有解码方法存在两大结构缺陷：1）基于置信度的评分机制忽略已解码token的邻居上下文支持；2）块划分策略阻碍了高就绪度语义锚点的全局访问，导致token在局部上下文未充分建立前就被提交，引发语义漂移和重复。

Method: 提出CACD（Context-Aware Cluster Decoding）无训练解码方法：1）采用softmax置信度与邻居邻近度的乘法复合评分，提升上下文就绪token优先级并抑制低置信度位置噪声；2）无块解码策略保持高就绪度语义锚点全局可访问；3）引入架构感知校准机制，处理不同视觉融合策略导致的置信度异质性。

Result: 在三个dMLLMs（如LLaVA等）和四个基准测试中，CACD相较于原始解码持续提升生成质量并减少幻觉，长文本生成场景下增益更为显著，验证了邻居支持和视觉融合策略对dMLLM解码设计的重要性。

Conclusion: CACD通过建模邻居上下文支持和消除块划分限制，有效缓解了dMLLMs长文本生成中的语义漂移和重复问题。实验表明邻居感知评分与架构适配校准是未来dMLLM解码方法设计的关键方向，为扩散模型文本生成质量提升提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) frequently produce long-form outputs marred by semantic drift and repetition, with quality generally degrading as output length increases. We identify two structural deficiencies in existing decoding methods as primary drivers of these failures: confidence-based scoring ignores decoded-neighbor support, and block partitioning prevents access to high-readiness semantic anchors, together causing tokens to be committed before their local context is sufficiently established. We propose \ours{} (\textbf{C}ontext-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{C}luster \textbf{D}ecoding), a training-free decoding method that scores each masked position by a multiplicative composite of softmax confidence and neighbor proximity, promoting contextually ready tokens above isolated candidates while suppressing low-confidence positional noise, operating block-free to keep high-readiness anchors globally accessible. \ours{} further applies architecture-aware calibration to handle confidence heterogeneity induced by diverse visual integration strategies. Experiments on three dMLLMs across four benchmarks demonstrate consistent quality gains and hallucination reduction over Original, with larger gains in several longer generation settings, highlighting the importance of neighbor support and visual integration strategy for future dMLLM decoding method design. Our code is openly available at https://github.com/zhaoyk-sysu/CACD-dMLLM.

</details>


### [65] [Can Large Language Models "Hyper-Thread"?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22376)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出模型超线程假设，探讨大语言模型在生成每个token时能否并发执行多任务。通过三种条件（基线、串行功能调度、并发功能加载）在AIME 2025开发集上实验，发现并发功能加载达到最高准确率，输出长度相似但大多数问题更短，注意力更分散且任务相关覆盖率更高，尽管输出长度尾部更重。结果表明更分散的注意力可与更高准确率共存，为超线程假设提供初步行为和相关证据，建议推理扩展从“生成更多token”转向“每个生成步骤承载更多任务”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理扩展方法主要依赖更长生成、更多样本或额外验证阶段，而注意力分散常被视为干扰或错误信号。串行生成中的任务并发性仍未被充分探索。作者假设更广泛的注意力分配可能为任务并发提供机制，旨在验证模型是否能在生成每个token时并发执行多任务。

Method: 设计三种实验条件：基线（Baseline）、串行功能调度和并发功能加载。在共享同一问题状态的多个协调任务上评估，使用准确率、输出token分布和注意力指标衡量收益与成本。在AIME 2025开发集上进行实验。

Result: 并发功能加载达到最高准确率。相对于串行功能调度，其典型输出长度相似且在大多数问题上更短，表现出更大的注意力分散度和更高的任务相关覆盖率，但输出长度尾部更重。步内并发性及其因果机制仍需直接测试。

Conclusion: 更分散的注意力可以与更高准确率共存，为超线程假设提供初步行为和相关证据。这激励推理扩展视角从“生成更多token”转向“让每个生成步骤承载更多任务”，为提升推理性能指明新途径。

Abstract: Large language models generate tokens sequentially, but can they execute multiple tasks concurrently while forming each token? Broader attention allocation may provide a mechanism for such task concurrency. Existing approaches to scaling inference primarily rely on longer generations, more samples, or additional verification stages, while attention dispersion is often treated as a signal of interference or error. Task concurrency within serial generation therefore remains underexplored. We propose the Model Hyper-Threading Hypothesis and evaluate its predictions using multiple coordinated tasks that share state within the same problem. We design three conditions (Baseline, Serial Functional Scheduling, and Concurrent Functional Loading) and evaluate their benefits and costs using accuracy, output-token distributions, and attention metrics. On an AIME 2025 development set, Concurrent Functional Loading achieves the highest accuracy. Relative to Serial Functional Scheduling, its typical output length is similar and it is shorter on most problems, while exhibiting greater attention dispersion and higher task-relevant coverage, albeit with a heavier output-length tail. Within-step concurrency and its causal mechanism still require direct tests. Our results show that more dispersed attention can coexist with higher accuracy, providing preliminary behavioral and correlational evidence for the hyper-threading hypothesis. These findings motivate a shift in perspective on inference scaling from "generating more tokens" toward "having each generation step carry more tasks," pointing to a new avenue for improving reasoning performance.

</details>


### [66] [ProBel: Propaganda Detection with Techniques, Spans, and Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22388)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Elisa Sartori,Giovanni Da San Martino,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProBel，一个阿拉伯语和英语的双语宣传检测资源，包含多层级标注（二分类、23 种技术的多标签、六大粗类、片段级标注及解释），并在统一设置下评估了零样本提示、任务特定微调和联合训练。单一双语多任务模型整体表现最佳，跨任务分析显示监督层级影响迁移：联合分类训练保持二分类性能，仅片段训练可能削弱句级预测；双语联合训练最稳定，单语微调会降低跨语言迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宣传检测涉及从句级判断到技术分类再到片段识别的多个预测层级，但这些层级的监督在阿拉伯语和英语联合学习时如何相互作用尚不清楚，缺乏统一的多语言、多层级基准资源与系统评估。

Method: 构建 ProBel 双语数据集，对齐二分类标签、23 种宣传技术的多标签标注（归为六大粗类）、技术级片段标注及参考解释，覆盖相同新闻句子，英语部分规模显著更大。在统一实验设置下，分别评估零样本提示、任务特定微调、单语/双语联合多任务训练，并分析不同监督层级（二分类、粗类、细类、片段）及语言组合对迁移效果的影响。

Result: 单一双语多任务模型在整体性能上达到最佳，且在各任务和语言上均具竞争力。联合分类训练能保持二分类性能，而仅进行片段级训练会削弱句级预测能力。双语联合训练产生最稳定的结果，单语微调则会降低向另一语言的迁移性能。

Conclusion: ProBel 为多语言、多层级宣传检测提供了统一基准。研究表明，联合建模多层级监督并利用双语数据能显著提升跨任务和跨语言的泛化能力，未来将公开数据、代码与评估脚本以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Propaganda detection includes several related prediction levels, ranging from sentence-level decisions to technique classification and span identification. However, it remains unclear how supervision at these levels interacts when learned jointly across Arabic and English. We present ProBel, an Arabic and English resource that aligns binary labels, multi-label annotations over 23 propaganda techniques grouped into six coarse categories, technique-labeled spans, and reference explanations for the same news sentences. It includes a substantially larger English collection and supports matched binary, coarse-grained, multi-label, and span-level tasks in both languages. We evaluate zero-shot prompting, task-specific fine-tuning, and joint training under a shared setup. A single bilingual multi-task model achieves the best overall performance and remains competitive across tasks and languages. Cross-task analysis shows that transfer depends on the supervision level. Joint classification training preserves binary performance, whereas span-only training can weaken sentence-level prediction. Joint bilingual training yields the most stable results, while monolingual fine-tuning can reduce transfer to the other language. We will release the data, code, and evaluation scripts.

</details>


### [67] [SchemaGUI: A Schema-Driven Benchmark for Controllable GUI Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22390)
*Jiarui Dong,Yin Cai,Zhouhong Gu,Chenmou Wu,Ci Tao,Yiran Chen,Jialing Li,Xiaoran Shi,Juntao Zhang,Zhijun Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 SchemaGUI，一种基于模板的可控 GUI 生成评测基准，通过参数化界面模式合成自然语言指令与确定性函数调用参考，无需人工标注即可秒级生成数千个确定性标注任务。在六个双语场景、每场景每语言 1000 个实例上评测五个主流模型，揭示三大核心发现：精确几何空间控制仍是瓶颈，模型扩大提升 Schema 可行性但几何得分提升有限；布局复杂度显著影响生成难度，模型在简单顺序布局表现好，但在密集网格和多区域组合中出现严重坐标漂移；思考模式增加 token 消耗且普遍降低 GUI 得分，对小模型影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在 GUI 生成方面潜力巨大，但可靠评测面临数据分布不可控、标注噪声大、布局场景覆盖有限等挑战，亟需一种可控、可扩展、无需人工标注的评测基准。

Method: 构建 SchemaGUI 基准：利用参数化界面模式自动合成成对的自然语言指令与确定性函数调用参考，实现秒级生成数千个确定性标注任务。选取六个代表性双语场景，每场景每语言 1000 个实例，评测 Qwen3.5 系列、Qwen3-Coder-30B、DeepSeek-R1 等五个主流模型，并从 Schema Feasibility、Geometry 等指标进行综合分析。

Result: 1) 几何空间控制是主要瓶颈：Qwen3.5 从 4B 扩展到 27B，Schema Feasibility 从 91.56% 提升至 99.63%，但 Geometry 得分仅从 67.05% 提升至 75.30%。2) 布局复杂度高度影响生成难度：模型擅长简单顺序排列，但在密集网格和多区域组合中出现严重坐标漂移。3) 思考模式增加 token 消耗且普遍降低 GUI 得分，小模型受影响更明显。

Conclusion: SchemaGUI 为 GUI 生成提供了可控、可扩展、无需人工标注的评测范式。实验表明，当前 LLM 在精确几何控制和复杂布局处理上仍存在显著短板，单纯扩大模型规模难以根本解决几何精度问题；思考模式虽增强推理但反而损害 GUI 生成质量。未来研究需聚焦几何感知能力提升与复杂布局鲁棒性增强。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong potential in graphical user interface (GUI) generation, but reliable evaluation remains challenging due to uncontrolled data distributions, noisy annotations, and limited layout scenario coverage. To address this, we propose SchemaGUI, a template-based benchmark for controllable GUI generation evaluation. By synthesizing paired natural language instructions and deterministic function-call references from parameterized interface schemas, SchemaGUI can generate thousands of deterministically annotated tasks in seconds without human labeling. Based on 1,000 evaluated instances per scenario and language across six representative bilingual scenarios, we benchmark five mainstream models, including the Qwen3.5 family, Qwen3-Coder-30B, and DeepSeek-R1. Our extensive analysis reveals three key insights. First, precise geometric spatial control remains an important bottleneck; while scaling Qwen3.5 from 4B to 27B improves Schema Feasibility from 91.56% to 99.63%, the Geometry score improves more modestly (from 67.05% to 75.30%). Second, generation difficulty is highly sensitive to layout complexity, with current LLMs excelling at simple sequential arrangements but suffering severe coordinate drift in dense grids and multi-region compositions. Third, thinking mode increases token consumption while generally reducing GUI Score, particularly for smaller models.

</details>


### [68] [Don' t Box Me In: Dynamic Cultural Adaptation and Cognitive Tracking for Social Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22411)
*Chongyuan Dai,Yaling Shen,Shengeng Tang,Hui Ma,Jinpeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DyCAC，一种无需训练的框架，通过动态文化适应和持续认知追踪实现多文化交互中的流体社交对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将文化视为静态人口属性，无法适应混合和动态表达的交际偏好，限制了大语言模型在多文化场景下的社交理解能力。

Method: DyCAC将文化相关交际偏好建模为人口级文化参考档案的时变混合，利用对话风格信号校准，并引入基于心智理论的记忆模块持续追踪对话者认知状态。

Result: 在交互式社交和文化基准上的大量实验表明，DyCAC优于现有基线，展现出增强的社交智能和跨多元多文化语境的广泛适应性。

Conclusion: DyCAC通过动态文化表示和认知追踪，有效解决了多文化交互中文化流动性和交际行为动态变化的问题，提升了大语言模型的社交智能。

Abstract: Social interaction increasingly takes place in multicultural settings, where individuals may draw on multiple cultural influences and adapt their communicative behavior across contexts. Despite recent advances in equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) with social understanding capabilities, existing approaches often model culture as a static demographic attribute, limiting their ability to accommodate hybrid and dynamically expressed communicative preferences. Therefore, in this paper, we propose \textbf{DyCAC}, a training-free framework that achieves fluid social alignment by incorporating \underline{Dy}namic \underline{C}ultural \underline{A}daptation with continuous \underline{C}ognitive tracking. Rather than inferring a fixed cultural identity, DyCAC models culturally relevant communicative preferences as a time-varying mixture of population-level cultural reference profiles. This reference-based representation is further calibrated using dialogue-style signals observed in the ongoing interaction, enabling the model to capture both composite cultural influences and turn-level shifts in communicative behavior. In parallel, a memory module driven by Theory of Mind (ToM) continuously tracks the cognitive states of the interlocutor. Extensive experiments on interactive social and cultural benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach. The proposed framework outperforms existing baselines, exhibiting enhanced social intelligence and broad adaptability across varied multicultural contexts.

</details>


### [69] [Rank Reversal in Multilingual LLM Judges: A Label-Free Double-Centering Calibrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22432)
*Alhasan Mahmood,Samir Abdaljalil,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文针对多语言LLM裁判在不同提示语言下产生的评估骨干网络排名不一致问题，提出基于共识的校准（CBC）方法，通过双中心化单元均值矩阵恢复语言-骨干交互项，无需人工标签即可校准裁判分数，并给出有限样本聚集界和无偏性证明。实验表明CBC显著提升跨任务排名一致性（τ从0.650升至0.902）并改善与人类偏好的一致性（从68.7%升至76.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言LLM裁判在不同提示语言下对同一骨干网络的评分排名不一致（7/15骨干对显示统计学显著的成对排名反转），这被视为测量问题：裁判分数包含语言-骨干交互偏差，导致跨语言评估不可比，需要一种无标签的事后校准方法来消除该偏差。

Method: 提出Consensus-Based Calibration (CBC)估计器：将多语言裁判分数加性分解为任务难度、骨干技能、语言-骨干交互项；通过双中心化单元均值矩阵（即双因子方差分析交互项恢复，采用和为零对比）无标签恢复交互项；给出O(1/√n)有限样本聚集界，方差常数为(1-1/m)(1-1/k)；证明即使存在任务-语言交互，估计器仍无偏。

Result: 在7,920次裁判运行（6骨干、8语言、55任务、3框架）中，CBC使保留任务的跨任务排名一致性τ从0.650提升至0.902，与保留加性模型预言机在各语言决策上的一致性从68.5%提升至100%。在独立收集的M-RewardBench面板（7语言、1,500项/语言、10,500实例、5评估器）上，面板与公开人类黄金偏好的一致性从68.7%提升至76.6%（增益7.9百分点，95% CI [6.0, 9.9]）。

Conclusion: CBC作为一种无标签事后校准器，有效解决了多语言LLM裁判的语言-骨干交互偏差，显著提升跨语言评估的一致性并改善与人类偏好的对齐。其核心贡献在于将标准双因子方差分析交互项恢复操作应用于多语言LLM裁判校准，并提供了显式有限样本聚集界和鲁棒无偏性保证。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM judges produce different evaluator-backbone rankings depending on the prompt language: on an eight-language Agent-as-a-Judge benchmark, the top-ranked backbone alternates across English, Arabic, Chinese, Hindi, Japanese, Spanish, Turkish, and Swahili, and 7 of 15 backbone pairs show statistically significant pairwise rank reversal. We treat this as a measurement problem. The multilingual judge score decomposes additively into task difficulty, backbone skill, and a language-backbone interaction term, the last of which is recoverable without human labels by double-centering the cell-mean score matrix. We make this estimator (\textbf{Consensus-Based Calibration}, CBC) explicit, give an $O(1/\sqrt{n})$ finite-sample concentration bound with variance constant $(1-\tfrac{1}{m})(1-\tfrac{1}{k})$, and show that it is unbiased even when task-language interactions are present. Across 7{,}920 judge runs (6 backbones, 8 languages, 55 tasks, 3 frameworks), CBC raises held-out cross-task rank consistency $τ$ from 0.650 to 0.902 and agrees with the held-out additive-model oracle in 100\% of per-language decisions versus 68.5\% raw; these are consistency diagnostics, not human-grounded correctness measures. On a separately collected M-RewardBench panel (7 languages, 1{,}500 items per language, 10{,}500 language-item instances, 5 evaluators), panel agreement with the public human gold preferences rises from 68.7\% to 76.6\% (gain 7.9 percentage points, 95\% CI $[6.0, 9.9]$), our strongest external evidence of downstream usefulness. The estimator is the standard two-way ANOVA interaction-recovery operation under sum-to-zero contrasts; our contribution is its application as a label-free post-hoc calibrator for multilingual LLM judges, an explicit finite-sample concentration bound, and an unbiasedness result that holds even under task-language misspecification.

</details>


### [70] [Aligned Alone, Misaligned Together: Forecasting Adversarial Capture in LLM Agent Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22444)
*Isotta Magistrali,Chen Shani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型代理群体中的安全评估问题，发现单智能体审计无法预测群体行为，但可从无对抗的群体运行中校准响应函数来预测受攻击时的群体漂移；展示推理过程可中和弱攻击但仅能延缓强攻击，且攻击后移除对抗代理后群体会漂回初始状态，证明群体俘获是暂时的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 安全评估以单一模型为单位，但语言模型代理越来越多地部署在相互交互的群体中。单智能体校准良好仍可能因同伴影响而改变决策，现有单智能体审计无法回答群体层面的安全问题。

Method: 在安全分流任务中构建语言模型监控器群体，决定是否升级或忽略告警；注入始终推向同一方向的承诺少数派作为对抗；从无对抗的群体运行中校准响应函数，预测后续攻击下的群体漂移；实验变量包括是否让代理看到彼此推理、攻击强弱、攻击后移除对抗代理。

Result: 1) 单智能体判断几乎相同的两条告警，在群体中可驱动截然不同的集体行为，单智能体审计无法揭示群体行为。2) 从良性运行校准的响应函数可提前预测承诺少数派将群体推向多远。3) 让代理看到彼此推理可中和弱攻击，但对强攻击仅能延缓收敛，将问题从‘是否收敛’变为‘何时收敛’。4) 移除承诺代理后群体会漂回初始状态，群体俘获是暂时而非不可逆的陷阱。

Conclusion: 隔离中的对齐不等于群体中的对齐，但群体在攻击下的行为可从无对抗时的行为中提前读取；群体安全评估需从单智能体转向群体动力学建模。

Abstract: The unit of AI safety evaluation is still the individual model, yet language-model agents are increasingly deployed in interacting populations that read and write one another's decisions. This raises a question no single-agent audit can answer: an agent that is well-calibrated on its own may still be pulled toward a different decision by the agents around it. We study this on a security-triage task, where populations of language-model monitors decide whether to escalate or dismiss alerts, and into which we can inject a committed minority that always pushes one way. We find that two alerts a single agent judges almost identically on its own can drive collective behavior far apart, so auditing any one member need not reveal what the population will do. Yet that collective behavior can be predicted in advance. From a population's benign, adversary-free operation alone, we calibrate a response function that forecasts, before any attack is run, how far a committed minority will later move it. We then ask what shifts the outcome and find that letting agents see each other's reasoning neutralizes a weak attack, while only delaying it against a strong one, turning the question from whether the population converges on the adversaries' choice into when. Finally, we exclude the hypothesis of capture being an irreversible trap: once the committed agents are removed, the population drifts back toward where it began, so capture is a temporary state. Alignment in isolation is not alignment in a population, yet what a population will do under attack can be read in advance, from how it behaves before any adversary arrives.

</details>


### [71] [Figurative Justice: Detecting metaphors in Hindi judgements with qualitative assessment and transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22446)
*Bhumika Bhattacharyya,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Indranil Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了印地语法律隐喻语料库(HiLeMe)，并基于mBERT和Transformer架构开发了印地语法律隐喻检测模型，填补了低资源语言法律隐喻检测的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻在法律语境中具有重要说服力，影响司法决策和人权案件判决。现有自动隐喻检测主要针对英语等高资源语言，印地语等低资源语言缺乏标注语料库和检测模型，阻碍了法律话语分析的发展。

Method: 1. 从印地语法律数据语料库(HLDC)中提取判决文书构建HiLeMe语料库；2. 邀请法律专家基于MIPVU标注体系进行隐喻标注；3. 微调多语言BERT(mBERT)；4. 构建基于Transformer的隐喻检测架构。

Result: 成功构建了首个印地语法律隐喻标注语料库HiLeMe，基于Transformer的模型在法律隐喻检测任务上超越传统模型，能为解读司法决策提供洞见。

Conclusion: 研究推进了低资源语言(如印地语)法律话语自动化分析，并计划扩展至印度22种法定语言，为法律NLP在多语言司法系统中的应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Metaphors are figurative use of words for conceptual mapping. Metaphor detection in the legal context has been crucial as metaphors are persuasive juridical means of creating legal meaning and concepts resulting in significant consequences. Metaphorical framing in legal discourse by judges, lawyers, and legislators brings about real-time implications upon individuals and influences judicial decision-making, argumentation and interpretation of laws. This is crucial in Human Rights infringement cases where language determines severity of punishment, public perception and judicial outcomes.
  While automatic metaphor detection in major languages like English, Spanish, Polish, Lithuanian have aided in understanding inherent intentions of metaphorical use of language, there is no such attempt in low-resource languages like Hindi. The dearth of annotated legal corpora in Hindi makes it difficult to develop NLP models and detect metaphors in judicial proceedings. In the Indian context, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have been used for classification of bail judgements, however there are no existing models designed for metaphor detection.
  We present a Hindi Legal Metaphor Corpus (HiLeMe) by isolating judgements from Hindi Legal Data Corpus (HLDC). Legal experts annotated HiLeMe to classify metaphorical constructions using the MIPVU schema. We downstreamed an mBERT on Hindi legal metaphor detection task. We built a transformer-based architecture for metaphor detection that are known to outperform traditional models in legal classification tasks. This model provides insights into the judicial psyche for decoding judicial decisions. Our research contributes to advancing automated models in legal discourse in low-resource languages like Hindi and envisages adoption into 22 Indian schedule languages.

</details>


### [72] [From Exposure to Expectation: Frequency, Surprisal, and Language Across Development in Spanish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22452)
*Francisco Portillo López*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过两项基于语料库的西班牙语实验，对比了词频和 surprisal（惊讶度，即语言模型给出的负对数概率）在解释儿童词汇习得年龄和成人阅读注视时长中的作用。结果显示：词频强烈预测习得年龄，surprisal 的额外解释力极低；而在成人阅读中，surprisal 在控制词频和词长后仍能稳健预测注视时长，且 surprisal-行为关联在阅读中显著强于习得。结论指出：累积词汇暴露（词频）主导早期词汇表征的建立，而语境可预测性反映已建立语言系统中的即时加工难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨 surprisal（单次语境下的可预测性）是否像在成人阅读中那样，也能显著解释儿童单词习得时间，从而区分累积暴露和语境可预测性在语言发展轨迹中的不同角色。

Method: 研究1：利用 225 个西班牙语名词的习得年龄数据，引入儿童导向语料中的词频、语境多样性，以及 BETO、BERTIN、mGPT 三个语言模型的 surprisal，构建回归模型预测习得年龄。研究2：使用 MECO Wave 2 智利西班牙语子语料的成人眼动数据，结合 mGPT surprisal 和两个独立词频指标，预测注视时长，并进行词类型层面的匹配比较。

Result: 研究1：词频与习得年龄强相关 (r=-.597, p<.001)；surprisal 在控制词频和词长后几乎不增加解释量，自然语境分析亦同。研究2：surprisal 在控制词频和词长后稳健预测更长的注视时长，且两个词频来源结果一致；匹配比较显示 surprisal-行为关联在阅读中显著强于习得 (z=3.63, p<.001)。

Conclusion: 累积词汇暴露和语境可预测性在语言轨迹中扮演不同角色：词频特别揭示早期词汇表征何时被习得；surprisal 捕捉已建立语言系统中的瞬时加工难度。这一模式支持基于使用和巩固的词汇发展理论，也对语言模型作为人类语言行为模型的评估有启示。

Abstract: Surprisal, the negative log-probability a language model assigns to a word given its preceding context, reliably predicts adult reading times. Does it contribute as much to explaining when children acquire individual words? Frequency reflects a learner's cumulative exposure to a word, whereas surprisal reflects how predictable a single occurrence is given its context. We investigate this question across two corpus-based studies of Spanish.
  In Study 1, we modeled age of acquisition (AoA) for 225 Spanish nouns using lexical frequency and contextual diversity from child-directed speech, plus surprisal from three language models differing in architecture and training language (BETO, BERTIN, mGPT). Frequency strongly predicted AoA (r=-.597, p<.001); surprisal added little beyond frequency and word length, including in a naturalistic-context analysis.
  In Study 2, we modeled adult fixation durations in the Chilean Spanish subsample of the Multilingual Eye-movement Corpus (MECO Wave 2), using mGPT surprisal alongside two independent frequency measures. Surprisal robustly predicted longer fixation durations after controlling for frequency and word length, consistent across both frequency sources. A matched word-type-level comparison showed the surprisal-behavior association was stronger in reading than in acquisition (z=3.63, p<.001).
  The findings suggest cumulative lexical exposure and contextual predictability play different roles across the language trajectory: frequency is particularly informative about when early lexical representations are acquired, whereas surprisal captures moment-to-moment processing difficulty in an already-established linguistic system. We discuss this pattern in relation to usage-based and entrenchment-based accounts of lexical development and to the evaluation of language models as models of human language behavior.

</details>


### [73] [GTA-RAG: Graph-Trajectory-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22479)
*Jun Chen,Yongchao Liu,Pengyu Qiu,Jiajun Zheng,Juelu Zhang,Yujie Zeng,Qin Zhang,Ziyue Qiao,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GTA-RAG，一种基于图轨迹增强的强化学习框架，用于多轮检索增强推理。通过实体-文档图采样路径、合成多跳问答轨迹并验证，获得可执行的轨迹级监督，结合GRPO和轨迹引导奖励优化检索策略，在多跳和简单问答基准上均超越现有RL-based RAG基线，显著提升证据链覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向代理式RAG的强化学习方法通常仅用最终答案奖励优化，监督信号稀疏，且忽视模型是否真正检索到所需的证据链，导致多跳推理中证据获取不足。

Method: 构建实体-文档图，采样连通文档路径并合成多跳问答轨迹，利用部署的检索器验证轨迹可执行性，获得轨迹级监督。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)优化检索策略，设计轨迹引导奖励同时鼓励答案准确与目标证据文档获取，随后在自然问答实例上进行答案奖励训练。

Result: 在三个多跳和两个简单问答基准上，GTA-RAG分别基于Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-7B backbone，持续优于现有RL-based RAG基线，并大幅提升证据链覆盖率。

Conclusion: GTA-RAG通过图轨迹增强提供细粒度轨迹级监督，有效解决了稀疏奖励与证据链检索不足的问题，显著提升多轮检索增强推理的性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enables LLMs to access external knowledge for answering knowledge-intensive questions. For complex multi-hop questions, multi-turn retrieval-augmented reasoning extends RAG into an iterative process that repeatedly searches for and integrates evidence across documents. However, existing reinforcement-learning (RL) approaches for agentic RAG are typically optimized with final-answer rewards, which provide sparse supervision and overlook whether the model actually retrieves the required evidence chain. We present \textsc{GTA-RAG}, a graph-trajectory-augmented RL framework for multi-turn retrieval-augmented reasoning. From an entity--document graph, we sample connected document paths, synthesize multi-hop QA trajectories, and validate them with the deployed retriever to obtain executable trajectory-level supervision. We then optimize the retrieval policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and a trajectory-guided reward that encourages both accurate answers and acquisition of target evidence documents, followed by answer-reward training on natural QA instances. Experiments on three multi-hop and two simple QA benchmarks show that \method{} consistently outperforms RL-based RAG baselines with both Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-7B backbones, while substantially improving evidence-chain coverage. Our code is available at https://github.com/cjcj46262/GTA-RAG.

</details>


### [74] [Claim-Level Confidence Calibration for Reliable Decision Making with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22483)
*Toghrul Abbasli,Kentaroh Toyoda,Yuan Wang,Li Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种声明级置信度校准框架，将LLM响应分解为原子可验证声明，利用推理时信号（一致性采样和自我验证）为每个声明分配校准后的置信度，在闭盒设置下实现后验校准，支持对低置信度声明进行选择性干预（如证据检索或人工审核）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险决策领域越来越多地被使用，但存在幻觉且置信度与事实正确性错位。响应级置信度粒度太粗，单次生成可能混合正确和错误陈述，单一数值无法为必须逐条接受、拒绝或验证信息的用户提供可行动指导。因此需要更细粒度的、决策相关的不确定性信号。

Method: 将每个响应分解为原子、可验证的声明；利用推理时信号（跨采样一致性和自我验证）为每个声明分配校准后的置信度；框架在闭盒设置下运行（无需logits、无需微调），直接在声明层面进行后验校准；支持对低置信度声明进行选择性干预，如证据检索或人工审核。

Result: 在TriviaQA和TruthfulQA上评估七个基线方法和六个近期模型（Llama-3.1、Mistral、Qwen2.5、DeepSeek-R1、GPT-4、GPT-4o），声明级分解结合后验校准在事实性问题上降低了期望校准误差，同时在对抗性虚假前提问题上暴露了失效模式——这些正是决策者最需要可靠不确定性估计的场景。

Conclusion: 声明级置信度校准为高风险决策提供了可行动的不确定性信号，支持选择性验证，并揭示了模型在对抗性输入上的弱点。该方法无需模型内部访问即可部署，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support decision-making in high-stakes domains, but they often hallucinate and express confidence that is misaligned with factual correctness. Response-level confidence is a coarse signal: a single generation can mix correct and incorrect statements, so a single number is not actionable for users that must accept, reject, or verify individual pieces of information. We study claim-level confidence calibration as a decision-relevant uncertainty signal: each response is decomposed into atomic, verifiable claims, and each claim is assigned a calibrated confidence using inference-time signals from consistency across samples and self-verification. Our framework operates in closed-box settings (no logits, no fine-tuning) and applies post-hoc calibration directly at the claim level, enabling selective intervention such as evidence retrieval or human review for low-confidence claims. Across TriviaQA and TruthfulQA we evaluate seven baselines on six recent models (Llama-3.1, Mistral, Qwen2.5, DeepSeek-R1, GPT-4, GPT-4o), and show that claim-level decomposition combined with post-hoc calibration reduces expected calibration error on factual questions while exposing failure modes on adversarial false-premise questions where decision-makers most need reliable uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [75] [Who Pays More for Safety? Measuring the Disparate Cost of Safety Alignment across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22490)
*Chanwoong Yoon,Jungsoo Park,Alan Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了安全对齐对不同语言群体效用的影响，发现非英语用户承担更高的“安全成本”，且存在双重惩罚、虚假效用增益、高资源语言也面临更大成本等系统性不平等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐虽能使模型符合人类价值观，但往往降低响应效用。现有研究较少关注这种效用损失是否在不同语言群体中均等分布，亟需量化并揭示跨语言的安全对齐成本差异。

Method: 提出严谨协议度量仅由安全对齐导致的效用损失（即 Safety Cost），通过安全对齐模型与其未对齐版本的直接成对比较，跨多语言评估拒绝行为及隐性质量差异。

Result: 发现系统性不平等：非英语用户 Safety Cost 显著高于英语用户。三大模式：1) 多语言处于双重惩罚区，安全保护弱且效用损失大；2) 部分语言表现出的效用增益实为安全过滤失效导致；3) 即使高资源语言，为达到同等安全水平也需支付比英语更大的 Safety Cost。差异源于显性拒绝与隐性多维质量差异。

Conclusion: 当前安全对齐实践存在系统性跨语言差异，非英语用户承担不成比例的成本。准确度量这些差异效应有助于推动更公平的多语言安全对齐方法发展。

Abstract: Safety alignment helps models adhere to human values, but it often reduces response utility. We ask a critical but understudied question: Does safety alignment impose the cost equally across language groups? To answer this, we introduce a rigorous protocol to measure the utility loss imposed solely by safety alignment, which we term Safety Cost. Through direct pairwise comparisons between safety-aligned models and their unaligned counterparts, we find a systematic inequity: non-English users consistently bear a higher Safety Cost than English users. We further identify three underlying patterns. First, multiple languages lie in a double-penalty zone, experiencing both weaker safety protection and larger utility loss. Second, certain languages exhibit apparent utility gains that are in fact a consequence of safety filters failing to engage. Third, even high-resource languages pay a larger Safety Cost than English to reach the same level of safety. We show that these disparities arise from both explicit refusals and implicit qualitative differences across multiple dimensions. By accurately measuring the disparate effects of safety alignment, our findings expose a systematic disparity in current safety alignment practices.

</details>


### [76] [Kernel Token Contradiction: a Fast and Principled Approach for LLM Claim Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22506)
*Jérémie Dentan,Alexi Canesse,Mahammed El Sharkawy,Sonia Vanier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kernel Token Contradiction (KTC)，一种轻量级的声明级不确定性量化方法，利用正半定核融合 LLM 条件分布与基于维基百科词频的 token 矛盾度，并用 Von Neumann 熵量化不确定性。仅需 CPU 即可实现比 GPU 跨编码器方法快 8.2 倍、比同类 CPU 方法快 65 倍，且在高精度区间超越现有基线，使 LLM 输出的实时监控成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声明级 UQ 方法多依赖昂贵的跨编码器或大规模采样，推理延迟高，难以满足生产环境实时监控需求；同时多数工作仅在英语、少量模型上评估，泛化性未充分验证。

Method: 1. 将候选 token 表示为正半定核矩阵，融合 LLM 条件分布与 token 矛盾度；2. 基于维基百科语料的词频统计估算 token 矛盾度；3. 使用 Von Neumann 熵对核矩阵量化不确定性；全流程仅在 CPU 上运行，无需 GPU 加速。

Result: 在两个基准、四种欧洲语言、16 个模型上实验：平均性能持平 SOTA，高精度区间显著优于基线；CPU 推理比 GPU 跨编码器快 >8.2×，比同类 CPU 方法快 >65×。

Conclusion: KTC 以极低计算开销实现了与重型方法相当甚至更优的声明级 UQ 性能，证明了基于核矩阵与熵度量的轻量化路线可行，为 LLM 生成内容的实时可靠性监控提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Claim-level Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) aims to mitigate the lack of reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) by evaluating the factuality of each claim in their outputs. We introduce Kernel Token Contradiction (KTC), a lightweight approach to compute claim-level UQ under realistic white-box conditions. KTC represents the candidate tokens involved in LLM generation as a positive semi-definite kernel that integrates both the LLM's conditional distribution and a token contradiction score. We then use the Von Neumann entropy to quantify the uncertainty of this kernel. To estimate token contradiction, we develop a new approach based on frequency statistics from the Wikipedia corpus. Although CPU-only, our approach achieves over an 8.2x speedup compared to state-of-the-art GPU-accelerated methods based on cross-encoders, and over a 65x speedup compared to CPU-only methods with comparable performance. Our evaluation spans two benchmarks across four European languages and 16 different models. KTC not only matches the average performance of existing methods but also outperforms them in high-precision regimes. This combination of computational efficiency and accuracy makes real-time monitoring of LLM outputs practical in production.

</details>


### [77] [From Diagnosis to Redesign: Using Quantitative Ethnography to Improve Multi-Agent LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22566)
*Vedant Khatri,Anthony Cusimano,Zachari Swiecki,Zhen Xu,Xiner Liu,Renzhe Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出定量民族志（QE）方法，利用认知网络分析（ENA）诊断多智能体LLM系统的交互话语模式，并指导提示词重设计。以自动作文评分为例，五智能体辩论系统修改后，准确率从27.78%提升至40.28%，错误辩论的话语模式向正确辩论的评分标准导向模式收敛，验证了QE支持“诊断-重设计”闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统虽通过任务分解提升推理，但多智能体的存在不保证连贯推理或目标对齐。缺乏系统性方法诊断智能体间交互模式如何影响系统性能，以及如何据此进行针对性的提示词优化。

Method: 采用定量民族志（QE）方法：构建五智能体辩论系统进行自动作文评分；使用认知网络分析（ENA）建模智能体交互话语网络；对比正确与错误评分决策的话语模式差异；基于发现修改智能体提示词；评估重设计前后的准确率变化及话语模式演变。

Result: 初始系统中，正确决策以评分标准导向的辩护、一致性达成和精细阐述为特征；错误决策表现为冗长的命题-质疑-回应循环，且与评分标准关联较弱。提示词修改后，精确评分准确率从27.78%提升至40.28%，错误辩论的话语模式显著向正确辩论的评分标准导向模式靠拢，两者几乎无法区分。

Conclusion: 定量民族志（QE）结合ENA能有效追踪智能体交互模式与系统性能的关系，支持从诊断到重设计的闭环：识别失效交互模式→指导提示词重设计→验证结果与交互模式的双重改进。该方法为多智能体系统的系统化优化提供了可操作框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are designed to improve reasoning by decomposing tasks across multiple agents with specialized functions, but the presence of multiple agents does not inherently guarantee coherent reasoning or outputs that align with task objectives. This paper introduces a quantitative ethnographic (QE) approach for diagnosing and redesigning multi-agent LLM systems based on the discourse produced through agent interactions. We test this approach using automated essay scoring as an example context, applying Epistemic Network Analysis (ENA) to model a five-agent multi-agent debate system and examine differences between debates that produced correct versus incorrect scoring decisions. Results show that, in the initial system, correct scoring decisions were characterized by rubric-grounded justification, agreement, and elaboration. Incorrect scoring decisions, in contrast, were characterized by extended proposition-challenge-response exchanges that were less consistently tied to rubric criteria. We then used the findings to revise the agents' prompts. The revised system improved exact scoring accuracy from 27.78% to 40.28% and shifted the discourse of incorrect debates toward the rubric-grounded pattern of correct ones, making the two nearly indistinguishable. Based on these results, we argue that QE can support a diagnostic-to-redesign loop for AI reasoning by tracing how patterns of agent interaction relate to system performance, informing prompt redesign, and evaluating whether those redesigns change both outcomes and interaction patterns.

</details>


### [78] [Hybrid Panels: Toward Human-AI Collaboration in Survey Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22582)
*Julia Romberg,Tobias Gummer,Gabriella Lapesa,Tanja Kunz,Claudia Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了混合面板概念，结合人类参与者与大语言模型（LLMs）构建纵向AI赋能调查基础设施，旨在解决大规模人口调查面临的响应率下降、成本上升、数据延迟和非响应偏差等挑战，并展示了首个试点研究的结果与开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模人口调查对于产生稳健的社会科学洞察至关重要，但面临响应率下降、数据收集成本增加、数据收集与提供间延迟长、非响应偏差风险等重大挑战。AI进步为构建AI支持的调查基础设施提供了新机遇，旨在不降低数据质量前提下克服上述挑战。

Method: 提出混合面板框架：一种纵向AI赋能调查，将人类参与者与LLMs作为设计基本要素。通过迭代改进LLMs与目标人口的对齐度，利用误差信息指导下一波调查设计（如参与者招募、问题分配）。框架涵盖数据收集到数据验证全流程，并实施首个试点研究加以验证。

Result: 试点研究展示了混合面板的可行性，但识别出多个开放性挑战：包括LLMs与人口对齐度的迭代改进机制、人类参与者与LLMs协作模式优化、数据验证方法、非响应偏差缓解策略、以及大规模部署的技术与伦理问题等。

Conclusion: 混合面板为解决传统调查困境提供了有前景的新范式，通过人机协作实现调查基础设施的智能化升级。但目前仍处早期阶段，需进一步研究解决对齐度提升、验证体系构建、偏差控制及伦理治理等核心挑战，以实现大规模实际应用。

Abstract: Large-scale population surveys are essential for generating robust social and scientific insights, yet they face significant challenges, including declining response rates, increasing data collection costs, long delays between data collection and data provision, and the risk of nonresponse bias. Advances in artificial intelligence (AI) have opened up new opportunities for AI-supported survey infrastructures where the goal is to overcome these challenges without limiting the data quality. A promising AI-enabled survey infrastructure for which we build a first pilot is a hybrid panel. A hybrid panel is a longitudinal AI-enabled survey which allows to iteratively improve the alignment between large language models (LLMs) and the population they aim to simulate and use the errors to inform the design and implementation of the next survey wave (e.g., inform the participant recruitment, assignment of questions to participants). It incorporates both human participants and LLMs as fundamental elements of its design. In this research note, we introduce the concept of a hybrid panel by providing a definition and outlining an overarching framework, spanning data collection to data validation. We detail results from a first pilot study to illustrate (open) challenges that we identify for hybrid panels.

</details>


### [79] [Teaching LLMs How ICU Physicians Approach Clinical Reasoning Through OMOP-Aligned Retrieval Improves Reasoning Across Clinical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22622)
*Miguel Contreras,Scott Siegel,Subhash Nerella,Jessica Sena,Jiaqing Zhang,Heng Sun,Hruday Tej Akkaladevi,Peiyu Lu,Jordan Rosen,Sumit Kapoor,Sasank Desaraju,Grace R. Thompson,Jacob Purcell,Michael Petrauskis,Philip KW. Hong,Meghan Brennan,Sarah Chrabaszcz,Tierra Smith,Ronnie Ren,Michel S. Kabbash,Ceyhun Haziroglu,Rushi Patel,Gabriel Gomez,Charlotte Chaiklin,Randy Leung,Kenneth N. John,Whitman Wiggins,Philip Kayser,Vincent Bird,Maria Bruzzone,Tyler J. Loftus,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了ICU-REACT数据集和Clin-REACT模型，通过专家临床推理监督训练大语言模型，显著提升了模型在重症监护及更广泛临床推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策依赖于识别相关患者信息以指导诊断和治疗，在数据密集且快速变化的ICU环境中尤为困难。现有LLM应用和数据集多侧重表层检索或事实回忆，而非临床医生实践的归纳和演绎推理。研究假设在专家ICU推理上训练LLM可产生泛化至重症监护之外的临床推理技能。

Method: 采用临床医生参与的循环框架，与19名临床医生共同开发ICU-REACT推理数据集，教授LLM执行信息检索和情境感知的临床推理。使用该数据集微调了跨越8B-70B参数、三个模型家族的Clin-REACT模型。

Result: 在五个临床推理基准测试中，Clin-REACT模型持续优于其主干模型及开源通用/医学LLM。性能提升扩展至不同任务，包括脚本一致性测试、下游诊断和治疗任务。

Conclusion: 重症监护领域的专家推理监督可改善更广泛的临床推理能力，但在实际临床应用前仍需前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making relies on identifying relevant patient information to guide diagnosis and treatment, a challenge that is especially difficult in the data-dense and rapidly changing intensive care unit (ICU). Large language models (LLMs) could support this task. However, existing applications and datasets mostly emphasize surface-level retrieval or factual recall rather than the inductive and deductive reasoning clinicians practice to select and reason over decision-relevant evidence. We hypothesized that training LLMs on expert ICU reasoning could yield clinical reasoning skills that generalize beyond critical care. Here we introduce ICU-REACT, a reasoning dataset developed with 19 clinicians through a clinician-in-the-loop framework to teach LLMs to perform information retrieval and context-aware clinical reasoning in the ICU. Using ICU-REACT, we fine-tuned Clin-REACT models spanning 8B-70B parameters and three model families. Across five clinical reasoning benchmarks, Clin-REACT consistently outperformed its backbone models and open-source general-purpose and medical LLMs. Gains extended to different tasks including script concordance tests, and downstream diagnosis and treatment tasks. These findings suggest that expert reasoning supervision in critical care can improve broader clinical reasoning, although prospective evaluation is needed before real-world clinical use.

</details>


### [80] [GeoRisk-RAG: A Hierarchy-Aware Risk Framework for Improving RAG Reliability through Selective Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22634)
*Meenu Ravi,Shailik Sarkar,Lulwah AlKulaib,Yordanos Tessema,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GeoRisk-RAG是一个层级感知框架，通过DAG距离估计地理适用性并选择性回答，显著降低地理依赖问题的虚假置信率，提升人类偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG、知识图谱增强和强化学习方法虽能提升语义相似度和忠实度，但难以区分语义相似性与地理有效性，在自然灾害管理等地理粒度关键的领域，错误自信回答风险大于拒答。

Method: 提出GeoRisk-RAG框架，利用有向无环图(DAG)基础距离进行上下文检索以显式估计地理适用性，结合选择性回答机制，在野火相关QA数据集上实验验证。

Result: 实验显示GeoRisk-RAG将位置依赖问题的虚假置信率降至0.009，远低于语义相似性和重排序基线的~0.090，且持续实现更高人类偏好对齐。

Conclusion: 该工作通过整合地理有效性和选择性回答行为，为地理空间领域更安全的决策提供了更全面的端到端RAG管道评估。

Abstract: Current work on improving reliability in large language model (LLM)- generated answers has primarily leveraged Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), knowledge-graph augmentation, and reinforcement learning. While these methods are adept at enhancing and measuring reliability through semantic similarity and faithfulness, they often struggle to distinguish semantic similarity from geographic validity. This is especially critical in natural hazard management domains where geographic granularity (i.e., town vs. city vs. state) is significant for decision-making, as responses valid in one municipality may not transfer to another. In such domains, a confidently wrong answer carries greater risk than abstaining. We present GeoRisk-RAG, a novel hierarchy-aware framework that addresses this geographic-validity gap through selective answering. This framework explicitly estimates geographic applicability using a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG)-based distance for context retrieval before response generation. Experiments on a novel held-out wildfire-related question-answering (QA) dataset show that GeoRisk-RAG significantly reduces false confidence rates for location-dependent questions, lowering the rate to 0.009 compared with ~0.090 for standard semantic similarity and reranking baselines, while consistently achieving higher human preference alignment. This work provides a more comprehensive assessment of end-to-end RAG pipelines by integrating geographic validity and selective-answering behavior for safer decision-making in geospatial domains.

</details>


### [81] [Iteration Without Elaboration: A Simple ReAct Architecture Suffices for Text-to-SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22651)
*Jian Lu,Haiwei Yu,Raymond M Xiong,Anru Zhang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReAct-SQL 是一种基于 ReAct 风格的零样本文本到 SQL 框架，使用迭代推理和受限的 DSL 动作空间，在保持高准确率的同时大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到 SQL 系统变得越来越复杂，依赖模式链接、检索增强提示、候选生成和多阶段优化流水线，虽然有效但引入了大量延迟和工程开销。

Method: 提出 ReAct-SQL，一个简单有效的零样本 ReAct 风格框架，仅基于迭代推理和由 15 个关系操作组成的类型化领域特定语言 (DSL) 定义的受限动作空间，而非自由形式的 SQL 生成。模型增量式地发出 DSL 调用，观察编译后的 SQL 执行反馈，并通过交互修正推理。

Result: 在修正后的 BIRD mini-dev 和 EHR-SQL 上分别达到 84.5% 和 73.9% 的准确率，与更复杂的基线持平，同时运行速度快达 8 倍。消融实验表明迭代主要提升 grounding，DSL 提升组合可靠性。

Conclusion: ReAct-SQL 证明了简单的迭代推理配合受限 DSL 动作空间可以在不牺牲准确率的前提下大幅降低复杂度和延迟，为高效文本到 SQL 提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern text-to-SQL systems have become increasingly elaborate, relying on schema-linking modules, retrieval-augmented prompting, candidate generation, and multi-stage refinement pipelines. While effective, these additions introduce substantial latency and engineering overhead. To this end, we present \textbf{ReAct-SQL}, a simple yet effective zero-shot ReAct-style framework built solely on iterative reasoning and a constrained action space defined by a typed Domain-Specific Language (DSL) of 15 relational operations, rather than free-form SQL generation. The model incrementally issues DSL calls, observes compiled-SQL execution feedback, and revises its reasoning through interaction. On corrected BIRD mini-dev and EHR-SQL, ReAct-SQL achieves \textbf{84.5\%} and \textbf{73.9\%} accuracy, respectively, matching substantially more elaborate baselines while running up to $8\times$ faster. Incremental ablations further show that iteration primarily improves grounding, while the DSL improves compositional reliability.

</details>


### [82] [WnW: Waxing-and-Waning KV Cache for Long-Form Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22704)
*Yiming Yao,Chenyang Lyu,Xuanfan Ni,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Yazheng Yang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文针对长音频输入下语音大模型KV缓存内存占用大的问题，提出WnW（Waxing-and-Waning KV cache）方法，通过离线校准将KV头分为锚点、潮汐、固定三类角色，结合GPU常驻锚点头作为解码时重要性观察者，CPU常驻潮汐头按需分块回调，固定头仅保留GPU子集，实现仅保留20%音频token即可达到接近全缓存精度，且泛化性强、解码开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长音频输入使KV缓存成为语音LLM的主导内存开销。现有仅预填阶段的KV压缩方法永久丢弃被驱逐的音频KV位置，解码阶段无法恢复。研究发现预填注意力集中在音频开头（注意力汇聚效应），而解码时注意力广泛分布，两者重要性排序重叠度低，导致预填仅压缩在长音频上脆弱失效。

Method: 提出WnW方法：离线校准将KV头分为三类——锚点头常驻GPU，作为解码时重要性观察者；潮汐头在CPU保留完整副本，根据聚合锚点头得分分块回调；固定头仅在GPU保留子集，其余永久丢弃。解码时动态根据锚点头注意力分数决定潮汐头回调，实现KV缓存的动态膨胀与收缩。

Result: 在LibriSpeech-Long基准上，使用Voxtral-mini-3b和Qwen2.5-Omni-3B两个3B骨干模型，WnW仅保留20%音频token即可达到接近全缓存精度，而预填仅基线无法正常终止生成。结果在语言、任务、领域迁移下均泛化良好，CPU-GPU回调带来的解码额外开销极小。

Conclusion: WnW通过锚点头观察解码时注意力分布，动态回调潮汐头，有效解决了长音频KV缓存内存瓶颈，在极低显存占用下保持高精度，为语音LLM长序列推理提供了实用的内存优化方案。

Abstract: Long-form audio inputs make the KV cache the dominant memory cost of speech LLMs. Prefill-only KV compression methods permanently discard audio KV positions once evicted, with no pathway to recover them during decoding.
