We show this is fragile on long-form audio: prefill attention concentrates near the audio start (an attention-sink effect), while decode-time attention distributes broadly, and the two rankings overlap weakly. We propose WnW (Waxing-and-Waning KV cache), which classifies KV-heads into anchor, tidal, and fixed roles via offline calibration. Anchor heads remain on GPU and serve as a decode-time importance observer; tidal heads keep a CPU-resident complement that is recalled chunk-by-chunk based on aggregated anchor-head scores; fixed heads keep only an on-GPU subset, with the rest permanently discarded. On LibriSpeech-Long with two 3B backbones (Voxtral-mini-3b and Qwen2.5-Omni-3B), WnW preserves near-Full-Cache accuracy while keeping only 20% of audio tokens on GPU, where prefill-only baselines fail to terminate. Results generalize across language, task, and domain shifts, and CPU-GPU recall adds little decode-time overhead in our measurements.

</details>


### [83] [A Source-Grounded Framework for Constructing and Evaluating Progressive Multimodal Diagnostic Dialogues from Clinical Case Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22713)
*Yufan Wang,Rui Yang,Yi Liu,Yi Lin,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于来源的框架，将病例报告转换为渐进式多模态诊断对话，并设计了评估策略来测试多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在最终诊断、诊断推理和影像发现解读方面的能力。实验表明该框架能高精度构建参考对话（诊断F1 0.99，推理质量4.79/5），但两个前沿MLLMs（o4-mini和Claude Haiku 4.5）的推理质量仅为2.75和2.50，且诊断、推理、影像发现F1显著更低。结果揭示流畅回复不等于循证临床推理，该框架为评估多模态诊断推理提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态医学基准多评估固定输入或终点答案，而完全交互式诊断智能体将证据选择与证据解读混淆。临床诊断需要逐步整合病史、体检、实验室检查、医学影像和诊断性检测，因此需要一种能模拟这种渐进式、多模态、循证诊断过程的评估框架。

Method: 1. 提出源接地框架，从病例报告自动构建渐进式多模态诊断对话，保留证据选择与解读的分离。2. 设计评估策略，分别测量最终诊断、诊断推理过程、影像发现解读三个维度的F1分数和推理质量评分（1-5分）。3. 在24份内科病例报告上验证框架转换准确性，并用于评估两个前沿MLLMs（o4-mini、Claude Haiku 4.5）。

Result: 框架转换准确性高：诊断F1达0.99，推理质量评分4.79/5。两个MLLMs表现明显较差：o4-mini推理质量2.75，Claude Haiku 4.5为2.50；三者在诊断、推理、影像发现F1上均显著低于参考对话。说明模型生成的流畅回复不一定反映真实的循证临床推理能力。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了首个能将病例报告转化为渐进式多模态诊断对话的源接地框架，并建立了细粒度评估体系。实验证明该框架能高保真还原临床诊断过程，且揭示了当前最强MLLMs在循证诊断推理上的显著短板。该工具可推动多模态医学诊断智能体的严格评估与改进。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis requires progressive integration of patient history, physical examination, laboratory findings, medical images, and diagnostic-informative tests. However, most multimodal medical benchmarks evaluate fixed inputs or endpoint answers, while fully interactive diagnostic agents conflate evidence selection with evidence interpretation. We present a source-grounded framework to construct progressive multimodal diagnostic dialogues from case reports and an evaluation strategy for assessing MLLMs on final diagnosis, diagnostic reasoning, and image-finding interpretation. Evaluation on 24 internal medicine case reports showed that our framework can accurately convert case reports into reference dialogues, achieving a diagnosis F1 of 0.99 and a reasoning-quality score of 4.79 out of 5. Evaluation on two frontier MLLMs (o4-mini and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieved reasoning-quality scores of 2.75 and 2.50, respectively, with substantially lower diagnosis, reasoning, and image-finding F1 scores. The results demonstrate that fluent responses do not necessarily reflect evidence-grounded clinical reasoning and highlight the utility of the proposed framework for evaluating multimodal diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [84] [DiaRelay: Relaying Dialogue Context with a Constant-Size Memory for Emotion Recognition in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22745)
*Zihao Zhou,Bin Yang,Jinghui Qin,Kebing Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiaRelay，一种基于LoRA的轻量级适配器，通过选择性中继记忆转移和双轴中继记忆读取，使LLM能显式维护对话级记忆，解决固定上下文窗口丢失长距离情感线索及LoRA缺乏动态对话状态建模的问题，在MELD上达到SOTA，在IEMOCAP上具竞争力，仅增加7.1M可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ERC方法依赖固定上下文窗口：短窗口丢弃长距离情感证据，大窗口导致重复编码、计算内存开销大且引入无关上下文。参数高效微调（如LoRA）仅引入固定低秩变换，不维护对话级状态，也不根据演化的对话上下文动态调整变换。

Method: 基于LoRA设计DiaRelay，包含两个协同组件：1) 选择性中继记忆转移：将有用历史证据逐步聚合到有界中继记忆，并在连续话语预测间传播，使早期情感线索在离开局部窗口后仍能影响后续预测，无需重新编码完整历史或扩展骨干上下文长度。2) 双轴中继记忆读取：利用传播的记忆动态调制低秩特征变换，实现上下文相关的表示适应，无需测试时梯度更新。

Result: 大量实验表明，DiaRelay在MELD上达到最先进的加权F1和准确率，在IEMOCAP上获得有竞争力的结果，仅增加7.1M可训练参数，验证了其在增强基于LLM的情感理解方面的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: DiaRelay通过显式对话级记忆机制和动态低秩适应，以极低参数开销解决了ERC中的长距离依赖建模和上下文敏感适应问题，为基于LLM的对话情感识别提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。}

Abstract: Emotion Recognition in Conversation (ERC) requires models to identify subtle emotional cues that are often distributed across distant dialogue turns. Existing methods typically incorporate dialogue history through a fixed context window. However, short windows discard potentially useful long-range evidence, while enlarging the window repeatedly re-encodes overlapping utterances, increases computational and memory costs, and may introduce irrelevant context. Moreover, commonly used parameter-efficient adaptation methods, such as LoRA, mainly introduce fixed low-rank transformations in the feature space and do not explicitly maintain a dialogue-level state or condition their transformations on the evolving conversational context. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight adapter, DiaRelay, to enable LLMs to explicitly maintain a dialogue-level memory for accurate ERC. Based on LoRA, DiaRelay introduces two extra tightly collaborative components, Selective Relay Memory Transition and Dual-axis Relay Memory Read. Selective Relay Memory Transition progressively aggregates useful historical evidence into a bounded relay memory and propagates it across successive utterance predictions. This allows earlier emotional cues to influence later predictions after they leave the local context window, without re-encoding the complete dialogue history or expanding the backbone context length. Dual-axis Relay Memory Read uses the propagated memory to dynamically modulate low-rank feature transformations, enabling context-dependent representation adaptation without test-time gradient updates. Extensive experiments show that DiaRelay can achieve SOTA weighted F1 and accuracy on MELD while obtaining competitive results on IEMOCAP with only an extra 7.1M trainable parameters, indicating the effectiveness and generalizability of our DiaRelay in enhancing LLM-based emotional understanding.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Factual Knowledge: Benchmarking and Learning Step-Level Procedural Rule Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22753)
*Bohan Yu,Pengfei Cao,Chen Han,Chenxi Zhou,Zhiheng Zhang,Zhiyang Xie,Wenhao Teng,Xiangwen Liao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RuleWorld基准评估LLM大规模应用程序规则的能力，并提出DynaRule框架通过KV缓存注入规则和分步注意力训练实现动态规则重注意力，显著提升多步推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在文本理解和生成上表现优异，但在大规模可靠理解和应用外部提供的程序性规则方面仍面临挑战。现有评估多关注实例特定事实，缺乏对全局可复用抽象规则单元的系统性评估。

Method: 1. 构建RuleWorld基准：将规则重构为全局可复用抽象单元，涵盖单规则、并行多规则、多跳推理三种场景。2. 提出DynaRule框架：将给定规则注入KV缓存，采用堆叠分步级注意力训练，引入特殊<search>标记，使检索成为内部可学习的分步过程，支持推理时动态重注意力和更新过时规则。

Result: 实验表明现有LLM在大规则池下表现困难；DynaRule将平均问答准确率提升高达19个百分点，在10K规模规则下实现85%以上的Recall@1，大幅超越强基线。

Conclusion: DynaRule通过内部化规则检索和动态分步注意力机制，有效解决了LLM大规模应用程序规则的难题，为程序性知识推理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at text understanding and generation, yet still struggle to reliably understand and apply externally provided procedural rules at scale. To evaluate this capability, we introduce RuleWorld, a large-scale benchmark that reformulates rules as globally reusable abstract units rather than instance-specific facts. In RuleWorld, several scenarios, including single-rule, parallel multi-rule, and multi-hop reasoning, are settled for comprehensive evaluation. We further propose DynaRule, an end-to-end framework that injects the given rules into the KV cache and turns retrieval into an internal, learnable, step-wise process. Specifically, DynaRule employs Stacked Step-Level Attention Training with a special <search> token to enable dynamic rule re-attention and updating during inference. In this way, the model can re-attend to the most relevant rules at each step, dynamically replacing outdated ones to support more stable multi-step reasoning. Experiments on RuleWorld show that existing LLMs face challenges under large rule pools, while DynaRule improves average QA accuracy by up to 19 points and achieves over 85% Recall@1 at 10K rules, outperforming strong baselines by large margins. We make our code and dataset available here: https://github.com/SharkSpicy-NLP/Beyond-Factual-Knowledge.

</details>


### [86] [XTC: Head-Aware Sampling by Excluding Top Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22758)
*Philipp Emanuel Weidmann,Allen Roush,Judah Goldfeder,Sanjay Basu,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 XTC（Exclude Top Choices），一种轻量级解码操作符，通过排除高概率的通用选项来提升开放式生成的多样性，同时保持流畅度和指令遵循准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码策略（如温度缩放、截断）忽略了多个延续均合理但概率质量过度集中在最通用选项的情况，导致生成缺乏多样性且重复。

Method: XTC 设定绝对合理性阈值 τ，识别概率超过 τ 的 token；若至少有两个符合条件，则以概率 ρ 移除占优势的合格选项，仅保留最弱的合理替代项，再重新归一化分布。

Result: 在四个模型上，创造性生成中 Distinct-2 提升 11–15%，三元组重复率降低 27–47%；结合温度缩放可达 38% 和 71%。人类盲测显示 62.3% 偏好 XTC（p<10⁻⁴）且流畅度不降。IFEval 严格准确率仅下降 1.7 个百分点，而匹配 Distinct-2 的温度设置会下降 8.8 点。效果与温度、重复惩罚可加性结合，跨量化级别、模型家族及 12 种提示体裁均鲁棒，已被主流推理框架采用。

Conclusion: XTC 通过直接针对“概率过度集中于通用选项”这一机制，以极小开销显著改善多样性–重复率帕累托前沿，兼顾指令遵循与流畅性，是一种实用且可泛化的解码增强方法。

Abstract: Standard decoding rules for autoregressive language models promote diversity by rescaling the full next-token distribution or truncating its low-probability tail. These strategies overlook a common regime of open-ended generation in which several continuations are plausible but too much probability mass remains concentrated on the most generic choice. We introduce XTC (Exclude Top Choices), a lightweight head-aware decoding operator that targets this regime directly. XTC identifies tokens whose probabilities exceed an absolute plausibility threshold $τ$: when at least two qualify, it removes the dominant eligible choices with probability $ρ$ and retains only the weakest plausible alternative before renormalization. Across 60 experiments on Gemma 3 27B Q4, Gemma 3 12B Q6, and DeepSeek R1 14B Q6, with scaling validation on Llama 3.3 70B Q4, XTC improves the diversity-repetition Pareto frontier. On creative generation, Distinct-2 increases by 11--15% and repeat trigrams decrease by 27--47% across the four models. Combined with temperature scaling, gains reach 38% in Distinct-2 and 71% in repeat-trigram reduction over baseline. A blinded Amazon Mechanical Turk study with 150 Master raters yields a 62.3% creativity preference for XTC ($p<10^{-4}$) without reduced fluency, while a GPT-4o control judge reproduces the Anthropic-judge direction on every measure. On IFEval with Llama 3.3 70B Q4, XTC preserves prompt-level strict accuracy within 1.7 percentage points of baseline while recovering most of the diversity gain; a temperature setting matched on Distinct-2 reduces IFEval by 8.8 points. The effect is additive with temperature and repetition penalties, robust across quantization levels and model families, and consistent across twelve prompt genres. XTC has been adopted by llama.cpp, ExLlamaV2, and text-generation-webui.

</details>


### [87] [Don't Repeat Yourself: Stopping Verbatim Loops at Sampling Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22761)
*Philipp Emanuel Weidmann,Allen Roush,Judah Goldfeder,Sanjay Basu,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DRY (Don't Repeat Yourself)，一种采样时的 logit 调整方法，仅在生成候选 token 会将当前后缀延伸为早先上下文中某片段的精确延续时才施加惩罚，有效抑制逐字循环且不损害格式与流畅度，已被主流开源推理框架采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型自回归生成易产生 verbatim looping（逐字循环），即重复上下文中已出现的片段。现有惩罚手段（重复/存在/频次惩罚、n-gram blocking）针对 token 复现而非循环的序列结构，往往需以牺牲格式或流畅度为代价才能抑制循环。

Method: DRY 在采样时调整 logit：仅当候选 token 会使当前后缀成为上下文中某先前片段的精确延续时，才对该 token 施加惩罚；引入序列断点保护聊天模板与格式 token。

Result: 在 1.5B–120B 参数模型、九类提示词族及 600 对人类评测中，DRY 将后缀延伸率降低 47% 并提升词汇多样性；安慰剂对照实验确认后缀匹配为核心机制；在 AWQ 量化的 70B/120B 模型上，循环率约减半且 MT-Bench、MMLU、GSM8K 表现不降反增，而标准替代方案均有性能损失。

Conclusion: DRY 通过精准针对循环的序列结构而非单一 token 复现，在保持生成质量的前提下大幅抑制逐字循环，已集成至 llama.cpp、ExLlamaV2、text-generation-webui 等主流推理框架，展现显著的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models generate text autoregressively, but open-ended generation is prone to verbatim looping, in which models repeat spans already present in context. Standard defenses such as repetition, presence, and frequency penalties and n-gram blocking act on token recurrence rather than the sequential structure of a loop, and often suppress looping only at strengths that also degrade formatting or fluency. We propose Don't Repeat Yourself (DRY), a sampling-time logit adjustment that penalizes a candidate token only when generating it would extend the current suffix into an exact continuation of a span seen earlier in the context. Sequence breakers protect chat templates and formatting tokens. Across models from 1.5B to 120B parameters, nine prompt families, and a 600-pair human study, DRY reduces suffix-extension rate by 47% while improving lexical diversity. An intervention-matched placebo produces no comparable reduction, identifying suffix matching as the operative mechanism. On AWQ-quantized 70B and 120B models, DRY reduces loop rate by roughly half while preserving MT-Bench, MMLU, and GSM8K performance, whereas standard alternatives lose measurable ground. DRY has been adopted by popular open-source LLM inference frameworks including llama.cpp, ExLlamaV2, and text-generation-webui, highlighting its practical impact on text generation.

</details>


### [88] [DelistBench: Evaluating Search-Enabled LLMs for Auditable Corporate-Event Database Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22770)
*Xuan Yao,Li Shuping,Dai Yang,Zhou Yi,Ke-Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Search-to-Record 任务和 DelistBench 基准，评估搜索增强大语言模型在金融公司事件记录重构中的表现，发现网络检索显著提升准确率，低成本系统可接近最佳性能，并给出部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构需要独立方式检测供应商数据库中缺失、过时和分类错误的公司事件记录。

Method: 引入 Search-to-Record 任务：让具备搜索能力的大语言模型从公开来源重构机构定义的事件记录，针对已知证券范围和历史截止点。构建 DelistBench 基准（1200 条退市公告记录）。在闭卷和联网两种条件下对五个模型进行配对评估。

Result: 联网访问使 7 天内公告日期准确率提升 34.0-48.0 个百分点，事件状态准确率提升约 2.8-21.7 个百分点；最佳系统达 81.5% 整体联合准确率。经济型联网系统以最贵系统 4.5-6.6% 的 API 成本达到 75.9-78.3% 准确率。风险分流识别出低错误子集，但最高覆盖率操作点仍需人工复核 27.3% 的平衡测试集。评估显示网络检索是时效性提升的主要来源，低成本系统可逼近最佳精度。

Conclusion: Search-to-Record、DelistBench 和评估共同提供具体部署指导：根据本地事件流行度和市场结构校准分流阈值，保持正向事件召回，将正向和模糊案例路由至针对性复核。

Abstract: Financial institutions need an independent way to detect missing, stale, and misclassified corporate-event records in vendor databases. We introduce Search-to-Record, a database-assurance task in which search-enabled large language models reconstruct institution-defined event records from public sources for a known security universe and historical cutoff, and DelistBench, a 1,200-record benchmark for security-level delisting announcements. We evaluate five models in paired closed-book and web-enabled conditions. Web access raises announcement-date accuracy within seven days by 34.0 to 48.0 percentage points and event-status accuracy by approximately 2.8 to 21.7 points; the best system achieves 81.5% overall joint accuracy within seven days. Economy web systems achieve 75.9-78.3% overall joint accuracy within seven days at 4.5-6.6% of the API cost of the most expensive web system. Risk-based triage identifies low-error subsets, although the highest-coverage operating point still sends 27.3% of the balanced test set to review. The evaluation identifies web retrieval as the main source of timing gains and shows that low-cost systems can approach the best system's accuracy. Together, Search-to-Record, DelistBench, and the evaluation provide concrete deployment guidance: calibrate triage to local event prevalence and market mix, preserve positive-event recall, and route positive and ambiguous cases to targeted review.

</details>


### [89] [SPOC-SQL: Stage-wise Preference Optimization for Controllable Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22772)
*Yingnan Chen,Chun Ding,Tianshi Xu,Xu Yang,Si Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SPOC-SQL 框架，将 Text-to-SQL 任务分解为四个顺序子任务，引入细粒度偏好优化和结构化分解策略，实现分阶段可控生成，显著提升 SQL 生成的可靠性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 方法将任务视为单步生成，缺乏关键决策点的针对性反馈，且无法干预和控制中间生成过程，导致结构化推理能力不足。

Method: 1. 按标准 SQL 执行逻辑将任务分解为四个顺序子任务；2. 在各阶段关键决策点实施细粒度偏好优化，强化结构化决策；3. 设计结构化分解策略，通过显式中间表示支持分阶段干预与纠错。

Result: 实验表明，融入分阶段人类知识能持续提升模型性能，验证了分阶段感知可控生成的有效性。

Conclusion: SPOC-SQL 通过分阶段分解与针对性优化，解决了单步生成的局限，实现了更可控、更可靠的 Text-to-SQL 生成，为结构化推理任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over relational databases, requiring multi-stage structured reasoning over database schemas and query constraints. However, existing methods treat this task as single-step generation, where models optimize entire SQL sequences without targeted feedback at key decision points and lack support for interacting with and controlling the intermediate generation process. To address this issue, we propose SPOC-SQL, which decomposes Text-to-SQL into four sequential subtasks following standard SQL execution logic and designs stage-specific optimization strategies for the model to learn key decisions. Specifically, we propose the implementation of fine-grained preference optimisation at key decision points across SQL stages, with the objective of enhancing structured decision-making during query construction. Furthermore, a structured decomposition strategy is designed, facilitating stage-wise intervention and correction through explicit intermediate representations. This results in more controllable and reliable SQL generation. Experiments demonstrate that incorporating stage-wise human knowledge consistently improves performance, validating the effectiveness of stage perception controllable generation.

</details>


### [90] [TRACE: A Self-Evolving Skill Bank for Consistent, Limit-Aware LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22793)
*Wenhao Wu,Menghao Zhang,Xin Wang,Zhi Wang,Kun Shao,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 TRACE (TRAjectory-Contrastive Evolution) 方法，通过技能库自我进化闭环，在不修改模型权重的情况下显著提升 LLM Agent 在车载助手场景下的行为一致性（Pass^3 从 59.9% 提升至 94.5%）并缩小潜在性能与可靠性能差距至 4.0 点，在隐藏测试集上以 70% Pass^3 获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 在面向用户产品中的可靠部署取决于一致性与边界感知，而非单次任务解决能力。现有前沿模型在 Pass@3（至少一次成功）与 Pass^k（多次一致成功）之间存在巨大鸿沟，缺乏在多轮对话、工具调用及策略约束下的稳定表现。

Method: TRACE 构建模块化可检索的技能库，每个技能封装独立的工具调用规则与行为准则。通过 Agentic 自我进化循环：每轮评估后，按调用技能分组轨迹，对比成功与失败行为迭代优化各技能；部署时 Actor 基于状态进行逐轮技能编排。全程冻结模型权重，仅更新行为知识。

Result: 在 GPT-5.5 上，Pass^3 从 59.9% 跃升至 94.5%（+34.6 点），潜在与可靠性能差距收窄至 4.0 点。在官方隐藏集上，使用 GPT-5.6-Sol 达到 70% Pass^3，相对基线提升 40% 并排名第一。

Conclusion: TRACE 证明了通过轨迹对比式技能进化，可将模型的高潜在能力转化为稳定、一致的实际性能增益，为 LLM Agent 的可靠落地提供了一条无需微调的新范式。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of LLM agents in user-facing products depends not on raw task-solving ability but on consistency and limit-awareness: behaving the same way across repeated trials, and recognizing when a request cannot, or cannot yet, be safely fulfilled. CAR-bench exposes this reliability gap in the domain of in-car assistants: an LLM-simulated user issues incomplete or ambiguous requests, requiring the agent to resolve uncertainty through multi-turn dialogue and tool use while strictly adhering to domain policies. Even frontier models show a substantial gap between what they can solve at least once (Pass@3) and what they solve consistently across trials (Pass^k). We bridge this gap with TRACE (TRAjectory-Contrastive Evolution), which iteratively improves a skill-based agent's behavioral knowledge without modifying model weights. This knowledge is organized as a Skill Bank of modular, retrievable skills, each encoding a self-contained set of tool-use rules and behavioral guidelines. TRACE evolves this bank through an agentic self-evolution loop: after each evaluation round, it groups trajectories by the skills invoked and refines each skill by contrasting successful and failed behaviors. The updated bank then guides subsequent rounds, while during deployment the Actor performs state-conditioned skill orchestration at every turn. On GPT-5.5, TRACE improves consistency (Pass^3) by 34.6 points, from 59.9% to 94.5%, while shrinking the gap between potential and reliable performance to just 4.0 points. On the official hidden set, TRACE achieved first place using GPT-5.6-Sol, attaining a Pass^3 score of 70%-a 40% relative improvement over the baseline. These results show that TRACE converts high model potential into stable, consistent performance gain. Project homepage: https://darwin-agent.github.io/Car-bench-TRACE.

</details>


### [91] [SDoH-Aware Narrative Anchoring Bias in Medical LLMs for Trustworthy Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22802)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Ramkrishna Saha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估医学大语言模型在面对不同患者叙事风格时的叙事锚定偏差，构建了NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA反事实数据集，测试Qwen2.5系列三个模型，发现虽然7B模型准确率和正确一致性最好，但叙事敏感性误差仍高达31.67%，提示临床决策支持需同时评估平均正确性和跨叙事稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学LLM评估主要关注临床问题回答正确率，忽略了模型在面对医学等价但叙事风格不同的患者描述时可能改变回答的实际风险，即SDoH感知的叙事锚定偏差。

Method: 构建NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA反事实医学问答数据集，每个病例以不同人设叙事呈现但答案固定；将数据从宽格式重塑为病例分组人设行；评估Qwen2.5指令微调模型（1.5B、3B、7B）；300个临床病例，三种提示条件，产生8100个模型响应；报告人设级准确率、反事实一致性、正确一致性、叙事敏感性误差；使用配对McNemar精确检验比较模型差异。

Result: Qwen2.5 7B达到最佳准确率56.33%和最佳正确一致性40.33%；配对McNemar检验显示7B在所有提示设置下准确率显著优于3B；但叙事敏感性依然存在，最低误差仍为31.67%。

Conclusion: 可信临床决策支持系统的评估应同时包含平均正确性和跨医学等价患者叙事的稳定性，仅关注平均准确率不足以保证临床应用的可靠性。

Abstract: Medical large language models are often judged by how many clinical questions they answer correctly. That view is useful, but it misses a practical risk. A model may know the right answer and still change its response when the same case is written in a different patient voice. This paper evaluates that risk as SDoH aware narrative anchoring bias. We use NarrativeShield SDoH MedQA, a counterfactual medical question answering dataset in which each case appears in persona based narratives while the answer key remains fixed. The dataset is reshaped from wide format into case grouped persona rows. We evaluate three open source instruction tuned LLMs from the Qwen2.5 family: 1.5B, 3B, and 7B. The final experiment uses 300 clinical cases and produces 8,100 model responses across three prompting conditions. We report persona level accuracy, counterfactual consistency, correct consistency, and narrative sensitivity error. Qwen2.5 7B achieves the best accuracy at 56.33 percent and the best correct consistency at 40.33 percent. Paired McNemar exact tests show significant accuracy gains for 7B over 3B in all prompt settings. Even so, narrative sensitivity remains, with the lowest error still at 31.67 percent. These results suggest that trustworthy clinical decision support should be evaluated by both average correctness and stability across medically equivalent patient narratives.

</details>


### [92] [DIAG: Diagnostic Iterative Alignment and Generation for Data-Efficient Mathematical Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22806)
*Guhan Chen,Songtao Tian,Bohan Li,Hejin Wang,YeXin Xie,Zixiong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出DIAG框架，通过自适应重塑练习分布来解决迭代偏好优化中的信号稀缺问题，结合诊断阶段（基于经验贝叶斯收缩估计器分配主题配额）和生成阶段（教师模型基于失败轨迹合成变体），在数学推理任务上显著提升了有效偏好对产出率和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代偏好优化在数学推理对齐中面临信号稀缺瓶颈：随着模型能力提升，静态问题集与模型不断演进的能力边界逐渐错位，产生的rollout要么过易要么过难，导致有效偏好对稀缺，严重制约训练效率。

Method: DIAG包含两阶段：1) 诊断阶段：通过诊断有效偏好对产出率校准探索-利用权衡，利用经验贝叶斯收缩估计器分配主题配额，优先选择高产出概念；2) 生成阶段：教师模型基于学生模型的失败轨迹合成针对性练习变体。理论上将DIAG解释为教师中介的KL正则化分布重加权近似，将练习分布推向学生能力边界以最大化有效偏好对产出。

Result: 实验表明DIAG跨迭代持续提升有效偏好对产出率，在等效训练预算下实现更强的数学推理性能，验证了其能蒸馏更多信息量的偏好监督信号。

Conclusion: DIAG通过自适应诊断与生成机制，有效解决了迭代偏好优化中的分布错位与信号稀缺问题，为LLM数学推理对齐提供了更高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Iterative preference optimization is essential for aligning Large Language Models on mathematical reasoning tasks, yet its efficiency is often throttled by signal scarcity: as the model improves, static problem sets become increasingly mismatched to the model's evolving competence, producing rollouts that are either too easy or too hard and therefore non-informative, which leads to a scarcity of valid preference pairs. We propose DIAG, a Diagnostic Iterative Alignment and Generation framework that adaptively reshapes the practice distribution to increase informative supervision and focus training near the student's current competence boundary. DIAG consists of two phases: (1) diagnosing valid preference-pair yield to calibrate the exploration-exploitation trade-off and allocate topic quotas via an Empirical Bayes shrinkage estimator, thereby prioritizing high-yield concepts; and (2) generating targeted practice, where a teacher synthesizes variants from the student's failure traces. We further provide a theoretical view interpreting DIAG as a teacher-mediated approximation to KL-regularized reweighting of the practice distribution toward the student's competence boundary, where valid preference-pair yield is maximized. Experiments show that DIAG boosts yield across iterations and delivers stronger reasoning performance under an iso-effective training budget, demonstrating that it can distill more informative preference supervision for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [93] [Industrial-Instruction: An End-to-End Framework for Building Instruction-Tuning and Benchmark Datasets from Industrial Technical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22817)
*Parsa Bakhtiari,Hassan Bashiri,Alireza Khalilipour,Masoud Nasiripour,Moharram Challenger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Industrial-Instruction，从真实工业技术报告（906 份 Panasonic 文档，7,525 页）构建两个开放 QA 数据集及端到端生成管线。管线采用版面感知抽取、语义检索索引，合成基于检索证据的多项选择 QA，覆盖五种查询-文档关系。经过滤后每个数据集约 1.36 万 QA 对。在 <10B 参数开源 LLM 上微调，Panasonic 基准上 Set-Match Accuracy 从 28.5% 提升至 42.0%，F1 从 46.6% 提升至 63.5%。同一管线分别用开源 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct 和闭源 Claude-Opus-4.6 生成两版数据：Claude 版语料更干净、微调增益更大但成本高约两数量级；MMLU 显示 Claude 版基本保留通用知识，Qwen 版有轻微遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业技术报告包含高价值维护、故障排查与产品工程知识，但其异构结构（密集文本、规格表、表格）导致标准检索与 QA 管线难以索引和推理，且缺乏基于此类文档的公开指令微调或基准数据集。

Method: 1. 使用版面感知抽取处理 906 份公开 Panasonic 文档（7,525 页）；2. 构建语义检索索引；3. 基于检索证据合成多项选择 QA，覆盖五种查询-文档关系（无关检索、单/多文档支持、单/多文档答案）；4. 从初始 2.39 万样本过滤得到约 1.36 万 QA 对，含源文档与保留基准分割；5. 分别用开源 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct 和闭源 Claude-Opus-4.6 生成两版平行数据集，便于开源与前沿模型数据生成的直接对比。

Result: 在 <10B 参数开源 LLM 上微调：Panasonic 基准 Set-Match Accuracy 从 28.5% 提升至 42.0%，F1 从 46.6% 提升至 63.5%。Claude-Opus-4.6 生成的数据集语料更干净、微调增益更大，但成本约高两数量级。MMLU 评估显示：Claude 版模型基本保留所有通用知识；Qwen 版模型出现轻微但可测度的遗忘效应。

Conclusion: Industrial-Instruction 提供了可复现、可扩展的工业基准与训练数据构建路径，同一管线下的开源/闭源模型对比揭示了数据质量与成本的权衡，为真实世界文档的大规模工业 QA 研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Industrial technical reports contain high-value knowledge for maintenance, troubleshooting, and product engineering, but their heterogeneous structure (dense prose, specifications, tables) makes them difficult to index and reason over with standard retrieval and QA pipelines, and no public instruction-tuning or benchmark datasets are built from such documents. We address this gap with Industrial-Instruction, contributing (i) two open QA datasets built from real industrial technical reports and (ii) the end-to-end pipeline that produces them. Using 906 public Panasonic documents (7,525 pages), we apply layout-aware extraction, build a semantic retrieval index, and synthesize multiple-choice QA grounded in retrieved evidence under five query-document relationships (irrelevant retrieval, single-/multi-document support, single-/multi-document answer). After filtering an initial 23.9k generated samples, each dataset provides approximately 13.6k QA pairs with source documents and a held-out benchmark split. Fine-tuning small open LLMs (under 10B parameters) improves Set-Match Accuracy from 28.5% to 42.0% and F1 from 46.6% to 63.5% on the Panasonic benchmark. We release two parallel versions built by the same pipeline: one generated with the open-weight Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct model and one with the closed, API-based Claude-Opus-4.6 model, enabling a direct comparison of open- versus frontier-model data generation. The Claude-Opus-4.6 dataset yields a cleaner raw corpus and larger fine-tuning gains, at roughly two orders of magnitude higher cost. MMLU evaluation shows models trained on the Claude-Opus-4.6 data retain essentially all general knowledge, versus a small but measurable forgetting effect for the Qwen-generated data. Together, these datasets and pipeline offer a practical, reproducible path toward scalable industrial benchmarks and training data from real-world documentation.

</details>


### [94] [SAVER: Selective Auditing of Verbal Evidence for Error Recovery in VLM Change Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22857)
*Youdi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出SAVER方法，通过审计VLM回答中的语言证据并选择性重提示，显著提升视觉变化推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在视觉变化推理任务中常失败，即使视觉编码器包含足够信息。观察到正确输出包含显式语言证据（物体名称、颜色、空间位置），而错误输出缺乏此类证据。

Method: SAVER（选择性语言证据审计错误恢复），轻量级基于规则的方法，解析VLM回答寻找证据，仅当证据缺失或不一致时触发结构化重提示。

Result: 在三个变化检测基准和四个VLM上，SAVER显著提升表达失败导致的错误任务准确率，CLEVR-Change上最高提升+25.8%。证据模式也可由LLM单次调用生成，效果媲美手工调优门控。消融实验证实证据门控而非单纯重提示驱动改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式语言证据审计和选择性重提示，可有效解决VLM在视觉变化推理中的表达失败问题，且方法轻量、可泛化。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) frequently fail at visual change reasoning, even when their vision encoders contain sufficient information. We observe that correct VLM outputs tend to contain explicit verbal evidence (object names, colors, spatial locations) that supports the claimed change, while incorrect outputs often lack such evidence. We propose SAVER (Selective Auditing of Verbal Evidence for Error Recovery), a lightweight, rule-based method that parses VLM responses for this evidence and triggers structured reprompting only when evidence is missing or inconsistent. Across three change detection benchmarks and four VLMs, SAVER significantly improves accuracy on tasks where errors stem from the model failing to articulate what it saw (expression failures), with gains up to +25.8% on CLEVR-Change. The evidence patterns can also be generated by an LLM in a single call, matching the hand-tuned gate on CLEVR-Change. Ablation experiments confirm that the evidence gate, not reprompting alone, drives the improvement.

</details>


### [95] [AraDetox: A Multi-Dialect Arabic Detoxification Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22894)
*Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AraDetox，一个多方言阿拉伯语去毒化数据集，包含10,500条有害社交媒体帖子和84,000条去毒化重写，使用GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash生成，覆盖现代标准阿拉伯语、海湾、黎凡特和埃及阿拉伯语。通过人工评估和自动分析验证，证明去毒化主要是保持语义的重写任务，且生成的变体在方言风格上与参考语料库一致。数据集公开可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语有害语言检测已受关注，但文本去毒化研究不足，缺乏大规模多方言去毒化资源，阻碍了阿拉伯语安全文本生成和多方言NLP研究。

Method: 利用GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash生成84,000条去毒化重写，覆盖四种阿拉伯语方言；通过人工评估和自动分析（词汇变化、语义保持、情感、方言风格）评估输出质量；对比现有资源，分析两种去毒化方法：最小编辑词汇替换和保持语义的重构。

Result: 结果表明去毒化是主要保持语义的重写任务：大量词汇和结构重构伴随高语义相似性；人工评估确认成功移除有害语言且基本保留原意；方言分析显示生成变体与参考方言语料库有可测量的风格一致性；对比揭示两种互补去毒化方法。

Conclusion: 研究证明可通过大模型辅助生成和人工验证构建大规模阿拉伯语去毒化资源，数据集公开支持未来阿拉伯语去毒化、安全文本生成和多方言NLP研究。

Abstract: Arabic harmful-language detection has received considerable attention, yet Arabic text detoxification remains underexplored. We introduce AraDetox, a multi-dialect Arabic detoxification dataset comprising 10,500 harmful social-media posts and 84,000 detoxified rewrites generated using GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash across Modern Standard Arabic, Gulf, Levantine, and Egyptian Arabic. The generated outputs were assessed through human evaluation and automatic analyses of lexical change, semantic preservation, sentiment, and dialectal style. Results show that detoxification is primarily a meaning-preserving rewriting task: substantial lexical and structural reformulation is accompanied by consistently high semantic similarity. Human evaluation confirms successful harmful-language removal while largely preserving the original meaning. Dialectal analyses further indicate that the generated variants exhibit measurable stylistic alignment with reference Arabic dialect corpora. Comparison with existing resources highlights two complementary approaches to detoxification: minimal-edit lexical substitution and meaning-preserving reformulation. Our findings demonstrate that large-scale Arabic detoxification resources can be constructed through LLM-assisted generation and human verification. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/ArabicNLP-UK/AraDetox to support future research on Arabic detoxification, safe text generation, and multi-dialect Arabic NLP.

</details>


### [96] [SelFusion: Self-distillation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22898)
*Hyeongsoo Lim,Jinyoung Kim,Eunseo Seo,Minho Jang,Jiwon Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SelFusion，一种针对扩散语言模型（DLM）的自蒸馏框架，通过双向知识蒸馏在不同掩码级别的前向传播间动态传递知识，无需外部教师模型即可显著提升生成质量，甚至在多种配置下超越 LLM 教师性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽缓解了自回归 LLM 的延迟瓶颈，但生成质量下降限制了实际应用；常规知识蒸馏（KD）引入外部教师模型效果有限甚至有害，亟需一种无需外部教师、能有效提升 DLM 生成质量的方法。

Method: SelFusion 采用自蒸馏策略：进行两次不同掩码概率的前向传播，定义硬模式（较大掩码概率）和易模式（较小掩码概率）；引入双向 KD，基于 token 级正确性动态决定蒸馏方向，避免易模式在错误 token 上过度自信导致的负面影响。

Result: 在指令遵循任务上，SelFusion 显著优于使用外部 LLM 和 DLM 教师的其他 KD 方法；在许多配置下，经过 SelFusion 训练的学生模型甚至超越了 LLM 教师的性能。

Conclusion: SelFusion 为提升扩散语言模型生成质量提供了一条实用路径，证明了自蒸馏配合动态双向知识传递可有效克服常规 KD 的局限，且无需依赖外部教师模型。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) alleviate the inherent latency bottleneck of autoregressive (AR) large language models (LLMs), but their degraded generation quality limits practical applicability. Although knowledge distillation (KD) can be a promising direction for improving performance, we empirically find that naively applying conventional KD yields only marginal gains, or even degrades generation quality. Based on these observations, we propose a novel self-distillation framework for DLMs, namely SelFusion. To enable effective KD without an external teacher model, SelFusion performs two forward passes with different masking levels, defining the hard mode with a larger masking probability and the easy mode with a smaller masking probability. However, the easy mode is not always more accurate than the hard mode and can be overconfident on incorrect tokens. Thus, we introduce bidirectional KD between the two modes, which can dynamically determine the distillation direction based on token-level correctness. Experimental results on instruction-following tasks show that the proposed self-distillation substantially outperforms other KD methods with external LLM and DLM teachers. In many configurations, the student trained with SelFusion even surpasses the performance of the LLM teacher, providing a practical path toward improving DLM generation quality. Source code can be found at https://github.com/scai-research/SelFusion_official

</details>


### [97] [Do Spoken Language Models Hear Speech as They Read Text? Bridging Structural Gaps Between Speech and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22908)
*Hyeonyu Kim,Hwayeon Kim,Youngwon Choi,Myeongkyun Cho,Huu-Kim Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前语音语言模型（SLM）中语音与文本表示存在弱对齐问题，提出一种解耦长度错位与语义对齐的简单框架，显著提升指令遵循能力和跨任务泛化，在多个基准上达到竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLM虽在下游任务表现强劲，但指令遵循能力较弱且泛化有限。分析发现语音与文本表示在模型内部仍弱对齐，根源在于连续时变语音与离散文本的结构差异未被充分解决。

Method: 提出解耦长度错位与语义对齐的框架，通过显式处理语音与文本的结构差异，促进两者表示间更紧密的对应关系。

Result: 在多个基准实验中，该方法对比强基线取得竞争性性能，验证了显式解决语音-文本结构差异的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模和解决连续语音与离散文本的结构差异对SLM训练至关重要，解耦长度与语义对齐是提升模型指令遵循和泛化能力的关键。

Abstract: Spoken Language Models (SLMs) generate textual responses directly from speech, offering an alternative to cascaded systems. Despite recent advances, existing SLMs still exhibit weaker instruction-following behavior and limited generalization across diverse tasks compared to text-based language models. Our analysis shows that speech and text representations in current SLMs remain weakly aligned despite strong downstream performance, indicating that structural differences between continuous, temporally varying speech and discrete text remain insufficiently addressed. To address this, we propose a simple framework that decouples length mismatch from semantic alignment and encourages closer correspondence between speech and text representations. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate competitive performance against strong baselines, underscoring the importance of explicitly addressing structural differences between speech and text in SLM training. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/jaykim9870/Do_SLMs_Hear_Speech_as_They_Read_Text.

</details>


### [98] [Exploring Dowker Homology for Sentence Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22909)
*Marius Huber,Juri Opitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨Dowker同调在句子相似度任务中的应用，将句子token嵌入视为点云，发现其能捕捉相似度信息，但单数值摘要未超越传统池化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望利用拓扑数据分析工具Dowker同调来分析句子对在Transformer潜在空间中的相对位置，从而捕捉句子相似度信息，并探索其可视化和单数值摘要的潜力。

Method: 将句子对的token嵌入视为共同潜在空间中的两个点云，计算Dowker同调特征，并通过回归预测真实相似度分数；同时尝试从同调特征导出单数值摘要直接度量相似度。实验涵盖微调和未微调的Transformer模型。

Result: Dowker同调特征能有效捕捉句子相似度信息，回归预测表现良好，且可用于可视化检查相似度数据和模型。然而，从同调导出的单数值摘要虽表现尚可，但未能超越基于传统池化方法的标准句子相似度度量。

Conclusion: Dowker同调为句子相似度分析提供了新的拓扑视角，具备捕捉相似度信息和可视化能力，但目前其单数值摘要在实际相似度度量任务中尚不优于成熟的池化方法，未来可进一步改进。

Abstract: Dowker homology is a topological tool that may be used to analyze the relative position of two point clouds living in a common space. We investigate whether Dowker homology captures sentence similarity information by treating the embeddings of the tokens that constitute a sentence pair as a pair of point clouds in the latent space of a transformer model, using both models that have and have not been fine-tuned for sentence similarity. We find that Dowker homology captures sentence similarity information, as measured by regressing Dowker homology features onto ground-truth similarity scores, and that it can be used for visual inspection of similarity data and models. In an attempt to make Dowker homology readily applicable, we derive from it single-number summaries that we expect to capture sentence similarity directly. These turn out to work reasonably well, but without outperforming standard sentence similarity measures based on established pooling methods.

</details>


### [99] [Knowing Isn't Always Saying: When Do Spatial Encodings Reach Answers in Vision-Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22916)
*Zeyu Wang,Xinming Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过方向修补（direction patching）这一因果干预方法，系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中空间信息从编码到被用于回答的传输过程。研究发现，空间信息对答案logits的因果影响仅在中深层出现；思维链提示抑制了大多数模型中即时的目标词argmax级传输，而视觉接地提示则保持传输通道开放；正向目标logit增益可能低于argmax阈值，且传输可在最终前缀token或深层回答步骤重新出现。跨10个VLM的这些局部效应形成了描述性的传输模式，互补实验揭示了模式随数据集、属性和编码幅度的变化。结论将编码-接地鸿沟重构为VLM中的条件传输问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已知在其隐藏状态中编码空间信息，但在回答时常无法利用这些信息。然而，目前不清楚编码的空间信息何时、在何处到达答案生成过程。论文旨在填补这一认知空白，揭示空间信息从编码到被因果用于答案的传输动力学。

Method: 提出方向修补（direction patching）方法：一种跨层、token位置和提示格式的类条件因果干预。基于先前编码证据构建空间-ID方向，通过干预测量空间信息对答案logits的因果影响。在10个VLM上系统实验，涵盖不同数据集、属性和编码幅度。

Result: 1. 空间信息对答案logits的因果影响仅在中深层（mid-to-deep depths）显现。2. 文本思维链抑制了大多数模型中即时的目标词argmax级传输；视觉接地提示保持传输通道开放。3. 正向目标logit增益可能低于argmax阈值，传输可在最终前缀token或深层回答步骤重新出现。4. 跨10个VLM形成描述性传输模式；互补实验表征模式随数据集、属性、编码幅度的变化。

Conclusion: 编码-接地鸿沟（encoding-grounding gap）并非简单的编码缺失，而是VLM中条件传输（conditional transport）的问题：空间信息虽被编码，但其传输到答案生成受层深、提示格式、推理步骤等条件调控。理解这种条件传输机制对提升VLM的空间推理能力至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-language models are known to encode spatial information in their hidden states, yet often fail to use it when answering. However, it remains unclear when and where this encoded information reaches the answer. We address this with direction patching, a class-conditioned causal intervention applied across layers, token positions, and prompt formats. Using spatial-ID directions constructed following prior encoding evidence, we find that causal influence on answer logits emerges only at mid-to-deep depths. Text chain-of-thought suppresses immediate object-word argmax-level transport in most models, while visually grounded prompts keep it open. Positive target-logit gain can remain below the argmax threshold, and transport can re-emerge at the final prefix token or at the answer step in deeper layers. Across the ten VLMs we study, these local effects form descriptive transport patterns. Complementary experiments characterize how these patterns shift across datasets, attributes, and encoding amplitudes. Together, these results reframe the encoding-grounding gap as a problem of conditional transport in VLMs.

</details>


### [100] [HelaBERT: Enhancing Sinhala Language Understanding with Dual Pooling Classification Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22922)
*Thisen Ekanayake,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HelaBERT是一个基于BERT的两个掩码语言模型家族，从头在约10亿个僧伽罗语token上预训练，针对僧伽罗语的黏着形态和复杂文字定制了SentencePiece Unigram分词器，并在四个下游分类任务上评估，提出双池化分类头并发现其在情感分析和新闻类别分类上有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为僧伽罗语NLP研究提供预训练模型，解决低资源语言缺乏高质量预训练模型的问题，支持进一步的僧伽罗语自然语言处理研究。

Method: 从头预训练两个模型：HelaBERT-Small（约2330万参数，6层）和HelaBERT-Large（约1.1亿参数，12层），使用约10亿token的僧伽罗语语料（MADLAD-400、CulturaX、自定义新闻、维基百科、网络爬虫），采用词表大小32,000的SentencePiece Unigram分词器。在四个下游分类任务（新闻类别、新闻来源、情感分析、写作风格）上评估，使用5个独立随机种子和分层80/20划分。提出双池化分类头并系统对比标准[CLS]-线性头。

Result: 双池化分类头在情感分析上持续提升，在新闻类别分类上对HelaBERT-Small有中等提升；标准[CLS]-线性头在新闻来源分类（短标题任务）上保持竞争力。两个模型均已发布。

Conclusion: HelaBERT模型推动了僧伽罗语NLP发展，双池化头在特定任务有效，标准头在短输入任务仍具优势，发布模型以支持后续研究。

Abstract: We present HelaBERT, a family of two BERT-based masked language models pre-trained from scratch on approximately 1 billion tokens of Sinhala text sourced from MADLAD-400, CulturaX, and a custom corpus comprising news articles, Sinhala Wikipedia, and web crawl data. HelaBERT-Small (~23.3M parameters, 6 layers) and HelaBERT-Large (~110M parameters, 12 layers) both use a SentencePiece Unigram tokenizer (vocabulary size 32,000) tailored to Sinhala's agglutinative morphology and complex script. We evaluate both models on four downstream Sinhala text classification tasks: news category classification, news source classification, sentiment analysis, and writing style classification, using 5 independent seed runs with stratified 80/20 train/test splits. We additionally propose a dual pooling classification head and evaluate it systematically across all four tasks, finding consistent improvements on sentiment analysis and a moderate gain on news category classification for HelaBERT-Small, while the standard [CLS]-linear head remains competitive on news source classification, a headline-level task with short average input length. We release both models to support further research in Sinhala NLP.

</details>


### [101] [What Proves You Wrong: Benchmarking Language Models on Falsifiable Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22948)
*Ziyue Wang,Aomufei Yuan,Yiran Yao,Linli Yao,Hongyao Zuo,Ziwen Gong,Yuanxin Liu,Shicheng Li,Yishuo Cai,Tong Yang,Xu Sun,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Lit2Test基准，用于评估大语言模型生成的科研提案质量。该基准通过六字段合同围绕可证伪结果设计，使提案预先承诺能证伪的观察，从而使质量可判定而非仅可争辩。实验表明该基准能稳健区分四个前沿模型的排名，且区分度源于提案的测试和指标质量而非表面流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估大语言模型生成科研想法的方法缺乏共享决策规则：自由形式评判受风格和立场影响，而基于后续论文的评分仅奖励对单一已实现轨迹的复现。需要一种能使提案质量在事前即可判定的评估框架。

Method: 构建Lit2Test基准：从200个真实论文邻域前瞻性构建，采用围绕可证伪结果的六字段合同，引导四个前沿模型生成提案；通过1200次盲测配对比较（双向呈现顺序）进行评估；引入诊断性控制和有界人工校准审计可靠性，三名标注者在明确可靠性边界内佐证结论。

Result: 在所有10000次bootstrap重采样中，Lit2Test均恢复了四个模型的严格排名；模型间的区分度主要来自提案中测试设计和评估指标的质量，而非表面语言流畅度。

Conclusion: Lit2Test提供了一个可靠、可审计的基准，能基于可证伪性标准客观评估LLM科研提案质量。作者已公开发布基准、构建流水线和审计工件供社区使用。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to propose research ideas, yet the prevailing ways of judging such ideas supply no shared decision rule: free-form judging sways with style and position, and scoring against a later paper rewards recovery of one realized trajectory. We introduce a benchmark that carries a proposal from Literature to Test: the Lit2Test benchmark centers on a six-field contract organized around a falsifying outcome, so that every proposal precommits the observation that would prove it wrong, making its quality decidable in the first place rather than merely arguable. Built prospectively from 200 real-paper neighborhoods, Lit2Test elicits proposals from four frontier models and compares them through 1,200 pairwise comparisons judged blind in both presentation orders. The protocol audits its own reliability through diagnostic controls and bounded human calibration, with three annotators corroborating the conclusions within explicitly stated reliability bounds. Lit2Test recovers a strict ranking of the four models in all 10,000 bootstrap replicates, and the separation comes from the quality of the proposed tests and metrics rather than from surface fluency. We release the benchmark, construction pipeline, and audit artifacts for public use.

</details>


### [102] [The Illusion of Control: Why Bare Classifier Inversion Silently Fails in Concept-Bottleneck Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22956)
*Qi Bing,Xiaowei Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究概念瓶颈可控生成中多属性控制的推理时代码获取方法，对比了分类器反演、参考文本编码和后验标签条件先验三种方式，发现所有反演变体均不如简单的后验先验，且裸反演会静默崩塌至随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念瓶颈可控生成通过低维概念代码实现多属性控制，但在部署时需从目标属性配置合成代码。由于概念代码缺乏直接的语言模型流畅度项，反演正则化需将代码约束向编码器训练分布，因此需要系统研究不同获取推理时代码的方法及其有效性。

Method: 在多轴组合泛化设置下，比较三种获取推理时代码的方法：分类器反演（针对编码器头）、参考文本编码、后验标签条件先验。测试裸反演及三种正则化变体：标签无关和标签条件的马哈拉诺比斯惩罚、条件归一化流密度基线。使用三个主干模型家族（124M至8B参数）进行实验，在真实基准和外部评估器上验证。

Result: 所有反演变体均逊于简单的后验先验（基于每组合编码器均值拟合）。裸分类器反演静默崩塌至随机水平，归因于可直接测量的离流形代码。在真实基准和外部评估器上验证了该诊断，并能与已发布基线公平比较。

Conclusion: 后验标签条件先验是获取推理时代码的更优选择，分类器反演存在离流形问题导致性能崩塌，正则化反演仍不如简单先验。这为概念瓶颈可控生成的部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Concept-bottleneck controllable generation routes multi-attribute control through a low-dimensional concept code that, at deployment, must be synthesised from a target attribute configuration. We study this problem in concept-bottleneck text generation under multi-axis compositional generalisation, comparing three ways to obtain the inference-time code: classifier inversion against the encoder heads, reference-text encoding, and a post-hoc label-conditioned prior. Since a concept code admits no direct LM-fluency term, regularising inversion must instead constrain the code toward the encoder's training distribution. We therefore test bare inversion and three regularised variants: label-agnostic and label-conditioned Mahalanobis penalties, and a conditional normalising-flow density baseline. Every inversion variant we test underperforms a simple post-hoc prior fitted to per-combination encoder means on the same checkpoints, across three backbone families spanning $124$M to $8$B parameters. The bare form of classifier inversion also silently collapses to chance, traceable to a directly measured off-manifold code. We validate this diagnosis on real-world benchmarks and under external evaluators, enabling fair comparison with published baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Closed-Loop Bayesian Molecular Inverse Design with Semantic LLM Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22967)
*Yaoyao Xu,Xinjian Zhao,Xiaozhuang Song,Lei Bai,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的闭环分子逆向设计框架，将代理模型视为设计选择的核心，利用冻结的LLM直接在文本形式下推理任务指令、SMILES优化历史和神谕反馈，生成结构化决策信号指导分子生成器，实现可解释的优化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际分子逆向设计通常是闭环候选池富集问题，标准高斯过程代理在压缩连续嵌入中操作，丢失了化学家决策所依赖的子结构和参考相似性信号，需要一种能直接处理文本形式历史和反馈的代理模型。

Method: 提出框架，使用冻结的大语言模型作为代理，输入任务指令、SMILES优化历史和神谕反馈，输出结构化决策信号（选择参考分子、探索利用原则、可选指导句），转化为下一轮生成器的条件文本，实现自然语言的可检查优化轨迹。

Result: 在MolQA药物和材料设计任务上，优于一次性提示，与基于GP的BO基线相当或更强；发现领域依赖接口：仅参考迁移最适合二元药物靶点，而添加简洁代理总结对连续材料属性更有益。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了LLM作为代理在分子逆向设计中的有效性，提供了可解释的自然语言优化过程，并揭示了不同任务类型对代理输出形式的不同需求。

Abstract: Practical molecular inverse design is rarely a one-shot generation problem; it often takes the form of closed-loop candidate-pool enrichment, where under a limited oracle budget the goal is to \emph{increase the fraction of generated molecules that match a desired property profile}. Bayesian optimization (BO) offers a natural framework for this setting, yet standard Gaussian-process surrogates typically operate in compressed continuous embeddings, which discard the substructural and reference-similarity signals that chemists naturally use to decide where to look next. We propose \textbf{\method}, a closed-loop framework in which the surrogate, rather than the generator, is treated as the locus of design choice, and instantiate it with a frozen large language model that reasons directly over the task instruction, SMILES-level optimization history, and oracle feedback in their native textual form. At each iteration, the surrogate returns a structured decision signal that selects informative reference molecules under an exploration and exploitation principle, optionally with a concise guidance sentence. This signal is converted into next-round conditioning text for a frozen molecular generator, yielding an inspectable optimization trace in natural language. Experiments on MolQA drug and material design tasks show that \method improves over one-shot prompting, is competitive with or stronger than GP-based BO baselines, and reveals a domain-dependent interface: reference-only transfer works best for binary drug targets, while adding a concise surrogate summary is more beneficial for continuous material

</details>


### [104] [What Does Activation Steering Control? Attribution Across Answer Encodings and Output-Sensitive Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22985)
*Zhiwei Gao,Shaowen Peng,Shoko Wakamiya,Eiji Aramaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation（跨编码转向评估）方法，揭示激活转向在不同答案编码下表现出“提取索引跟随”而非语义标签跟随的现象，证明单一编码下的转向增益无法确定干预控制的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活转向评估通常使用构建方向时的答案编码，报告的增益可能反映预期判断，也可能仅反映与构建时见到的答案标识符兼容，缺乏区分语义控制与表面形式跟随的方法。

Method: 提出Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation：冻结干预方向，重新编码答案到相同保留项；在NormBank、MNLI、SC101数据集上测试；变换标识符词汇（A/B/C, X/Y/Z, 1/2/3）和行序；匹配层间方向范数；分析低秩输出敏感成分；对比CAA和ITI风格方法。

Result: 1) NormBank上CAA表现出提取索引跟随，而非语义标签跟随，效应随标识符词汇和行序变化跟踪提取索引而非行位置；2) 匹配范数后效应主要出现在深层；3) 含15.4%方向平方范数的低秩输出敏感成分保留96.3%效应；4) ITI风格方法在三模型上也偏好提取索引跟随；5) MNLI偏好提取索引跟随，SC101偏好语义标签跟随；6) 多选题和开放式评估得出不同行为结论。

Conclusion: 单一答案编码下的转向增益本身无法识别干预控制的内容；跨编码评估揭示转向可能控制提取索引而非语义标签，不同数据集和评估形式得出的行为结论可能不同，需谨慎解释转向结果。}

Abstract: Activation steering is often evaluated under the answer encoding used to construct the direction. A reported gain may reflect the intended judgment or compatibility with answer identifiers seen during construction. We introduce Cross-Encoding Steering Evaluation, which freezes an intervention while re-encoding answers to the same held-out items. On NormBank, after A/B/C identifiers are reassigned, contrastive activation addition (CAA) induces larger target-versus-source score changes for the extraction indices than for the semantic labels under the new mapping. We call this extraction-index following. Varying identifier vocabulary (A/B/C, X/Y/Z, or 1/2/3) and row order shows that the effect tracks extraction index rather than row position. After matching direction norms across layers, extraction-index following emerges mainly at later depths. A low-rank output-sensitive component containing 15.4% of the direction's squared norm retains 96.3% of this effect. An Inference-Time Intervention (ITI)-style method also favors extraction-index over semantic-label following on NormBank in three models. In aggregate, MNLI favors extraction-index following, whereas Social Chemistry 101 (SC101) favors semantic-label following. Multiple-choice and open-ended evaluations can yield different behavioral conclusions. Thus, a steering gain under one answer encoding does not by itself identify what the intervention controls.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Runtime Action Interference for AI Control of AlphaStar in StarCraft II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21398)
*Jaymari Chua,Chen Wang,Liming Zhu,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出运行时动作干扰（RAI）机制，在保持策略参数不变的情况下，通过冷却条件和内容检测器调节动作节奏并过滤有毒行为；在《星际争霸II》人类参与者研究中发现，相同受控对手在能力声明披露与否下，用户对公平性、信任度、毒性的感知显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练好的强化学习策略并不完全决定用户遇到的行为：部署代码仍会调度、准入、抑制或替换其提议的动作。需要一种在不改变策略参数的前提下，于推理后控制动作节奏和过滤配置动作模式的机制，并研究能力披露对用户感知的影响。

Method: 1. 设计并实现运行时动作干扰（RAI）机制，包含冷却条件控制动作速率、内容检测器过滤有毒行为（如工人单位骚扰），不满足条件时派发空操作。2. 在AlphaStar actor.py复现版中集成RAI并开源。3. 开展《星际争霸II》人类参与者研究，对比同一高能力、速率受限对手在隐瞒能力声明与披露能力声明两种呈现下的用户感知，测量公平性、信任度、毒性三个维度（1-5分量表）。

Result: 隐瞒能力声明组：公平性均值3.90、信任度3.50、毒性2.00。披露能力声明组：公平性均值2.62、信任度4.31、毒性2.85。披露导致所有专业水平组的感知公平性降低、感知毒性升高；信任度在新手和专家组上升、中级组下降。说明相同控制配置下，能力信息呈现方式显著改变用户感知。

Conclusion: 人机评测必须将执行栈内的控制机制与能力披露分离，并将公平性、信任度、毒性作为用户体验的独立维度进行评估。

Abstract: A trained reinforcement learning policy does not determine the complete behavior that users encounter: deployment code still schedules, admits, suppresses, or replaces its proposed actions. We contribute \emph{runtime action interference} (RAI), an AI control mechanism that preserves policy parameters while regulating action pacing and filtering configured action patterns after inference. RAI releases a proposed action only when its cooldown condition is satisfied and its content detector does not flag the action; otherwise, it dispatches a no-op. The detector covers specified toxic behaviors, including worker-unit harassment, while the cooldown controls action rate. We implement RAI in a replication of AlphaStar actor.py and make the implementation and reproducibility materials available through an open source code repository. We deployed RAI in a \textit{StarCraft~II} human participant study that compared two presentations of the same opponent with high capability and rate limited actions; we withheld its capability claim in one presentation and disclosed it in the other. On response scales from 1 to 5, we observed pooled fairness, trust, and toxicity means of 3.90, 3.50, and 2.00 under claim withholding, compared with 2.62, 4.31, and 2.85 under disclosure. Disclosure corresponded with lower perceived fairness and higher perceived toxicity across every expertise group, whereas trust increased among novices and experts but decreased among intermediate participants. Our human evaluation therefore shows that perceptions of an opponent controlled through RAI can vary substantially with the capability information presented to users, even when the configured control remains constant. We conclude that human-computer evaluations must separate control within the execution stack from capability disclosure and assess fairness, trust, and toxicity as distinct dimensions of human experience.

</details>


### [106] [Federated Ensemble Forecasting Under Supply-Chain Market Volatility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21399)
*Shunmukha Sagar Puppala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出FEF NCL（基于负相关学习的联邦集成预测），一种分布式供应链预测方法，在合成数据集上显著优于现有联邦基线，降低MAPE并提升延迟风险识别能力，适用于异构供应商、运费和商品条件下的客户端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决供应链预测系统在市场冲击、非独立同分布区域需求、以及商业数据不愿中心化等挑战下的预测准确性和鲁棒性问题。

Method: FEF NCL框架包含：时序特征编码器、客户端级漂移评分、可靠性加权聚合、可解释性层（揭示市场与供应商变量对预测的贡献），并采用负相关学习训练专业化预测专家，抑制模型错误冗余。

Result: 在合成测试集上，加权MAPE从最佳联邦基线的13.9%降至12.4%，延迟风险宏F1从0.755提升至0.801，高波动分位数误差比SCAFFOLD降低2.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 负相关专业化在客户端面临不同供应商、运费和商品条件时有效；但实际部署仍需更强的隐私分析、实时漂移监控和运营校准。

Abstract: Supply chain forecasting systems increasingly operate under market shocks, non-identically distributed regional demand, and limited willingness to centralize commercial data. This work proposes Federated Ensemble Forecasting with Negative-Correlation Learning (FEF NCL), a distributed method that trains specialized forecasting experts across client nodes while discouraging redundant model errors. The framework combines temporal feature encoders, client level drift scoring, reliability-weighted aggregation, and an explain ability layer that exposes the market and supplier variables most responsible for each forecast. A single synthetic dataset is used to evaluate the design. It contains 124,800 weekly SKU region observations from ten regional client nodes, 60 product families, 40 suppliers, five commodity groups, and a 2021-2024 volatility profile with explicit price-shock regimes. Because the dataset is synthetic, the reported results should be interpreted as controlled evidence of internal consistency rather than real-world validation. Across the synthetic test split, FEF NCL reduces weighted mean absolute percentage error from 13.9% for the best federated baseline to 12.4%, improves delay-risk macro-F1 from 0.755 to 0.801, and lowers the high volatility quintile error by 2.1 percentage points relative to SCAFFOLD. The analysis suggests that negative-correlation specialization is useful when clients face different supplier, freight, and commodity conditions, although deployment would require stronger privacy analysis, live drift monitoring, and operational calibration. Index Terms federated learning, ensemble learning, negative correlation learning, supply chain forecasting, market volatility, data drift, demand planning, risk governance

</details>


### [107] [Class-Conditioned Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Imbalanced Time Series Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21473)
*Md Shahriar Kabir,Mayesha Maliha R. Mithila,Anne H. H. Ngu,Mylène C. Q. Farias,Byron Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对不平衡时间序列数据中的量化问题，提出了CC-GMNet-TS，一种基于类条件高斯混合模型的量化器，结合Transformer特征提取器与逐类别潜在混合模型，在三个基准测试中优于传统聚合器和最新深度量化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化（估计未标记实例包中的类别流行度）在生物信号监测、跌倒检测和活动识别等领域至关重要，但现有方法在处理不平衡时间序列数据时表现不足，特别是缺乏针对罕见但信息丰富模式的建模能力。

Method: 提出CC-GMNet-TS：使用Transformer作为特征提取器，为每个类别分配独立的紧凑高斯混合模型在有界潜在空间中，对片段嵌入按类别特定组件评分以生成包级表示；采用人工流行度协议（APP）和先验偏移包采样（PShift）构建训练包，覆盖广泛的类别流行度场景；采用量化导向损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在EMG手势数据、SmartFallMM和UCI-HAR三个基准测试中，CC-GMNet-TS的量化误差均低于传统聚合器和近期深度量化器；消融实验验证了Transformer骨干网络和类条件混合模型在PShift训练中的贡献。

Conclusion: 类条件高斯混合模型结合Transformer特征提取能有效捕捉不平衡时间序列中的罕见模式，通过PShift训练策略实现跨多种流行度场景的鲁棒量化，为生物信号监测等实际应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Quantification, estimating class prevalences in bags of unlabeled instances is vital in domains where aggregate statistics are more important than individual instance labels, such as biosignal monitoring, fall detection, and activity recognition. We investigate this issue in the challenging setting of imbalanced time series data and develop CC-GMNet-TS, a class-conditioned Gaussian mixture quantifier that combines a Transformer-based feature extractor with per-class latent mixtures. Unlike previous mixture-based quantifiers, which use a single Gaussian mixture shared by all classes, CC-GMNet-TS assigns each class its own compact mixture in a bounded latent space and scores segment embeddings against these class-specific components to create bag-level representations that emphasize rare but informative patterns. Bags are constructed from labeled pools using the Artificial Prevalence Protocol (APP) and prior shift bag sampling (PShift) to cover a wide range of class prevalence scenarios, and the model is trained end-to-end with a quantification-oriented loss. Experiments on three benchmarks: EMG Data for Gestures, SmartFallMM, and UCI-HAR show that CC-GMNet-TS achieves lower error across the three benchmarks compared to traditional aggregators and recent deep quantifiers, while ablations confirm the contributions of both the Transformer backbone and class-conditioned mixtures during PShift.

</details>


### [108] [Congruence Decomposition with Neural Block Solvers for Large-Scale PCI Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21485)
*Yeqing Qiu,Chengpiao Huang,Ye Xue,Akang Wang,Fan Xu,Zhipeng Jiang,Dong Zhang,Ruoyu Sun,Qingjiang Shi,Zhi-Quan Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于同余分解的神经块求解器框架，用于大规模PCI分配，将模干扰目标分解为块级Min-k-Partition子问题，结合图神经网络高效求解，实验表明优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G密集网络中PCI复用不可避免，导致碰撞、混淆和多种模干扰，联合缓解这些效应构成大规模多目标组合优化问题，难以在实际网络规模下高效求解。

Method: 利用PCI值的算术结构，将多个模干扰目标解耦为一系列块级Min-k-Partition子问题，再通过图着色解决PCI冲突；对NP-hard的Min-k-Partition子问题，将其松弛的二次形式参数化为图神经网络，开发神经块求解器实现大规模高效优化，最后通过条件期望舍入并伴随理论保证恢复离散分配。

Result: 在合成蜂窝图和真实5G网络上实验，该方法在模干扰降低、冲突消除和计算效率方面持续优于现有模干扰感知基线。

Conclusion: 同余分解结合神经块求解器为大规模PCI分配提供了高效且有效的解决方案，展示了将组合优化与图神经网络结合的潜力。

Abstract: Physical Cell Identity (PCI) assignment is essential for interference management in dense 5G networks. As cellular networks scale, PCI reuse becomes unavoidable, which may cause collisions, confusions, and multiple forms of modular interference. Jointly mitigating these effects gives rise to a large-scale, multi-objective combinatorial optimization problem that is difficult to solve efficiently at practical network scales. In this work, we propose a congruence decomposition framework with neural block solvers for large-scale PCI assignment. The proposed decomposition exploits the arithmetic structure of PCI values to decouple multiple modular interference objectives into a collection of blockwise Min-$k$-Partition subproblems, followed by a graph coloring procedure to resolve PCI conflicts. For the resulting NP-hard Min-$k$-Partition subproblems, we develop neural block solvers by parameterizing their relaxed quadratic formulations with graph neural networks, enabling efficient optimization at large scales. Discrete assignments are recovered through conditional expectation rounding with theoretical guarantees. Experiments on synthetic cellular graphs and real-world 5G networks show that the proposed method consistently outperforms existing modular-interference-aware baselines in modular interference reduction, conflict elimination, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [109] [KAN-Robust-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Robustness of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21488)
*Mohammad Meymani,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）在面对对抗性逃避攻击时的认证鲁棒性和经验鲁棒性，提供了随机平滑和区间界传播的数学基础，并系统评估了不同防御策略和架构下的KAN模型在FGSM、PGD、C&W攻击下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在面对对抗性攻击（尤其是逃避攻击）时存在严重脆弱性，攻击者可生成人眼难以察觉的扰动样本欺骗模型。现有研究多关注传统神经网络，而KAN作为一种新型架构，其对抗鲁棒性尚未系统探索，因此需要研究KAN的认证与经验鲁棒性，寻找最优防御策略。

Method: 1. 提供随机平滑和区间界传播的数学理论基础；2. 报告KAN模型在随机平滑下的ℓ2认证鲁棒性；3. 对多种防御和非防御KAN架构进行系统评估，使用FGSM、PGD、C&W三种强攻击进行经验鲁棒性测试；4. 对比不同防御策略和架构，寻找最优配置。

Result: 论文给出了KAN在随机平滑下的ℓ2认证鲁棒性边界，并通过实验对比了不同KAN架构及防御策略在FGSM、PGD、C&W攻击下的经验鲁棒性表现，识别出了最优的防御策略和架构组合。

Conclusion: KAN架构在经过适当防御（如随机平滑、对抗训练等）后能获得较好的认证和经验鲁棒性，特定架构设计可显著提升对抗鲁棒性，为构建安全可靠的KAN模型提供了理论指导和实证依据。

Abstract: While machine learning models have demonstrated strong performance in many domains, these models have shown profound vulnerabilities when they are exposed to adversarial threats. While adversarial attacks fall into various categories, the most prominent category in research studies is evasion. In evasion attacks, the adversary generates perturbed versions of samples, which might not be observable by human eyes. These samples generally fool the machine learning models with high confidence. This phenomenon poses a significant security violation against machine learning models. In this paper, we investigate the certified and empirical robustness of various Kolmogorov-Arnold network architectures against strong evasion attacks. At first, we provide the mathematical foundations for randomized smoothing and interval bound propagation, and report the $\ell_2$-certified robustness of the models under randomized smoothing. After that, we systematically evaluate the robustness of various defended and undefended KAN models under FGSM, PGD, and C&W attacks in order to find out the optimal defense strategies and architectures.

</details>


### [110] [The geometry of AI validation: Exact certification limits for iid best-of-N search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21496)
*Ricardo Fitas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI系统中验证与部署的规则，将其表示为可靠性曲面上的核，通过几何跨度区分复制与新干预方向，针对iid best-of-N搜索给出精确的模糊度宽度公式，并提出两阶段审计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地生成备选方案、检查证据并部署选定输出，验证具有目标相对性：证据仅在产生它的干预所解决的方向上认证部署。需要形式化验证与部署规则，区分采样噪声降低与结构性盲区降低，并为最佳N搜索提供精确的可靠性审计框架。

Method: 将验证和部署规则表示为可靠性曲面上的核，利用跨度几何分离复制（减少采样噪声）和新干预方向（减少结构性盲区）。在标量排序、随机打破平局、最大选择、有界二元真值和稳定秩-真值关系等假设下，推导iid best-of-N搜索的精确模糊度宽度公式。分析治理尺度m^2/N，利用单调性给出精确均匀逼近前沿，利用Lipschitz界给出精确截尾对偶和阶锐L/m^2模糊度。基于此提出两阶段审计规则：先建立结构覆盖，再添加独立任务提高精度。通过数学推理和代码选择的回顾性研究进行实证说明。

Result: 得到精确模糊度宽度公式B_{m,N}=1+2∑_{r=1}^m (-1)^r cos^{2N}(rπ/[2(m+1)])。显式有界世界达到整个区间，完整前缀是在n≤m约束下的信息最大可靠性均值审计。治理尺度为m^2/N：当m∝√N时模糊度约0.83，宽度ε需要m为√(N log(1/ε))阶。单调性给出精确均匀逼近前沿，Lipschitz界给出精确截尾对偶和阶锐L/m^2模糊度。提出两阶段审计规则，回顾性研究显示冻结在82个发现任务上的分数尾部审计规则大幅降低保留集误差。

Conclusion: 本文为iid best-of-N搜索建立了精确的可靠性审计理论，揭示了结构覆盖与精度提升的分离原则，提出了实用的两阶段审计规则。实证分析仅为说明性而非前瞻性干预，超越iid搜索时几何仅适用于已知或独立估计的核。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly generate alternatives, inspect evidence, and deploy a selected output. Validation is therefore target-relative: evidence certifies deployment only in directions resolved by the interventions that produced it. We represent validation and deployment rules as kernels over a reliability surface. Their span geometry separates replication, which reduces sampling noise, from new intervention directions, which reduce structural blindness. We make this principle exact for iid best-of-$N$ search. Under scalar ranking, randomized ties, maximum selection, bounded binary truth, and a stable rank-truth relation, knowing best-of-$n$ reliability through $n=m$ leaves exact ambiguity width $B_{m,N}=1+2\sum_{r=1}^{m}(-1)^r\cos^{2N}{rπ/[2(m+1)]}$. Explicit bounded worlds attain the entire interval, and the complete prefix is information-maximal among reliability-mean audits confined to $n\le m$. The governing scale is $m^2/N$: when $m$ is proportional to $\sqrt{N}$, ambiguity remains about 0.83, while width $\varepsilon$ requires $m$ of order $\sqrt{N\log(1/\varepsilon)}$. Monotonicity gives an exact uniform-approximation frontier; a Lipschitz bound gives an exact capped-tail dual and order-sharp $L/m^2$ ambiguity. These results yield a two-gate audit rule: establish structural coverage, then add independent tasks for precision. Retrospective studies of mathematical reasoning and code selection construct compatible deployment values with wide separation and show that a score-tail audit rule frozen on 82 discovery tasks substantially reduces held-out error. Beyond iid search, the geometry applies only to known or independently estimated kernels; the empirical analyses are illustrative rather than prospective interventions.

</details>


### [111] [Selection of Heart Sound Segments for Synchronous Classification of Multi-channel Heart Sounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21499)
*Marcelo Nogueira,Jorge H. Oliveira,Carlos F. Ferreira,Miguel T. Coimbra,Alípio M. Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同步多通道心音分析方法，结合片段选择算法与多输入CNN，在CirCor DigiScope数据集上实现96.5%准确率，显著优于单通道和异步多通道方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动心音分析多采用单通道或异步多通道分析，未模拟医生同步听诊四个部位的临床流程，因此研究同步多通道分析是否能通过捕捉通道间干扰现象提升分类性能。

Method: 设计片段选择算法从四个听诊部位各选取最优心音片段，送入多输入CNN同步分析四通道信号，实现患者级分类。

Result: 同步多通道方法准确率达96.5%，比最佳单通道/异步多通道方法高9.1%；片段选择策略优于随机选择（p=0.003），在735例完整四通道数据上验证。

Conclusion: 同步多通道分析结合优化片段选择能显著提升心脏疾病筛查准确率，验证了模拟医生同步听诊流程的有效性，但需进一步探讨泛化能力。

Abstract: Cardiac auscultation remains the most cost-effective screening procedure for cardiovascular diseases, and requires listening at the four main auscultation spots. Despite this, automatic heart sound analysis algorithms mostly classify patients using a single heart sound (single-channel), or, when using more than one (multi-channel), analyze each channel individually. To our knowledge, no prior work classifies patients through the synchronous analysis of multi-channel heart sounds, following the procedure used by physicians. This motivates us to study whether synchronous multi-channel analysis outperforms single-channel approaches, and whether it holds an advantage over asynchronous multi-channel methods that analyze channels one by one, potentially by capturing inter-channel interference phenomena. To answer these questions, we introduce a selection algorithm that identifies optimal heart sound segments from each of the four auscultation spots, which are then fed into a multi-input CNN that classifies patients by analyzing the four selected sounds simultaneously. Our synchronous approach, combining the proposed selection algorithm with a multi-input CNN, achieves a superior overall accuracy of 96.5\%, a 9.1\% gain over the best-performing single-channel and asynchronous multi-channel methods. The benefit of the proposed segment selection strategy over random selection is confirmed by a paired statistical significance test ($p = 0.003$). These results were obtained on 735 patients from the CirCor DigiScope dataset with complete recordings from all four spots, and their scope and generalizability are discussed in light of this and other methodological considerations.

</details>


### [112] [ChemDIRT: A Diversified Instruction, Representation, and Task Benchmark for Robust Chemistry-LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21504)
*Eric Inae,Tim Gunn,Chris Bond,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChemDIRT是一个全面评估框架，用于测试大语言模型在化学推理任务中的鲁棒性，通过系统变化指令和分子表示，揭示模型对提示敏感、依赖表示且任务表现不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学基准任务有限，忽略问题表述和化学表示变化的鲁棒性，导致模型性能被高估，需要更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 构建ChemDIRT基准，包含八大类化学任务，系统变化指令和分子表示，在受控扰动下评估准确性和一致性。

Result: 在多样开闭源LLMs上测试，发现显著的提示敏感性、表示依赖性和任务家族间表现不均。

Conclusion: ChemDIRT提供比传统单一格式基准更可靠的化学推理能力评估，揭示当前LLMs在化学推理中的鲁棒性不足。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has led to increasing interest in their application to scientific domains such as chemistry. However, existing chemistry benchmarks often provide only a narrow view of model capability, focusing on limited task sets while overlooking robustness to variations in problem formulation and chemical representation. As a result, reported performance may overestimate a model's true ability to reason consistently across realistic settings. To address this challenge, we introduce ChemDIRT (Diversified Instruction, Representation, and Task Benchmark), a comprehensive evaluation framework designed to assess the robustness of chemical reasoning in LLMs. ChemDIRT systematically measures model performance across variations in instructions and molecular representations while spanning eight categories of chemistry tasks. By evaluating both accuracy and consistency under these controlled perturbations, ChemDIRT provides a more reliable assessment of model reasoning capabilities than conventional single-format benchmarks. We benchmark a diverse set of open- and closed-source LLMs, revealing substantial prompt sensitivity, representation dependence, and uneven performance across task families.

</details>


### [113] [Multimodal Injury Risk and Performance Prediction in Tennis Using Weighted Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21530)
*Weihao Qu,Dongyang Wang,Ling Zheng,Francisco E. Alvarez,Shobharani Polasa,Jiacun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态加权集成学习框架 PART，融合可穿戴设备、问卷、视频等多源数据，通过自适应加权集成监测网球运动员健康状态并预测近期受伤风险，在大学生运动员数据上表现优异，且对业余选手亦有应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动表现与受伤预测研究受限于数据获取难、依赖主观评估，且多模态融合方法在网球领域尚未充分探索，亟需一种能整合异构数据并自适应加权的框架来提升预测准确性与实用性。

Method: 构建 Predictive Athlete Readiness for Tennis (PART) 框架：接收生理指标、训练/比赛数据、睡眠记录、自报问卷、垂直跳测试、比赛视频动作分析六大模态；分别训练专用机器学习/深度学习模型提取整体健康、受伤风险、身体机能、比赛风格四大运动员特征；采用监督加权集成策略，根据各模型可靠性分配自适应权重进行融合决策。

Result: 在 9 名大学生网球运动员的多模态数据上评估，PART 在健康监测与近期受伤易感性估计上均取得强性能；实验表明该框架亦适用于业余网球选手，可提供个性化见解以降低受伤风险并优化表现。

Conclusion: PART 通过多模态异构数据融合与自适应加权集成，有效解决了网球领域数据稀缺与主观评估局限，实现了对运动员健康状态的精准监测与受伤风险的早期预警，具有向业余运动人群推广的潜力。

Abstract: Machine learning has had a positive impact on the sports industry, with one of its most promising applications being the prediction of athlete performance and injury risk. Recent advances have employed state-of-the-art models to improve prediction accuracy, yet progress remains limited by data availability and the reliance on subjective observations or expert assessments. To address these limitations, researchers in sports such as soccer, basketball, and wrestling have begun integrating heterogeneous data sources, such as wearable device readings, with traditional subjective assessments. However, similar multimodal approaches remain underexplored in tennis. In this work, we propose a multimodal weighted ensemble learning framework, Predictive Athlete Readiness for Tennis (PART), to monitor athlete wellness and estimate near-term injury risk in tennis players. PART processes a wide range of inputs, including physiological metrics, training and match data, sleep information from wearable devices, self-reported questionnaires, vertical jump assessments, and motion analysis from match-play videos. From these modalities, specialized machine learning and deep learning models independently extract four athlete-specific characteristics: overall wellness, injury risk, physical capability, and playing style. To overcome the complexity of combining these diverse modalities, PART employs a supervised weighted ensemble integration strategy, assigning adaptive weights to each predictive model based on its reliability. Evaluation of multimodal data collected from nine collegiate tennis players demonstrates that PART achieves strong performance in monitoring athlete wellness and estimating near-term injury susceptibility. Beyond collegiate athletes, the framework also shows promise for recreational tennis players, offering personalized insights to mitigate injury risk and optimize performance.

</details>


### [114] [Federated Continual Learning as a Distributed Drift-Plus-Penalty Control Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21539)
*Nazreen Shah,Naveen Kumar Reddy Somireddy,Zubair Shaban,Ranjitha Prasad,B. N. Bharath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Lyapunov漂移加惩罚优化的联邦持续学习框架FedQCL，通过虚拟队列跟踪遗忘累积，实现稳定性-可塑性权衡的显式控制，在标准基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习方法将持续学习建模为逐任务优化问题，依赖启发式机制（如重放、正则化、投影约束），但遗忘本质上是长期分布式现象，源于任务时间演化与跨客户端异构性的交互，缺乏显式调控。

Method: 将FCL建模为随机控制问题，提出FedQCL框架，基于Lyapunov漂移加惩罚（DPP）优化。引入虚拟队列跟踪跨任务和客户端的遗忘累积，通过优化DPP目标联合提升当前任务性能，队列机制提供可解释且可调的单参数平衡适应与保持，无需梯度投影或额外通信开销。

Result: 在Split-CIFAR-10、Split-CIFAR-100、Split-TinyImageNet基准上，FedQCL在准确率上超越SOTA基线，并在异构数据分布下显著降低遗忘。

Conclusion: FedQCL通过随机控制视角和队列调节机制，有效解决了联邦持续学习中的灾难性遗忘和客户端漂移问题，提供了理论保障和实用的单参数调控，为分布式持续学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated Continual Learning (FCL) is fundamental to real-world distributed learning systems, requiring models to adapt to sequential, non-IID data across clients while mitigating catastrophic forgetting and client drift. Existing approaches formulate continual learning (CL) as a sequence of per-task optimization problems, applied locally at each client and coupled through aggregation, using heuristic mechanisms such as replay, regularization, or projection-based constraints. However, forgetting in FCL is inherently a long-term, distributed phenomenon, arising from the interaction of temporal task evolution and cross-client heterogeneity, which is not explicitly regulated. In this work, we cast FCL as a stochastic control problem and propose Federated Queue-regulated Continual Learning (FedQCL), a framework based on Lyapunov drift-plus-penalty (DPP) optimization. FedQCL introduces virtual queues to track the accumulation of forgetting across tasks and clients, enabling explicit control of the stability-plasticity trade-off. By optimizing a DPP objective, the method jointly improves current-task performance while the queue-based formulation provides an interpretable and tunable mechanism to balance adaptation and retention through a single parameter, without requiring gradient projection or additional communication overhead. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks, including Split-CIFAR-10, Split-CIFAR-100, and Split-TinyImageNet, demonstrate that FedQCL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines with respect to accuracy while significantly reducing forgetting under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Sparse Weights: When Is Attention Compressible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21541)
*Chiwun Yang,Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有KV-cache压缩方法仅关注注意力图的稀疏性是不充分的，提出CertKV方法：为每个头保留一个尾部摘要槽，其余按值分散度分配，无需训练即可在长上下文基准上达到SOTA压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV-cache压缩常以注意力图中少数大权重为由，但忽略了：大权重不一定包含大部分质量、被省略值可能相互抵消、保留注意力输出不等于保留任务性能。需要将这些问题分离，从质量保留、值抵消、未来查询和任务目标四个维度重新审视压缩性。

Method: 1. 理论分析：全局分数间隙决定保留目标质量所需token数；实现行的加权省略值求和是精确缺失统计量；构建受控检索-聚合模型解释截断何时有益/有害。2. CertKV算法：每头预留一个尾部摘要槽（聚合被截断token的值），剩余预算按值分散度（如方差或范数）分配，无需训练。

Result: 在LongBench-v2九项设置中七项排名前二；在128K RULER基准上保持领先压缩梯队；在打包的Llama原型上实现十倍缓存预算。压缩性取决于质量、值、未来查询和任务，而非仅稀疏注意力图。

Conclusion: KV-cache压缩不能只看注意力稀疏性，必须考虑质量分布、值抵消、查询分布和任务目标。CertKV通过尾部摘要槽和值分散度分配，在无训练情况下实现了最优压缩权衡，为长上下文推理提供了实用且理论 grounded 的解决方案。

Abstract: KV-cache compression is often justified by attention maps with a few large weights. This is incomplete: large weights may not contain most of the mass, omitted values can cancel, and preserving the attention output may not preserve the task. We separate these questions. Global score gaps -- not threshold counts -- determine how many tokens are needed to retain a target mass. For a realized row, the weighted sum of omitted values is the exact missing statistic. A controlled retrieval--aggregation model explains when truncation helps and when it hurts. These results motivate CertKV, a training-free compressor that reserves one tail-summary slot per head and allocates the rest by value dispersion. Under matched budgets, CertKV is top-two in seven of nine LongBench-v2 settings, remains in the leading compressed tier on 128K RULER, and realizes a ten-fold cache budget in a packed Llama prototype. Compressibility depends on the mass, values, future queries, and task -- not on a sparse-looking map alone.

</details>


### [116] [Loss-Parameterized Fisher Width Along Learning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21561)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 Fisher 宽度在学习轨迹上的演化，以及训练损失何时可作为其有效坐标。在高斯教师-逻辑斯蒂模型中，教师对齐状态在损失低于 log 2 时极值化 Fisher 宽度和参数范数；梯度流渐近选择该分支，导出 Fisher 宽度与损失平方根的比值极限。实验显示 GD/SGD 在匹配损失下轨迹接近，Adam 则偏离，支持分支而非通用的损失参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解 Fisher 宽度（探针集在局部 Fisher 几何下的高斯宽度）如何随训练演化，以及训练损失能否作为刻画其演化的有效单一坐标，从而揭示优化几何与泛化的联系。

Method: 1. 推导固定紧致探针的精确迹-形状分解与确定性稳定性界。2. 在群体高斯教师-逻辑斯蒂模型中分析教师对齐状态的极值性质。3. 研究群体梯度流的渐近动力学，给出对齐与正交坐标的显式收敛速率。4. 通过受控的全 Fisher 实验验证理论预测。5. 在非线性 MLP 中采用对角模型-Fisher 近似，对比 GD、SGD、Adam 的轨迹。

Result: 1. 教师对齐状态在损失 < log 2 时最小化参数范数、最大化 Fisher 迹与欧氏球 Fisher 宽度。2. 群体梯度流渐近选择该分支，且对于 d≥2，有 w_F(B_2^d;θ(t))/√L(θ(t)) → (√6/π) E[χ_{d-1}]。3. 全 Fisher 实验支持匹配损失分支与群体预测。4. 非线性 MLP 中，GD 和 SGD 在匹配损失下轨迹接近，Adam 走向显著不同的分支；固定探针的时间形状高度相似。

Conclusion: Fisher 宽度的演化不能用单一通用的损失函数参数化，而应采用分支参数化：不同优化器（或初始化）选择不同的损失分支，导致 Fisher 宽度随损失变化的关系不同。

Abstract: Fisher width measures the Gaussian width of a probe set after deformation by the local Fisher geometry. We study its evolution along learning trajectories and ask when training loss can serve as an effective coordinate for this quantity.
  We first derive an exact trace--shape factorization and a deterministic stability bound for fixed compact probes. In a population Gaussian-teacher logistic model, the teacher-aligned state is extremal on every loss level below $\log 2$: it has minimal parameter norm and maximizes both Fisher trace and Euclidean-ball Fisher width. We then show that population gradient flow asymptotically selects this branch, with explicit rates for the aligned and orthogonal coordinates. This yields, for $d\geq2$, \[
  \frac{w_F(B_2^d;θ(t))}
  {\sqrt{L(θ(t))}}
  \longrightarrow
  \frac{\sqrt6}π\mathbb E[χ_{d-1}]. \] Controlled full-Fisher experiments support the matched-loss branch and the population predictions. In a nonlinear MLP with a diagonal model-Fisher approximation, GD and SGD remain close at matched loss, whereas Adam follows a substantially displaced branch; the fixed probes tested retain highly similar temporal shapes. These results support a branchwise, rather than universal, loss parametrization of Fisher width.

</details>


### [117] [Anchoring Bias: A Persistent Fairness Backdoor Attack against MLLMs under Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21577)
*Yuyang Luo,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.LG
