TL;DR: 本文提出持久公平后门攻击（PFBA），向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）注入能在持续学习（CL）过程中存活的群体特定歧视性后门，揭示了MLLM持续学习流程中存在的公平性安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在高风险领域部署时公平性是关键安全需求，实际部署中模型需通过持续学习适应不断变化的任务和数据分布。现有研究表明后门攻击可操控MLLM响应，但朴素植入的后门会随CL更新而退化。尽管公平性已成为MLLM部署的核心关注点，但后门诱导的公平性违规能否在CL中存活仍未被探索，留下两个关键问题：(1)后门能否可靠诱导MLLM的公平性违规；(2)此类针对公平性的后门能否在持续学习中持久存在。

Method: PFBA通过两大创新机制实现持久公平性后门：(1)潜在空间公平性强化：重塑模型深层特征几何，锚定特权群体表示以保持效用，同时排斥并聚类目标群体表示以维持歧视；(2)持续学习模拟：针对模拟的参数漂移迭代优化触发器，确保后门在未来更新中持久有效。

Result: 大量实验表明，PFBA能诱导严重的公平性差异，且该差异能跨越多轮持续学习持续存在，同时能规避标准的后门防御机制。相关数据和代码已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: PFBA成功填补了研究空白，证明了针对公平性的后门攻击可以在MLLM的持续学习过程中持久存活，这暴露了MLLM持续学习管道中严重的公平性安全隐患，提示未来需开发针对持久公平性后门的专门防御策略。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes domains where fairness is a critical safety requirement. In practice, these models are continually updated through continual learning (CL) to adapt to evolving tasks and data distributions. Prior work has shown that backdoor attacks can manipulate MLLM responses through hidden triggers, but naively implanted backdoors degrade as models undergo subsequent updates of CL. Although fairness has emerged as a central concern for MLLM deployment, whether backdoor-induced fairness violations can survive CL remains unexplored, leaving two critical questions unanswered: (1) whether a backdoor can reliably induce fairness violations in MLLMs, and (2) whether such fairness-targeted backdoors can persist through continual learning. We bridge this gap by proposing Persistent Fairness Backdoor Attack (PFBA) to inject persistent and group-specific discrimination into MLLMs. Specifically, PFBA achieves this through two novel mechanisms. The Latent Space Fairness Reinforcement reshapes the model's deep feature geometry by anchoring privileged-group representations to preserve utility while repelling and clustering targeted-group representations to sustain discrimination, and the Continual Learning Simulation iteratively optimizes the trigger against simulated parameter drift to ensure backdoor persistence across future updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PFBA induces severe fairness disparities that persist across continual learning rounds, evading standard backdoor defenses. The data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/lyygua/PFBA.

</details>


### [118] [Sorting from Counterexamples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21579)
*Noga Alon,Shay Moran,Shlomo Moran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在存在至多 k 个不可靠逆序对反馈的情况下，通过主动查询完整排序来精确识别未知全序的问题。确定了最优查询复杂度为 Θ(n log n + nk)，并将模型推广到低维几何表示的排序，给出了 O(d² log n + dk) 的上界和 Ω(d log n + dk) 的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典排序问题假设比较结果完全可靠，但在实际交互式学习或人工标注场景中，反馈可能包含错误。本文旨在量化在最多 k 个逆序对反馈可能不真实（k 未知）的情况下，精确恢复未知全序所需的查询轮数，并探索目标排序具有低维几何结构时的复杂度。

Method: 上界：利用排列的几何表示（排列多面体）和 Grünbaum 定理（关于通过超平面分割凸体体积的定理），设计主动查询策略，每次查询近似减半一致假设空间的体积；结合对不可靠反馈的鲁棒聚合机制。下界：结合经典排序下界 Ω(n log n) 与 Condorcet 型构造，通过构造难以区分的假设集合，证明每个不可靠反馈需额外付出 Ω(n) 的查询代价。低维情形下，利用投影排序的几何性质，将问题归约为在 d 维空间中学习未知方向。

Result: 一般全序：最优查询复杂度为 Θ(n log n + nk)。无噪声时匹配经典排序下界；每个不可靠反馈增加 Θ(n) 代价。低维几何排序（d 维投影）：上界 O(d² log n + dk)，下界 Ω(d log n + dk)，无噪声项存在因子 d 的差距。

Conclusion: 在逆序对反馈可能包含最多 k 个错误的对抗模型下，精确学习未知全序的查询复杂度为 Θ(n log n + nk)，揭示了噪声容忍度的线性代价。对于具有低维几何结构的排序类，复杂度显著降低至与 d 相关，但无噪声项的最优依赖关系（d 还是 d²）尚待解决。

Abstract: Consider the following problem of learning an unknown linear order on $n$ items. In each round, the learner guesses a complete ordering of the items and receives either confirmation that the guess is correct or a counterexample: a pair of items in the wrong order. The goal is to identify the unknown order using as few queries as possible. We study this problem when up to $k$ of the returned counterexamples may be untruthful, where $k$ is not known in advance. We determine the optimal query complexity up to constant factors: \[
  Θ(n\log n + nk). \] Thus, while the noiseless complexity matches the classical complexity of sorting, each untruthful counterexample incurs an additional cost of order $n$. The upper bound is based on a geometric representation of permutations and Grünbaum's theorem, while the lower bound combines sorting arguments with a Condorcet-type construction. We also study the case where the target ranking has a low-dimensional geometric representation: each item is represented by a point in $\mathbb{R}^d$, and the ranking is obtained by projecting the points onto an unknown direction. For these classes we give an upper bound of $O(d^2\log n+dk)$ and a lower bound of $Ω(d\log n+dk)$, leaving a factor of $d$ gap in the noiseless term.

</details>


### [119] [Reading the Room: Implicit Confusion Encoding in Recurrent World Model States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21582)
*Donald Aadithiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于RSSM架构的世界模型（如DreamerV3）中的循环隐藏状态$h_t$，发现其不仅用于减少预测误差，还隐含地追踪自身的“困惑度”。该困惑信号与$h_t$的主导方差方向近乎正交，无法被基于方差的方法检测到，且与集成分歧和重构误差功能上不同。通过线性探针、因果编辑实验等验证了该信号的存在性、因果作用及泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型中的隐藏状态仅针对预测误差优化，但作者假设其可能隐含地编码了模型对自身预测可靠性的评估（即“困惑”），这对于决定何时信任想象规划、何时查询真实环境至关重要。现有方法（如集成分歧、重构误差）无法捕捉这种内在困惑信号。

Method: 1. 在控制预测误差固定而困惑度变化的解离测试中，使用线性探针检测$h_t$中的困惑信号；2. 分析近期高误差步数的折扣计数与探针输出的关系；3. 直接编辑$h_t$（包括使用其他轨迹的真实值）观察行为变化，验证因果作用；4. 在三个控制任务上测试几何特性、闭式形式及实际应用（决定何时查询现实）的泛化性。

Result: 线性探针在解离测试中达到AUROC 0.72（5次运行），集成基线低于随机水平；近期高误差步数的折扣计数解释了探针输出80%的方差（$R^2=0.80$）；直接编辑$h_t$导致行为改变，确认因果作用；几何和闭式形式在三个任务中泛化，但决定性解离测试仅在一个任务中成立，实际应用（决定何时查询现实）仅在两个任务中泛化。

Conclusion: RSSM隐藏状态$h_t$隐含地编码了模型的内在困惑信号，该信号与主导方差正交，区别于现有不确定性度量，且被因果用于决策。然而，其作为可靠困惑指标的泛化性有限：核心解离测试仅在一个任务通过，实际应用仅在两个任务有效，提示该机制可能依赖特定任务结构，需进一步研究提高鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models built on the RSSM architecture, such as DreamerV3, keep a recurrent hidden state $h_t$ trained only to reduce prediction error. We show this state also tracks its own confusion, hiding in plain sight: nearly orthogonal to $h_t$'s directions of greatest variance, invisible to any variance-based method. It is functionally distinct from ensemble disagreement, which flags new inputs, and reconstruction error, which flags bad predictions right now. On a test holding prediction error fixed while confusion varies, a linear probe on $h_t$ finds the signal (AUROC 0.72, 5 runs), while an ensemble baseline scores below chance. A discounted count of recent high-error steps explains 80% of the probe's output ($R^2=0.80$). We confirm the signal is causally used, not merely present, by editing $h_t$ directly and watching behaviour change, including a check using real values from other trajectories instead of synthetic edits. Its geometry and closed form generalize across three control tasks; the decisive dissociation test itself holds cleanly on only one, and its practical use, deciding when to check reality instead of trusting imagination, generalizes to only two of the three tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Predicting Early Functional Decline from Longitudinal Laboratory and Vital Sign Trajectories: A Large-Scale Study Using the All of Us Research Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21589)
*Rashmita Kudamala,Aravind V. Kuruvikkattil,Lalitha Pranathi Pulavarthy,Saptarshi Purkayastha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用常规生物标志物的时间轨迹预测老年人运动功能下降的临床前阶段，LightGBM模型显著优于静态指标，可实现零负担的EHR集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人功能下降通常在跌倒或步态受损后才被识别，预防窗口已关闭。现有常规生物标志物的纵向数据未被充分利用，研究旨在探索其时间轨迹是否能识别临床前运动功能下降患者。

Method: 使用All of Us研究项目数据（N=297,861，11.1%病例），提取12种生物标志物在索引前三年窗口的轨迹特征（斜率、变异性、变化量、均值），构建LightGBM模型，与静态实验室汇总对比；进行1:1年龄性别匹配分析及预测时间范围分析（3-12个月前）。

Result: 轨迹模型AUROC 0.797 vs 静态0.755（p<0.001），AUPRC 0.380 vs 0.304；匹配分析AUROC 0.727 vs 人口学0.680；预测时间范围3-12个月前AUROC 0.768-0.740。

Conclusion: 仅使用常规医疗已订购的测量值，模型支持被动、零负担的EHR集成，可实现临床前功能下降的早期检测。

Abstract: Functional decline in older adults is typically recognized only after falls or observable gait impairment, closing the window for prevention. We investigated whether temporal trajectories of routine biomarkers, already recorded but rarely analyzed longitudinally, can identify patients in the pre-clinical phase of mobility decline. Using the All of Us Research Program (N = 297,861; 11.1% cases), we derived trajectory features (slope, variability, delta, mean) for twelve biomarkers over a three-year pre-index window. LightGBM models incorporating trajectories significantly outperformed static laboratory summaries (AUROC 0.797 vs. 0.755; DeLong p < 0.001; AUPRC 0.380 vs. 0.304). A 1:1 age- and sex-matched analysis confirmed an independent trajectory signal (AUROC 0.727 vs. demographics-only 0.680). A horizon analysis demonstrated sustained prediction 3-12 months before decline onset (AUROC 0.768-0.740). Because the model uses only measurements already ordered in routine care, it supports passive, zero-burden EHR integration for early detection of pre-clinical functional decline.

</details>


### [121] [Reaching the Tail: Calibration Diversity Drives Conformal Coverage under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21591)
*Donald Aadithiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多步预测稀有宏观经济事件（如衰退）的不确定性量化问题，揭示自适应一致性推断的覆盖率缺陷主要源于校准集大小而非稀有事件数量，提出基于非一致性得分分布支持宽度的多样性最大化选择策略，显著提升长期预测覆盖率（六个月从67.8%升至81.4%），并指出多样性是必要但非充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观经济长序列中稀有事件标签稀缺，且自相关破坏了标准不确定性量化所需的可交换性假设，导致多步预测的覆盖率不可靠，需要解决校准集构建对覆盖率的影响。

Method: 通过受控消融实验分析200个随机校准集，比较非一致性得分分布支持宽度与稀有事件计数对覆盖率方差的解释力；在合成数据和五国真实数据上验证；构建基于多样性最大化的校准集选择器，对比Mondrian、分布漂移鲁棒、极值理论等替代策略；在两阶段美国衰退预测框架（RegressorChain）上演示。

Result: 支持宽度解释高达85%的覆盖率方差，稀有事件计数仅2%；跨条件复现（五国Spearman ρ 0.45-0.66 vs 0.02-0.23）；多样性最大化选择器是唯一提升长期覆盖率的策略（六个月67.8%→81.4%）；Mondrian甚至在 oracle 标签下恶化覆盖率；提出覆盖率赤字取决于校准集上分位数逼近测试分布的程度。

Conclusion: 校准集大小和多样性是关键，稀有事件阈值假象被揭穿；多样性必要但不充分；六个月覆盖率能否达90%仍是开放问题，本文量化而非解决该问题。

Abstract: Multi-horizon rare-event forecasting is hard under long macroeconomic series' data constraints: labeled events are scarce, and standard uncertainty quantification assumes an exchangeability that autocorrelation violates. A controlled ablation shows an apparent rare-event threshold for Adaptive Conformal Inference instead reflects calibration-set size. Across 200 random calibration sets, support width of the nonconformity-score distribution explains up to 85% of coverage variance versus 2% for rare-event count; the same, not the same magnitude, replicates across synthetic conditions and five countries (five-country Spearman $ρ$ 0.45-0.66 vs. 0.02-0.23). A diversity-maximizing selector built on this is the only strategy tested that improves long-horizon coverage (67.8% to 81.4% at six months); Mondrian, shift-robust, and extreme-value alternatives fail to close it. Mondrian even worsens coverage under oracle labels. A compact proposition explains why: coverage deficit reflects how closely the calibration set's upper quantile reaches the test distribution's. Diversity is necessary, not sufficient. Demonstrated on a two-stage U.S. recession-forecasting framework with RegressorChain, whether six-month coverage reaches 90% under honest scoring remains open, a question this paper quantifies rather than resolves.

</details>


### [122] [Subzero matrix completion for sparse data analysis: large-scale learning of latent low-rank structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21607)
*Lawrence K. Saul,Ningyuan Huang,Dennis Bollweg,Jeff Soules,Diana C. Halikias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究稀疏非负矩阵如何从低秩实值矩阵通过去除负元素恢复，提出了一种随机交替最小二乘算法，可处理大规模问题，并应用于果蝇连接组学的突触权重矩阵分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稀疏性与低秩结构之间的数学联系，解决现有算法需要存储大型稠密矩阵的扩展性问题。

Method: 开发随机交替最小二乘算法，在稠密矩阵的较小块上操作，结合稀疏优化和定制CUDA内核加速。

Result: 算法成功应用于139,255×139,255的果蝇突触权重矩阵，揭示了尽管奇异值谱缓慢衰减，但存在潜在低秩结构，能预测多层级细胞类别。

Conclusion: 稀疏非负矩阵可通过低秩矩阵截断负值得到恢复，提出的算法实现了大规模问题的可扩展计算，并为神经科学连接组学分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We investigate when a sparse nonnegative matrix can be recovered from a real-valued matrix of much lower rank by zeroing out its negative elements. The potential for such decompositions suggests a mathematical connection between sparsity and rank; we analyze a number of sparse matrices with this latent low-rank structure and use them to illustrate the geometric origins of this connection. Previous algorithms have discovered these decompositions via an alternating minimization over the factors of a low-rank matrix, but to do so, they have also needed to compute and store another matrix, neither sparse nor low-rank, that is the size of their product. We develop a stochastic, alternating least-squares algorithm that operates on smaller blocks of this dense matrix and scales as a result to much larger problems. We also show how to further accelerate this algorithm with sparse optimizations and customized CUDA kernels. As one example, we use the algorithm to analyze the sparse matrix of synaptic weights for the recently published $\textit{Drosphilia}$ connectome. The nonzero elements of this matrix, with 139,255 rows and columns, record the number of synapses between cells in the nervous system of a female fruit fly. Despite a slowly decaying spectrum of singular values, this matrix exhibits a latent low-rank structure that is predictive of cell categories across multiple levels of specificity.

</details>


### [123] [FrugalSOT - Frugal Search Over the Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21621)
*Pradheep P,Yuvanesh S,Harish KB,Keerthan Saai Reddy S,Joshva Devadas T,Naveenkumar J,Hemalatha K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FrugalSOT 是一种面向边缘设备（如 Raspberry Pi 5）的资源感知模型选择架构，通过提取提示词长度、命名实体密度、句法复杂度等特征估算请求复杂度，动态路由至最小可行模型，并在输出未达相关性阈值时级联至更复杂模型；阈值采用低通滤波机制基于历史验证结果自适应更新。实验表明，该方法显著降低平均推理时间和计算资源消耗，同时保持与最复杂模型相当的输出相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入式硬件（如 Raspberry Pi 5）上部署 NLP 任务时，计算资源、内存和能耗受限，单一大模型推理开销过大，而固定的小模型又难以保证质量；需要一种自适应、资源感知的模型选择策略，在保证输出相关性的前提下最小化推理延迟和资源占用。

Method: 1. 特征提取：从输入提示词中计算长度、命名实体密度、句法复杂度等轻量级特征。2. 复杂度估计与初始路由：根据特征预测请求复杂度，优先发送给最可能通过相关性阈值的最小模型。3. 级联回退：若模型输出未达阈值，自动转发至下一级更复杂模型。4. 阈值自适应：采用低通滤波机制，利用历史验证结果持续更新相关性阈值，适应输入分布变化。

Result: 在 Raspberry Pi 5 上实验显示，FrugalSOT 相比单模型基线，平均推理时间和整体计算资源消耗大幅降低，输出相关性未显著下降，接近最复杂模型的表现。

Conclusion: 自适应模型选择机制能够在资源受限的边缘设备上实现高效、高质量的 NLP 推理，验证了动态复杂度感知路由与阈值自适应相结合的有效性。

Abstract: In on-device NLP tasks, limited resources of embedded hardware, such as the Raspberry Pi 5, require efficient inference strategies. This paper introduces FrugalSOT (Frugal Search Over The Models), a resource-aware model selection architecture for on-device NLP inference. FrugalSOT estimates each request's complexity by extracting features such as prompt length, named entity density, and syntactic complexity. The request is first made to the least complex model that is likely to pass a relevance threshold. If the output of that model falls short of the threshold, the request is made to a more complex model. It is important to note that the relevance threshold undergoes continuous updates in the background. using past validation outcomes in an adaptation process using a low-pass filtering mechanism, thus imparting adaptation to changing input patterns. Experimental results achieved on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that FrugalSOT reduces average inference time and overall computational resource use to a significant extent compared to a single-model baseline approach, without compromising output relevance to the same extent as the most sophisticated model. These results confirm that adaptive model selection can enable efficient, high-quality natural language processing inference on limited devices.

</details>


### [124] [Rethinking Communication Metrics: How Should We Measure Meaning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21626)
*Niloofar Tavakolian,Hakimeh Purmehdi,Jungyeon Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向文本和图像的语义通信系统的关键性能指标(KPI)，从统一的评价视角对现有KPI进行分类、比较，分析了当前语义评价面临的核心挑战，并指出了未来标准化、可解释、自适应、任务感知和通信感知评价框架的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信将通信目标从符号准确重构转向语义保真、任务完成和高效信息交换，但现有评价指标在电信、NLP、计算机视觉、机器学习等领域碎片化，缺乏能跨模态、跨任务、跨信道条件统一表征语义质量的单一指标，亟需系统梳理和统一评价框架。

Method: 采用以评价为中心的分类法，按通信目标、源模态、接收端输出、参考可用性、评价层级、信道/资源约束对现有KPI分类；综述重构类、任务导向类、无参考类、表征层级、感知类、信道感知等指标，并进行跨模态对比，分析其角色、优势与局限。

Result: 梳理了六大类语义KPI及其跨模态适用性；揭示了缺乏通用语义成功准则与标准化语义真值、语义漂移、有限的无参考评价、机器学习指标与通信约束融合薄弱、缺乏关系级和多模态KPI等核心开放问题；阐述了这些问题对监控、质量保障、资源优化、故障诊断和标准化的影响。

Conclusion: 未来研究应致力于建立标准化、可解释、自适应、任务感知且通信感知的语义评价框架，推动跨领域统一语义KPI体系的形成，支撑语义通信系统的工程落地与标准化进程。

Abstract: Semantic communication shifts the objective of communication systems from accurate symbol reconstruction toward meaning preservation, task accomplishment, and efficient information exchange. However, its evaluation remains fragmented across telecommunications, natural language processing, computer vision, and machine learning, and no single metric can characterize semantic quality across modalities, tasks, and channel conditions. This article surveys key performance indicators (KPIs) for text- and image-based semantic communication systems from a unified, evaluation-centered perspective. Unlike prior surveys primarily organized around architectures, applications, or transmission strategies, this work focuses on how semantic success should be defined and measured. Existing KPIs are classified according to communication goal, source modality, receiver output, reference availability, evaluation level, and channel or resource constraints. The survey reviews reconstruction-based, task-oriented, reference-free, representation-level, perceptual, and channel-aware metrics, and presents a cross-modality comparison of their roles, strengths, and limitations. It further analyzes how unresolved semantic-KPI challenges affect monitoring, quality assurance, resource optimization, fault diagnosis, and standardization. Key open problems include the absence of universal semantic success criteria and standardized semantic ground truth, semantic drift, limited reference-free evaluation, weak integration of machine-learning metrics with communication constraints, and the lack of relation-level and multimodal KPIs. Finally, future research directions are outlined toward standardized, interpretable, adaptive, task-aware, and communication-aware evaluation frameworks.

</details>


### [125] [ChequeMark: An Ensemble Machine Learning Framework for After-Hours Business Deposit Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21629)
*Ann Youduo Xu,Emily Yu,Justin Leski,William Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对非营业时间商业存款操作中的支票欺诈风险，提出了一个多视角集成机器学习框架，结合XGBoost、Isolation Forest和GraphSAGE三种模型，生成客户级风险评分。在稳定条件下性能与XGBoost相当，但在分布偏移场景下表现更优（F1: 83.77%，FPR: 0.69%），同时保持了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支票欺诈是非营业时间商业存款操作中的重大风险，因为资金可能在一个工作日内释放，而支票清算需要数天，这种时间差创造了欺诈暴露窗口。现有缓解措施依赖静态的存款级检查，无法捕捉历史客户行为和不断演变的欺诈模式。

Method: 提出多视角集成ML框架，结合三个模型：1) XGBoost用于检测已知欺诈模式；2) Isolation Forest用于无标签异常检测；3) GraphSAGE用于捕捉交易活动相关的关系模式。将三个模型输出融合为单一客户级风险评分。

Result: 在稳定条件下，框架性能与XGBoost相当；在针对性分布偏移场景下，框架表现最优（F1: 83.77%，FPR: 0.69%），优于XGBoost（F1: 82.77%，FPR: 0.72%）。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持可解释性（通过基于行为、异常和关系证据的自然语言解释）的同时，显著提高了对分布偏移的鲁棒性，为金融机构提供了更有效的支票欺诈检测方案。

Abstract: Cheque fraud is a material risk in after-hours business deposit operations because funds may be released within one business day, while cheque clearing takes several days. This timing gap creates a fraud exposure window for financial institutions. Prior mitigation relies on static, deposit-level checks and therefore miss historical client behavior and evolving patterns. To address this gap, we propose a multi-view ensemble ML framework that combines: Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) for known fraud patterns, Isolation Forest for label-free anomaly detection, and Graph Sample and Aggregate (GraphSAGE) for relational patterns associated with transaction activities. We then combine the three outputs into a single client-level risk score. Under stable conditions, performance is comparable to XGBoost; under a targeted distribution shift, our framework performs best (F1: 83.77%, FPR: 0.69%) versus XGBoost (F1: 82.77%, FPR: 0.72%). These results indicate improved robustness to distribution shift while preserving interpretability through plain-language explanations grounded in behavioural, anomaly, and relational evidence.

</details>


### [126] [Large-Scale Evaluation of Advanced Imputation Methods for Missing Values in Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21638)
*Daniela Stojcheska,Marija Markovska,Dimitar Taskovski,Branislav Gerazov,Boris Nikolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在大规模真实电力消费数据集上实证评估了三种缺失值插补算法（OWA、SoftImpute、形状建模自编码器），发现OWA在各种缺口长度下整体重构误差最低且稳定性最强，自编码器方差较大，SoftImpute稳定但精度较低，建议根据负荷曲线特性选择插补方法，并展望混合算法架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网运行（特别是非技术损失检测）依赖AMI准确可靠的用电数据采集，但实际数据集常因通信故障出现缺失值，现有研究缺乏大规模数据集验证。

Method: 在北马其顿17428个商业智能电表两年的真实数据集上，模拟1-168小时连续缺失，实证对比OWA、SoftImpute、形状建模自编码器三种算法的鲁棒性。

Result: OWA在所有评估缺口长度下整体重构误差最低，且在长达一周的缺口中表现出强稳定性；自编码器方差较高；SoftImpute稳定但精度较差。

Conclusion: 插补方法应根据负荷曲线数据特性选择，未来电网管理系统可探索混合算法架构。

Abstract: Accurate and reliable collection of electricity consumption data through Advanced Metering Infrastructure (AMI) is of great importance for the operation of smart grids, especially for the detection of non-technical losses (NTL). However, real-world datasets frequently suffer from missing values due to communication failures. This paper presents an empirical evaluation of three advanced algorithms for large-scale data imputation: the Optimally Weighted Average (OWA) method, Low-Rank Matrix Completion via SoftImpute, and a Shape-Modeling Autoencoder. Existing studies on missing value imputation in electricity consumption data often lack validation on larger datasets. Therefore, the goal of this paper is to validate the selected algorithms on a large-scale real-world electricity consumption dataset from North Macedonia that includes 17,428 commercial smart meters over two years. The robustness of each algorithm is evaluated by simulating continuous gaps in the data ranging from 1 to 168 hours. The results indicate that OWA provides the lowest overall reconstruction error across the evaluated gap sizes and strong stability in worst-case scenarios for gaps of up to one week. In contrast, the autoencoder exhibits higher variance, while SoftImpute has stable but inferior accuracy. These findings suggest that imputation methods should be selected based on the characteristics of load curve data and highlight the potential for hybrid algorithmic architectures in future grid management systems.

</details>


### [127] [Power-Performance Characterization of TinyML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21646)
*Yujie Zhang,Dhananjaya Wijerathne,Zhaoying Li,Tulika Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地表征了微控制器上多样化 TinyML 应用的性能与功耗，分析了多层抽象带来的开销，提出了抽象层成本估算模型，并给出了降低成本的建议，为神经架构搜索和边缘端 CNN 推理优化提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TinyML 系统虽使机器学习推理下沉至边缘，但缺乏定量分析；需在微控制器上对神经网络模型、软件库、操作系统和硬件架构等多维度的 TinyML 应用进行系统的性能与功耗表征，重点量化多层抽象对性能和能效的影响。

Method: 在微控制器上对多样化 TinyML 应用开展系统的性能与功耗表征，覆盖神经网络模型、软件库、操作系统和硬件架构；聚焦多层抽象的影响，提出抽象层成本估算模型，并基于实测数据给出最小化成本的建议。

Result: 获得了不同抽象层的量化开销数据，建立了成本估算模型，提出了针对性的优化建议；研究成果可直接辅助设计者开展神经架构搜索和边缘设备上的 CNN 推理优化。

Conclusion: 本文填补了 TinyML 系统定量分析的空白，通过实测揭示了抽象层对性能与能效的具体影响，提供的成本模型和优化建议为资源受限微控制器上的 TinyML 部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: TinyML systems are enabling machine learning (ML) inference at the edge. However, there is little quantitative analysis of such systems. This paper presents a systematic performance and power characterization of diverse TinyML applications on microcontrollers (MCUs), spanning neural network models, software libraries, operating systems, and hardware architectures. We focus on the impact of the multiple layers of abstraction that provide higher programmability at the expense of performance and energy efficiency. We propose a model to estimate the costs of different abstraction layers and make recommendations for minimizing those costs. Our findings can help designers with Neural Architecture Search (NAS) and CNN inference optimization on edge devices.

</details>


### [128] [GeoQ: Geometry-Aware Conditional Quantile Error Estimation for Scientific Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21652)
*Khoa Nguyen,Daniel Serino,Aviral Prakash,Marc Klasky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GeoQ，一种基于几何感知条件分位数的非侵入式校准框架，用于在单个查询点估计神经网络代理模型的预测误差，通过锚点平均校准误差加上学习到的非负修正量，利用表示空间位移和局部支持密度等几何特征建模误差增量的上条件分位数，并采用交叉拟合生成近似样本外校准元组，通过 k-近邻支持分数识别误差模型有数据支持的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络代理模型广泛用于加速科学模拟，但在外推和自回归场景下部署时，需要依赖输入的预测误差估计，以确保模型的有效性和可靠性。

Method: GeoQ 将查询点的误差表示为锚点平均校准误差加上一个学习到的非负修正量。该修正量建模为锚点相对误差增量的上条件分位数，使用编码表示空间位移和局部支持密度的几何特征。采用交叉拟合过程生成近似样本外校准元组，并利用特征空间 k-近邻支持分数识别校准数据支持的区域。

Result: 在标量回归、混沌动力学、中程天气预报和 Richtmyer-Meshkov 不稳定性预测等任务上评估，GeoQ 证明了几何感知条件分位数建模为科学代理模型提供了一种实用且非侵入式的有效性感知误差估计方法。

Conclusion: GeoQ 通过几何感知的条件分位数误差建模，实现了对科学代理模型在单点查询处的误差进行非侵入式、输入相关的校准估计，增强了模型在外推和自回归部署中的可信度。

Abstract: Neural-network surrogate models are increasingly used to accelerate scientific simulations, but their deployment in extrapolative and autoregressive settings requires input-dependent estimates of prediction error. In this work, we introduce GeoQ (Geometry-Aware Conditional Quantile Error Estimation), a non-intrusive calibration framework for estimating surrogate error at individual query points. GeoQ represents the error at a query point as an anchor-averaged calibration error plus a learned nonnegative correction. This correction is modeled as an upper conditional quantile of the anchor-relative error increment, using geometry-based features that encode representation-space displacement and local support density. A cross-fitting procedure generates approximately out-of-sample calibration tuples, while a feature-space k-nearest-neighbor support score identifies regions \textcolor{black}{where the learned error model is supported by calibration data}. We evaluate GeoQ on scalar regression, chaotic dynamics, medium-range weather forecasting, and Richtmyer-Meshkov instability prediction. The results demonstrate that geometry-aware conditional quantile modeling provides a practical and non-intrusive approach for validity-aware error estimation in scientific surrogate models.

</details>


### [129] [Bounded Precision-Geometry Scaling for Robust Multi-Task Learning under Loss Scale Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21653)
*Krishna Subedi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界精度-几何缩放（BPGS）方法，解决多任务学习中损失尺度差异导致的同方差不确定性加权失效问题。BPGS 通过有界 sigmoid 参数化映射各任务的对数方差，并锚定在分离的批次损失统计量上，将网络优化与不确定性优化解耦。实验证明 BPGS 对均匀重缩放具有不变性，在合成压力测试和 NYUv2、Yeast、RF1 三个真实基准上均表现出优异的尺度鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习常需组合跨越多个数量级的损失函数，导致传统同方差不确定性加权（如 Kendall 加权）严重退化。现有方法在损失尺度差异巨大时无法保持稳定的任务权重，亟需一种对损失重缩放不变且计算开销低的鲁棒加权机制。

Method: 提出有界精度-几何缩放（BPGS）：将每个任务的对数方差通过有界 sigmoid 函数参数化，并锚定在分离的批次损失统计量上；将网络参数优化与不确定性参数优化解耦；理论证明其归一化任务权重在非退化损失尺度下对均匀重缩放不变。

Result: 合成压力测试中，损失从 ×1 重缩放到 ×1000 时，BPGS 宏观得分仅从 0.777 变为 0.778，而 Kendall 加权从 0.780 跌至 0.637，L1 归一化无法弥补差距。NYUv2 上 BPGS 取得最低深度绝对相对误差（0.223）、深度 RMSE（0.790）和总损失（1.891），优于 Nash-MTL 等方法。Yeast 多标签分类微观 F1 最高（0.616）。RF1 多目标回归上具有竞争力，但 PCGrad 在 RMSE 和 MAE 上领先。批大小与校准敏感性研究显示变化极小，运行时开销低于 1%。

Conclusion: BPGS 作为一种尺度鲁棒的同方差不确定性加权替代方案，在损失尺度差异主导多任务优化时表现尤为有效，具有理论保证的缩放不变性、极低的计算开销和跨基准的稳健性能。

Abstract: Multi-task learning often combines losses that span several orders of magnitude, causing homoscedastic uncertainty weighting to degrade severely. We propose Bounded Precision-Geometry Scaling (BPGS), a method that maps each task's log-variance through a bounded sigmoid parameterisation anchored to detached batch loss statistics, and decouples network optimisation from uncertainty optimisation. Its normalised task weights are provably invariant to uniform rescaling under non-degenerate loss scales. We evaluate BPGS on synthetic stress tests and three real-world benchmarks: NYUv2 dense prediction, Yeast multi-label classification, and RF1 multi-target regression. Under pure loss rescaling from $\times 1$ to $\times 1000$, its macro score changes from 0.777 to 0.778, whereas Kendall weighting drops from 0.780 to 0.637; $\ell_1$-normalising Kendall's weights does not close the gap. On NYUv2, BPGS records the lowest depth absolute relative error (0.223), depth RMSE (0.790), and total loss (1.891) among all compared methods, including Nash-MTL. Sensitivity studies on batch size and calibration show small variation across the tested ranges, and runtime overhead relative to Kendall is under 1%. BPGS posts the highest Yeast micro-F1 (0.616) and is competitive on RF1, though PCGrad leads RMSE and MAE there. These findings establish BPGS as a scale-robust alternative to homoscedastic uncertainty weighting, notably effective when loss-scale disparities dominate multi-task optimisation.

</details>


### [130] [Variational Structure at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21660)
*Eric Regis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将 '边缘耦合' 扩展到重球法和 Nesterov 动量，证明其临界点刻画不动点与两点轨道，Hessian 刻画稳定性，并揭示边缘耦合等价于对称 Verlet 作用量，从而形式化离散优化器在稳定性边缘与离散力学（辛积分子）的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散时间优化器在稳定性边缘呈现近两周期振荡，类似守恒系统（如辛积分子），但二者的精确数学联系尚未充分探索。Litman 提出的边缘耦合仅针对梯度下降，需推广到动量方法并建立与离散力学的严格对应。

Method: 将边缘耦合泛化到重球法和 Nesterov 动量，分析其临界点与 Hessian；证明边缘耦合函数可识别为对称 Verlet 离散作用量，建立优化动力学与离散力学变分原理的等价性。

Result: 扩展后的边缘耦合临界点精确对应重球法与 Nesterov 动量的不动点和两点轨道，其 Hessian 判定这些轨道的稳定性；边缘耦合等价于对称 Verlet 作用量，确立了稳定性边缘行为与辛积分子的严格数学等价。

Conclusion: 论文为离散优化器在稳定性边缘的振荡动力学提供了统一的几何力学解释，揭示了动量加速方法与辛数值积分的深层联系，为步长选择、稳定性分析及新型优化算法设计奠定理论基础。

Abstract: When discrete-time optimizers operate at the edge of stability, they exhibit near-two-periodic behavior. These oscillatory dynamics are reminiscent of conservative systems, such as the dynamics generated by symplectic integrators. However, a precise formulation of the connection between discrete-time optimizers at the edge of stability and discrete mechanics remains underexplored. Recently, Litman introduced the "edge coupling": a functional on consecutive gradient descent iterates whose critical points encode the fixed points and two-point orbits of the gradient descent dynamics. Here we extend the edge coupling to heavy-ball and Nesterov momentum. We show that its critical points characterize the fixed points and two-point orbits, with its Hessian characterizing their stability. We also show that the edge coupling can be identified with the symmetric Verlet action, formalizing the connection between the edge of stability and discrete mechanics.

</details>


### [131] [SynEHR: Joint Modeling Inter-visit Temporal Evolution and Intra-visit Clinical Structure for Longitudinal EHR Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21673)
*Ximiao Li,Lin Jiang,Rongchao Xu,Dahai Yu,Zhe He,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SynEHR，一种基于轻量级自适应大语言模型的纵向电子健康记录（EHR）合成框架，通过时间状态条件模块和时序关系自适应模块显式建模访问间不规则时间演化和访问内临床事件结构，利用LoRA适配的语言模型生成下一访问序列，在多个真实EHR数据集上在保真度、隐私和下游效用评估中优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实纵向EHR难以获取且包含大量患者敏感信息，合成EHR可保留统计模式和临床结构。现有生成模型虽能生成未来访问序列，但缺乏对访问间不规则时间演化和访问内临床事件结构的显式整合，导致生成序列临床不一致且时间不真实。

Method: SynEHR包含两个新设计：(1) 时间状态条件模块捕捉跨访问的不规则时间状态；(2) 时序关系自适应模块将这些状态与患者历史结合，动态构建患者特定的关系表示。在此基础上，采用参数高效的LoRA适配语言模型生成器具备下一访问生成能力，联合训练两模块实现时间和临床信息感知的生成。

Result: 在多个真实EHR数据集上进行保真度、隐私和下游效用的广泛实验，SynEHR在生成临床连贯且时间忠实的纵向EHR数据方面优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: SynEHR通过显式建模不规则时间演化和临床事件结构，结合轻量级LLM适配，有效解决了纵向EHR合成中的临床一致性和时间真实性问题，为受限真实记录场景下的建模和分析提供了高质量合成数据。

Abstract: Longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) document patients' sequences of clinical visits over time, preserving the temporal evolution of disease progression and care delivery. However, real longitudinal EHRs are difficult to access because they contain large amounts of fine-grained, patient-specific information. Synthetic EHR generation therefore provides a valuable approach for preserving the statistical patterns and clinical structure of patient visit trajectories, enabling broader modeling and analysis when real records are limited. Although recent generative models have made progress in producing future visit sequences, they remain limited in explicitly integrating inter-visit irregular temporal evolution and intra-visit clinical event structures in EHRs, leading to clinically inconsistent and temporally unrealistic visit sequences. In this work, we propose SynEHR, a lightweight adaptive LLM-based framework for longitudinal EHR synthesis. There are two novel designs in SynEHR, i.e., a Temporal State Conditioning Module captures irregular temporal states across visits and a Temporal-Relational Adaptation Module combines these states with patient history to dynamically construct patient-specific relational representations. SynEHR then builds on a parameter-efficient LoRA-adapted language-model generator with next-visit generation capability to train the two modules for temporally and clinically informed generation. Extensive experiments on real-world EHR datasets across fidelity, privacy, and downstream utility evaluations demonstrate that SynEHR outperforms state-of-the-art models by generating more clinically coherent and temporally faithful longitudinal EHR data.

</details>


### [132] [Read, Write, Relax: Why Neural PDE Surrogates Need Both Global and Local Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21677)
*Anuj Kumar,Heiko Zimmermann,Josiah Bjorgaard,Jacan Chaplais,Nikolaos Bouklas,Matteo Salvador,Alexander Lavin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Read-Write-Relax (RWR) 方法，通过交织潜在注意力与消息传递松弛，解决了全局模型（低通滤波）和局部模型（缺乏全局传播）在工业级网格模拟中的互补缺陷，实现了全频谱误差降低，在多个基准上达到最高精度且具备数据高效性和大规模扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的网格模拟代理模型分为全局潜在 token 类和局部消息传递类，二者均无法胜任高维、大规模工业问题：全局注意力相当于空间低通滤波器，只能修正低频误差；局部消息传递缺乏长程传播能力，只能修正高频误差。两者在误差频谱上互为互补，单独使用均不足。

Method: 提出 Read-Write-Relax (RWR) 统一框架：将潜在注意力（Read/Write 阶段）与图消息传递松弛（Relax 阶段）交织迭代。注意力负责全局低频信息聚合与分发，消息传递负责局部高频误差平滑，二者交替执行形成类多重网格循环，覆盖全频谱误差修正。

Result: 在工业与公开基准上，RWR 在几乎所有对比中精度最高；在稀缺数据条件下表现出显著的数据效率；能准确预测工程关注量（如升力、阻力）；并成功将全场预测扩展至大规模、高复杂度网格问题。

Conclusion: RWR 通过统一全局注意力与局部松弛，弥补了单一模型在频谱覆盖上的短板，为工业级大规模网格模拟提供了一个精度高、数据效率好、可扩展的神经代理模型范式。

Abstract: Recent mesh-based simulation advances have, in no small part, relied on neural surrogates of two distinct families: global models that route information through a small set of latent tokens, and local models that perform message passing across mesh edges. Consistent with both classes is the inability to perform beyond low-dimensional problems and small-scale or oversimplified meshes, the simulation regimes where industrial problems reside. Our work shows this explicitly and presents a unified formulation. In global approaches, latent-token attention acts as a spatial low-pass filter, while local message passing lacks the global reach necessary to propagate information across large mesh spaces. Viewed through the error, the two operators are the halves of a multigrid cycle: one corrects errors at the lower end of the spectrum, the other at the higher end, and neither can do the other's job. We introduce Read-Write-Relax (RWR), which interleaves latent attention with message-passing relaxation under a unified formulation. The interleaved processor lowers error across the entire spectrum, making RWR the most accurate model in nearly every comparison across our industrial and public benchmarks. It is also markedly data-efficient in the scarce-data regimes, accurate on the engineering quantities of interest, and scales full-field predictions to challenging, large-scale problems.

</details>


### [133] [Benchmarking Composable Compression Techniques in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21693)
*Afsara Benazir,Chen Chen,Rongxiao Qu,Jiabo Huang,Jingtao Li,Lingjuan Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoEXBench 是首个系统性基准，用于评估 MoE 模型在真实部署流水线中多种压缩技术（专家剪枝、权重量化、KV-cache 压缩）的组合效果。实验覆盖 10 个 30B–235B 参数的 MoE 模型，揭示了压缩技术间的非平凡交互：单独评估无法预测组合效果，压缩率不等于质量损失或加速比，专家剪枝是主要退化来源，平均指标会掩盖特定负载/架构的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 大模型虽通过稀疏激活高效扩容，但专家参数量大、路由不平衡、长上下文 KV-cache 增长导致在通用硬件上部署困难。实际部署常需叠加多种压缩技术，但现有工作多孤立评估单一技术，缺乏对组合压缩端到端效果的系统研究，亟需统一基准指导实用部署。

Method: 构建 MoEXBench 基准：选取 10 个 MoE 模型（标准注意力、混合线性注意力、滑动窗口注意力），测试 20%–50% 专家剪枝率、1–16 bit 权重量化、多种 KV-cache 精度，单独与组合应用。设计八模块评估套件，联合测量组合压缩质量、负载/架构鲁棒性、剪枝/量化/KV-cache 敏感度、部署效率（延迟、内存、吞吐），并在通用硬件上复现。

Result: 1) 组合压缩效果不可由单技术外推预测；2) 压缩率不能可靠预测质量损失或运行时增益；3) 专家剪枝是质量下降的主导因素；4) 平均质量指标会掩盖特定工作负载和架构的失效模式；5) 发布归一化模块得分、压缩产物与可复现脚本，支持跨 MoE 族与硬件后端的准确性-内存-延迟权衡对比。

Conclusion: MoEXBench 填补了 MoE 组合压缩端到端评估的空白，揭示了多技术叠加时的复杂交互，为在通用硬件上落地大规模 MoE 模型提供了实证指导与可复现的基准设施。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs scale model capacity efficiently through sparse activation, but their large expert parameter footprint, routing imbalance, and long-context KV-cache growth make deployment difficult on commodity hardware. Practical deployment often requires stacking multiple compression techniques: expert pruning removes redundant experts, weight quantization lowers model memory footprint, and KV-cache compression reduces long-context memory pressure. However, these techniques are typically evaluated in isolation, leaving open how they interact when applied together in realistic deployment pipelines.
  In this work, we present MoEXBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating composable MoE compression as an end-to-end deployment workflow. MoEXBench studies 10 MoE models ranging from 30B to 235B total parameters across standard-attention, hybrid linear-attention, and sliding window attention architectures. It evaluates 20%-50% expert pruning rates, 1 to 16 bit weight-quantization schemes, and multiple KV-cache precision settings, applied both individually and in combination.
  MoEXBench introduces an eight-module evaluation suite that jointly measures composable-compression quality, workload and architecture robustness, pruning/quantization/KV cache sensitivity, and deployment efficiency on commodity hardware. Our results reveal non-trivial interactions among compression methods: composable compression cannot be predicted from standalone techniques, compression rate alone does not reliably predict quality loss or runtime gain, expert pruning is the dominant degradation source, and average quality can hide workload and architecture-specific failures. By releasing normalized module scores, compressed artifacts, and reproducible scripts, MoEXBench enables practical accuracy-memory-latency comparison across MoE families and hardware backends.

</details>


### [134] [Posterior Information Dynamics of Diffusion Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21709)
*Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过平滑似然力研究扩散模型作为线性逆问题先验时测量信息在反向去噪过程中的进入时机和分配方式，建立了I-MMSE恒等式，分析了力能量衰减规律，并通过可解模型和实验验证了条件化对类别分离、熵减少及算子-先验对齐的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型广泛用作线性逆问题的先验，但终端质量无法揭示测量信息何时进入反向去噪过程及其在信号方向上的分配方式，因此需要深入研究这一过程。

Method: 利用平滑似然力（精确后验分数与先验分数之差）研究测量信息在反向去噪中的演变；通过固定测量的期望平方范数分析后验-先验相对熵耗散和反向路径相对熵增长；平均测量得到I-MMSE恒等式；在有限二阶矩假设下分析力能量及其与先验分数能量比率的衰减；用可解模型研究条件化对类别分离、均匀指数熵及算子-先验对齐的影响；在可处理后验模型和冻结FFHQ模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 平滑似然力的期望平方范数给出后验-先验相对熵耗散和反向路径相对熵增长；I-MMSE恒等式将信息增益与去噪误差减少联系起来；力能量及其与先验分数能量比率在高噪声下随噪声核信号系数二次衰减；条件化移除测量已解释的类别分离，将n个经验样本的均匀指数熵从log n降至H(I|r)，且同化依赖于算子-先验对齐即使奇异值相同；具有相同谱的掩码产生不同的先验归一化零空间轨迹统计。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解扩散模型在线性逆问题中如何利用测量信息提供了理论框架，揭示了信息进入反向去噪的动力学机制，并通过实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used as priors for linear inverse problems, yet endpoint quality does not reveal when measurement information enters reverse denoising or how it is allocated across signal directions. We study this process through the smoothed likelihood force, the difference between exact posterior and prior scores at each noise level. For a fixed measurement, its expected squared norm gives both posterior--prior relative-entropy dissipation and reverse-path relative-entropy growth. Averaging over measurements yields an information--minimum mean-square error (I-MMSE) identity linking information gain to denoising-error reduction. Under finite second moments, the force energy and its ratio to prior-score energy decay quadratically in the noising kernel's signal coefficient at high noise. Solvable models show that conditioning removes class separation already explained by the measurement, reduces a uniform index entropy over \(n\) empirical samples from \(\log n\) to \(H(I\mid r)\), and makes assimilation depend on operator--prior alignment even for identical singular values. Experiments in models with tractable posteriors evaluate these predictions. In a separate illustration with a frozen FFHQ model, masks sharing the same spectrum yield different prior-normalized null-space trajectory statistics.

</details>


### [135] [Reinforcement Learning on Benign Facts Amplifies Leakage of Memorized Private Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21727)
*Renfei Zhang,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现：在不含任何隐私数据的良性事实数据上进行可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR），会显著增加指令模型对已记忆的个人身份信息（PII）的抽取率，且推理能力与拒答率保持不变，揭示了无需接触隐私数据即可激活潜在隐私泄露的新攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 虽广泛用于提升模型推理能力，但其对模型“会泄露什么信息”的副作用研究不足。本文旨在探究：在完全不含 PII 的良性事实数据上施加 RLVR，是否会让指令模型中原本潜在的已记忆 PII 变得更易被提取。

Method: 1. 确认指令模型已记忆 PII 但呈潜在态（极少主动泄露）。2. 在不含任何 PII 的良性事实数据集上对模型施加 RL 训练。3. 训练后用两种探针复测：定向探针（姓名→邮箱配对）与非定向自由回忆提示（直接要求列出已知地址）。4. 在 8B–671B 三个量级模型上对比训练前后的逐字召回率、推理基准与拒答率。

Result: DeepSeek-V3.1 上 verbatim recall@k 从 0.155 跃升至 0.370（2.4 倍增益）；绝对泄露量随模型规模单调增大；推理能力与拒答率均无显著下降，表明 RL 仅选择性地改变了哪些已记忆信息可被访问，而非整体改变模型行为。

Conclusion: 良性 RL 微调可在完全不接触隐私数据、不使用任何隐私相关训练信号的前提下，显著激活模型潜在的 PII 记忆。这为攻击者提供了一条低成本、隐蔽的隐私提取路径，凸显了对齐与安全评估中需纳入“记忆可访问性”变化的必要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is deployed to make models better at reasoning tasks, but its side effect on what models will divulge is under studied. Here we show that RLVR on facts increases extraction of personally identifiable information (PII) the instruct model had already memorized. We first confirm that instruct models have already memorized PII but leave them latent, rarely surfacing one when asked. We then apply RL on benign factual data that contains no PII of any kind, and re-probe: a targeted probe over name->email pairs, and an untargeted free-recall prompt that simply asks the model to list the addresses it knows. PII extraction rises sharply under both: on DeepSeek-V3.1, verbatim recall@k increases from 0.155 to 0.370, a 2.4x gain. The effect scales with model size: across three models spanning 8B to 671B parameters, absolute leakage is largest in the biggest model. Meanwhile model's reasoning abilities and refusal rates are retained, indicating that RL selectively changes which memorized information is accessible rather than broadly altering the model. In summary, memorized private data can be made markedly more extractable by training that never touches it. This gives an adversary a route to memorized data that requires no privacy-relevant training signal and no access to the data itself -- only the ability to fine-tune on something innocuous.

</details>


### [136] [Adaptive Multilevel Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo for Rare Events Estimation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21736)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多层扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛方法，通过渐进式中间事件学习罕见事件扭曲函数，解决标准扭曲学习依赖正样本稀缺的问题，显著提升大语言模型罕见不安全行为概率估计的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署规模达百万/十亿级交互时，极小概率的不安全行为仍具实际危害性；现有扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛方法依赖目标罕见事件的正样本，但在学习初期正样本极度稀缺，导致扭曲函数学习不可靠，罕见事件概率估计不准。

Method: 自适应多层扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛：构建一系列逐渐稀疏的中间事件序列，在每一层利用已学扭曲函数生成更有信息量的正样本，指导下一层扭曲函数学习，最终得到目标罕见事件的精准扭曲函数。

Result: 在多样化任务和模型规模上的实验表明，该方法能产生更准确的罕见事件概率估计，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为可靠发现难以观测的不安全行为提供了实用工具，有助于强化已部署大语言模型的评估与安全对齐。

Abstract: Rare unsafe behaviors in large language models can remain practically significant even when their probability is extremely small, particularly at deployment scales involving millions or billions of interactions. Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) provides a principled framework for rare-event probability estimation by learning twist functions that guide generation toward a target event. However, the standard twist learning framework relies on positive samples from the rare-event target distribution, which may be nearly absent before an informative twist has been learned, resulting in unreliable rare-event estimation. We propose Adaptive Multilevel Twisted SMC, which learns the rare-event twist through a sequence of progressively rarer intermediate events. At each level, the learned twist provides more informative positive examples for learning the next twist, ultimately leading to a more accurate final twist for the target rare event. Experiments across diverse tasks and model scales show that the proposed method produces more accurate rare-event probability estimates. By enabling more reliable discovery of hard-to-observe unsafe behaviors, our method provides a practical tool for strengthening the evaluation and safety alignment of deployed language models.

</details>


### [137] [The Laws of Context Allocation: Causal Measurement and Closed-Loop Orchestration in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23252)
*Peiyang Liu,Xi Wang,Di Liang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RAG中证据利用度量失效和上下文预算分配次优两大瓶颈，提出因果留一法探针准确度量生成依赖，并通过去混淆因子网格证明迭代序列生成优于单块上下文扩展，最终构建闭环子模块调度器结合归因引导对比解码器，实现生成式搜索的序列化反馈驱动编排新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在多样化组合生成中面临两大核心瓶颈：标准相关性代理指标在困难负例上灾难性失效，导致证据利用度量失真；单块上下文扩展策略因相关性衰减而陷入架构陷阱，上下文预算分配次优。

Method: 1) 提出高效因果留一法探针，替代标准相关性代理，精准隔离生成依赖并形式化校准LLM注意力结构性稀释。2) 部署该探针于去混淆因子网格，证明迭代序列生成跨多步分配计算优于单块上下文扩展。3) 统一为可部署闭环子模块调度器，辅以归因引导对比解码器克服LLM注意力惯性，强制鲜活证据整合。

Result: 迭代序列生成带来16.7-20.5个绝对百分点的组合召回率提升，稳健扩展至32B模型；闭环架构在多个基准上超越经典开环基线。

Conclusion: 序列化、反馈驱动的编排是生成式搜索的终极范式，代码、数据及因果度量工具已开源。

Abstract: As Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) shifts toward diverse portfolio generation, it is stymied by two critical bottlenecks: flawed measurement of evidence utilization, and suboptimal context budget allocation. We resolve both sequentially.
  To resolve measurement, we expose a pervasive ``diagnostic illusion'': standard relevance proxies fail catastrophically on hard negatives. We replace them with an efficient causal leave-one-out probe that accurately isolates generative reliance and formally calibrates the structural dilution of LLM attention.
  To resolve allocation, we deploy this causal probe in a deconfounded factorial grid. We prove that the prevailing strategy of monolithic context widening is an architectural trap penalized by relevance decay. Instead, allocating compute iteratively across multiple sequential generations drives transformative portfolio recall gains of 16.7--20.5 absolute percentage points, scaling robustly up to 32B models.
  Finally, we unify these solutions into a deployable closed-loop submodular scheduler. Augmented by an attribution-steered contrastive decoder to override LLM attention inertia, our architecture systematically forces fresh evidence integration. By dominating classical open-loop baselines, we establish sequential, feedback-driven orchestration as the definitive paradigm for generative search. Our code, data, and causal measurement instruments are available at https://github.com/PeiYangLiu/ascp.

</details>


### [138] [Width-Independent Compressibility of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21752)
*Hong-Yi Wang,Mingze Wang,Liu Ziyin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明了具有解析激活函数的深层多层感知机（MLP）的统一可压缩性定理：对于任意宽度的教师网络，存在一个同深度的窄网络可近似表示相同函数，压缩后的宽度与原始宽度无关，仅取决于误差预算 ε 和有效输入维度 d_in，呈 O((log(1/ε))^{d_in})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来已知训练良好的神经网络可被强压缩而不损性能，但这一现象缺乏理论理解。本文旨在为深层 MLP 的可压缩性提供统一的理论保证。

Method: 提出新颖的导数匹配技术，利用低维输入结构；结合逐层重加权策略，保持输入-输出映射不变，从而构造出宽度大幅降低的近似网络。

Result: 证明压缩后网络宽度为 O((log(1/ε))^{d_in})，与原始网络宽度无关；该界在误差 ε 和有效输入维度 d_in 下近似最优。

Conclusion: 深层 MLP 具有与宽度无关的统一可压缩性，压缩宽度主要取决于输入内在维度和目标精度，为网络压缩提供了理论基础。

Abstract: It has long been known that well-trained neural networks can be compressed very strongly without affecting their performance, an important phenomenon that remains poorly understood. We prove a uniform compressibility theorem for deep multilayer perceptrons with analytic activations. For a deep, wide fixed teacher network, there exists a narrow (same depth) network that approximately represents the same function as the original. The reachable compressed width is strikingly independent of the original width, but is $O((\log(1/\varepsilon))^{d_{in}})$, where $\varepsilon$ is the error budget and $d_{in}$ is the effective input dimension. Our construction involves a novel derivative-matching technique which is aware of the low-dimensional input, and a layer-wise reweighting that preserves the input-output mapping.

</details>


### [139] [How Architecture and Training Affect TPC Representations Across Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21756)
*Tyler Wheeler,Michelle P. Kuchera,Raghuram Ramanujan,William Sieland,Ryan Krupp,Daniel Bazin,Connor L. Cross,Hoi Yan Ian Heung,Andrew J. Jones,Ruchi Mahajan,Saiprasad Ravishankar,Pranjal Singh,Benjamin Votaw,Chris Wrede*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了时间投影室（TPC）实验中深度学习表示的跨实验和跨探测器复用性，发现架构本身是任务相关结构的主要来源，随机初始化的PointNet风格编码器在多个下游任务上表现优异，且两种架构在跨探测器迁移中均未出现系统性效用损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习正向基础模型范式转变，实验物理中希望复用模型和学习到的表示。需要量化表示在不同实验、探测器系统和任务间的复用性，并区分架构先验与训练带来的贡献。

Method: 以TPC数据为测试床（事件可表示为变长稀疏张量），使用Sparse ResNet和PointNet风格编码器生成512维嵌入，覆盖GADGET II TPC和AT-TPC的四个数据集。先用随机初始化编码器隔离架构贡献，再在分类任务上监督训练并冻结参数，最后为每个下游任务训练线性或非线性探针。

Result: 架构诱导的结构在跨实验和跨探测器场景下保持实用；随机初始化的PointNet表示在多任务上高度信息量；两种架构组织嵌入空间的方式不同，但均未表现出显著的跨探测器效用系统性下降。

Conclusion: 架构是TPC嵌入中任务相关结构的主要来源，评估表示学习和开发可复用探测器模型时必须显式考虑架构因素。

Abstract: Deep-learning efforts have increasingly shifted toward foundation model approaches. In experimental physics, this allows models and learned representations to be reused beyond the experiments in which they were developed. This work evaluates the reusability of representations across experiments and detector systems using probes on frozen encoders. These probes reveal task-relevant structure before downstream adaptation, complementing fine-tuning. Together with random-weight controls, they distinguish contributions from architecture and encoder training that downstream performance alone cannot resolve.
  Time projection chamber (TPC) data provide a useful testbed because events from TPC systems can be represented as variable-length sparse tensors, while detector geometries, event topologies, and scientific tasks can differ substantially. We investigate whether fixed-dimensional TPC event representations can be reused across classification tasks, experiments, and detector systems. Sparse ResNet and PointNet-style encoders produce 512-dimensional embeddings for four datasets from the GADGET II TPC and AT-TPC. Randomly initialized encoders isolate the contribution from architecture before supervised training. We then train each encoder on a classification task, freeze its parameters, and train a linear or nonlinear probe for each downstream task. We find that this architecture-induced structure remains useful across experiments and detector systems. The randomly initialized PointNet-style representation is highly informative on several tasks. The two architectures organize their embedding spaces differently, but neither exhibits a large, systematic loss of utility cross-detector. These results show that architecture is a major source of task-relevant structure in TPC embeddings and should be treated explicitly when assessing representation learning and developing reusable detector models.

</details>


### [140] [A Fixed-Radius Distance-Band Benchmark for Dimensionality-Reduction Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21779)
*Yoshio Takaeda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的降维保真度评估指标——固定半径距离带 Shepard rho，以解决现有基于邻居图的评估指标（如 recall@k、trustworthiness、continuity）存在的偏见问题，并在合成数据集上对比了八种降维方法，揭示了现有指标无法捕捉的过度压缩等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的降维评估指标（recall@k 等）基于每个点的变半径和硬阈值，偏向邻居图方法（t-SNE、UMAP），惩罚保留绝对距离的方法，无法公平评估全局距离保真度。

Method: 提出固定半径距离带 Shepard rho：计算高维与二维成对距离的 Spearman 相关性，限制在累积距离带内，分别报告局部和全局结构，所有点使用相同的绝对半径。在已知真实几何的合成数据集（非均匀密度、密集簇、闭环过渡、离群点、不平衡两类别数据）上基准测试八种方法（PCA、Isomap、t-SNE、UMAP、PyMDE、PCC、DREAMS、toorPIA），并引入基于值的过度压缩指标和成员限制 Shepard rho 进行补充评估。

Result: (i) 全局 Shepard rho 可能很高，但簇内尺度却塌缩约 93 倍，这种过度压缩被基于秩的指标掩盖，却在基于值的指标中显现；(ii) recall@k 与固定半径带系统性分歧，方向符合偏见预测；(iii) 成员限制 Shepard rho 能解决单点和少数群体问题，许多成对统计量无法解决，即使是局部-全局混合方法 DREAMS 也会静默失败。补充的离样本测试考察未见异常点是否落在正常区域外及方向是否指示来源。

Conclusion: 固定半径距离带 Shepard rho 提供了更公平、可复现的降维保真度评估，能揭示现有指标遗漏的过度压缩和少数群体保真度问题，且所有指标基于成对距离精确计算，独立于方法内部，结果可离线复现。

Abstract: Dimensionality-reduction (DR) methods are routinely judged by how well each point's k nearest neighbors survive the 2-D embedding (recall@k, trustworthiness, continuity). We argue this family is a biased measure of distance fidelity: its per-point variable radius and hard inclusion threshold favor neighbor-graph methods (t-SNE, UMAP) and penalize methods that preserve absolute distances. We instead score DR fidelity with a fixed-radius distance-band Shepard rho: the Spearman correlation between high-D and 2-D pairwise distances, restricted to cumulative distance bands so that near and global structure are reported separately, with every point judged on the same absolute radius. On synthetic datasets with known ground-truth geometry (non-uniform density, dense clusters, a closed-loop transition, off-subspace outliers, imbalanced two-population data) at realistic noise (SNR=1, D=768, N=1000), we benchmark eight methods -- PCA, Isomap, t-SNE, UMAP, PyMDE, PCC, DREAMS, and the closed-source toorPIA -- and show that (i) high global Shepard rho can coexist with a ~93x collapse of within-cluster scale, invisible to rank-based scores but obvious in a value-based over-compression metric; (ii) recall@k and the fixed-radius band disagree systematically, in the direction the bias predicts; (iii) a membership-restricted Shepard rho resolves single-point and minority-population questions that many-pair statistics cannot -- questions on which even DREAMS, a recent local-plus-global hybrid, fails silently. A supplementary out-of-sample (addplot) test asks whether a never-seen anomaly lands outside the normal region and whether its direction identifies its source. All metrics are computed exactly on all pairwise distances, independently of any method's internals, and every number is reproducible offline: the closed-source method's output coordinates (not its algorithm) are committed to the artifact.

</details>


### [141] [MSM-Mem: A Universal Medical Structured Multimodal Memory Framework for Medical AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21810)
*Md Asaduzzaman Jabin,Khoa Le,Lin Zhao,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Medical Structured Multimodal Memory (MSM-Mem)，一种医疗结构化多模态记忆框架，使医疗AI代理能通过积累临床经验进化。将异构经验组织为语义、情景和视觉记忆，推理时增量更新，检索先前经验辅助当前推理，实现决策的渐进优化。在MoE-LLaVA上评估显示性能持续提升，持续使用进一步增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于多模态大语言模型的医疗AI代理多为无状态推理系统，无法保留或内化经验知识，限制了其通过使用逐步提升推理可靠性和适应纵向患者语境的能力，与临床医生基于经验的决策过程存在差距。

Method: 构建Medical Structured Multimodal Memory (MSM-Mem)框架，将异构临床经验结构化为语义记忆、情景记忆和视觉记忆三类，在推理过程中增量更新记忆，并支持检索先前经验以辅助当前推理，实现决策的渐进式优化。

Result: 基于MoE-LLaVA主干的评估显示，MSM-Mem带来一致的性能提升，且随着持续使用观察到进一步增益。

Conclusion: MSM-Mem为医疗AI代理提供了一条可行路径，使其能像临床医生通过实践学习一样，随时间推移进化推理能力。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making is inherently experience-driven: physicians progressively refine their reasoning by synthesizing patient history, multimodal observations, and prior diagnostic experiences across interactions. In contrast, current multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based medical AI agents largely operate as stateless inference systems, generating decisions independently for each interaction without retaining or internalizing experiential knowledge. This discrepancy limits their ability to progressively improve reasoning reliability through usage and adapt to longitudinal patient contexts in real-world clinical workflows. In this study, we propose Medical Structured Multimodal Memory (MSM-Mem), an agentic memory framework that enables medical AI agents to evolve through accumulated clinical experiences. MSM-Mem organizes heterogeneous clinical experiences into semantic, episodic, and visual memory and incrementally updates them during inference, allowing the agent to retrieve prior experiences to inform current reasoning and progressively refine decision-making over time. Evaluations on MoE-LLaVA backbones demonstrate consistent performance improve- ments with further gains observed through continued usage. In general, MSM-Mem offers a viable pathway toward medical AI agents capable of evolving their reasoning competence in a manner analogous to the way clinicians learn from practice over time.

</details>


### [142] [Resilient Concurrent Causal Discovery for Topological Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21815)
*Jiyu Tian,Junhao Dong,Mingchu Li,Lingling Fang,Liming Chen,Andreas Holzinger,Zheng Yan,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 RCCD 的弹性并发因果发现方法，通过影响感知超边因果注意力机制和基于掩码的交替因果优化框架，解决了现有方法难以捕捉并发事件复杂因果关系且对不完整事件序列缺乏鲁棒性的问题，在电信网络数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑事件序列因果发现方法难以捕捉并发事件引起的复杂因果关系，且对不完整事件序列缺乏鲁棒性，限制了在实际网络环境中的应用。

Method: 1. 影响感知超边因果注意力机制：将事件时长融入嵌入表示，通过超边因果卷积聚合并发事件特征，并注入网络先验知识以捕捉多对一复杂因果交互。2. 基于掩码的交替因果优化框架：采用自监督掩码重构，强制模型根据上下文恢复被掩码的事件类型，增强预测器对缺失数据的鲁棒性。

Result: 在仿真和真实电信网络数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在准确性和鲁棒性上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCCD 方法能够从拓扑事件序列中稳健学习因果图，更适合真实电信网络环境，为网络可靠性保障提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery on topological event sequences is crucial for ensuring the reliability of networks. However, existing methods struggle to capture the complex causal relationships arising from concurrent events and lack robustness to incomplete event sequences. To address these issues, we propose a resilient concurrent causal discovery method, termed RCCD, enabling robust learning of causal graphs from topological event sequences. Specifically, we first introduce an influence-aware hyperedge causal attention mechanism, which incorporates event duration into the embedding representation, aggregates concurrent event features via hyperedge causal convolution, and injects network prior knowledge to capture the complex many-to-one causal interactions. Furthermore, we design a masked-based alternating causal optimization framework, which forces the model to recover masked event types based on context through self-supervised mask reconstruction, thereby enhancing the resilience of the predictor to missing data. To validate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world telecommunication network datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and robustness, making it more suitable for real-world telecommunication network environments.

</details>


### [143] [A Physics-informed Neural Network Approach for Robust Buckling Load Prediction and Reliability-Based Design of Thin Truncated Conical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21818)
*Devasmit Dutta,Budhaditya De,Rohan Majumder,Sudip Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息神经网络(PiNN)框架,用于预测薄壁截头圆锥壳的临界屈曲载荷,并将其集成到基于可靠性的设计(RBD)中,实现了面向不确定性的不完美敏感壳体结构的高效设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壁截头圆锥壳因高强重比和几何效率被广泛应用,但其轴向压缩屈曲抗力高度敏感于几何缺陷、制造公差、材料变异性和非线性失稳效应。传统设计依赖保守的敲降因子(KDF,如NASA SP-8019),未显式考虑壳体几何、制造质量、数据不确定性或目标可靠性,因此需要一种能量化不确定性、兼顾物理一致性的预测与设计方法。

Method: 构建物理信息神经网络(PiNN),输入包含几何与材料描述符以及基于壳体稳定性理论和局部降低刚度法(LRSM)的力学信息特征;设计物理信息损失函数,惩罚超过理论弹性屈曲载荷的力学不可接受预测;利用133个Mylar截头圆锥壳轴向压缩实验数据训练并评估模型;将训练好的PiNN代理模型嵌入可靠性设计(RBD)框架,计算可靠性指数并校准满足目标可靠度水平的安全一致KDF。

Result: 与传统深度神经网络(DNN)相比,PiNN显著提高预测精度,降低平均绝对误差,增强物理一致性;基于PiNN的RBD框架成功评估可靠性指数,并为给定目标可靠度校准出安全一致的敲降因子,验证了该框架在不完美敏感壳体结构不确定性感知设计中的高效性。

Conclusion: 该研究建立的PiNN-RBD框架有效融合力学知识与数据驱动方法,实现了对薄壁截头圆锥壳屈曲载荷的高精度、物理一致预测,并支持面向目标可靠度的敲降因子校准,为不完美敏感壳体结构的不确定性感知设计提供了高效可行的新范式。

Abstract: Thin-walled truncated conical shells are widely used in aerospace, marine, offshore, and lightweight infrastructure systems due to their high strength-to-weight ratio and geometric efficiency. Their buckling resistance under axial compression, however, is highly sensitive to geometric imperfections, manufacturing tolerances, material variability, and nonlinear instability effects. Conventional design procedures rely on conservative knockdown factors (KDFs), such as those recommended in NASA SP-8019, which do not explicitly account for shell geometry, fabrication quality, data uncertainty, or target reliability. This study develops a physics-informed neural network (PiNN) framework for predicting critical buckling loads of thin truncated conical shells and integrates the trained surrogate within a reliability-based design (RBD) formulation. The model combines geometric and material descriptors with mechanics-informed features derived from shell stability theory and the localized reduced stiffness method (LRSM). A physics-informed loss function penalizes mechanically inadmissible predictions exceeding the theoretical elastic buckling load. The framework is trained and evaluated using 133 experimental Mylar conical shell tests under axial compression. Compared with a conventional deep neural network (DNN), the PiNN improves predictive accuracy, reduces mean absolute error, and enhances physical consistency. The trained PiNN is then used to evaluate reliability indices and calibrate safety-consistent KDFs for prescribed target reliability levels. Results demonstrate that the PiNN-RBD framework provides an efficient approach for uncertainty-aware design of imperfection-sensitive shell structures.

</details>


### [144] [More Experts, Worse Dynamics: Inverse Scaling and Spectral Bias in Mixture-of-Experts State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21840)
*Chandresh Pandey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文批判性地评估了混合专家模型在谱状态空间模型中处理多机制时间序列的能力，通过控制合成实验发现：算子级混合模型持续失败于超越单专家基线，专家数量增加导致反向缩放，路由机制失效，即使完美机制监督也无法防止全局性能下降；MSE改善往往源于破坏吸引子几何的时域平滑而非真实动力学建模，揭示了算子插值的根本局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设混合稳定算子能使谱状态空间模型适应异构或机制切换的时间序列，但缺乏在隔离动力学而非表征挑战的受控环境下的系统性验证。论文旨在通过合成基准测试，严格检验这种直觉是否成立，以及算子级混合是否真能捕捉不同动力学机制。

Method: 构建包含三种动力学机制的次步预测任务：Mackey-Glass混沌系统、稳定振荡机制、噪声主导的自回归机制。进行大量消融实验：容量扩展、神谕路由、冻结专家变体、与输出级MoE基线对比。采用相空间分析而非仅依赖MSE，评估模型对底层吸引子几何的保真度。

Result: 算子级混合模型在所有设置下均未能超越单专家基线；增加专家数量导致反向缩放；路由机制崩溃或无法诱导有意义的专业化；即使提供完美的机制监督，全局性能仍下降；混沌轨迹上的MSE改善往往源于破坏吸引子几何的时域平滑，而非忠实建模动力学。

Conclusion: 在所研究的参数化和训练协议下，算子插值存在根本局限性，无法有效组合异构动力学。评估机制切换动力系统时，必须采用几何感知的评估指标（如相空间结构保持），而非仅依赖MSE等点-wise误差度量。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are commonly motivated as a way to increase expressivity by decomposing complex systems into simpler local dynamics. This intuition has recently been extended to spectral state-space models, where mixing stable operators is assumed to enable adaptation to heterogeneous or regime-switching time series. We critically evaluate this assumption in a controlled synthetic setting designed to isolate dynamical rather than representational challenges. We study a next-step prediction task on sequences composed of three regimes: chaotic dynamics generated by the Mackey-Glass system, a stable oscillatory regime, and a noise-dominated autoregressive regime. Across extensive ablations including capacity scaling, oracle routing, frozen-expert variants, and comparisons to output-level MoE baselines, operator-level mixture models consistently fail to outperform a single-expert baseline. Increasing the number of experts leads to inverse scaling, routing collapses or fails to induce meaningful specialization, and even perfect regime supervision does not prevent degradation in global performance. Furthermore, we show that apparent improvements in mean squared error on chaotic trajectories can be misleading. Phase-space analysis reveals that lower error often arises from temporal smoothing that destroys the geometry of the underlying attractor rather than from faithful modeling of the dynamics. These results identify a likely limitation of operator interpolation under the studied parameterization and training protocol, and underscore the need for geometry-aware evaluation when assessing regime-switching dynamical systems.

</details>


### [145] [ChainPrune: Evaluating and Reducing Redundancy in Long Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21860)
*Weihang Pan,Zhengxu Yu,Yuxiang Zhang,Wenzhi Li,Zhongming Jin,Binbin Lin,Xiaofei He,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChainPrune，一种推理路径语义结构优化方法，通过将自生成推理路径整合为树结构并进行多准则主导路径选择，构建偏好数据，结合 DPO 偏好学习与监督损失，有效缓解大推理模型的过度思考问题，显著降低推理步长和计算开销，同时保持或提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的思维链推理虽增强了大语言模型的多步问题解决能力，但高级大推理模型常表现出过度思考行为，如推理步骤过长、冗余步骤多、计算开销大。现有的 token 长度奖励策略虽旨在促进简洁输出，但往往导致伪简洁性，即 token 数减少但冗余推理依然存在，导致链条更长且结构效率更低。

Method: 提出 ChainPrune 方法：首先将自生成的推理路径整合为基于树的结构，然后通过多准则主导路径选择过程构建偏好数据，形成浅层推理轨迹同时保留关键推理步骤；进一步结合基于 DPO 的偏好学习方法与监督损失，有效缓解虚假奖励抑制，提升推理框架的效率和有效性。

Result: 全面的实验结果显示，ChainPrune 在显著减少推理步长和计算开销的同时，保持甚至提升了准确率。

Conclusion: ChainPrune 通过语义结构优化和偏好学习，有效解决了大推理模型的过度思考问题，实现了推理效率与效果的双重提升，为高效推理提供了一种可控且高质量的训练数据合成方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the multi-step problem-solving capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by introducing explicit intermediate reasoning. However, advanced Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often exhibit overthinking behaviors, including excessively long reasoning steps, redundant steps, and high computational overhead. Existing token-length reward strategies aim to promote concise outputs, but often result in pseudo-conciseness, where token count is reduced, yet redundant reasoning persists, leading to longer and less structurally efficient chains. To address these limitations, we propose ChainPrune, a novel reasoning path semantic structural optimization method to efficiently and controllably synthesize self-generated high-quality training data. We initially consolidate self-generated reasoning paths into a tree-based structure, followed by a multi-criteria dominant path selection process for preference data construction that formulates shallow reasoning trajectories while preserving essential reasoning steps. To further enhance the quality of reasoning, we incorporate a DPO-based preference learning method combined with supervised loss, effectively mitigating false reward suppression. This innovative integration significantly enhances both the efficiency and effectiveness of our reasoning framework. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in step length and computational overhead, while maintaining or even enhancing accuracy.

</details>


### [146] [BioMed-Agent-RL: A Meta Learning, All You Need for Biomedical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21864)
*Md Asaduzzaman Jabin,Zihao Wu,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioMed-Agent-RL，一种统一的医疗智能体，结合自适应编排、基于策略与奖励的强化学习（RL），解决临床视觉大语言模型（C-VLLMs）中的病灶噪声、模态错位、幻觉及上下文缺失等问题，显著超越 GPT-5 等现有模型，准确率达约 73%（提升约 5%），为构建可靠、鲁棒、专家级的独立临床推理智能体树立新标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床视觉大语言模型（C-VLLMs）在数字诊断中虽取得进展，但仍面临病灶噪声、模态错位、幻觉、复杂病例上下文接地缺失等难题；主流智能体系统依赖静态、非自适应流水线，缺乏复杂医学推理所需的通用性。因此，需要一种能自适应编排多模型专长、融合临床上下文偏好优化与强化学习的统一智能体框架。

Method: 提出 BioMed-Agent-RL，包含自适应编排、策略与奖励型强化学习模型。引入临床上下文感知偏好优化（CPO）、直接偏好优化（DPO）、组相对策略优化（GRPO）并配合动态熵正则化。采用多模态元学习方法，充当领域专家与人类判断的综合器。智能体自适应调用临床接地与推理器、病灶分割器、领域特定综合器等模型级专长，跨模态（如 X 光）迭代自适应强化学习，学会在专家建议失效时综合误导性、冲突性视觉线索并信任内在推理。

Result: 在多个基准上进行大量消融研究，BioMed-Agent-RL 显著优于现有最先进模型（如 GPT-5），准确率达到约 73%，较当代基线提升约 5%。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建事实性、可靠、鲁棒、专家级的智能体系统确立了新标准，能够实现独立的临床推理。

Abstract: The current progress of Clinical Vision Large Language Models (C-VLLMs) has substantially improved digital diagnostics, still these frameworks often endure lesion noises, modality misalignment, hallucination, and missed contextual grounding in complex clinical cases. Moreover, prevailing agent systems usually depend on static and non-adaptable pipelines and lack the versatility necessary for complex medical reasoning. To resolve these difficulties, we present BioMed-Agent-RL, a unified medical agent that incorporates adaptive orchestration, policy, and reward-based reinforcement learning (RL) models for biomedical applications. To ensure reliability, it invokes clinical context-aware preference optimization (CPO), direct preference optimization (DPO), and group relative policy optimization (GRPO) with dynamic entropy regulation. This pipeline utilizes a multimodal meta-learning approach that operates as a field-specific expert and human judgment synthesizer. The agent adaptively utilizes a set of model-level expertise, such as clinical grounding and reasoner, lesion segmenter, and field-specific synthesizer, across various clinical modalities (e.g., X-ray) by utilizing an iterative and adaptive RL approach. The agent learns to seriously synthesize misleading, conflicting vision cues and trust in inherent reasoning, while specialist advice is faulty. An intensive ablation study is conducted across multiple benchmarks, and the agent significantly outperforms existing state of the art models, such as GPT-5, attaining up to ~73% accuracy (gain of ~5%) over contemporary baselines. As a result, the framework suggests a new standard for building factual, reliable, robust, and expert-like intelligent agent systems for independent clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [147] [CD-LoRA: Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation for Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21909)
*Qian Zha,Jinda Liu,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CD-LoRA 是一种无路由的多任务低秩适应方法，通过一致性驱动的对齐机制解决训练-推理不一致问题，实现更稳定、更简单的多任务参数高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LoRA 的多任务学习方法依赖复杂的路由机制来分区任务特定知识，但这类路由设计存在训练-推理差异：随机路由决策在分布偏移下导致推理不稳定。二阶泰勒分析揭示了路由方差引起的不稳定性。

Method: 提出 Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation (CD-LoRA)，完全移除路由器，采用一致性驱动的对齐机制，在共享低秩空间中强制跨任务表示一致性，学习任务无关的鲁棒特征，避免显式分区开销。

Result: 大量实验表明 CD-LoRA 持续优于最先进的多适配器基线，提供了更简单、无路由、更稳定的多任务 PEFT 解决方案。

Conclusion: CD-LoRA 通过消除路由器并引入一致性对齐，解决了多任务 LoRA 中的训练-推理差异，实现了更稳健、高效的多任务适应，代码已开源。

Abstract: While Multi-Task Learning (MTL) is essential for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to diverse domains, prevailing LoRA-based methods rely on complex routing mechanisms that partition task-specific knowledge. In this work, we reveal that such routing-based designs are prone to a training-inference discrepancy, where stochastic routing decisions under distribution shifts compromise inference stability. Driven by a second-order Taylor analysis that exposes the instability induced by routing variance, we challenge the training-inference discrepancy and propose Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation (CD-LoRA). By eliminating routers entirely, CD-LoRA employs a consistency-driven alignment mechanism to enforce representation congruence across tasks in a shared low-rank space. This paradigm fosters robust, task-agnostic features without explicit partitioning overhead. Extensive experiments show that CD-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-adapter baselines, offering a simpler, router-free, and more stable solution for multi-task PEFT. The code is available at the anonymous link https://github.com/zhaqian21/CD-LoRA.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Fixed Directions: Adaptive Representation Analysis of Reasoning and Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21919)
*Shaheen Nabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了语言模型中推理与记忆是否可由单一表示方向刻画及其在GRPO训练后的稳定性。实验发现，在研究的任务组上，一维投影即可达到AUROC=1.00的完美分离，支持单方向可解码性；但GRPO后该方向发生显著重组（余弦相似度~0.45，表示漂移0.511），尽管探针性能保持完美，几何实现方式已改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作提出推理和记忆可由单一表示方向表征，且该方向在强化学习中保持固定。本文旨在验证该观点的两个核心假设：(1) 推理导向与事实回忆任务组是否大致可单方向分离？(2) 该几何结构在GRPO训练后是否保持稳定？

Method: 使用Qwen3-0.6B模型和受控的400样本数据集，对比一维投影与全1024维线性探针的分离性能，测量GRPO训练前后的方向余弦相似度（均值方向、探针方向）及表示漂移。

Result: 预训练阶段：一维投影匹配全维探针，AUROC=1.00。GRPO后：均值方向余弦均值0.453，探针方向余弦0.445，最终层直接表示漂移0.511，但探针AUROC维持1.00。

Conclusion: 证据支持所研究任务组的单方向可解码性，但挑战了固定方向稳定性假设：任务相关信息持久存在，但其几何实现方式发生了重组。

Abstract: Recent work has proposed that reasoning and memorization in language models can be characterized by a single representation direction, including methods that keep this direction fixed during reinforcement learning. We test two assumptions behind this view. First, are reasoning-oriented and factual-recall task groups approximately single-direction separable? Second, does the resulting geometry remain stable after GRPO? Using Qwen3-0.6B and a controlled 400-example dataset, we find that a one-dimensional projection can match a full 1024-dimensional linear probe with AUROC = 1.00 on the studied task groups. However, after GRPO, the corresponding direction is substantially reorganized: mean-direction cosine averages 0.453, probe-direction cosine 0.445, while direct representation drift reaches 0.511 at the final layer. Probe AUROC nevertheless remains 1.00. The evidence therefore supports single-direction decodability for the studied task groups but challenges fixed-direction stability: the information persists while its geometric realization changes.

</details>


### [149] [Bi-EZP: LLM-Guided Bilevel Program Evolution for Ensemble Zero-Cost Proxy Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21927)
*Yutao Lai,Kezhao Lai,Hai-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bi-EZP，一种双层框架，用于自动发现集成零成本代理。上层利用大语言模型生成聚合程序结构，下层用 CMA-ES 优化连续参数，并通过验证集上的 Kendall 秩相关性评估泛化能力，实现结构发现与参数校准的解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理能在初始化时对候选网络排序，避免重复训练，但单一代理捕获的属性有限且在不同搜索空间中排名不一致。集成代理虽能融合互补信号，但自动发现需同时优化离散聚合结构和连续系数，导致结构质量难与参数校准分离。

Method: Bi-EZP 双层框架：上层用大语言模型生成基于四个互补基础代理的可执行聚合程序及其参数边界；下层对固定程序结构，用 CMA-ES 在内部训练集上优化连续参数；再在不相交验证集上用 Kendall 秩相关性评估校准后程序，指导进化选择泛化性强的结构。

Result: 在 NATS-Bench 和 Network Design Spaces 上测试排序性能，在 DARTS 上评估下游架构搜索。实验表明，将程序发现与数值校准分离，能有效构建自动化集成零成本代理。

Conclusion: Bi-EZP 通过双层优化解耦离散结构搜索与连续参数校准，利用大语言模型生成程序结构、CMA-ES 优化参数、验证集秩相关性驱动进化选择，实现了跨异构搜索空间的高效零成本代理集成自动构建。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies enable neural architecture search (NAS) to rank candidate networks from statistics computed at initialization, avoiding repeated training. However, different proxies capture different properties and often produce inconsistent rankings across search spaces. Ensemble proxies can combine complementary signals, but automated discovery must optimize both discrete aggregation structures and their continuous coefficients, making structural quality difficult to separate from parameter calibration. We propose Bi-EZP, a bilevel framework that decouples these decisions. At the upper level, a large language model generates executable aggregation programs over four complementary base proxies with program-specific parameter bounds. At the lower level, covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimizes the continuous parameters of each fixed program on an inner training split. The calibrated programs are then evaluated using Kendall's rank correlation on a disjoint validation split, enabling evolutionary selection to favor structures that generalize beyond their calibration data. Experiments on NATS-Bench and Network Design Spaces evaluate ranking performance across heterogeneous search spaces, and DARTS experiments assess downstream architecture search. Results show that separating program discovery from numerical calibration provides an effective approach to automated ensemble zero-cost proxy construction. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Bi-EZP-318D

</details>


### [150] [Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits: A Unified Theory of Contextual Bandits with Supervised-Calibrated Action Scaling and Pre-Execution Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21993)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出门控解耦组合型多臂老虎机（GDCB），一种新的上下文多臂老虎机算法家族，包含三大结构创新：动作由离散/分层老虎机选出的标称臂与上下文相关的缩放器组合；缩放器在独立的监督学习循环中学习，而非与臂选择联合学习；每个动作执行前均经过预执行门控，可修改或否决动作。作者形式化该算法类，证明四个结构性定理，并展示六个工业级系统均为GDCB实例。核心成果：解耦方差缩减定理表明校准良好的缩放器可消除上下文诱导的方差，将非平稳问题转为近似平稳问题；门控诱导等价定理表明在平稳门控下，历史数据可直接作为热启动初始化器，无需重要性采样修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文多臂老虎机分类（LinUCB、LinTS、HierTS、分解老虎机、神经上下文老虎机、RLHF）无法涵盖工业界常见的“组合动作+独立缩放学习+执行前门控”这一模式。在受监管、高风险领域（定价、给药、信贷、电网、内容审核、LLM工具调用）中，合规门控、安全护栏通常被视为部署阻力，实则可成为加速部署的机制。因此需要一种统一理论框架来刻画此类系统的统计行为并指导算法设计。

Method: 1. 形式化定义GDCB算法族：动作 = 组合算子(标称臂, 缩放器(上下文))，缩放器在独立监督回路学习，动作经门控函数修正后再执行。2. 证明四个结构性定理：解耦方差缩减定理、门控诱导等价定理、组合泛化界、门控稳定性定理。3. 将六大工业系统映射为GDCB实例，仅差异于组合算子、缩放器族、门控函数。4. 配套论文在真实生产数据上验证短期租赁动态定价实例。

Result: 解耦方差缩减定理：校准良好的缩放器可移除上下文诱导的方差，将非平稳老虎机问题转化为近似平稳问题，显著降低样本复杂度。门控诱导等价定理：在平稳门控下，任意先前策略收集的历史数据均可作为有效热启动初始化器，无需重要性采样修正，推广了P-HITL结果。实验表明六大工业系统均符合GDCB框架，验证了理论在真实场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: GDCB为受监管、高风险领域的上下文学习提供了统一理论框架，将合规门控、安全护栏从部署障碍转化为加速学习的机制。解耦缩放器学习与门控机制协同实现了方差缩减与数据复用，使得非平稳问题可近似平稳求解，历史数据可无偏热启动。该框架已在六大工业系统中落地验证，具有广泛的工程指导意义。

Abstract: We introduce Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits (GDCB), a family of contextual bandit algorithms with three structural innovations that jointly fall outside the taxonomy of LinUCB, LinTS, HierTS, factored bandits, neural contextual bandits, and RLHF. In a GDCB system: (i) the action delivered to the environment is the composition of a nominal arm, drawn by a discrete or hierarchical bandit, with a context-dependent scaler; (ii) the scaler parameter is learned in a separate supervised loop, not jointly with arm selection; and (iii) every action passes through a pre-execution gate that may modify or veto the composed action before it reaches the environment. We formalise this class of algorithms, prove four structural theorems characterising its statistical behaviour, and show that six industrially significant systems -- short-term rental dynamic pricing, clinical drug dosing, credit origination, grid demand response, content moderation, and LLM tool-use agents -- are all instances of GDCB, differing only in the composition operator, scaler family, and gate. The central result is the Decoupling Variance Reduction theorem: a well-calibrated scaler removes context-induced variance from the arm-to-reward mapping, turning a non-stationary bandit problem into an approximately stationary one. The Gate-Induced Equivalence theorem shows that under a stationary gate, historical data collected under any prior policy is a valid warm-up initialiser without importance-sampling correction, generalising the companion P-HITL result (arXiv:2606.02595) from human approval to arbitrary gates. In regulated, high-stakes domains, constraints usually treated as deployment frictions -- approval gates, compliance rules, safety shields -- are the mechanism that makes fast deployment possible, not an obstacle to it. The companion paper validates instance 1 (STR dynamic pricing) on real production data.

</details>


### [151] [Variance Driven Exploration: A Provable and Efficient Methodology for Pure Exploration in Highly Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21995)
*Khang Luong,Nam Nguyen,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了方差驱动探索，一种针对高随机环境下纯探索的原则性方法，通过将采样努力分配给最小化最终决策不确定性的原则，在最佳臂识别、蒙特卡洛树搜索和最佳策略识别三个核心问题上取得了理论保证和实证性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高随机环境中，探索过程主要受随机方差主导，现有方法未能显式地根据随机噪声对最终决策可靠性的影响来分配采样资源，因此需要一种原则性方法来最小化最终决策的不确定性。

Method: 构建平滑决策函数形式化最终决策的不确定性，推导出显式捕捉各组件随机噪声如何影响输出可靠性的采样分配规则，并将该方法应用于最佳臂识别、蒙特卡洛树搜索和最佳策略识别三个纯探索问题。

Result: 在理论上获得了方差衰减和简单遗憾的保证；实验上展示了VarDE相对于现有方法的一致且显著的改进，特别是在高随机环境中增益尤为强劲。

Conclusion: VarDE通过最小化最终决策不确定性的原则，为高随机环境下的纯探索提供了一个统一且有效的框架，在多个核心问题上均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: We propose Variance Driven Exploration (VarDE), a principled approach for pure exploration in highly stochastic environments, where the exploration process is dominated by stochastic variance. VarDE is built on a fundamental principle: sampling effort should be allocated to minimize the uncertainty of the final decision. We formalize the uncertainty of the final decision through a smooth decision function and derive allocation rules that explicitly capture how stochastic noise in individual components affects the reliability of the final output. We apply this methodology to three core problems of pure exploration -- Best Arm Identification (BAI), Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), and Best-Policy Identification (BPI) -- with theoretical guarantees on variance decay and simple regret.
