Empirically, we demonstrate consistent and significant improvements of VarDE over existing methods, with especially strong gains in highly stochastic environments.

</details>


### [152] [DySCo: Dynamically consistent data-driven downscaling of extremes in climate projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21998)
*S. Stamatelopoulos,M. Wang,I. Lopez-Gomez,L. Zepeda-Nunez,Z. Y. Wan,R. Carver,F. Sha,T. P. Sapsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DySCo（动态与统计一致的降尺度框架），通过数据驱动的向式重述创建动态配对训练轨迹，训练两阶段算子，在保持与粗分辨率GCM动态一致的同时，实现高分辨率气候投影的统计性能，优于现有无监督模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域气候风险评估需要高空间分辨率数据，但全球气候模型（GCM）分辨率粗且计算成本高。降尺度方法虽能修正统计偏差、捕捉极端事件，但面临动态一致性挑战：自由演化的GCM轨迹无法追踪训练用的观测数据集，这对基于故事线的极端事件风险评估至关重要。

Method: DySCo框架：1) 利用数据驱动的向式重述生成动态配对的训练轨迹，无需侵入式修改GCM；2) 基于配对轨迹训练动态与统计一致的两阶段算子；3) 将CESM2大集合（LENS2）在时间和空间上向历史再分析数据降尺度进行评估。

Result: 实验表明DySCo实现了与粗GCM轨迹的优越动态一致性，本质上施加了最小因果修正，保持了与最先进无监督模型相当的顶级统计性能。

Conclusion: DySCo成功解决了降尺度中的动态一致性难题，在保持GCM动力学一致性的前提下提供高分辨率气候投影，为长重现期极端事件统计和基于故事线的风险评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Regional climate risk assessment is critical for applications such as infrastructure design, disaster forecasting, and insurance resource allocation. However, estimating regional (i.e., high-spatial-resolution) risk with global climate models (GCMs) remains computationally prohibitive, which has driven the development of downscaling methods for coarse GCM outputs. Downscaling is vital for rare events, since quantifying their extreme properties requires high spatial resolution and very long GCM simulations. These methods non-intrusively increase GCM resolution while correcting statistical biases from unresolved fine-scale processes, thereby improving the accuracy of extreme event statistics with long return periods. A key challenge is preserving dynamical consistency, as freely evolving GCM trajectories are not expected to track the observational dataset used for training the correction operator. This is critical for causal extreme event analyses, where storyline-based risk assessment, i.e., extreme event catalogs, is necessary for effective planning. We address this challenge by introducing Dynamically and Statistically Consistent downscaling (DySCo), a non-intrusive framework yielding high-resolution climate projections consistent with coarse GCM dynamics. DySCo relies on a data-driven reformulation of nudging to create dynamically paired training trajectories without intrusive GCM modifications. Using these paired trajectories, we train a dynamically and statistically consistent, two-stage operator. We evaluate the method by downscaling the Community Earth System Model v2 Large Ensemble (LENS2) in time and space towards historical reanalysis. Results show DySCo achieves superior dynamical consistency with the coarse GCM trajectories, essentially applying a minimal, causal correction to the GCM, preserving top statistical performance comparable to state-of-the-art unsupervised models.

</details>


### [153] [The Communication Map of a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22007)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于权重的“通信图谱”，系统绘制了Transformer模型中所有潜在通信通道（共18类连接），从GPT-2的6.3亿到Pythia-6.9B的1300亿候选通道。该图谱仅需消费级GPU几秒到十几分钟即可构建，70-89%的注意力头对表现出显著非随机耦合。两大应用验证了实用性：1) 最强头对耦合盲测复现已知诱导电路并聚类成社区，消融某社区破坏上下文复制能力；2) 汇聚所有头的耦合系数识别出一个独特的二维残差流子空间，删除后在六个模型（含Pythia-6.9B）中消除诱导能力，且该子空间不同于激活PCA或异常维度。论文开源了图谱、统计工具和干预套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性目前主要手动逐个电路分析Transformer组件间通过残差流的读写通信，缺乏系统性、自动化的全局通道绘制方法。现有组合评分仅覆盖部分连接类型，无法统一量化所有18类潜在通信通路（从完整注意力头电路到单个神经元）。需要一种仅从权重出发、计算高效的统一耦合系数，实现全模型通信拓扑的普查与下游功能定位。

Method: 将Elhage等人(2021)的组合评分推广为单一耦合系数，覆盖所有18类连接类别。仅利用模型权重矩阵（无需前向传播/激活值），通过线性代数运算计算每对组件间的耦合强度，构建完整通信图谱。对GPT-2（6.3e8候选通道）与Pythia-6.9B（1.3e11候选通道）进行全量普查，统计头对耦合分布；两大下游应用：① 提取最强头对耦合聚类诱导电路社区并消融验证；② 汇聚全头耦合系数提取二维残差流子空间，跨六模型干预消融测试。

Result: 1) 全图谱构建极快：GPT-2约15秒，Pythia-6.9B约11分钟（单消费级GPU）。2) 70-89%注意力头对耦合显著偏离随机基线，呈现强耦合与主动回避两极分化。3) 应用1：最强头对耦合盲测复现已知诱导电路，自动聚类为功能社区；消融单一社区即破坏模型上下文复制能力。4) 应用2：汇聚耦合系数识别出一个独特二维残差流子空间，删除后在GPT-2至Pythia-6.9B六模型中一致消除诱导能力；该子空间与激活PCA主成分及异常维度本质不同。

Conclusion: 基于权重的通信图谱为Transformer机制可解释性提供了可扩展、统一且计算高效的全局分析框架。它不仅能自动化发现并分组功能电路（如诱导头社区），还能定位因果关键的低维残差流子空间，揭示了区别于激活统计视角的全新功能几何结构。开源工具链将加速大模型内部机制的系统性解剖与可控编辑。

Abstract: The components of a transformer communicate by writing to and reading from a shared residual stream, and mechanistic interpretability has mapped these connections by hand, one circuit at a time. We present the communication map, which charts every potential communication channel in a language model from weights alone, generalizing the composition score of Elhage et al. (2021) into a single coupling coefficient covering all 18 connection classes, from entire attention head circuits to single neurons. The census of all candidate channels, from $6.3\times10^{8}$ in GPT-2 to $1.3\times10^{11}$ in Pythia-6.9B, finds that 70-89% of head pairs are oriented far from chance, some coupled strongly and others actively avoiding each other. The full map costs 15 seconds for GPT-2 and 11 minutes for Pythia-6.9B on one consumer GPU. Two applications demonstrate the utility of the map. In Application 1, the strongest head-to-head couplings recover the known induction circuits blind and group them into communities, and ablating one such community destroys the model's in-context copying. In Application 2, pooling every head's coupling coefficients identifies a distinct two-dimensional stream subspace, whose deletion abolishes the induction capability in six models up to Pythia-6.9B. This subspace is different from those identified by either activation PCA or outlier dimensions. We release the map, the statistical machinery, and the intervention suite.

</details>


### [154] [Spectral Pre-Filtering for Context-Adaptive Sensor Fusion: A Four-Role FFT-GDCB Integration for High-Stakes Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22023)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种四角色FFT预滤波器，用于解决自适应卡尔曼滤波器在存在周期性噪声结构时的协方差估计污染问题。该方法以O(N log N)计算复杂度同时实现四大功能：白化彩色噪声、净化创新残差、生成频谱上下文特征、去季节化输入特征。实验在六大领域验证均通过PROVES评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当传感器或信号包含周期性结构（如机械激光雷达旋转谐波、发动机振动、地面多径效应、周/年度需求周期、给药间隔节奏、周媒体购买节奏）时，基于创新残差的在线回归会受到污染，导致拟合的协方差矩阵建模的是结构模式而非真实的状态不确定性，破坏了卡尔曼滤波器的最优性假设。

Method: 引入四角色FFT预滤波器：(i) 在卡尔曼更新前白化彩色噪声，恢复最优性假设；(ii) 在协方差回归前清洗创新残差，防止周期性污染R̂和Q̂；(iii) 生成频谱上下文特征，丰富下游老虎机算法的机制选择状态；(iv) 在监督回归生成敏感度系数（beta、剂量偏移、出价修正器）前对输入特征向量去季节化。该算法定位于Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits (GDCB)家族，作为监督缩放器的预处理层，单次FFT调用服务四个下游消费者，计算开销<0.1%，可直接集成无需修改现有组件。

Result: 跨六大独立领域（火箭着陆、自动驾驶跟踪、短租定价、临床用药剂量、航空票价分发、广告运营出价校准）的实证验证，均在预注册评估协议下获得PROVES判定，证明了方法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 四角色FFT预滤波器以极低计算成本解决了周期性噪声对自适应卡尔曼滤波器协方差估计的污染问题，同时为下游决策系统提供增强特征，是一个即插即用的通用预处理层，在多个实际工程领域均表现优异。

Abstract: Context-adaptive Kalman filters calibrate their noise covariance matrices Q and R from innovation residuals via online regression. When the underlying sensor or signal carries periodic structure -- mechanical LiDAR rotation harmonics, engine vibration, ground multipath, weekly and annual demand cycles, dosing-interval rhythms, weekly media-buying cadence -- the regression input is contaminated and the fitted covariance models structural modes rather than genuine state uncertainty. We introduce a four-role FFT pre-filter that solves this problem at $O(N\log N)$ cost and serves three additional roles "for free": (i) it whitens coloured noise before the Kalman update, restoring the optimality assumption; (ii) it cleans innovations before covariance regression, preventing periodic contamination of $\hat{R}$ and $\hat{Q}$; (iii) it generates spectral context features that enrich the downstream bandit's regime-selection state; (iv) it deseasonalises the input feature vector before any supervised regression that produces a sensitivity coefficient (beta, dose offset, bid modifier). We position the algorithm inside the Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits (GDCB) family, where it acts as a preprocessing layer for the supervised scaler. The single $O(N\log N)$ FFT call thereby serves four downstream consumers, fits in <0.1% of the sensor-fusion or pricing-pipeline compute budget, and is a drop-in addition with no changes to the Kalman filter, bandit, or runtime composition operator. We summarise empirical validation across six independent domains (rocket descent, autonomous-vehicle tracking, short-term rental pricing, clinical drug dosing, airline fare distribution, and ad-operations bid calibration), all returning a PROVES verdict under a pre-registered evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [155] [ARCHER: Amortized cross-specimen pose estimation for cryo-electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22029)
*Nhan D. Nguyen,Bao Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCHER，一种摊销对比分类器，实现冷冻电镜姿态估计的跨结构泛化，零样本推理，性能媲美专用估计器，且保留构象信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冷冻电镜姿态估计需针对每个数据集从头迭代优化，缺乏泛化性；本文旨在证明姿态推断可作为一种与样本无关的操作，通过显式条件化参考体积实现跨结构迁移。

Method: 引入ARCHER，一种摊销对比分类器，在离散旋转网格上建模姿态后验。在多种蛋白质结构上训练，零样本操作无需针对每个结构重新训练。泛化性基于傅里叶空间信息力学，样本依赖性由参考结构的功率谱和空间范围捕获。

Result: 在100个保留测试结构上中位角度误差5.0°，实验颗粒上2.5°，三维重构分辨率与专用估计器相差0.16 Å内。下游构象信号保留：主要构象坐标与已存基准相关性0.97，忠实重构自由能盆地和移动域。

Conclusion: 结果表明冷冻电镜姿态估计可跨不同结构泛化，ARCHER实现了零样本、跨结构的姿态推断，且保持高精度和构象信息完整性。

Abstract: Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) pose estimation is traditionally solved anew for each dataset, where iterative refinement is done from scratch while the estimator learns to store the molecule in its weights. In this work, we show that pose inference is a generalizable, specimen-agnostic operation when conditioned explicitly on a reference volume. We introduce ARCHER, an amortized contrastive classifier that models the pose posterior over a discrete rotation grid. Trained across a variety of protein structures, it operates zero-shot without retraining per structure. This transferability is grounded in Fourier-space information mechanics, where all specimen dependence is captured by the reference structure's power spectrum and spatial extent. ARCHER achieves a median angular error of 5.0° on 100 held-out test structures and 2.5° on experimental particles, matching dedicated estimators within 0.16 Å in 3D reconstruction. Crucially, downstream conformational signal is preserved. The leading conformational coordinate correlates at 0.97 with deposited benchmarks, faithfully reconstructing free-energy basins and mobile domains. These results overall demonstrate that cryo-EM pose estimation can be generalized across different structures.

</details>


### [156] [ReMAP: Self-supervised learning to unveil brain representations and vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22042)
*Jade Perdereau,Virginie Loison,Kanssa El Ayeb,Louis Gervais,Melvin Berto Strouc,Fabrice Vallée,Thomas Moreau,Jérôme Cartailler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用基于相似度的自监督学习，在原始双电极额部EEG上学习低维表示，将麻醉深度作为一条可读轴，同时患者轨迹的几何形状编码了额外信息。验证显示该模型能准确预测麻醉深度（BIS MAE=3.2, R2=0.82），且参数量仅68k却能与大规模EEG基础模型竞争。学习空间无监督地将年龄组织为独立于深度的梯度，并与已知麻醉特征（额部α、慢δ、爆发抑制）对齐。在独立队列中，早期轨迹几何能区分30个月认知和死亡预后（AUROC 0.86），提示麻醉轨迹几何是潜在脆弱性的标签高效相关指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有术中EEG监测多被简化为单一专有深度指数（如BIS），丢弃了大脑在状态间转换的丰富轨迹信息。研究旨在探索麻醉诱导过程中EEG轨迹的几何结构（而非仅深度）是否包含临床意义信息，特别是是否能反映患者潜在的脆弱性（如认知预后、死亡率）。

Method: 采用基于相似度的自监督学习（无标签），在原始双电极额部EEG上训练紧凑模型（68k参数），将每段记录嵌入低维空间。麻醉深度成为空间中的一条可读轴，轨迹形状编码额外结构。在两个队列、两种采集系统共1000+患者上验证，并与大规模EEG基础模型对比。进一步分析学习空间中年龄梯度、神经生理特征对齐，以及在独立纵向队列中早期轨迹几何对30个月认知/死亡预后的预测能力。

Result: 1) 麻醉深度预测准确：BIS MAE=3.2, R2=0.82。2) 紧凑模型（68k参数）在稀疏导联设置下性能与参数量大4M-157M的EEG基础模型相当，表明表示匹配录制特性比单纯规模更重要。3) 学习空间无监督地将年龄组织为独立于深度的梯度。4) 空间与可解释麻醉特征（额部α、慢δ、爆发抑制）对齐，联系神经生理学。5) 独立队列纵向随访显示，早期轨迹几何能区分30个月认知和死亡预后（AUROC 0.86），且与年龄互补。

Conclusion: 大脑在麻醉过程中描绘的轨迹几何是潜在脆弱性的标签高效相关指标，包含超越麻醉深度的临床预后信息。这激励未来进行前瞻性验证，以将轨迹几何纳入术中监测和围术期风险分层。

Abstract: General anesthesia offers a rare opportunity to observe the human brain under a standardized, controlled perturbation. Yet intraoperative electroencephalography (EEG) is almost always reduced to a single proprietary depth index, collapsing a rich trajectory into one number and discarding how a brain moves between states. Here we ask whether the geometry of that trajectory, not merely the depth it reaches, carries clinically meaningful information. Using similarity-based self-supervised learning on raw, two-electrode frontal EEG, with no labels, we place each recording within a low-dimensional space in which anesthetic depth becomes one readable axis while the shape of a patient's path encodes additional structure. We validate the representation across two cohorts and two acquisition systems totaling more than 1,000 patients. Depth of anesthesia is predicted accurately (BIS mean absolute error = 3.2, R2 = 0.82), and in the sparse-montage setting our compact ( 68k parameter) model remains competitive with EEG foundation models orders of magnitude larger (4M-157M parameters), indicating that matching the representation to the recording dominates raw scale. The learned space organizes age along its own gradient, independent from depth, without supervision. The same space also aligns with interpretable anesthetic signatures like frontal alpha, slow-delta, and burst suppression, linking this data-driven representation to established neurophysiology. On an independent cohort with longitudinal follow-up, the geometry of the early trajectory separates 30- month cognitive and mortality outcomes complementary to age (AUROC 0.86). These results suggest that the path a brain traces through anesthesia is a label-efficient correlate of latent vulnerability, motivating prospective validation.

</details>


### [157] [Personalized and Aspiration-Oriented Career Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22056)
*Kuleshwar Sahu,Girish Keshav Palshikar,Rajiv Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的个性化职业路径推荐方法，结合职业路径相似度(CPS)和志向相似度(AS)，对比了基于领域知识(DKD)和无监督表征学习与对齐(URLA)两种计算CPS的方法，实验表明URLA方法效果更优且不依赖领域知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决员工职业抱负实现对个人和组织成长的重要性，现有方法缺乏个性化和志向匹配的综合考虑，需要一种数据驱动的方法同时兼顾职业路径相似度和志向实现。

Method: 提出两种计算CPS的方法：(1) DKD基于领域知识提取职业路径特征计算相似度；(2) URLA利用LSTM学习职业事件名称的嵌入向量，结合事件属性(技能簇、领域)，通过加权余弦距离的最优Levenshtein对齐计算路径相似度。AS通过匹配志向与候选职业路径计算。最终结合CPS和AS对候选职业路径排序推荐。

Result: URLA方法在DCG指标上优于DKD方法，两种方法产生的排序结果连贯一致。URLA无需领域知识，通过时序对齐捕捉职业路径的语义和时间特征。

Conclusion: URLA方法是更优的职业路径推荐方案，它通过无监督学习事件嵌入和最优序列对齐，有效融合了职业路径相似度和志向相似度，实现了无需领域知识的个性化推荐，且在实验中表现更好。

Abstract: Fulfilling career aspirations is important for growth of employee and organization. We propose a data driven methodology to recommend personalized career path for a given aspirant's career path and aspirations. The pro-posed method uses the career path similarity (CPS) between aspirant's career and candidate career path, and 'aspirational similarity' (AS) between aspiration and candidate career paths to find suitable career path. CPS ensures personalized recommendation while AS ensures aspiration fulfillment. We defined two methods to compute the CPS between career paths which are (a) domain knowledge driven (DKD) and, (b) unsupervised representation learning and alignment (URLA) based, along with different AS measures. The DKD based similarity is defined in the terms of features extracted and summarized over career paths. In the URLA, we use the sequence of event names present in the career paths of the employees to learn the embedding for each event name. In URLA we use learned embedding vector of the career path event names and as-sociated event attributes (skill cluster and domain) to find the best alignment between two career paths. We hypothesized that relative position of event names in the sequence represents semantics of event name and that can be learned. We use LSTM neural network to learn the embedding vector of each career event name. We also define the matching method to compute the AS be-tween aspiration and career path in both proposed methods. We combine CPS and AS to rank available 'candidate career paths' of employees to find the suitable one. We get better DCG value in URLA as compare to DKD. We also showed that ranking are coherent using both the methods. URLA method is better since it does not require domain knowledge to model the similarity and includes temporal aspect by optimal Levenshtein alignment using weighted cosine distance.

</details>


### [158] [Improving Energy Efficiency of Oil Platforms Through Optimal Loading of Diesel Generators Using Machine Learning and Search Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22076)
*Khivishta Boodhoo,Josh Plumbly,Nicholas Watson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用机器学习和搜索算法优化海上石油平台柴油发电机的负载分配，实现每天平均节省27%柴油（约24,000升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上石油平台面临能源利用效率低、系统故障、可达性差及环境影响大等挑战，现有研究多聚焦增产而非节能，亟需提升平台能源效率。

Method: 基于苏格兰某平台18个月运行数据，针对四台柴油发电机进行探索性分析与异常检测，构建多元线性回归、人工神经网络、Extra Trees、XGBoost、随机森林等回归模型预测日柴油消耗，选取表现最佳的模型，结合搜索算法寻找最小化日柴油消耗的发电机负载组合。

Result: 多元线性回归与人工神经网络预测性能最优；优化负载组合后，日均柴油消耗较最差组合降低27%，相当于每天节省约24,000升柴油。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的优化方法能显著提升海上石油平台能源效率，为平台节能减排提供了可行的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: Rising energy demand, fossil fuel depletion and climate change highlight the need for more efficient energy production and consumption. Offshore oil and gas platforms face challenges related to inefficient energy use, system failures, accessibility and environmental impact. Machine learning (ML) offers opportunities to improve the safety, sustainability and efficiency of these systems; however, previous research has largely focused on increasing oil production rather than reducing energy consumption on platforms. This study investigates the use of ML and search algorithms to improve diesel efficiency on an offshore oil platform. Data collected over 18 months from a platform in Scotland were analysed, focusing on four diesel generators as the primary diesel-consuming equipment. Following exploratory data analysis and outlier detection, regression models were developed to predict daily diesel consumption for different generator power loads. Multiple Linear Regression and Artificial Neural Networks achieved the best predictive performance compared with Extra Trees Regression, Extreme Gradient Boosting and Random Forest. Search algorithms were then used to identify combinations of generator power loads that minimised daily diesel consumption. The results showed an average diesel saving of 27% per day compared with the worst daily power-load combinations, equivalent to approximately 24,000 litres/day. These findings demonstrate significant opportunities for improving energy efficiency on offshore oil platforms using ML-based optimisation.

</details>


### [159] [Counterfactual Quotient Models: Learning What Actions Change, Not What the World Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22092)
*Junlin Chen,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实商模型（Counterfactual Quotient Model），通过移除跨动作共享的共同成分，直接学习动作相关的效应，从而提升决策效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习模型常预测完整的未来状态、观测或特征占用，尽管动作选择仅依赖于候选动作后果的差异。这导致模型将大量统计和表征容量浪费在与智能体当前选择无关的高维现象上。

Method: 引入反事实商模型，将仅在跨动作共享成分上不同的动作条件未来视为等价。其规范中心化表示移除共同成分，同时保留建模奖励族可表达的所有成对动作比较。通过同步反事实回滚直接学习这些动作依赖效应，使共享随机动力学在函数逼近前抵消，而非在预测完整未来后。

Result: 建立了所得表示的决策充分性、可识别性、共模不变性、逼近行为和遗憾性质。在基于物理的环境中进行的受控实验提供了初步证据：直接效应学习抑制了动作无关变化，支持先前未见的奖励查询，并优于预测绝对未来的模型改进了动作排序。

Conclusion: 反事实商模型通过专注于动作间的差异而非完整未来预测，提供了更高效的决策表示，显著降低了对动作无关变化的建模负担，并展现出更好的泛化和决策性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning models commonly predict complete future states, observations, or feature occupancies, even though action selection depends only on differences between the consequences of candidate actions. As a result, these models may devote substantial statistical and representational capacity to high-dimensional phenomena that evolve independently of the agent's current choice. We introduce the Counterfactual Quotient Model, which treats action-conditioned futures as equivalent when they differ only by a component shared across actions. Its canonical centered representation removes this common component while preserving every pairwise action comparison expressible by the modeled reward family. The implemented model learns these action-dependent effects directly from synchronized counterfactual rollouts, so shared stochastic dynamics cancel before function approximation rather than after complete futures have been predicted. We establish the decision sufficiency, identifiability, common-mode invariance, approximation behavior, and regret properties of the resulting representation. Controlled experiments in physics-based environments provide initial evidence for these properties: direct effect learning suppresses action-independent variation, supports previously unseen reward queries, and improves action ranking relative to models trained to predict absolute futures.

</details>


### [160] [Beyond Fresh Starts: Stateful Inference for Streaming ASR in Conversational Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22101)
*Sameep Chattopadhyay,Alexander Erdmann,Mari Ostendorf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对流式语音识别模型在实时处理中因内存受限导致的长静默和回声等会话现象影响，提出两种状态管理策略以保留跨话语上下文，实验显示最佳方法在话语起始处平均相对词错误率降低15-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语音代理系统依赖流式语音识别模型，受严格延迟约束。实时处理的有限内存导致模型对长静默和回声等会话现象敏感，现有通过每轮重置状态的方法会丢弃关键上下文，损害话语起始处的性能。

Method: 提出两种状态管理策略，旨在保留跨话语上下文以减少起始错误。在两个最先进的流式模型和两个口语对话基准上进行实验。

Result: 最佳方法在两个基准上平均实现15-21%的相对词错误率（WER）降低，特别针对话语起始部分。

Conclusion: 通过保留跨话语上下文的状态管理策略，可有效缓解流式语音识别在实时处理中因内存受限导致的会话现象影响，显著提升话语起始处的识别准确性。

Abstract: Modern voice-agent systems rely on streaming speech recognition models that operate under stringent latency constraints. This study shows that, due to the limited memory constraints of real-time processing, these systems are adversely impacted by conversational phenomena such as long silences and backchannels. While many agentic pipelines mitigate this by resetting state at each turn, this approach discards vital context and impairs performance at turn onsets. We propose two state-management strategies that preserve cross-utterance context to reduce onset errors. In experiments with two state-of-the-art streaming models on two spoken dialogue benchmarks, our best method yields an average of 15-21% relative WER reduction at utterance onsets.

</details>


### [161] [What actually runs: a measurement study of language model placement and decode speed on the Apple Neural Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22110)
*Shahir M A*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过三条测量路径系统研究如何让语言模型在Apple Neural Engine(ANE)上高效运行，发现计算表达方式决定加速器放置，权重编码门控加速器，解码成本与编码宽度成正比，三元量化模型最小最快，提出“先选编码再分配字节预算”的设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究什么因素决定语言模型能否部署到ANE以及什么让它在ANE上运行得快，旨在为ANE上的LLM部署提供实证指导。

Method: 采用三条测量路径：1) 扫描64形状的LLM基元矩阵，固定计算内容变化表达方式，记录逐算子设备支持；2) 训练匹配的模型跨尺寸和精度，量化检查点与fp16对应结构字节级一致，确保测量的是真实训练产物；3) 读取ANE内存控制器字节计数器，获取实际运行而非编译器意图的数据。每个核心结论至少由两条路径支撑。

Result: 1) 放置取决于计算表达方式而非计算内容：融合RMSNorm全ANE合格，算术等价分解仅CPU；2) 权重编码门控加速器：25.85M卷积重fp16模型全分CPU(计数器证实零字节流经引擎)，同图int8/2-bit回到~83%驻留且快1.8-2.2倍，22.29M全注意力fp16达98.9%；3) 解码成本为每token流式字节，恒定~0.77倍标称编码宽度(跨fp16/int8/2-bit)；4) 最小最快为三元模型：匹配尺寸下算子组合几乎不影响指标，所有驻留25M三元模型落在10.0-10.8 MB和0.62-0.64 ms/token；5) 标题对：半注意力三元25M(10.5 MB,0.63 ms)和50M(16.8 MB,0.86 ms)比起初始卷积重fp16设计分别小9.8x/6.1x、快3.0x/2.2x。

Conclusion: 从实测导出设计流程：先选择编码方式，再在字节预算内分配参数量。

Abstract: We ask what gets a language model onto the Apple Neural Engine (ANE) and what makes it fast there, and we answer with three measurements. We sweep a 64-shape matrix of LLM primitives that varies how a computation is expressed while holding what it computes fixed, recording per-operation device support. We then train matched models across size and precision, with quantized checkpoints byte-identical in structure to their fp16 counterparts, so every deployment measurement is of a real trained artifact. And we read the ANE's memory-controller byte counters during inference, establishing what actually ran rather than what the compiler intended. We support every headline claim with at least two of these three measurement paths. We find that placement is a property of how a computation is expressed, not of what it computes: a fused RMSNorm is fully ANE-eligible while its arithmetically identical decomposition is CPU-only. Weight encoding gates the accelerator: CoreML assigns a 25.85M-parameter conv-heavy fp16 model entirely to the CPU (our counters confirm zero bytes through the engine), while the same graph in int8 or 2-bit returns to ~83% residency and runs 1.8-2.2x faster, and a smaller 22.29M all-attention fp16 model sits at 98.9%. Decode cost is bytes streamed per token, at a constant ~0.77 fraction of nominal encoding width across fp16, int8 and 2-bit. The smallest and fastest models we measured are ternary, and at matched size the operator mix barely moves either axis: every resident 25M ternary model lands within 10.0-10.8 MB and 0.62-0.64 ms/token. The headline pair is half-attention ternary at 25M (10.5 MB, 0.63 ms) and 50M (16.8 MB, 0.86 ms) - 9.8x and 6.1x smaller, 3.0x and 2.2x faster than the conv-heavy fp16 design this work began with. From these measurements we draw a design procedure: choose the encoding first, then spend the byte budget on parameters.

</details>


### [162] [Symbolic Neural ODEs: Learning interpretable models from time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22112)
*Nibodh Boddupalli,Jeff Moehlis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机器学习框架，用于从时间序列数据中识别稀疏、可解释的动力系统模型。通过多步预测损失训练神经网络参数化的向量场，并结合稀疏正则化，该方法在稳定性、泛化能力和统计性质保持方面优于单步回归方法，并给出了理论误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单步向量场回归方法在长期预测中稳定性差，且难以获得稀疏可解释的模型。需要一种能够保证组合一致性、提高稳定性并能恢复混沌系统统计性质的建模框架。

Method: 采用神经网络参数化向量场，通过最小化有限时域内的多步预测平均绝对误差进行训练，并在训练过程中渐进式增加预测时域。引入稀疏性正则化项以促进模型简洁性。该方法强制学习到的动力学在反复复合下保持一致性。

Result: 在稳定/不稳定不动点、周期轨道和混沌吸引子等多种系统中均实现了准确恢复。对于混沌系统，短期动力学预测准确，长期统计性质（均值、方差、李雅普诺夫指数）与真实系统高度一致。理论上建立了轨迹误差与统计精度之间的界。

Conclusion: 多步训练结合稀疏正则化能够学习到稳定、可解释且泛化性强的动力系统模型，尤其在混沌系统的统计性质保持上表现优异。理论分析为该现象提供了原则性解释，为数据驱动的动力系统建模奠定了新基础。

Abstract: We present a machine learning framework for identifying sparse, interpretable models of dynamical systems directly from time-series data. Our approach parameterizes the underlying vector field using a neural architecture and trains it by minimizing a multi-step prediction loss over a finite horizon. To ensure numerical tractability, we optimize a mean absolute error objective averaged across prediction steps, and progressively increase the horizon during training. A key feature of this formulation is that it enforces consistency under repeated composition of the learned dynamics. As a result, the identified models exhibit significantly improved stability compared with approaches based on one-step regression of the vector field. When combined with sparsity-promoting regularization, this leads to parsimonious models that generalize beyond the training data. We demonstrate accurate recovery of systems exhibiting a wide range of behaviors, including stable and unstable fixed points, periodic orbits, and chaotic attractors. For chaotic systems, while long-term trajectory prediction is inherently limited by sensitivity to initial conditions, we show that multi-step training yields models with accurate short-term dynamics and strong agreement in long-time statistical properties, including mean, variance, and Lyapunov exponents. Moreover, we establish theoretical bounds linking trajectory error to statistical accuracy, providing a step toward a principled explanation for this behavior.

</details>


### [163] [CST: Collaborative Selective Transmission for Communication-Efficient Multimodal Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22115)
*Hai Chi,Junrui Zhang,Rui Ning,Chonggang Wang,Robert Gazda,Huanrui Yang,Hongyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协同选择性传输（CST），一种主导向的查询-响应框架，仅检索与主设备表示互补的辅助信息，显著降低通信开销和端到端延迟，在多模态边缘感知任务中实现最优或近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同多模态推理通过融合分布式感知设备的观测提升边缘感知能力，但传输高维辅助表示带来巨大通信开销和高端到端延迟。现有通信高效方法虽通过压缩、语义编码或特征选择减少负载，却通常仅优化紧凑性或任务相关性，未显式考虑主设备已有的信息，导致任务相关但冗余的辅助特征仍消耗带宽。

Method: 基于部分信息分解（PID）和多视图冗余假设，CST 学习样本自适应、辅助设备特定的稀疏检索支持集，抑制检索主设备已覆盖或辅助设备间重复的语义。推理阶段，主设备仅发送支持集索引，各辅助设备返回对应潜在值，避免密集特征交换。

Result: 在三个真实多模态感知基准上，CST 传输的辅助特征值不超过 14.18%，却在评测方法中达到最优或近最优任务性能。在五节点 NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano 测试平台（5–100 Mbps）上，端到端推理速度比全传输基线提升高达 4.27 倍，验证了实际延迟降低效果。

Conclusion: CST 通过主导向的互补信息检索机制，有效解决了协同多模态推理中的通信冗余问题，在保持任务性能的同时大幅降低带宽占用和端到端延迟，为资源受限边缘设备的协同感知提供了实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative multimodal inference improves edge perception by combining observations from distributed sensing devices, but transmitting high-dimensional helper representations incurs substantial communication overhead and can lead to high end-to-end latency. Existing communication-efficient methods reduce payloads through compression, semantic coding, or feature selection, yet typically optimize compactness or task relevance without explicitly accounting for information already represented at the main device. Consequently, task-relevant but redundant helper features may still consume bandwidth. We present Collaborative Selective Transmission (CST), a main-directed query--response framework that retrieves only helper information complementary to the current main representation. Inspired by Partial Information Decomposition and the Multiview Redundancy Assumption, CST learns sample-adaptive, helper-specific sparse retrieval supports while discouraging retrieval of semantics already covered by the main device or duplicated across helpers. During inference, the main device transmits only support indices, and each helper returns the corresponding latent values, avoiding dense helper-feature exchange. Across three real-world multimodal sensing benchmarks, CST transmits no more than 14.18% of helper feature values while achieving best or near-best task performance among the evaluated methods. Experiments on a five-node NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano testbed across 5--100 Mbps demonstrate up to a $4.27\times$ speedup over Transmit-All in end-to-end inference, confirming practical end-to-end latency reductions.

</details>


### [164] [TANGO: Token-Aggregated Nonlinear Gating Operators for Natural and Formal Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22117)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种新型Transformer变体：TANGO（令牌聚合非线性门控算子）和WANGO（窗口化非线性门控算子聚合）。TANGO将标准Transformer块中的自注意力和前馈网络替换为单一的跨令牌门控残差更新，实现了最优验证损失但计算复杂度为二次方；WANGO通过窗口化机制和前缀统计将复杂度降为线性，在线性复杂度模型中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer块将跨令牌交互（自注意力）与逐位置非线性变换（前馈网络）分离，作者旨在设计一种更统一、高效的架构，通过单一门控残差更新融合这两种操作，同时探索二次方与线性复杂度的权衡。

Method: TANGO：每个源令牌生成SwiGLU门控向量，查询-键相似度决定源门控的加权平均，结果门控重缩放投影后的目标特征。WANGO：在近期窗口内保持未归一化分数，对较早源使用正特征图前缀统计，实现固定窗口和特征维度下的线性序列长度复杂度。两模型均共享单一块四次，参数量约44.3M非嵌入参数。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu、Lean和DeepMind Mathematics三个数据集上，TANGO获得最低平均验证负对数似然（NLL），但前向传播操作计数最大。WANGO在线性复杂度架构中FineWeb-Edu NLL最低，且在相近的乘加操作计数下超越Recurrent Transformer++。

Conclusion: TANGO展示了融合注意力与前馈网络的单一门控更新机制的有效性，虽计算成本较高；WANGO则证明了通过窗口化和前缀统计可实现线性复杂度且不显著牺牲性能，为长序列建模提供了实用的高效替代方案。

Abstract: A standard Transformer block separates cross-token interaction in self-attention from a nonlinear feed-forward network applied independently at each position. We introduce the TANGO model (Token-Aggregated Nonlinear Gating Operators), which replaces these two sublayers with one cross-token gated residual update. Each source token produces a SwiGLU gate vector. Query-key similarities determine a weighted average of source gates for each destination, and the resulting gate rescales projected destination features. TANGO assigns a separate weight to every causally visible source and is quadratic in sequence length. The WANGO model (Windowed Aggregation of Nonlinear Gating Operators) retains the same unnormalized scores within a recent window and uses positive feature-map prefix statistics for older sources, giving linear sequence-length complexity for fixed window and feature dimensions.
  We compare TANGO and WANGO with Recurrent and Untied Transformer++, full-attention GAU, and FLASH. All models have approximately 44.3M nonembedding parameters and are trained in three matched runs. TANGO, WANGO, and Recurrent Transformer++ apply one shared block four times; the other architectures use four independent blocks. TANGO obtains the lowest mean validation negative log-likelihood on FineWeb-Edu, Lean, and DeepMind Mathematics, although it has the largest analytical forward-pass operation count. WANGO obtains the lowest mean FineWeb-Edu NLL among the architectures with computation linear in sequence length and outperforms Recurrent Transformer++ at nearly the same analytical forward-pass multiply-accumulate count.

</details>


### [165] [The Price of Decentralization in Top-$K$ Arm Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22120)
*Larissa Xu,Jasmine Nguyen,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体多臂老虎机中的Top-K联合臂识别问题，即M个智能体协同选择动作组成联合臂，团队需识别出平均奖励最高的K个联合臂。针对三种观测模式（共享奖励隐藏动作、观测动作私有奖励、完全不对称），提出无通信消除算法（UCB-Intervals），利用各模式保留的信号重构隐式协调。给出固定预算和固定置信度下的匹配分析，并统一为由重数c和共识因子ρ索引的元保证。核心结果：基于测度变换的下界证明，共享奖励识别在通用对数因子内最优，移除通信的统计代价为样本复杂度上的乘法因子ρ²（完全不对称下固定4倍惩罚）。停止时间尺度为O(∑_a log(A^M/δ)/Δ_a²)，固定预算误差为exp(-Θ(T/H₁))，且对联合动作数A^M的依赖不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作团队常需就最优的少数选项达成共识，而非单纯累积奖励，且每个成员仅能观测团队集体经验的片段。现有研究多关注单智能体或全观测设定，缺乏对多智能体部分观测下Top-K联合臂识别的系统理论分析。

Method: 针对三种观测模式设计无通信消除算法UCB-Intervals：(A) 共享奖励隐藏动作时利用共享臂序；(B) 观测动作私有奖励时利用可观测偏差；(C) 完全不对称时扩大置信半径。算法在固定预算和固定置信度目标下均给出匹配分析，并将三种模式统一为由重数c和共识因子ρ参数化的元保证。

Result: 证明共享奖励识别在通用对数因子内最优；移除通信的统计代价为样本复杂度上的乘法因子ρ²（完全不对称下固定4倍惩罚）。停止时间尺度为O(∑_a log(A^M/δ)/Δ_a²)，固定预算误差为exp(-Θ(T/H₁))，且对联合动作数A^M的依赖通过下界证明不可避免。

Conclusion: 论文定量刻画了多智能体部分观测下Top-K联合臂识别的基本极限：无通信算法通过隐式协调可达到近似最优性能，通信缺失的代价被精确量化为共识因子ρ²的乘法惩罚，且联合动作空间规模A^M对样本复杂度的影响是内在不可消除的。

Abstract: Cooperative teams often need to agree on the best few options rather than simply accumulate reward, and they must do so while each member sees only a fragment of the team's collective experience. We study this as top-$K$ joint-arm identification in multi-agent multi-armed bandits: at every round $M$ agents simultaneously choose individual actions that compose a joint arm, and the team must ultimately return the $K$ joint arms of highest mean reward. The difficulty is that an agent may not observe the actions of others, their rewards, or either. We treat three observability regimes---(A) shared rewards with hidden actions, (B) observed actions with private rewards, and (C) full asymmetry---and design communication-free elimination algorithms (UCB-Intervals) that reconstruct implicit coordination from whatever signal each regime leaves intact: a shared arm ordering in (A), observable deviations in (B), and enlarged confidence radii under (C). We give matching analyses in both the fixed-budget and fixed-confidence objectives, then fold all three regimes into a single meta-guarantee indexed by a multiplicity $c$ and a consensus factor $ρ$. Our central result is quantitative rather than merely algorithmic: change-of-measure lower bounds show that shared-reward identification is optimal up to one universal logarithmic factor, and that the entire statistical price of removing communication is a multiplicative $ρ^2$ in sample complexity---a fixed $4\times$ penalty under full asymmetry. The resulting stopping time scales as $O\!\left(\sum_{\mathbf{a}} \frac{\log(A^M/δ)}{Δ_{\mathbf{a}}^2}\right)$ and the fixed-budget error as $\exp(-Θ(T/H_1))$, with the dependence on the joint-action count $A^M$ shown to be unavoidable.

</details>


### [166] [Decoupled Physical Modeling and Execution for Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22126)
*Ye Zhang,Xuehang Guo,Rui Pan,Pengfei Yu,Denghui Zhang,Manling Li,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种统一框架，通过显式构建物理建模中间表示，结合两阶段后训练（监督微调建立结构化建模、基于评分标准的强化学习优化建模质量），显著提升了小型大语言模型在多模态物理推理基准上的表现，平均比GRPO提升约3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理推理需要构建一致的物理系统模型，而非仅依赖符号或公式操作。大语言模型在数学和编程上表现强，但在物理问题上仍吃力，因为物理问题将物理建模过程与数学计算纠缠在一起。人类解决物理问题时会先建立系统表征再进行计算，受此启发，论文旨在显式编码物理建模过程以改进推理能力。

Method: 提出统一框架，蒸馏显式编码物理建模过程的中间表示；采用两阶段后训练策略：第一阶段用监督微调建立结构化建模能力，第二阶段用基于评分标准的强化学习（RL with rubric-based feedback）提升建模过程质量。

Result: 在PhysReason、PhyX、SeePhys等多模态物理基准上实验，该方法在不同模型和数据集上均带来一致的推理性能提升。物理建模输出平均比GRPO高出约3%，表明显式物理建模是提升小型LLM物理推理的高效策略。

Conclusion: 显式构建物理建模中间表示并通过两阶段后训练优化，能有效分离物理建模与数学计算，显著提升小型大语言模型在多模态物理推理任务上的表现，为物理推理提供了可扩展的改进路径。

Abstract: Physics reasoning requires constructing a consistent model of the underlying physical system rather than relying solely on symbolic or formula-based manipulation. Although large language models have shown strong ability in solving math and coding problems, they still struggle with physics problems, as these problems entangle the physical modeling process with mathematical calculations. Humans approach physics by first building a representation of the system before performing calculations. Inspired by this, we introduce a unified framework that distills intermediate representations that explicitly encode the physical modeling process and adopt a two-stage post-training strategy, where supervised fine-tuning establishes structured modeling, and reinforcement learning with rubric-based feedback improves the quality of the modeling process. Experiments on multiple multimodal physics benchmarks show that our approach leads to consistent improvements in reasoning performance across different models and datasets. On PhysReason, PhyX and SeePhys benchmarks, physical modeling output performs GRPO by an average ~3%, showing that explicit physical modeling is an efficient strategy of improving physics reasoning for small LLMs.

</details>


### [167] [Who Should Teach? Confidence-Aware Dual-Teacher Learning for Few-Shot Node Classification on Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22127)
*Hojin Kim,Sujin Yoon,Sungsu Lim,Dongwon Lee,David Yoon Suk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoTeach，一种置信感知的双教师学习框架，针对文本属性图的小样本节点分类任务，动态为每个节点选择更可靠的教师（GNN 或 LLM），在提升分类性能的同时降低 LLM 调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在小样本 TAG 学习中统一使用 LLM 生成的信息，忽略了其可靠性在不同节点间的巨大差异，且带来高昂的货币成本。GNN 擅长利用结构信息，LLM 擅长利用语义信息，二者优势互补，因此最适合的监督来源应因节点而异。

Method: 提出 CoTeach 框架，包含两个教师模型（GNN 教师和 LLM 教师）和一个学生模型。通过置信度评估机制动态判断每个节点哪个教师更可靠，并仅对高置信度节点使用 LLM 教师，其余节点由 GNN 教师监督，实现自适应的知识蒸馏。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的小样本节点分类实验中，CoTeach 持续优于基线方法，显著提升分类准确率，同时大幅减少 LLM 调用次数和相关货币成本。

Conclusion: CoTeach 通过置信感知的双教师动态选择机制，有效结合 GNN 与 LLM 的互补优势，解决了小样本 TAG 学习中 LLM 监督不可靠且成本高的问题，为高效、低成本的图文本联合学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs) integrate graph structures and node-associated textual attributes, and recent studies have increasingly leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to improve TAG learning in few-shot settings. However, existing approaches typically utilize LLM-derived information uniformly across all nodes, despite substantial variations in its reliability, while also incurring considerable monetary costs. We argue that the most appropriate source of supervision may differ across nodes, as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and LLMs exhibit complementary strengths in exploiting structural and semantic information, respectively. To this end, we propose CoTeach, a Confidence-aware dual-teacher learning framework that dynamically selects the more reliable teacher for each node. Experimental results demonstrate that CoTeach consistently improves few-shot node classification performance while reducing unnecessary LLM utilization and associated monetary costs.

</details>


### [168] [Blockwise Stabilized Adaptive Cubic Regularization with Subsolvers via Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22129)
*Rodion Podorozhny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种分块立方正则化牛顿优化器，通过在每个参数张量上独立最小化真实块Hessian的立方模型，并配合自适应立方常数和单调保护，实现了大规模张量的无矩阵Lanczos-Krylov子空间求解，保持精确立方步骤并达到最优O(ε^{-3/2})收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有立方正则化牛顿方法虽然具有最优全局收敛率O(ε^{-3/2})，但密集子问题求解限制了可行的块大小；现有的可扩展变体通过对角、低秩、Kronecker分解或草图近似替代真实块曲率，往往放弃了精确立方步骤。

Method: 引入分块优化器，对每个参数张量独立最小化基于真实块Hessian的立方模型，采用逐块自适应立方常数和全损失单调守卫；任意大张量通过Lanczos构建的Krylov子空间进行无矩阵求解，理论证明步骤最小化立方模型。

Result: 在9140万参数隐式神经表征任务中，提出的变体是唯一在每个块上保持精确立方步骤的立方牛顿方法；在FINER 2D图像拟合上，ARC-φ₁达到133.5 dB PSNR，而调优Adam在约70分钟内停滞于78.2 dB，同一时间ARC-φ₁已达95.6 dB。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持理论最优收敛率和二阶保证的同时，通过分块精确求解和无矩阵Krylov子空间技术，实现了大规模深度学习任务上的高精度收敛，显著优于Adam、SOAP、L-BFGS及其他立方牛顿变体。

Abstract: Cubic regularized Newton methods have the optimal $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3/2})$ global rate, but a dense subproblem solve limits the feasible block size. Scalable Cubic Newton variants replace the true block curvature with a diagonal, low-rank, Kronecker-factored, or sketched surrogate and, most often, give up the exact cubic step. We introduce a blockwise optimizer that minimizes an independent cubic model per parameter tensor over the true block Hessian, under a per-block adaptive cubic constant and a monotone guard on the full loss. Arbitrarily large tensors are handled matrix-free in a Lanczos-built Krylov subspace, where we prove that the step minimizes the cubic model. The theory also supplies the $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3/2})$ iteration complexity bound, a second-order guarantee, and monotone per-block descent. Four variants of this outer scheme are evaluated against the original adaptive regularization with cubics (ARC) optimizer, some other recent cubic Newton variants, Adam, SOAP, and L-BFGS. On a 91.4M-parameter implicit neural representation (INR), the variants introduced in this work are the only evaluated here cubic Newton methods whose steps stay exact on every block. Run to full convergence on FINER 2D image fitting, one of the ARC variants introduced here, ARC-$\varphi_1$, reaches 133.5 dB peak signal-to-noise ratio, while tuned Adam plateaus at 78.2 dB after about 70 minutes. In that time ARC-$\varphi_1$ reaches 95.6 dB.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Reduced-Order Dynamics with Singularity via Latent-Augmented Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22142)
*Xiaorui Wang,Yu Zhou,Wenjie Mei,Dongzhe Zheng,Yang Bai,Masaaki Nagahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对工业降阶建模中相空间自相交轨迹问题，提出潜在增强神经常微分方程（LA-NODEs）框架，通过增强神经ODE表达能力解决降阶系统中冲突向量场表示难题，理论推导最小增维条件，并在IPMSM驱动和分布式能源系统验证，显著提升预测精度与建模保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业降阶建模中相空间轨迹自相交导致向量场冲突，传统神经ODE难以捕捉此类特征，限制了复杂工业系统的高精度数据驱动建模能力。

Method: 提出LA-NODEs框架，通过潜在维度增强神经ODE，理论分析建立底层机制并推导最小增维维度判定条件，实现冲突向量场的有效表示。

Result: 在IPMSM驱动和分布式能源系统（DES）两个典型工业降阶系统上验证，实验表明该方法能恢复常规方法难以捕捉的系统特征，在预测精度和建模保真度上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: LA-NODEs为复杂工业系统的高精度数据驱动建模提供了有效途径，解决了降阶建模中自相交轨迹导致的向量场冲突问题，具有理论指导意义和工程应用价值。

Abstract: This paper addresses the issue of self-intersecting trajectories (in phase space) in industrial reduced-order modeling and proposes the Latent-Augmented Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (LA-NODEs) framework. From the perspective of artificial intelligence, the proposed method augments conventional neural ordinary differential equations to enhance model expressiveness, enabling the representation of conflicting vector fields that may arise in reduced-order systems, thereby improving learning accuracy. Through theoretical analysis, the underlying mechanism of the framework is established, and a condition for determining the minimum required augmentation dimension is derived. From the perspective of engineering applications, the effectiveness of the proposed method is validated on the reduced-order system of two representative industrial models, namely an interior permanent magnet synchronous motor (IPMSM) drive and a distributed energy system (DES). Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can recover system features that are difficult to capture using conventional approaches and achieve superior performance in terms of prediction accuracy and modeling fidelity, thereby providing an effective approach for high-precision data-driven modeling of complex industrial systems.

</details>


### [170] [Loss Landscape Features That Make Adam Stall: Definitions, Estimators, and the Preconditioned Hessian View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22145)
*Rodion Podorozhny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了Adam优化器在隐式神经表示(INR)任务中的表现，提出了一套指标体系来判断Adam能否缓解损失景观的病态条件，并通过FINER图像拟合案例研究展示了二阶方法的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam在深度学习中被广泛使用，但其在病态条件损失景观上的表现尚不清楚。现有工作缺乏量化指标来判断Adam何时能有效克服病态条件，何时会陷入高于二阶方法的平台期。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 定义量化指标：Hessian条件数、Adam预条件Hessian条件数、对角质量ρ、随机Lanczos求积估计的负谱质量、曲率带上的梯度能量分数（含指示Adam停滞的平坦分数）。2. 通过2x2解析案例说明对角预条件对轴对齐与交叉耦合病态条件的不同效果。3. 对FINER图像拟合架构进行全景分析：架构描述、Adam停滞原因、调优基线PSNR值与分块二阶方法120-134 dB结果对比、误差图、实际收益描述。

Result: Adam经充分调优（学习率从0.05到1e-8）可在某些病态景观达到极低损失，但会在交叉耦合病态条件下停滞在远高于二阶方法的平台期。提出的指标体系能有效诊断Adam能否缓解病态条件。FINER案例中，二阶方法比调优后的Adam提升PSNR达120-134 dB，误差图显示二阶方法能消除Adam遗留的高频伪影。

Conclusion: 对角预条件（如Adam）仅能消除轴对齐病态条件，无法处理交叉耦合病态条件。对于INR等高精度任务，二阶方法（如分块牛顿法）具有本质优势。提出的分析框架为优化器选择和景观诊断提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Across implicit-neural-representation (INR) architectures and analytic benchmarks we observe that a thoroughly tuned Adam (especially its learning rate (lr), e.g. in a hyperparameter sweep from $lr = 0.05$ to $10^{-8}$) can potentially reach a very low loss even on ill-conditioned loss landscape or converge at a plateau far above the loss attained by second-order methods. This report defines the measured metrics that help determine if Adam can mitigate the ill-conditioning on a given loss landscape. We provide the indicators by which each outcome is determined, that are: the condition number of the Hessian and of the Adam-preconditioned Hessian $D^{-1/2}HD^{-1/2}$ (with the derivation from Adam's update rule), the diagonal mass $ρ$ that distinguishes axis-aligned from cross-coupled ill-conditioning, the negative spectral mass estimated by stochastic Lanczos quadrature, and the gradient energy fractions over curvature bands, including the flat fraction that indicates the Adam stall. A worked out $2\times 2$ example and an illustration show the reasons why a diagonal preconditioning by Adam can remove axis-aligned ill-conditioning by rescaling and why it cannot do the same if the ill-conditioning is cross coupled. In addition, we present a case study of FINER image fitting architecture that goes over the whole loss landscape analysis framework: the fitting architecture description, reasons due to which its landscape stalls Adam at saddles, the measured PSNR values through our tuned baselines to the $120$--$134$\,dB results of the blockwise second order methods, the error maps behind those numbers, and description of the benefits such image fitting accuracy gives in practice.

</details>


### [171] [More accurate behavioral predictions with hybrid Bayesian-connectionist models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22154)
*Brenden M. Lake,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出贝叶斯蒸馏与行为微调（BBT）方法，融合贝叶斯模型与神经网络优势，在人类概念学习任务中实现更优的行为预测与心理洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究需在贝叶斯模型与神经网络间二选一：贝叶斯模型便于测试多样表征和归纳偏置，但难以避免过度简化；神经网络擅长避免过度简化，却难以系统探究表征与偏置。亟需一种兼具两者优势的统一范式。

Method: BBT分两步：1) 利用合成数据训练神经网络模仿贝叶斯模型（贝叶斯蒸馏）；2) 在人类行为数据上微调网络以捕捉额外结构与细微差别（行为微调）。

Result: 四大人类概念学习案例研究表明，BBT在预测人类行为上优于传统方法，且能揭示心理机制，生成的模型既能模拟贝叶斯先验，又能捕捉违背简单假设的启发式与偏见。

Conclusion: BBT提供了一种简单有效的模型构建配方，成功整合了贝叶斯建模的理论透明度与神经网络的灵活表达力，为计算认知科学提供了新的混合建模范式。

Abstract: Researchers must often choose between Bayesian or neural network models of behavior, two paradigms with complementary strengths and weaknesses. An ideal paradigm would facilitate testing many kinds of representations and inductive biases; Bayesian models make this easy, while neural networks do not. Similarly, an ideal paradigm would avoid over-simplifications; neural networks make this easy, while Bayesian models do not. Here, we introduce Bayesian distillation with Behavioral Tuning (BBT) as an approach to getting the best of both traditions. BBT offers a simple recipe for model building: first, a neural network is trained to mimic a Bayesian model through synthetic data, and second, the network is fine-tuned on human behavior to capture additional structure and nuance. Across four case studies in human concept learning, we find that BBT outperforms traditional approaches at predicting human behavior while also revealing psychological insights, resulting in models that can both mimic Bayesian priors and capture heuristics and biases that violate simple modeling assumptions.

</details>


### [172] [Why Does Robustness Reduce Superposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22155)
*Adam Elimadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过机制可解释性和超积视角，解释了对抗训练为何能减少超积：对抗训练放弃非鲁棒特征，导致需表示的总特征减少，从而降低超积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（Gorton & Lewis, 2025）虽实证发现对抗训练减少超积，但缺乏机制性解释；本文旨在填补这一空白，利用特征分类法（Ilyas et al., 2019）揭示因果链条。

Method: 基于Ilyas等人（2019）的特征分类（鲁棒/非鲁棒特征），通过实证分析追踪因果链：对抗训练 → 放弃非鲁棒特征 → 总特征数减少 → 超积降低。

Result: 实证支持因果链条：对抗训练确实导致模型放弃非鲁棒特征，从而减少需表示的特征总量，进而降低神经元中的超积现象。

Conclusion: 对抗训练通过减少非鲁棒特征的依赖，降低了特征表示的拥挤程度（超积），为对抗样本的机制起源提供了新解释。

Abstract: The study of adversarial examples and their origins remains an open area of research. Mechanistic interpretability, and superposition in particular, offers new avenues for approaching this problem. Gorton & Lewis (2025) demonstrate that adversarial examples arise from superposition and show empirically that adversarial training reduces superposition, yet provide no mechanistic account of why this occurs. We present an empirical explanation inspired by the feature taxonomy of Ilyas et al. (2019), tracing the following chain of causalities: adversarial training abandons non-robust features, leading to fewer total features to represent, resulting in less superposition.

</details>


### [173] [Unveiling the Depth-Performance Dilemma in Split-Federated Fine-tuning of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22188)
*Hariharan Ramesh,Someshwaran Murugaiyan,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了分割联邦微调（SFF）中存在的“深度-性能困境”：随着模型在客户端和服务器之间的切割层加深，虽然系统吞吐量和隐私性单调提升，但微调质量却发生灾难性崩塌。现有的联邦适配器聚合方法（AVG、STACK、SVD、FREEZE）在标准联邦学习中有效，但在分割架构下失效，根本原因在于 Transformer 的近等距拓扑导致聚合噪声无衰减传播，最终触发服务器分区的“注意力崩塌”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）规模激增，将模型深度在资源受限客户端与中心服务器间划分的分割联邦微调成为扩展训练的重要范式。然而，系统激励（吞吐量、隐私）倾向于更深的切割层，而这种配置对模型效用的影响仍缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 在四个模型规模（GPT-2 到 Llama-3-8B）和多样基准上进行全面审计，系统评估 AVG、STACK、SVD、FREEZE 等最先进的联邦适配器聚合方法在分割架构下的表现，并从机制层面追溯性能崩塌的根因。

Result: 实验表明：更深的切割层带来吞吐量和隐私的单调增益，但导致微调性能出现灾难性平台期；标准联邦学习中有效的聚合方法在分割架构下均无法缓解性能崩塌；机制诊断揭示 Transformer 的近等距拓扑使聚合噪声无衰减传播，最终在服务器分区触发注意力崩塌。

Conclusion: 切割层深度并非效用中性的调节旋钮，深度-性能困境挑战了现有假设。研究为稳定的分布式 LLM 微调提供了结构性基础，指明未来需设计针对分割架构噪声传播特性的新型聚合或正则化机制。

Abstract: Split Federated Fine-tuning (SFF) is a promising paradigm for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) by partitioning model depth between resource-constrained clients and a centralized server. While system incentives for throughput and privacy favor deep partitions, the impact of such configurations on model utility remains poorly understood. In this work, we identify and characterize the Depth-Performance Dilemma: the regime that maximizes system efficiency is precisely where fine-tuning quality collapses. Through a comprehensive audit across four model scales (GPT-2 to Llama-3-8B) and diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that deeper partitions provide monotonic gains in throughput and privacy at the cost of catastrophic performance plateaus. We evaluate a suite of state-of-the-art federated adapter aggregation methods including AVG, STACK, SVD, and FREEZE, revealing that while these techniques are effective in standard Federated Learning, they fail to mitigate the artifacts unique to split architectures. Finally, we provide a mechanistic diagnosis for this failure, tracing the collapse to the near-isometric topology of Transformers, which allows aggregation noise to propagate without attenuation until it triggers Attention Collapse in the server partition. Our findings challenge the prevailing assumption that partition depth is a utility-neutral tuning knob and provide a structural foundation for stable distributed LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [174] [On the Capability Separation Between World-Model Policy Learning and Imitated World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22197)
*Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文比较了世界-动作模型与直接行为克隆，证明在理想条件下两者恢复相同观察策略，但世界-动作模型改变学习因子分解而非策略类。进一步指出动作条件世界模型通过预测指定动作后果实现策略优化，观察数据无法识别动作因果效应，需干预数据实现零遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界-动作模型（先预测未来结果再推断动作）相较于直接行为克隆是否提供更强控制能力，以及动作条件世界模型在策略优化中的根本区别。

Method: 理论分析：在控制器类层面证明世界-动作策略可展平为直接随机策略；在总体层面在可实现性、精确优化、共同部署信息、分布保持部署假设下比较两者模仿目标；构造环境族证明观察学习者存在正最坏情况遗憾，而单次信息干预可达零遗憾。

Result: 1. 世界-动作模仿与直接行为克隆在理想条件下恢复相同观察行为策略，未来预测仅改变学习因子分解。2. 动作条件世界模型通过预测指定动作后果并优化控制目标，本质不同。3. 观察演示无法识别动作因果效应，需干预数据。4. 存在环境族使观察学习者正遗憾，一次干预即可零遗憾。

Conclusion: 核心区别在于预测观察行为关联的未来与预测指定动作后果用于策略优化。世界-动作模型不改变无限制策略类，动作条件世界模型才能实现因果策略优化。

Abstract: World-action models predict a future outcome and then infer an associated action. Although this factorization can improve representation learning and data efficiency, it is unclear whether it provides stronger control capability than direct behavior cloning when both are trained from the same observational demonstrations.
  We compare a direct behavior-cloning policy, an imitation-trained world-action policy, and a policy optimized with an action-conditioned world model. At the controller-class level, every world-action policy can be flattened into a direct stochastic policy with the same closed-loop trajectory distribution. At the population level, under realizability, exact optimization, common deployment information, and distribution-preserving deployment, direct behavior cloning and world-action imitation both recover the observational behavior policy. Thus, future prediction changes the learning factorization but not the unrestricted external policy class or ideal imitation target.
  Action-conditioned world-model learning differs by predicting outcomes under specified actions and comparing them through a control objective. We characterize the irreducible action-specific prediction error of future models that do not condition on the candidate action, identify conditions under which a world-action joint can recover an interventional forward model, and show that observational demonstrations do not identify action effects in general. Finally, we construct an environment family in which every observational learner has positive worst-case regret, whereas one informative intervention permits zero regret. The key distinction is therefore between predicting futures associated with observed behavior and predicting consequences of specified actions for policy optimization.

</details>


### [175] [Joint Causal Structure and Cluster Discovery Using Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22212)
*Avni Rajpal,Anubhav Kumar,Rishabh Karnad,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,P. K. Srijith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于变分推断的新方法，同时推断潜在簇和因果结构，解决了现有方法假设簇已知的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现通常关注单个变量关系，但在脑成像、气候建模等应用中，变量组间的交互更有意义。现有方法假设簇已知，但实际中簇及其因果关系是潜在的，因此需要同时推断。

Method: 基于变分推断，使用分类分布建模簇分配，伯努利分布建模图结构，推导变分下界和估计技术来学习变分参数和模型参数。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据集上验证了方法在簇发现和因果发现方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能同时推断潜在簇和因果结构，为组层面的因果发现提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to understand the relationships between individual random variables. In many applications, such as brain imaging and climate modeling, it is more meaningful to consider interactions among groups of variables. Existing methods assume that knowledge of such groups or clusters is explicitly available when modeling interactions. However, in practice, these clusters as well as the causal relationships among them, are latent. In this paper, we present a novel approach based on variational inference to simultaneously infer both the latent clusters and causal structures. We learn an approximate posterior over clusters and graph-structure by considering variational distributions based on categorical and Bernoulli models respectively. We derive variational lower bounds and estimation techniques to learn variational and model parameters. The effectiveness of our proposed methods for cluster and causal discovery are demonstrated on both synthetic and real data sets.

</details>


### [176] [Counterfactual Evaluation of Temporal Observation Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22221)
*Xizhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究反事实协议评估，即利用已部署观测协议收集的数据来评估从未部署的替代协议的预测价值。即使有无限基准数据，也可能无法确定替代协议的价值；提出了价值特定识别理论，针对线性目标和非线性聚合目标分析识别条件，并给出有限数据下的均匀误差界和边际增益，支持成本约束下的目标感知观测设计。实验表明宏观时间布局差异比精细放置更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观测协议收集的数据能否用于评估未部署的替代协议的预测性能？这是一个反事实评估问题。动机在于：在实际部署前，需要从现有数据中推断替代观测方案的价值，但潜在协方差结构的不确定性可能导致即使无限数据也无法识别替代协议的真实价值。

Method: 1) 定义协议价值为贝叶斯最优预测器在固定轨迹级目标上的总体R²。2) 建立价值特定识别理论：只关注改变替代协议价值的潜在模糊性。3) 对于线性目标，利用不可见协方差方向证明不可识别性，并展示有针对性的测量可在不恢复完整潜在协方差的情况下恢复识别；通过精确排列构造推广到非线性聚合目标。4) 在有限稠密校准数据下，给出均匀误差界以控制协议选择遗憾和可区分价值间隙。5) 基于精确边际增益，支持成本约束下的目标感知观测设计。

Result: 理论上证明了即使无限基准数据也可能无法识别替代协议价值；提出了价值特定识别条件，并展示了有针对性的测量如何恢复识别。有限数据下给出了均匀误差界和边际增益，实现了协议选择遗憾控制和成本约束设计。在Sleep-EDF和Long-Term AF数据集上的回顾性分析表明，宏观时间布局差异比基于有限数据选择的精细放置更可靠地被区分。

Conclusion: 该工作将识别理论、校准分辨率和观测设计联系起来，为未部署协议的评估提供了统一框架。结果表明，广泛的时序布局差异比精细传感器放置更可靠地从有限数据中识别，这对实际观测系统设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: We study counterfactual protocol evaluation: whether data collected under a realised observation protocol determine the predictive value of alternatives that were never deployed. Protocol value is the population $R^2$ of the Bayes-optimal predictor of a fixed trajectory-level target from the measurements an alternative would collect. We show that even infinite benchmark data need not determine this value: distinct latent covariance structures can induce the same benchmark measurement--target law while assigning different values to the same alternative. We develop a value-specific identification theory in which only latent ambiguity that changes the alternative's value matters. For linear targets, invisible covariance directions certify non-identification, while targeted measurements can restore identification without recovering the full latent covariance; an exact permutation construction extends the result to nonlinear aggregate targets. With finite dense calibration data, uniform error bounds control protocol-selection regret and distinguishable value gaps. Exact marginal gains then support cost-constrained, target-aware observation design. Simulations and retrospective analyses of Sleep-EDF and Long-Term AF show that broad temporal-layout differences can be more reliably distinguished than fine placements selected from finite data. Together, these results connect identification, calibration resolution and observation design for undeployed protocols.

</details>


### [177] [FreKoo++: Learning Continuous Spectral Dynamics for Temporal Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22224)
*En Yu,Xiaoyu Yang,Wei Duan,Guangquan Zhang,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreKoo++ 提出了一种连续谱动力学框架，统一连续 Koopman 模态动力学与自适应谱解耦，解决时间域泛化中多尺度漂移模式和不规则采样难题，在离散和连续基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间域泛化方法难以处理真实流式场景中的复杂多尺度漂移（长期周期性与短期增量变化交织）和局部不确定性，尤其是在观测不规则到达的连续设置中。

Method: 将源域参数映射到紧凑潜在空间，建模为可学习连续模态的叠加，复特征值联合编码振荡频率和时序增长/衰减；提出自适应软谱加权机制，配合稳定性与谱正则化，自动分离持久主导动力学与瞬态噪声；推导模态近似与泛化界以表征误差随预测地平线的传播。

Result: 在离散和连续时间域泛化基准上的大量实验表明，FreKoo++ 在复杂多尺度漂移和不规则采样条件下均实现最先进性能。

Conclusion: FreKoo++ 通过连续谱动力学建模和自适应谱解耦，有效解决了时间域泛化中的多尺度漂移与不规则采样挑战，为流式数据下的鲁棒泛化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Temporal Domain Generalization (TDG) aims to learn from historical domains and generalize to unseen future distributions under concept drift. Nevertheless, prevailing TDG methods struggle with complex real-world streaming scenarios involving both multi-scale drift patterns (e.g., long-term periodicity intertwined with short-term incremental changes) and local uncertainties, especially in continuous settings where observations arrive irregularly. To address this limitation, we propose FreKoo++, a novel continuous spectral-dynamical framework that pioneers the unification of continuous Koopman modal dynamics with adaptive spectral disentanglement. Specifically, FreKoo++ maps source-domain parameters into a compact latent space, modeling their evolution as a superposition of learnable continuous modes where complex eigenvalues jointly encode oscillatory frequency and temporal growth or decay. This formulation naturally accommodates irregular timestamps and supports arbitrary horizon extrapolation without rigid discrete stepping. Furthermore, we propose a new adaptive soft spectral weighting mechanism backed by stability and spectral regularization, which automatically isolates persistent dominant dynamics from transient noise without relying on manual frequency thresholds. We derive modal approximation and generalization bounds that characterize how amplitude and eigenvalue estimation errors propagate with the prediction horizon. Extensive experiments on both discrete and continuous TDG benchmarks demonstrate that FreKoo++ achieves state-of-the-art performance under complex multi-scale drifts and irregular sampling.

</details>


### [178] [Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning with Smoothed Quantile Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22227)
*Mohammad Alipour-Vaezi,Huaiyang Zhong,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCB-BQRL算法，针对分位数目标的风险敏感强化学习，通过缓冲分位数准则平滑目标，给出遗憾上下界并证明计算复杂度PP-hard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典RL不考虑风险敏感性，分位数目标非光滑且在估计误差下不稳定，需开发稳定的风险敏感模型型算法。

Method: UCB-BQRL：基于模型的乐观算法，维护转移核置信集，采用下缓冲分位数准则（平均邻近下分位数）平滑目标；引入EVI-BQ精确动态规划过程计算策略。

Result: 高概率遗憾上界O(e^{τ/ρ_τ}+H^2√(SAT))；下界Ω(H/ρ_τ√(AT))；精确点分位数评估和精确下缓冲分位数评估在两状态单动作有限时域MDP中为PP-hard。

Conclusion: 缓冲分位数准则有效提升稳定性，UCB-BQRL在风险敏感RL中提供理论保证，但精确计算分位数目标本质上难（PP-hard），需近似或启发式方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved tremendous success in recent years. However, the classical foundations of RL do not account for the risk sensitivity of the objective function, which is critical in various fields, including healthcare, finance, etc. A popular approach to incorporate risk sensitivity is to optimize a specific quantile of the cumulative reward distribution. However, exact quantile objectives are non-smooth and can change abruptly under small perturbations of the return distribution, making them difficult to optimize reliably when the transition model must be learned from data. Motivated by this instability, we develop UCB-BQRL, a model-based optimistic learning algorithm that maintains confidence sets for the transition kernel and plans using a lower-buffered quantile criterion. The buffered criterion smooths the exact quantile objective by averaging nearby lower quantiles, thereby improving stability under transition-estimation error. To compute the buffered-quantile policy at each episode, we introduce EVI-BQ, an exact dynamic-programming procedure. We establish a high-probability regret bound for UCB-BQRL, which up to logarithmic factors scales as $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{e}^{τ/ρ_τ}+H^2\sqrt{SAT})$, where $ρ_τ$ is denoted as the root-level left-plateau threshold, which is a problem-dependent constant. Further, we establish an information-theoretic lower bound of $Ω(H/ρ_τ\sqrt{AT})$ for the regret of any algorithm dealing with a quantile objective function. Finally, we prove that the exact point-quantile evaluation and exact lower-buffered quantile evaluation are PP-hard under polynomial-time Turing reductions, even for a fixed policy in a two-state, one-action finite-horizon MDP.

</details>


### [179] [When Test-Time Adaptation Helps, Harms, or Becomes Inactive: A Condition-Level Study on CIFAR-10-C](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22233)
*Sreeja Guha Majumdar,Aratrika Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在 CIFAR-10-C 基准上系统对比了三种测试时适应（TTA）策略：BatchNorm 统计量适应（BN-Adapt）、熵最小化适应（TENT）和可靠性过滤适应（EATA 变体）。虽然三种方法均显著提升平均准确率（+12.2–13.3 pp），但在 8.0–9.3% 的条件下表现劣于源模型，失效主要集中在低严重度、源模型已接近上限的腐败类型（亮度、雾、对比度、离焦模糊）。EATA 与无梯度的 BN-Adapt 极其接近（均差 0.09 pp），远小于与 TENT 的差异（1.08 pp），表明可靠性过滤过度限制了适应，使 EATA 退化为类 BN-Adapt 基线。研究强调聚合指标掩盖了系统性失效模式，呼吁进行条件级评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TTA 方法（如 TENT、EATA）在污染数据上展示了整体增益，但聚合准确率可能掩盖适应失效或收益微小的具体条件。需要细粒度分析何时适应有帮助、有害或实际上失效。

Method: 在完整的 CIFAR-10-C 基准（15 种腐败类型 × 5 个严重度）上，对比三种 TTA 策略：BN-Adapt（仅更新 BN 统计量）、TENT（熵最小化）、EATA 的作用域重实现（可靠性过滤）。使用 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验评估显著性，并进行条件级失效分析。

Result: 三种方法平均准确率均显著优于源模型（+12.2–13.3 pp, p < 1e-12）。但每种方法在 8.0–9.3% 条件下劣于源模型，失效集中于低严重度、源模型已近饱和的腐败（亮度、雾、对比度、离焦模糊）。EATA 与 BN-Adapt 平均绝对差仅 0.09 pp，远小于与 TENT 的 1.08 pp，表明可靠性过滤使 EATA 行为趋近 BN-Adapt 而非熵最小化。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率无法揭示 TTA 的系统性失效模式；EATA 的可靠性过滤可能过度抑制有效适应。未来工作应采用条件级评估，明确适应在何种分布偏移下有益、有害或失效。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to improve model robustness under distribution shift by adapting a source model using unlabeled test data. Although methods such as TENT and EATA have demonstrated gains on corrupted data, aggregate accuracy can obscure the conditions under which adaptation fails or provides little benefit. We present a controlled comparison of three TTA strategies---BatchNorm-statistics adaptation (BN-Adapt), entropy-minimization adaptation (TENT), and reliability-filtered adaptation (a scoped re-implementation of EATA)---against an unadapted source model on the full CIFAR-10-C benchmark, covering 15 corruption types and 5 severity levels. All three methods improve mean accuracy over the source model by 12.2--13.3 percentage points (Wilcoxon signed-rank $p < 10^{-12}$). However, each method underperforms the source model on 8.0--9.3\% of conditions, with failures concentrated in low-severity corruptions where the source model already performs near ceiling, particularly brightness, fog, contrast, and defocus blur. We further find that EATA closely tracks the gradient-free BN-Adapt baseline, with a mean absolute difference of 0.09 percentage points, compared with 1.08 percentage points relative to TENT. This suggests that reliability filtering can substantially restrict effective adaptation, causing EATA to behave more like a BatchNorm-statistics baseline than an entropy-minimization method. These results show that aggregate accuracy alone can mask systematic TTA failure modes and motivate condition-level evaluation of when adaptation helps, harms, or becomes effectively inactive.

</details>


### [180] [A Query-Time Framework for Transient 2D Pore-Scale Flow Prediction and Generative Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22235)
*Yiming Wang,Jiale Zhu,Zhichen Ye,Yandong Lv,Shiqi Wang,Jinlong Liu,Yucheng Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种连续时间孔隙尺度流动替代模型CT-PoreFlow，并构建了包含7606个二维多孔结构的QSGS-Transient-7606基准数据集，实现了快速瞬态流动预测与反向设计，无需微调即可跨几何泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孔隙尺度流动决定多孔介质中的传输和渗透行为，但重复进行格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）模拟成本高昂，难以大规模部署。因此需要一种高效的替代模型来实现快速瞬态流动预测和反向设计。

Method: 构建QSGS-Transient-7606基准数据集，包含7606个二维多孔结构及每个结构30个对数采样的LBM状态。提出CT-PoreFlow模型，集成拓扑感知几何编码、压缩谱混合、对数时间条件化和晚期通量校准目标。将替代模型嵌入反向设计工作流，筛选生成对抗网络（GAN）和扩散模型生成的候选结构，再进行LBM验证。

Result: 在未见测试几何上，速度相对L2误差为0.2248，终端渗透率误差为12.81%。冻结形态和CT图像审计确认了跨几何鲁棒性。引导式GAN采样达到98.11%贯穿连通性和72.28%条件设计成功率，优于扩散模型。筛选了9216个候选结构，覆盖18个属性目标。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了瞬态流动预测、传感知筛选和LBM验证的反向设计，为多孔介质工程应用提供了高效的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Pore-scale flow governs transport and permeability behaviour in porous media engineering applications, yet repeated lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) simulation across many geometries and design queries remains costly for repeated deployment. This study formulates transient pore-scale flow prediction as a geometry-conditioned query-time operator and introduces QSGS-Transient-7606, a benchmark of 7,606 two-dimensional porous structures each paired with 30 logarithmically sampled LBM states. The proposed continuous-time pore-scale flow surrogate model (CT-PoreFlow) integrates topology-aware geometry encoding, compressed spectral mixing, and log-time conditioning with a late-time flux-calibration objective. On unseen test geometries, CT-PoreFlow achieves a velocity relative L2 of 0.2248 and a terminal permeability error of 12.81%. Frozen morphology and computed tomography image audits confirm reasonable cross-geometry robustness without fine-tuning. The surrogate is then embedded in an inverse design workflow, screening 9,216 generative adversarial network and diffusion candidates across 18 property targets prior to LBM verification. Guided GAN sampling attains 98.11% through-connectivity and 72.28% conditional design success, exceeding diffusion-based generation. The framework unifies transient flow prediction, transport-aware screening, and LBM-verified inverse design for porous media.

</details>


### [181] [Toward a First-Principles Update Geometry for the Language-Model Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22253)
*Aditya Somasundaram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将语言模型的输出头和 Softmax 作为一个整体模块研究，从 Hilbert 投影距离出发分析更新几何，发现更新幅度由 token 行向量的欧氏直径决定，进而提出在约束直径的前提下最大化最小行间距，形成近似等距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常孤立地分析输出头的权重矩阵，忽略了与 Softmax 组合后的几何特性；受 Muon 奇异值调理启发，希望从组合模块的更新几何角度改进训练动态。

Method: 1. 将输出头与 Softmax 视为单一模块，在 Hilbert 投影距离下推导更新几何；2. 证明当隐藏状态范数受限时，最大输出变化量等于 H 乘以 token 行向量的欧氏直径；3. 提出在约束直径的同时最大化最小行间距，形成近似等距优化问题（当词表大小 V 远大于隐藏维度 d 时）。

Result: 理论上确立了更新幅度与 token 行向量直径的正比关系，提出的近似等距目标可显式控制更新几何，为输出头正则化提供新视角。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模输出头与 Softmax，利用 Hilbert 投影距离刻画更新几何，提出直径约束下的最大最小行间距优化，为大规模语言模型的输出层设计提供了几何指导原则。

Abstract: We study the language-model head and softmax as a single module, deriving an update geometry from their composition rather than from the weight matrix in isolation. Under Hilbert's projective distance, the maximum change caused by an update $S$ over $\left|\left|{h}\right|\right|_2\le H$ is $H\max_{i<j}\left|\left|{s_i-s_j}\right|\right|_2$, which is $H$ times the Euclidean diameter of its token rows. Motivated by Muon's singular-value conditioning, we propose maximizing the smallest row separation while constraining this diameter, producing an approximate-equidistance problem when $V\gg d$.

</details>


### [182] [DAW: Dynamics-Aware Weighting for Deep Learning Forecasts of Chaotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22277)
*Zhou Fang,Gianmarco Mengaldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamics-Aware Weighting (DAW)框架，利用动力系统理论中的局部维度d作为先验度量，重塑损失景观以提升混沌时空系统（如Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程）长期自回归预测精度，显著优于均匀训练、统计密度加权及消融基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理模型在混沌动力系统长期自回归预测中遭受灾难性误差累积；混沌系统相空间访问极不均匀，以低维静止态为主、高维拓扑跃迁为辅，均匀损失导致模型容量偏向统计占优的静止态，忽视触发局部巨大误差的瞬态高维区域；现有不平衡回归仅按目标空间统计密度加权，未捕捉吸引子几何结构导致的内在动力学稀疏性。

Method: 引入动力学感知加权（DAW），基于动力学系统理论中的局部维度d（表征状态活跃自由度）作为先验，重塑损失函数权重，将模型表示容量显式分配至稀疏高维度区域；在Kuramoto-Sivashinsky方程上对比均匀训练、统计密度加权及随机打乱消融实验。

Result: DAW在KS方程上持续降低长期自回归误差，优于所有基线；事件级分析表明DAW通过抑制局部维度d剧烈跳变（如波合并等复杂物理过程）时的局部误差放大来实现性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于动力学内在几何（局部维度）的损失重加权能有效缓解混沌系统预测中的灾难性误差累积，为数据中心的代理建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates for forecasting chaotic dynamical systems suffer from catastrophic error accumulation over long-term autoregressive rollouts. This behavior is partly tied to the underlying systems: chaotic spatiotemporal systems, such as the Kuramoto-Sivashinsky (KS) equation, visit phase space unevenly - dominated by recurrent, low-dimensional quiescent states (e.g., near-laminar flows) and punctuated by rare, dynamically complex topological transitions (e.g., wave-merging events). Under a sample-wise uniform objective, standard neural surrogates allocate their finite capacity to the statistically numerous quiescent states, under-representing the transient regimes that trigger disproportionate, localized errors. Existing imbalanced-regression methods reweight samples by target-space density. However, statistical target-space rarity need not coincide with the intrinsic dynamical rarity - the recurrence geometry of the attractor that is the source of the imbalance. To address this, we introduce Dynamics-Aware Weighting (DAW), a data-centric objective reweighting framework. Using the local dimension $d$ from dynamical systems theory as an a priori measure of a state's active degrees of freedom, DAW reshapes the loss landscape to allocate representational capacity toward the sparse, high-$d$ regimes where forecast errors are systematically large. On the chaotic KS equation, DAW consistently outperforms uniform training, purely statistical density weighting, and its randomly permuted ablation, reducing long-term autoregressive error relative to all baselines. Event-level analysis shows that DAW achieves this by suppressing the localized error amplifications incurred during sharp jumps in $d$, which accompany complex physical processes such as wave-merging in the KS system.

</details>


### [183] [StocBench: A Benchmark for Generative Modeling of Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22309)
*Sebastian Pfister,Benjamin Holzschuh,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了基于传输的生成模型（如流匹配）和蒸馏式少步方法（如DPM-2、DDIM、DDPM）在二维科尔莫哥洛夫随机强迫流概率预测中的表现，重点关注有限推理预算下的性能。结果显示：流匹配在高推理预算下单步分布最准确，DPM-2在极低NFE下最强；蒸馏方法在随机任务上有竞争力且能很好保持涡量谱，但在确定性控制任务上表现最差。随机扩散采样器（DDPM）在随机设定下更好地保持涡量谱，而确定性采样器（DDIM、DPM-2）在确定性设定下更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在系统评估不同生成模型在随机流体流概率预测中的表现，特别是在有限推理预算（低NFE）下的性能，并通过确定性控制任务分离偶然不确定性与认知不确定性，为实际部署提供模型选择指导。

Method: 在二维科尔莫哥洛夫随机强迫流上基准测试传输基生成模型（流匹配）和蒸馏式少步扩散模型（DPM-2、DDIM、DDPM等）。采用大规模模拟参考集合评估单步条件分布准确性，通过自回归推演中的涡量谱不变测度保持情况评估长期统计特性。另设计确定性控制任务（观测预测区间内的强迫）以区分偶然与认知不确定性。

Result: 随机任务中：流匹配在高推理预算下达到最准确的单步条件分布；二阶指数积分器DPM-2在极低NFE下表现最强。蒸馏式少步方法与多步方法竞争，且在自回归推演中特别好地保持了涡量谱。确定性控制任务中：模型性能不迁移，蒸馏模型在随机任务有竞争力但在控制任务中最不准确。随机扩散采样器（DDPM）在随机设定下更好保持涡量谱，确定性采样器（DDIM、DPM-2）在确定性设定下更好。

Conclusion: 在随机流体流预测中，模型选择需根据推理预算和任务类型权衡：高预算下流匹配最优，极低预算下DPM-2最优；蒸馏方法适合随机任务且能良好保持谱特性，但不适合确定性控制任务；采样器类型（随机 vs 确定性）需与任务设定匹配以最佳保持涡量谱。

Abstract: We benchmark transport-based generative models as well as distillation-based few-step methods for the probabilistic forecasting of stochastic fluid flows, with a particular focus on performance under limited inference budgets. All methods are evaluated on a two-dimensional Kolmogorov flow with stochastic forcing. We measure one-step distributional accuracy against large simulated reference ensembles and assess whether the invariant measure is preserved during autoregressive rollouts via the enstrophy spectrum. On the stochastic task, flow matching achieves the most accurate one-step conditional distribution at high inference budgets, while the second-order exponential integrator DPM-2 is strongest at very low NFE. Few-step distillation methods are competitive with the multi-step methods and preserve the enstrophy spectrum particularly well. A deterministic control task, in which the forcing over the prediction interval is observed, separates aleatoric from epistemic uncertainty. Model performance does not translate between the two settings: the distilled models are competitive on the stochastic task but least accurate on the control task. While stochastic diffusion samplers such as DDPM better preserve the enstrophy spectrum during rollouts in the stochastic setting, deterministic samplers such as DDIM and DPM-2 show better spectral preservation in the deterministic setting.

</details>


### [184] [Does a Modern-Handwriting Warm-Up Help Historical Arabic OCR? A Reproducible, Compute-Matched Evaluation on Muharaf and KHATT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22316)
*Sumaih Almarshad,Maram Alamri,Dona Aloraini,Fares Altuwaim,AlJawharh AlOtaibi,Reem Alyabis,Rayah Aldawsari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过多次消融实验测试现代阿拉伯手写（KHATT）中间训练对历史手稿（Muharaf）HTR的影响，发现效果极不稳定（-17.64 到 +14.52 CER），极值源于混杂因素；干净实验显示无显著效应。计算预算匹配实验显示 KHATT 预热比同域对照略差（+2.42 CER），手写域特有差距仅约 0.6 点。发布 SaudiHeritage-OCR 工具包供独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅凭单一实现与单次对比断言中间阶段是利是弊，证据基础太薄；需要系统评估实验稳定性，量化混杂因素影响，给出可复现的结论。

Method: 1) 同一名义消融跑四轮，仅改变基座检查点、编码器冻结策略、epoch 预算、精度、学习率调度等自然漂移项，固定归一化、评分器、区间估计。2) 每轮对比“KHATT 预训练→Muharaf 微调”与“直接在 Muharaf 微调”。3) 计算预算匹配的三种子实验，控制同域对照，隔离手写域特有效应。4) 发布完整复现包（归一化器、区间评分器、验证过的 KHATT 解码器、实验清单、VLM 基线、版本对齐协议）。

Result: 四轮消融的估计效应从 -17.64 到 +14.52 CER 且符号反转；两个极值分别对应五倍降低学习率和来源不明检查点两个可识别混杂因素；两轮干净实验落在 -0.25 和 +0.94 CER，基本无效应。计算匹配实验：KHATT 预热比同域对照差 +2.42 CER（95% CI [+0.60, +4.25]），其中手写域特有差距仅约 0.6 点，属小负效应，非普适结论。

Conclusion: 单一实现给出的紧区间对下一次实现无参考价值；中间阶段现代手写训练在历史阿拉伯 HTR 上无稳健正向收益，甚至在受控预算下呈微弱负向影响。结论仅适用于该配置，不具普适性。开放的 SaudiHeritage-OCR 包支持社区独立复核。

Abstract: Whether an intermediate stage of modern Arabic handwriting helps or hurts historical Arabic HTR is usually decided from one implementation and one comparison, too thin a basis for a claim either way. We test stability by running the same nominal ablation four times, letting the base checkpoint, encoder-freezing strategy, epoch budget, precision, and learning-rate schedule vary as they naturally did during development, while holding the normalization, scorer, and interval estimation fixed. Each run compares intermediate training on modern handwriting (KHATT) then fine-tuning on historical manuscripts (Muharaf) against fine-tuning on Muharaf directly. Across the four runs the estimated effect swings from -17.64 to +14.52 CER points and reverses sign. The two extremes are exactly the two runs with an identifiable confound (a fivefold lower learning rate in one; a checkpoint of undisclosed provenance in the other); the two clean runs land at -0.25 and +0.94, i.e. no effect. A tight interval from one implementation says nothing about the next. We then run a compute-matched experiment with identical budgets over three seeds: KHATT warm-up is +2.42 CER points worse than a matched same-domain control (95% interval [+0.60, +4.25]); the part of that gap specific to the handwriting domain is only about 0.6 points a small negative effect under this configuration, not a universal result. We release a SaudiHeritage-OCR package with the normalizer, interval scorer, a verified KHATT decoder, experimental manifests, VLM baselines, and an edition-alignment protocol, so the result can be checked independently. The Al-Mahd inscription line is held strictly out and is not offered as a benchmark.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Dense Adam States: Adaptive Log-Space Quantization for Memory-Efficient Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22322)
*Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应对数空间(AL)量化，针对优化器状态的异质性设计分块非负状态表示，结合符号动量编码和状态特定精度选择，在多种优化器和模型上实现显著存储压缩且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度优化器状态方法主要针对密集Adam风格矩估计设计，但内存高效优化器采用因子化、置信度或投影状态，其量化误差传播方式不同，需要针对异质优化器状态设计量化方案。

Method: 提出自适应对数空间(AL)量化，一种分块非负状态表示，能按块自适应调整非零范围并保留精确零值；结合AL8/AL16与独立符号动量编码及状态特定精度选择；并引入拓扑感知参数保护策略。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B上，AdamW使用AL8二阶矩和8位均匀动量达到72.90困惑度(FP32为72.48，8位动态量化基线73.54)，优化器状态存储从8392.7 MiB降至2119.2 MiB；CAME需更高精度：AL16达86.16困惑度(FP32 86.68)，全AL8为90.19；GPT-2 100K步实验中，拓扑感知保护将量化Adafactor后期损失差距从+0.1185降至+0.0159。

Conclusion: 实验支持状态感知和拓扑感知的优化器量化，AL量化能有效压缩优化器状态存储同时保持模型性能，不同优化器需针对性选择精度策略。

Abstract: Low-precision optimizer-state methods are commonly designed for dense Adam-style moments, but memory-efficient optimizers maintain factored, confidence-based, or projected states whose quantization errors propagate differently. We characterize this heterogeneity in optimizer-state traces from language-model pre-training and introduce Adaptive Log-Space (AL) quantization, a block-wise representation for non-negative states that adapts its nonzero range per block while preserving exact zeros. AL8 and AL16 are combined with independent signed-momentum encodings and state-specific precision choices.
  Across 96 runs totaling 214.7 GPU-hours, we evaluate AdamW, Adafactor, CAME, and APOLLO paths. On a 20K-step TinyLlama-1.1B benchmark, AdamW with AL8 second moments and 8-bit uniform momentum reaches 72.90 perplexity, versus 72.48 for FP32 and 73.54 for an 8-bit dynamic-quantization baseline, while reducing measured optimizer-state storage from 8392.7 to 2119.2 MiB. CAME requires higher precision for its non-negative states: AL16 reaches 86.16 perplexity versus 86.68 for FP32, while all-AL8 reaches 90.19. In a 100K-step GPT-2 experiment, topology-aware parameter protection reduces the late-loss gap of quantized Adafactor from +0.1185 to +0.0159. These results support state- and topology-aware optimizer quantization. End-to-end comparisons use a single training seed and are reported as empirical measurements.

</details>


### [186] [Gaussian process learning with flow map refinement for parameter estimation in dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22324)
*Yue Hao,Dongwei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG
