TL;DR: 提出基于高斯过程学习与流映射细化的两阶段参数估计框架（GPL-FMR），解决局部导数匹配导致的全局不一致问题，在稀疏噪声观测下显著提升参数估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯过程的梯度/导数匹配方法虽避免重复数值积分且强制局部导数一致性，但局部匹配可能导致与支配流映射的全局不一致，尤其在稀疏和噪声观测下表现不佳。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用高斯过程学习算法获得后验；第二阶段将该后验作为信息先验，结合基于全局动力学约束的流映射细化优化，进一步改进参数估计。

Result: 在Van der Pol振子、Lotka-Volterra模型和Lorenz-63系统上验证，结果表明该框架持续提高参数估计精度，特别是在稀疏和噪声观测条件下。

Conclusion: GPL-FMR框架通过融合局部高斯过程学习与全局流映射约束，有效解决了参数估计中的全局不一致问题，为动力学系统参数辨识提供了更鲁棒的方法。

Abstract: Parameter estimation is a central task in data-driven learning of dynamical systems. It aims to recover the underlying physical parameters from observed time-series data, thereby providing interpretable insights into the physical mechanisms governing the system. Gradient/derivative matching methods based on Gaussian process provide an efficient way to perform parameter estimation. Those methods avoid repeated numerical integration and enforce local derivative consistency. However, such local matching may result in global inconsistency with the governing flow map, particularly under scarce and noisy observations. To address this limitation, we propose a framework based on Gaussian process learning with flow map refinement (GPL-FMR), a two-stage parameter estimation framework. The first stage is based on Gaussian process learning algorithm and the posterior obtained from which is transferred as an informative prior to the second stage based on flow-map refinement. The second stage further improves the parameter estimation via optimisation based on global dynamical constraints. We demonstrate and analyse its performance on multiple numerical examples, including the Van der Pol oscillator, the Lotka-Volterra model, and the Lorenz-63 system. The results show that the proposed framework consistently improves parameter estimation accuracy, particularly under scarce and noisy observations.

</details>


### [187] [SANE: State Anomaly Neutralization for Stable Extreme-Context Delta-Rule Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22354)
*Qingwen Lin,Boyan Xu,Xiao Liu,Zhifeng Hao,Ruichu Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究Delta-Rule循环模型在极长上下文下的数值不稳定问题，发现RWKV-7在1亿token序列中出现局部范数爆炸而非全局状态饱和，提出State Anomaly Neutralization (SANE)方法，在分块边界应用自适应tanh压缩，在安全阈值范围内保持推理能力，解决数值溢出同时揭示容量-稳定性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delta-Rule循环模型具有O(1)推理内存优势，但在极端上下文外推下可能变得不稳定。现有研究缺乏对长序列数值失效模式的系统分析，需要识别具体失效机制并设计既能数值稳定又能保持推理能力的压缩策略。

Method: 通过追踪RWKV-7在高达1亿token序列上的状态演化，实证识别出'局部范数爆炸覆盖稀疏基底'的失效模式；分析循环更新机制，发现持久衰减使弱更新项保持微小，而不均匀注入导致少数通道累积极端值。基于此诊断，提出SANE：在分块边界应用自适应tanh压缩（阈值参数α），保留分块内并行结构，压缩幅度由α控制。

Result: 在安全阈值范围（3 ≤ α ≤ 5）下，SANE在11个短上下文推理基准上与基线无统计学显著差异。在1亿token前缀（超训练长度24,000倍）后，SANE保持功能性推理（33.46-35.56分），而基线遭遇数值溢出。过度宽松阈值（α ≥ 8）虽数值稳定但完全丧失推理能力，证明单纯数值稳定不保证功能推理，揭示状态压缩中的容量-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: SANE通过自适应tanh压缩有效解决Delta-Rule模型的极长上下文数值不稳定问题，实现了数值稳定与推理能力的平衡。研究揭示了局部范数爆炸的具体失效机制，并证明了状态压缩存在容量-稳定性权衡：过度压缩虽稳定但损害推理，适度压缩可同时实现稳定与功能保持。

Abstract: Delta-Rule recurrent models maintain a fixed-size state, enabling $O(1)$ inference memory but potentially becoming unstable under extreme-context extrapolation. By tracking RWKV-7 over sequences of up to 100M tokens, we empirically identify a distinct failure pattern: \textbf{localized norm explosion atop a relatively sparse substrate}, rather than global state saturation. Analysis of the recurrent update suggests that persistent decay keeps weakly updated entries small, whereas uneven injections allow a few channels to accumulate extreme values. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose \textbf{State Anomaly Neutralization (SANE)}, which applies adaptive $\tanh$ compression at chunk boundaries while preserving the intra-chunk parallel structure. Within a safe threshold range ($3 \le α\le 5$), SANE matches the baseline on 11 short-context reasoning benchmarks with no statistically significant degradation. After a 100M-token prefix, which exceeds the training length by over $24{,}000\times$, SANE retains functional reasoning ($33.46$--$35.56$) while the baseline encounters numerical overflow. In contrast, overly permissive thresholds ($α\ge 8$) remain numerically stable but lose reasoning capability entirely, showing that numerical stabilization alone does not guarantee functional reasoning and revealing a capacity--stability trade-off in state compression.

</details>


### [188] [Tracing the Unlabeled Storm: Cross-Variable Transfer in a Lagrangian Atmospheric JEPA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22358)
*K M Anirudh,S Sandeep,Hariprasad Kodamana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M-JEPA（多尺度季风联合嵌入预测架构），通过在五个连续代理场（如OLR）上利用拉格朗日切片预训练，无需降水监督即可学习对流组织的潜在世界模型。冻结骨干网络迁移至逐日降水预报，在重雨阈值和精细空间尺度上超越运行级ECMWF集合预报（CRPS 6.81 vs 6.89 mm/day，Brier技巧 +0.05 vs -0.04），仅用15.4M参数单消费级GPU。消融实验证实代理预训练比直接降水训练降低36% CRPS误差（5.54 vs 7.52 mm/day），为季风降水预报提供了基于季节内振荡动力学的竞争性模型及表征迁移诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层大气对流主导南亚季风变率，但直接从零膨胀、重尾分布的降水数据学习潜在世界模型效果不佳。连续大气代理变量（如出射长波辐射OLR）能更连贯地表达对流组织，因此需要跨变量代理学习来弥合这一错配。

Method: 提出M-JEPA：多尺度季风联合嵌入预测架构。在五个连续代理场上，利用追踪移动对流系统的拉格朗日切片进行自监督预训练，全程无降水监督。预训练后冻结骨干，通过共享解码干（含概率与确定双分支）迁移至逐日降水预报。采用冻结骨干探针框架，设置两个对照：仅用降水训练的同架构模型、随机初始化骨干。

Result: 1) 代理预训练显著优于直接降水训练：CRPS误差降低36%（5.54 vs 7.52 mm/day）。2) 迁移模型在重雨阈值和精细空间尺度上超越51成员ECMWF运行集合：CRPS 6.81 vs 6.89 mm/day（统计显著），Brier技巧 +0.05 vs -0.04。模型仅15.4M参数，单消费级GPU运行。集合预报在邻域技巧和点尺度确定性指标上仍占优。

Conclusion: 跨变量代理学习（M-JEPA）能从连续代理场中提取对流组织的预测性信息，实现无监督降水预报迁移。该方法提供了基于季节内动力学的竞争性季风降水预报模型，并建立了评估大气表征迁移的诊断框架，展示了自监督预训练在气象预报中的潜力。

Abstract: Deep atmospheric convection governs South Asian monsoon variability, yet attempting to learn its latent world model directly from zero-inflated, heavy-tailed precipitation yields suboptimal predictive representations. Continuous atmospheric proxies, such as outgoing longwave radiation (OLR), express this convective organization far more coherently. We address this mismatch with \emph{cross-variable proxy learning}: M-JEPA, a multiscale Monsoon Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture, is pretrained on five continuous proxy fields over Lagrangian patches tracking moving convective systems---without rainfall supervision at any point. The resulting frozen representation is transferred to daily precipitation forecasts through a shared decoder trunk featuring parallel probabilistic and deterministic branches. Because rainfall is strictly unobserved during pretraining, downstream skill directly measures the predictive information captured in the latent rollout. A frozen-backbone probing framework with two controls (an identical architecture trained on rainfall alone, and a randomly initialized backbone) attributes the transfer specifically to proxy pretraining: direct rainfall training exhibits $36\%$ higher CRPS error ($7.52$ vs.\ $5.54$\,mm/day). Against the 51-member operational ECMWF ensemble, the transferred model attains a statistically resolved CRPS advantage ($6.81$ vs.\ $6.89$\,mm/day) and higher Brier skill ($+0.05$ vs.\ $-0.04$) using $15.4$M parameters on a single consumer GPU, concentrated at heavy-rain thresholds and fine spatial scales, while the ensemble retains an advantage in neighborhood skill and deterministic references on point metrics. The result provides a competitive monsoon precipitation forecast grounded in intraseasonal dynamics and a diagnostic framework for evaluating transferred atmospheric representations.

</details>


### [189] [Self-Supervised Graph Representation Learning for In-The-Wild Wearable and Smartphone based Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22387)
*Ioannis N. Ziogas,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Aamna Al Shehhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自监督学习图掩码增强的少样本可穿戴情感识别方法，在K-EmoPhone数据集上仅使用20%-25%的标签即超越全资源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对可穿戴设备情感识别中野外标签收集困难、主体间/内情感变异性大、标注资源有限的挑战，探索基于图节点分类的少资源学习方案。

Method: 采用子图采样训练，结合有标签和无标签数据，在多任务归纳式图神经网络架构中融合监督、半监督和自监督学习机制，利用SSL图掩码增强任务进行联合训练。

Result: 在K-EmoPhone数据集留一组交叉验证中，二分唤醒和效价任务分别仅使用20%和25%标签，平均准确率较全资源设定提升4.3%和7.8%。

Conclusion: SSL驱动的子图训练有效缓解野外可穿戴情感识别的标签稀缺问题，SSL图增强与情感唤醒/效价维度存在内在关联，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Wearable and smartphone-based emotion recognition (WER) remains a challenging setting in affective computing, due to the notorious difficulty and bias associated with in-the-wild label collection. The high inter-and intra-subject emotional variability motivates us to explore WER modeling through graph node classification in a limited resources learning scheme powered by Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) graph masking augmentation tasks. We employ a subgraph sampling approach during training, utilizing labeled and unlabeled data, along with supervised, semi-supervised, and SSL mechanisms in a multi-task inductive graph neural network architecture. Our evaluations on K-EmoPhone through leave-one-group-out cross-validation in the binary arousal and valence tasks yield average accuracy gains of 4.3% and 7.8%, compared to the full resource setting, utilizing only 20% and 25% of the labels, respectively. Our model analysis sheds light on the relation of SSL graph augmentations to emotional arousal and valence and justifies the approach of SSL-driven subgraph training for in-the-wild WER.

</details>


### [190] [Dual-Scale State-Space Modeling with Speaker-Wise Dynamic CRF for Speech Emotion Recognition in Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22399)
*Guan-Hua Wen,Kuan-Yu Chen,Hou-Chiang Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DSSM-CRF 模型，用于会话语音情感识别，显式分离跨说话人上下文影响与说话人内部情感演变两大交互过程，在 IEMOCAP 和 MELD 数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会话语音情感识别需协调不同时间尺度的声学证据，同时处理两种交互过程：跨说话人上下文影响和说话人内部情感演变。现有方法难以同时建模这两者，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出 DSSM-CRF 音频端到端架构：双向状态空间模型在帧级和对话级编码融合的自监督语音表示，使每个话语表示捕捉局部韵律和所有说话人的上下文；解码器将每个说话人的话语排序为独立的动态条件随机场链，相邻话语形成转移对，其得分结合语料库级转移矩阵与两个上下文化话语预测的残差；辅助目标监督情感是否变化但不参与 Viterbi 推理，从而让对话轮次影响上下文情感得分而不被视为另一说话人情感轨迹的转移。

Result: 在 IEMOCAP 上达到 75.81% UA 和 74.90% WA；在 MELD 上达到 54.72% WA 和 49.31% WF1。对照实验表明说话人级因子分解与 CRF 建模带来互补增益。

Conclusion: DSSM-CRF 通过显式分离跨说话人上下文与说话人内部情感演变，有效建模会话语音情感动态，在多个基准数据集上取得最优或接近最优性能，验证了说话人级因子分解与 CRF 序列建模的互补优势。

Abstract: Conversational speech emotion recognition must reconcile acoustic evidence across temporal scales with two interaction processes: cross-speaker contextual influence and within-speaker emotion evolution. We propose DSSM-CRF, an audio-only architecture that explicitly separates these processes. Bidirectional state-space models encode fused self-supervised speech representations at frame and dialogue scales, so each utterance representation captures local prosody and context from all speakers. The decoder then orders each speaker's utterances into an independent dynamic conditional random field chain. Consecutive utterances in a speaker's chain form a transition pair whose score combines a corpus-level transition matrix with a residual predicted from the two contextualized utterances. An auxiliary objective supervises whether each pair changes emotion but does not participate in Viterbi inference. Thus, interlocutor turns affect contextual emotion scores without being treated as transitions in another speaker's emotion trajectory. DSSM-CRF achieves 75.81% UA and 74.90% WA on IEMOCAP, and 54.72% WA and 49.31% WF1 on MELD. Matched controls demonstrate complementary gains from speaker-wise factorization and CRF modeling.

</details>


### [191] [Geometric Structures on Graphs: a Holonomy-Based Discretization of Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22453)
*Hao Li,Yuhan Peng,Junwen Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于全纯度的框架，在配备局部对称正定度量的图上离散化曲率。利用顶点纤维度量和有向边的可逆度量兼容传输，通过有向三角回路的全纯度对数作为有限回路曲率观测，建立全纯度与曲率的几何对应。引入两种聚合机制生成保正定的度量响应，并给出边传输的可逆度量兼容参数化，实现几何感知的可学习模型。单位球基准实验验证了全纯度-曲率关系、曲率保持及边传输的经验恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络常将全纯度作为启发式特征，缺乏几何严谨性。本文旨在建立离散图上全纯度与曲率的严格几何对应，实现保正定的度量学习，并保证局部正交规范等变性，为几何深度学习提供理论基础。

Method: 1. 定义顶点纤维度量 g_i 和有向边可逆度量兼容传输 F_ij。2. 有向三角回路的有序乘积得到全纯度 H_C，归一化对数 Ω_C = -s_C^{-1} Log(H_C) 作为有限回路曲率观测。3. 两种聚合机制：(a) 与对称响应矩阵的对易子产生对称 Ricci 型度量响应；(b) 关注关联的曲率诱导边通量协方差散度，反映迹与协方差散度关系。4. 响应局部正交规范等变，驱动指数更新保正定。5. 给出边传输的可逆度量兼容参数化，支持正交边因子、回路尺度、权重、响应矩阵的几何感知学习。

Result: 单位球已知几何校准实验表明：(1) 全纯度-曲率关系在离散图上成立；(2) 非平凡局部度量表示下曲率得以保持；(3) 从局部观测可经验性恢复边传输。方法实现了几何一致的曲率离散化和保正定度量学习。

Conclusion: 本工作为图上曲率离散化提供了严格的全纯度几何框架，实现了局部正交规范等变的保正定度量学习，并通过可逆度量兼容参数化支持端到端几何感知学习。实验验证了理论构造的有效性，为几何深度学习奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose a holonomy-based framework for discretizing curvature on graphs equipped with local symmetric positive-definite metrics. Each vertex carries a fibre metric \(g_i\), and each directed edge carries a reversible metric-compatible transport \(F_{ij}\). The ordered product around an oriented triangular loop \(\mathcal C\) gives a holonomy \(H_{\mathcal C}\), whose normalized logarithm \(Ω_{\mathcal C}=-s_{\mathcal C}^{-1}\operatorname{Log}(H_{\mathcal C})\) is used as a finite-loop curvature observation. Thus the construction discretizes the geometric principle that infinitesimal holonomy is controlled by curvature, rather than treating holonomy as a heuristic feature. Since \(Ω_{\mathcal C}\) lies in the \(g_i\)-orthogonal Lie algebra, it is not itself a velocity of an SPD metric. We therefore introduce two aggregation mechanisms: a commutator with a symmetric response matrix, producing symmetric Ricci-type metric responses, and an incidence-aware covariant divergence of curvature-induced edge fluxes, reflecting the relation between trace and covariant divergence. The resulting responses are locally orthogonal-gauge equivariant and can drive exponential updates that preserve positive definiteness. We also give a reversible metric-compatible parametrization of edge transports, allowing orthogonal edge factors, loop scales, weights, and response matrices to be learned while respecting the graph geometry. Known-geometry calibrations on the unit sphere test the holonomy--curvature relation, curvature preservation under nontrivial local metric representations, and the empirical recovery of edge transports from local observations.

</details>


### [192] [KPI-Conditioned Generative Design of Automotive Hood Inner Panels: A Two-Stage Retrieval-Generation Pipeline with Surrogate-Based Performance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22457)
*Sudeep Chavare*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段管道，用于解决工业零件（以发动机盖内衬板为例）的逆向设计问题：根据性能要求（挠度、应力、质量）生成几何形状。该管道首先通过可达性阶段筛选可行的拓扑族，再利用条件变分自编码器生成点云几何，并配合神经算子代理模型评估性能。系统基于公开数据与免费算力构建，并部署为交互式工具。主要发现指出：代理模型在整体上准确，但其误差与需区分的类内性能差异相当，这限制了对单个生成设计的性能声称；代理误差与类内信号之比被认为是此类管道能否奏效的关键决定量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习代理模型已使正向映射（几何到性能）变得快速且常规化，但反向映射（从性能要求生成几何）在工业零件中仍 largely unaddressed。工业零件的设计空间通常组织为离散的拓扑族，而非连续参数化，这使得传统的连续优化方法难以直接应用。因此需要一种能够处理离散拓扑选择并生成具体几何的逆向设计流程。

Method: 提出两阶段管道：1) 可达性阶段：给定需求向量（挠度、应力、质量目标），判断哪些拓扑族有可能满足要求；2) 几何生成与评估阶段：在选定拓扑族内，利用条件变分自编码器生成点云几何，再通过神经算子代理模型快速预测其性能。整个管道基于公开数据集与免费算力训练，并部署为交互式工具供工程师使用。

Result: 管道在整体上可行：代理模型在聚合层面准确，能指导拓扑族选择与几何生成。但定量分析显示，代理模型的预测误差与同一拓扑族内不同设计的性能差异（类内信号）量级相当。这意味着：对于单个生成设计，无法基于代理预测做出高置信度的性能声称；代理误差与类内信号之比是决定此类“生成-评估”管道能否真正工作的核心指标。

Conclusion: 该工作为离散拓扑空间下的逆向设计提供了可落地的端到端管道，并公开部署为交互工具。核心贡献在于揭示了代理模型误差与类内性能信号之比这一关键度量：只有当该比率足够小（即代理能可靠区分候选设计）时，生成式逆向设计才能从“整体可行”走向“单个设计可信”。未来工作需致力于降低代理误差或增大类内信号（如引入更精细的拓扑族划分、高保真数据、主动学习等），以跨越这一可信阈值。

Abstract: An inner hood panel must meet a deflection target, stay below a stress limit, and hit a mass target. Machine-learned surrogates have made the forward direction, geometry to performance, fast and routine. The inverse direction, producing geometry from a stated requirement, remains largely unaddressed for industrial parts whose design space is organized into discrete topology families rather than a continuous parameterization. This work presents a two-stage pipeline for that inverse problem. A reachability stage determines which topology families can satisfy a given requirement vector. A conditional variational autoencoder then generates point-cloud geometry within a selected family, and a neural-operator surrogate estimates the performance of each candidate. The pipeline is built entirely from public data and freely available compute, and is deployed as an interactive tool. The pipeline works, with qualifications that are reported as primary findings rather than caveats. The surrogate is accurate in aggregate, but its error is comparable to the performance differences it is asked to discriminate, which bounds what can be claimed for any individual generated design. That ratio of surrogate error to within-class signal is argued to be the quantity that determines whether a pipeline of this kind can work at all.

</details>


### [193] [MASH-Bench: Diagnosing Cross-Source Failure in Mass-Shooting Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22460)
*Neha Sharma,Ritesh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 MASH-Bench 基准，整合四个美国大规模枪击事件数据库，评估跨数据源风险分类的泛化能力，发现特征完整性和标签分布差异是主要瓶颈，而非分类器选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模枪击事件数据库在覆盖范围、特征可用性和报告实践上差异巨大，导致机器学习模型难以跨数据源泛化，亟需统一基准以诊断跨源泛化失效的根因。

Method: 构建包含 6,968 起事件的 MASH-Bench 基准，采用留一数据集出 (LODO) 评估，对比随机森林、XGBoost、LightGBM 等分类器；通过特征掩码消融实验移除 GVA 缺失的五个特征；评估 DANN、CORAL、重要性加权三种领域自适应方法及 Oracle 先验偏移重校准；进行按群体审计分析媒体归因心理健康标签的差异。

Result: 在精心策划的数据源上，模型 VeryHigh-risk 召回率达 0.68-0.89，但在 GVA 上平均召回率降至 0.20，精确率仅 0.0004。特征掩码消融导致召回率崩塌至零，证明特征完整性是跨源失效主因。DANN 将 GVA 上 VeryHigh-risk 召回率提升 0.282 (95% CI [0.11, 0.47], p=0.003)，但精确率仍低；CORAL 和重要性加权召回率为零；Oracle 先验偏移重校准无法恢复预测。群体审计揭示媒体归因心理健康标签存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 在 MASH-Bench 中，跨源泛化受限于特征完整性和标签流行率，而非分类器选择；该基准为诊断跨源风险分类中的这些效应提供了受控环境。

Abstract: Public mass-shooting databases differ substantially in coverage, feature availability, and reporting practices, creating challenges for machine-learning models that must generalize across data sources. We introduce MASH-Bench, a harmonized benchmark of 6,968 incidents from four U.S. databases: Kaggle, Mother Jones, Stanford MSA, and the Gun Violence Archive (GVA). We evaluate cross-source risk classification using leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) evaluation. Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM achieve VeryHigh-risk recall of 0.68-0.89 on the curated sources but generalize poorly to GVA, where mean recall drops to 0.20 and precision to 0.0004. To investigate the source of this degradation, we conduct a controlled feature-masking ablation that removes the five features unavailable in GVA from the curated sources. The resulting recall collapse to zero provides evidence that feature completeness is a major contributor to the observed cross-source failure. We further evaluate three domain-adaptation approaches: DANN, CORAL, and importance weighting. DANN improves VeryHigh-risk recall on GVA by 0.282 (95% CI [0.11, 0.47], p = 0.003), although precision remains low, whereas CORAL and importance weighting yield zero recall. Oracle prior-shift recalibration likewise fails to recover VeryHigh-risk predictions, indicating that label-side correction alone is insufficient under the observed feature deficiencies. A per-group audit further identifies substantial disparities associated with media-attributed mental-health labels. Overall, these results indicate that, in MASH-Bench, cross-source generalization is constrained more by feature completeness and label prevalence than by classifier choice. The benchmark provides a controlled setting for diagnosing these effects in cross-source risk classification.

</details>


### [194] [Functional compatibility as a determinant of persistent neural learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22462)
*Hossein Javidnia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出功能兼容性作为决定神经网络新学习是否持久的关键因素，通过实验操纵兼容性发现持久学习随兼容性增加，跨架构、模态均成立，重新定义了稳定性-可塑性困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在获取新能力时往往损害现有功能，但决定新学习能否持久的机制尚不清楚，需要找到可实验操纵的因果决定因素。

Method: 从相同神经状态出发，操纵功能兼容性（新学习与必须保留行为的共存程度），同时匹配无约束学习机会并施加统一保持要求，跨独立方向、卷积与Transformer架构、视觉与文本任务进行十种子复现。

Result: 持久学习随功能兼容性单调增加，跨架构、模态、独立方向均一致；学习规则和保持约束决定兼容机会的保留量，非线性几何在大更新范数下限制匹配干预。

Conclusion: 功能兼容性将稳定性-可塑性问题从防止遗忘重构为确定哪些新学习能与现有功能共存并持久化，为持续学习提供新理论框架。

Abstract: Neural networks can acquire new capabilities while damaging existing ones, but what determines whether new learning persists remains unclear. We identify functional compatibility, the extent to which incoming learning can coexist with behaviour that must be preserved, as an experimentally manipulable causal determinant of persistence. From identical neural states, we vary compatibility while matching unrestricted learning opportunity and imposing a common retention requirement. Persistent learning increases with compatibility across independent directions, convolutional and transformer architectures, vision and text, and a ten-seed replication. Learning rules and retention constraints determine how much compatible opportunity is retained, whereas nonlinear geometry limits the matched intervention at larger update norms. Functional compatibility therefore reframes stability-plasticity from preventing forgetting to determining which new learning can coexist with existing function and persist.

</details>


### [195] [The Variance of Thought: Policy Variance, Critical Forks, and Local Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22467)
*Yingru Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析长时程语言模型任务中的信用分配问题，引入策略方差概念，提出三个关键结果：策略方差作为发现预算、由基尼离散度上界约束、剩余时域决定估计成本并主张对数价值参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程语言模型任务（多步推理和工具使用代理）受限于信用分配难题，需从策略方差角度理解关键决策点的采样复杂度和估计成本。

Method: 通过策略方差 σ_π²(s) = Var_{a∼π}[Q_π(s,a)] 分析确定性 MDP 中的回报方差来源，识别关键分叉状态，推导采样下界、基尼离散度上界、蒙特卡洛优势估计的信噪比及自举法转换乘积为求和的理论分析。

Result: (i) 策略方差即发现预算：观测优势为 c 的动作需 Ω(c²/σ_π²(s)) 次采样，在经典两点分叉上界精确。(ii) 策略方差由基尼离散度上界：σ_π²(s) ≤ 1 - ‖π(·|s)‖₂²，可仅从 logits 无rollout判断关键性。(iii) 剩余时域决定估计成本：下游成功概率为 P 的分叉，蒙特卡洛优势估计信噪比约 √P，样本成本随 1/P 增长；自举法将生存概率乘积转为求和，需价值表示具有乘法精度，支持对数价值参数化。

Conclusion: 策略方差为长时程任务信用分配提供统一度量框架，揭示关键分叉的采样与估计基本极限，主张采用对数价值参数化以实现乘法精度的自举学习。

Abstract: Long-horizon language-model tasks --- multi-step reasoning and tool-using agents alike --- are limited by credit assignment. We analyze it through the policy variance $σ_π^2(s)=\operatorname{Var}_{a\simπ}[Q_π(s,a)]$, which in a deterministic MDP is the sole source of return variance and is injected in discrete pulses at states we call critical forks. Three results follow. (i) Policy variance is a discovery budget: observing an action of advantage $c$ requires $Ω(c^2/σ_π^2(s))$ draws, a bound that is exact on the canonical two-point fork. (ii) Policy variance is bounded by the policy's Gini dispersion, $σ_π^2(s)\le 1-\|π(\cdot|s)\|_2^2$, a rollout-free necessary condition for criticality computable from logits alone. (iii) The remaining horizon sets the estimation cost: at a fork whose downstream success probability is $P$, the Monte Carlo advantage estimate has signal-to-noise ratio of order $\sqrt{P}$, so its sample cost scales as $1/P$ --- a cost that branched sampling shares. Bootstrapping removes it by converting a product of survival probabilities into a sum, provided the value representation is multiplicatively accurate, which argues for log-value parameterization.

</details>


### [196] [Quantum-Inspired Hybrid Neural Networks for Neural Decoding: A Controlled Ablation Study of Learnable Quantum Sidecar Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22475)
*Diana Legziel Levy,Menachem Finkelstein,Peter Chin,Eilon Vaadia,Sarel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究将参数化量子电路（PQC）作为残差侧车模块集成到ResNet-50骨干网络中，用于31类神经群体解码（想象手写分类），在严格控制条件下对比四种变体，发现骨干梯度训练的投影变体在3/4种随机种子下提升准确率（均值+0.19%），且持续降低线性CKA相似度（Δ=-0.025），表明表示结构发生真实重组；消融实验显示浅层简单架构最有效且可复现；测量引导训练持续改善表示几何且不损失准确率。所有实验基于4量子比特无噪状态向量模拟，反映近期超导硬件约束，未声称量子计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索参数化量子电路作为残差侧车模块增强经典深度神经网络在神经解码任务中的表现，验证量子模块是否能带来真实的表示结构重组而非单纯集成，同时在近期量子硬件约束（4量子比特、无噪模拟）下评估可行性与复现性。

Method: 基于ResNet-50骨干网络，集成4量子比特PQC作为残差侧车模块，处理多神经元脉冲栅格数据进行31类想象手写分类。固定数据划分、随机种子和优化器，对比四种变体：基线、冻结输入投影的量子侧车、骨干梯度训练投影的量子侧车、测量引导变体（将角度编码与电路测量结果对齐）。使用线性CKA分析表示相似度，进行九变体消融实验，所有量子模拟均为无噪状态向量模拟。

Result: 骨干梯度变体在3/4种子下提升准确率（均值+0.19%，95% CI [-1.10%, +1.48%]），4/4种子持续降低线性CKA相似度（Δ=-0.025），表明表示结构真实重组。消融实验确定简单浅层架构最有效且可复现。测量引导训练持续改善表示几何且不降低准确率。无量子计算优势声称。

Conclusion: 在近期量子硬件约束下，将PQC作为残差侧车模块集成到ResNet-50可实现表示结构的真实重组并略微提升神经解码准确度，浅层简单架构最优，测量引导训练能改善表示几何。结果为量子增强神经解码提供了可复现的实验基准，但未展示量子计算优势。

Abstract: We study parameterized quantum circuits (PQCs) integrated as residual sidecar modules within a ResNet-50 backbone for 31-class neural population decoding---imagined handwriting classification from multi-neuron spike rasters. Under strictly controlled conditions (fixed data splits, seeds, and optimizer), we compare four model variants: baseline, quantum sidecar with frozen input projection, quantum sidecar with backbone-gradient-trained projection, and a measurement-guided variant that aligns angle encodings with circuit measurement outcomes. The backbone-gradient variant improves accuracy in 3/4 seeds (+0.19% mean, 95% CI [-1.10%, +1.48%]) and consistently reduces Linear CKA similarity to baseline features ($Δ=-0.025$, 4/4 seeds), indicating genuine structural reorganization of representations. A nine-variant ablation identifies simple shallow architectures as the most effective and reproducible configuration. Measurement-guided training consistently improves representation geometry without reducing accuracy. All results use noiseless statevector simulation on 4 qubits, a regime chosen to reflect the practical constraints of current near-term superconducting hardware; no quantum computational advantage over classical methods is claimed.

</details>


### [197] [From Symmetry to Invariance: Learning Galois Equivalent Representations in Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22513)
*Zheng Zhang,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在有限域乘法任务中，跨数学等价表示（基变换）的泛化能力。利用伽罗瓦作用将基表示组织为轨道，同一轨道内的基诱导相同的坐标乘法映射。主要方法是训练模型预测基表示间的伽罗瓦作用，通过重复应用学习到的变换构建每个轨道的规范代表，实现对未见基的精确规范匹配，从而将学习到的代数对称性转化为可迁移的不变表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络能从有限样本学习代数运算，但不清楚这种能力能否迁移到同一运算的数学等价表示（如有限域乘法的不同基表示）上。需要一种机制将学习到的代数对称性转化为不变表示以实现跨表示迁移。

Method: 1. 利用伽罗瓦作用将基表示划分为轨道，同一轨道内基共享相同坐标乘法映射。2. 训练模型预测基表示间的伽罗瓦作用（变换矩阵）。3. 重复应用学习到的变换，将任意基映射到轨道的规范代表。4. 在规范代表上执行乘法，再通过逆变换映射回目标基，实现对未训练基的精确迁移。

Result: 该方法成功实现了从训练基到未见基的乘法迁移，通过规范匹配机制将学习到的伽罗瓦对称性转化为不变表示，验证了神经网络可通过学习代数对称性实现跨等价表示的泛化。

Conclusion: 神经网络能够学习代数对称性（伽罗瓦作用）并将其转化为不变表示（规范代表），从而实现对数学等价表示的系统性迁移。这为神经网络处理抽象代数结构提供了具体机制，展示了结合代数结构的表示学习在泛化中的潜力。

Abstract: Neural networks can learn algebraic operations from finite examples, but it remains unclear whether this ability transfers across mathematically equivalent representations of the same operation. We study this question through multiplication in finite fields under changes of basis. The Galois action organizes basis representations into orbits, and bases in the same orbit induce the same coordinate multiplication map. This structure allows us to separate learning multiplication from transferring it to basis representations that are not used for training. We examine several ways of providing or recovering the relevant orbit structure, including invariant labels, basis matrices, orbit recognition, and algebraic decomposition. Our main approach trains a model to predict the Galois action between basis representations. Repeated applications of the learned transformation are then used to construct a canonical representative for each orbit, which supports multiplication on held-out bases through exact canonical matching. This provides a concrete mechanism for converting a learned algebraic symmetry into an invariant representation that can be used for transfer.

</details>


### [198] [From Detrimental to Beneficial: Dynamic Influence-based Valuation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22522)
*Adrian Nyakairu,Hongfu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIVE框架通过动态批量级样本价值估计和梯度方向反转，将有害样本转化为有益贡献，提升分类性能、数据效率及优化稳定性，并泛化至大语言模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据估值方法主要将训练样本分为有益或有害，但后续数据干预手段有限，通常丢弃或降权有害样本，导致数据资源利用不足。需要一种能将有害样本转化为有益贡献的方法。

Method: 提出DIVE框架，在批量级动态估计样本价值，不修改原始数据，而是在优化层面通过策略性反转有害样本的梯度方向，使其成为有益贡献，且能无缝集成到标准训练流程，开销极小。

Result: 大量实验表明DIVE持续提升分类性能，最大化数据效率，稳定优化过程，并有效泛化至大语言模型微调任务。

Conclusion: DIVE通过梯度反转机制实现了有害数据的利用，解决了数据估值与干预的脱节问题，为数据中心学习提供了高效、通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Data valuation is a cornerstone of data-centric learning, where prior efforts primarily focus on designing algorithms to classify training samples as either beneficial or detrimental for the learning task. However, leveraging these valuation estimates for subsequent data intervention remains underexplored; conventional approaches typically discard or downweight harmful samples, thereby underutilizing available data resources. In this paper, we present Dynamic Influence-based Valuation and Editing (DIVE), a novel and efficient framework that dynamically estimates sample values at the batch level and transforms detrimental data into beneficial contributions. Rather than altering the raw data, DIVE operates at the optimization level by strategically reversing the gradient directions of harmful samples during training, ensuring seamless integration with standard learning procedures with minimal overhead. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that DIVE consistently improves classification performance, maximizes data efficiency, stabilizes optimization, and effectively generalizes to large language model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [199] [Stress Testing Unlearning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22527)
*Noam Diamant,Ethan Fetaya,Neta Glazer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 WMDP++，扩展 WMDP 基准，解决现有遗忘基准缺乏强制提取测试和边界问题评估的不足，提供更严格的 LLM 遗忘评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器遗忘在大语言模型中因输入输出模糊而具有挑战性，需要严格评估安全性与实用性。现有基准未主动测试遗忘信息是否仍可被强制提取，也未评估边界问题（语义接近遗忘内容的良性查询）上的性能保持。

Method: 构建 WMDP++，在 WMDP 基础上增加针对性遗忘信息提取测试和系统性边界问题评估。

Result: WMDP++ 提供了更严格且信息量更大的遗忘评估基准，能更全面衡量遗忘方法的有效性和模型实用性。

Conclusion: WMDP++ 填补了现有遗忘基准的关键空白，推动大语言模型遗忘方法的评估与进步。

Abstract: Recently, machine unlearning, the removal of specific training data influence from a model, has gained increasing attention. In large language models (LLMs), unlearning is particularly challenging due to the ambiguity of inputs and outputs. Con- sequently, rigorous evaluation is critical for assessing both safety and utility, and for driving progress in unlearning meth- ods. We identify two key shortcomings in existing unlearning benchmarks: (1) they do not actively test whether unlearned information can still be forcibly extracted, and (2) they fail to evaluate performance preservation on boundary questions, be- nign queries that are semantically close to the unlearned con- tent. Here we introduce WMDP++, an extension of WMDP that addresses these gaps by incorporating targeted extrac- tion of unlearned information and systematic evaluation on boundary questions. WMDP++ provides a more stringent and informative benchmark for evaluating unlearning in LLMs.

</details>


### [200] [BLADE: Bilevel Low-rank Augmented-Lagrangian Erasure for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22557)
*Md Toufikuzzaman,Ahmad Mousavi,Dongwon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 BLADE，一种约束双层优化框架，用于大语言模型遗忘任务，通过三大机制实现平滑、可预测的优化控制，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，并展现出强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 遗忘方法存在鲁棒性不足：无界遗忘损失破坏模型连贯性、固定权重平衡无法适应训练中保留任务难度变化、在扩展或重复应用时失效。

Method: BLADE 包含三大机制：(1) 钳制熵遗忘损失，令 token 达到足够不确定性时梯度恰为零；(2) 非对称增广拉格朗日，在保留约束被违反后永久提升保护力度；(3) 限制在 LoRA 适配器上的双层结构，在每次遗忘步骤前修复保留性能损伤。

Result: 在三大基准族上全面领先：TOFU 平均综合分提升 6%，MUSE Books 提升 9%，KnowUndo 提升 7%；在 MUSE News 上经受 4× 规模扩展与 4 次顺序遗忘仍保持稳定，而最强竞品完全崩溃。

Conclusion: BLADE 通过约束双层优化与针对性损失设计，解决了遗忘鲁棒性难题，实现了可控、可扩展、可重复的 LLM 遗忘，为实际部署提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Existing LLM unlearning methods struggle with robustness: unbounded forget losses degrade model coherence, fixed-weight balancing cannot adapt as retain difficulty shifts mid-training, and methods that work on one benchmark falter under scaling or repeated application. We propose BLADE, a constrained bilevel framework whose three mechanisms give smooth, predictable control over the optimization landscape: a clamped-entropy forget loss whose gradient is exactly zero once a token reaches sufficient uncertainty; an asymmetric augmented Lagrangian that permanently ratchets retain protection after any violation; and a bilevel structure confined to LoRA adapters that repairs retain damage before each forgetting step. BLADE dominates across three benchmark families, improving average composite scores over the strongest baselines by $6$% on TOFU, $9$% on MUSE Books, and $7$% on KnowUndo, and it remains stable under $4\times$ scaling and $4$ sequential unlearning steps on MUSE News where the best competing method collapses entirely.

</details>


### [201] [Clinical Graph-JEPA: Predictive Patient-State Knowledge Graphs for Cognitive Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22583)
*Kushagra Yadav,Nalin Prabhath,Amit Lamba,Goeun Han,Yining Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个临床知识图谱构建与精炼框架，结合多智能体关系提议、本体感知归一化、确定性证据评分和基于JEPA的潜在精炼，将知识图谱视为预测性患者状态表示。在MIMIC-IV数据上，实体接地的出院笔记注入使留一法边恢复MRR相对提升31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床记录包含丰富的患者状态证据，但将其转化为可靠的结构化知识图谱仍很困难，因为提取错误、本体不匹配、缺失关系和时间歧义会传播到下游系统。

Method: 提出临床知识图谱构建与精炼框架：多智能体关系提议、本体感知归一化、确定性证据评分、JEPA基础的潜在精炼。将知识图谱视为预测性患者状态表示。对每次入院，从结构化MIMIC-IV记录和推断的临床跨链接构建证据评分图，再学习从观察图上下文恢复保留的临床关系。使用无泄漏留一法边恢复（MRR和Hits@k）和保留批次掩码评估（AUC和MRR）。对比无笔记嵌入配置与注入真实出院笔记表示到笔记接地实体的笔记增强配置，隔离出院笔记上下文的贡献。

Result: 在相同队列和评估协议下，实体接地的笔记注入使总体留一法MRR相对提升31%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效整合多源临床证据，通过JEPA潜在精炼和笔记增强显著提升知识图谱的预测性能，为下游临床应用提供更可靠的结构化知识表示。

Abstract: Clinical records contain rich evidence about patient state, but converting that evidence into reliable, structured knowledge graphs remains difficult because extraction errors, ontology mismatch, missing relations, and temporal ambiguity can propagate into downstream systems. We propose a clinical knowledge graph construction and refinement framework that combines multi-agent relation proposal, ontology-aware normalization, deterministic evidence scoring, and JEPA-based latent refinement. Rather than treating a clinical knowledge graph as a static extraction artifact, we treat it as a predictive patient-state representation. For each admission, the system constructs an evidence-scored graph from structured MIMIC-IV records and inferred clinical cross-links, then learns to recover held-out clinical relations from the observed graph context. We evaluate the refiner with leakage-free leave-one-out edge recovery (MRR and Hits@k) and held-out batch-mask evaluation (AUC and MRR). To isolate the contribution of discharge-note context, we compare a note-embedding-free configuration with a note-augmented configuration that injects real discharge-note representations only into note-grounded entities. Under the same cohort and evaluation protocol, entity-grounded note injection improves overall leave-one-out MRR by 31% relative improvement.

</details>


### [202] [GCA: Global Centroid Alignment in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22593)
*Jong-Ik Park,Harry Jiang,Logan Blakely,Georgios Fragkos,Shamina Hossain-McKenzie,Carlee Joe-Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出全局质心对齐 (GCA)，一种基于自编码器的联邦学习协议，通过交换潜在码和全局质心而非模型参数，大幅降低通信开销（最高 99.15%）并增强隐私保护（重构攻击下误差更高、余弦相似度更低），同时在多个基准上提升测试准确率（最高 +5.76%），且在差分隐私失效时仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习在自编码器场景下需交换模型参数或梯度，导致通信开销大且因自编码器显式优化重构输入，存在训练数据隐私泄露风险。需要一种低通信、强隐私的联邦异常检测方案。

Method: GCA 采用三步迭代：(1) 客户端本地训练自编码器并上传采样的编码器潜在码；(2) 服务端聚合潜在码、拟合聚类模型，仅广播全局潜在质心及其支持计数；(3) 客户端使用反计数加权将本地潜在码与最近质心对齐更新编码器。通信仅涉及潜在码和质心统计量，成本取决于潜在维度而非模型大小。

Result: 在 5 个表格和 2 个视觉基准上共 21 次对比中，GCA 在服务端数据提取攻击下重构误差全胜，余弦相似度 20/21 胜 FedAvg/FedProx/FedNova；测试准确率最高超越 FedAvg 5.76%；提取防御效果媲美 DP-FedAvg，在 DP-FedAvg 失效时仍有效；单轮通信量最高降低 99.15%。

Conclusion: GCA 通过潜在码中介的全局质心对齐，实现了低通信、强隐私、高精度的联邦自编码器异常检测，为资源受限和隐私敏感场景提供了优于现有基线和差分隐私方案的实用替代。

Abstract: Autoencoder (AE)-based federated learning (FL) is attractive for anomaly detection when clients have limited local data. However, conventional FL exchanges AE parameters or gradients, incurring substantial communication overhead and potentially exposing input training data information, since AEs are explicitly optimized to reconstruct their inputs. We introduce \emph{Global Centroid Alignment (GCA)}, a latent-code-mediated FL protocol that coordinates clients without transmitting AE parameters or gradients. In each round, (1) clients first train their local AEs using a \emph{reconstruction} update and upload a small subset of encoder latent codes to the FL server. (2) The server pools these codes, fits a clustering model, and broadcasts only \emph{global latent centroids and their support counts}. (3) Each client then updates its encoder by aligning its local latent codes with the \emph{nearest} centroid using \emph{inverse-count} weighting to emphasize globally underrepresented patterns. Steps (1)--(3) repeat over communication rounds. Because GCA exchanges only sampled latent codes and centroid statistics, its communication cost depends on latent dimensionality and the numbers of uploaded codes and returned centroids rather than on AE model size. Across five tabular and two vision benchmarks, GCA yields higher reconstruction error under a server-side client data extraction attack in all 21 comparisons and clearly lower cosine similarity in 20 of 21 comparisons with FedAvg, FedProx, and FedNova, showing its ability to protect training data. It even improves test accuracy over FedAvg by up to $5.76\%$. GCA achieves extraction defense comparable to DP-FedAvg, remains effective when DP-FedAvg does not reduce target resemblance, and lowers per-round communication by up to $99.15\%$.

</details>


### [203] [Tabular foundation models for non-tabular tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22594)
*Goran Nakerst,John Brennan,Wouter Beugeling,Masudul Haque*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索表格基础模型（TFM）在非表格任务上的泛化能力，使用 TabPFN v3 在 MNIST 手写数字识别、法德语单词语言识别和 Tiny ImageNet 图像分类三个任务上进行零样本评估，结果显示其在无显式空间/序列结构建模的情况下，部分任务性能可媲美专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型近期在表格数据上展现出跨数据集泛化能力，由于许多机器学习数据集可表示为表格，研究者希望验证 TFM 的能力是否能延伸至传统非表格任务（如图像、文本分类）。

Method: 将原始非表格数据（图像像素、词符序列）转换为表格行，将分类任务建模为缺失标签预测。直接使用预训练的 TabPFN v3，无微调或额外训练，仅随上下文样本数量变化评估性能。

Result: 在三个非表格分类任务中，TabPFN v3 随上下文样本增加性能提升，部分任务准确率接近针对性设计的专用模型/方法，尽管模型未显式利用空间或序列结构信息。

Conclusion: 表格基础模型具备超越表格领域的泛化潜力，通过统一的表格表示即可处理多模态分类任务，为通用基础模型研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for machine learning on tabular data, offering the ability to generalize across datasets without task-specific training. Since many machine learning datasets can be represented as tables, this raises the question: does TFM capability extend beyond tasks traditionally regarded as tabular? We address this question by using TabPFN v3 on three non-tabular classification problems: handwritten digit recognition on MNIST, language identification of French and German words, and image classification on Tiny ImageNet. In each case, the original data are represented as rows of a table and classification is formulated as prediction of a missing label. We evaluate performance as a function of the number of context samples provided to the pretrained model, with no additional training or fine-tuning. Despite having no explicit access to the spatial or sequential structure characterizing the data, TabPFN v3 in some cases achieves accuracies comparable with that of models or methods geared specifically toward the corresponding tasks.

</details>


### [204] [Adversarial Agents on Topology Optimization: Understanding the Fragility and Robustness of Deep Learning-based and Physics-Based Design Models under Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22606)
*Hoang Anh Nguyen,Yuan Hong,Hongyi Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于力学的可靠性评估框架，通过对抗代理测试深度学习代理模型在拓扑优化中的鲁棒性，发现有界初始化噪声会导致灾难性机械失效，且更丰富的物理梯度条件不保证单调鲁棒性；物理闭环恢复表明，用扰动拓扑初始化经典SIMP优化器能恢复性能，建议学习代理作为物理验证的初始化器而非完全替代物理求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理模型在拓扑优化中因在线生成速度快被广泛采用，但其在输入扰动下的脆弱性未被充分研究，需要评估其在网络制造系统中的可靠性。

Method: 提出基于力学的可靠性评估框架，构建对抗代理，采用严格非侵入式威胁模型，仅对初始密度通道引入有界扰动，保持物理边界条件、合规性梯度通道、网络架构和求解器不变；在U-Net、卷积和生成式架构上评估不同物理梯度条件深度的代理模型。

Result: 有界初始化噪声导致灾难性机械失效，合规性增加多个数量级，切断负载路径和断开支撑；更丰富的物理梯度条件不保证跨代理家族的单调鲁棒性；物理闭环恢复显示，用扰动拓扑初始化SIMP优化器能高概率将合规性恢复至接近基线水平。

Conclusion: 学习代理应作为物理验证的初始化器而非完全替代物理求解器；提出的对抗代理为训练抗噪声和针对性扰动的生成式设计代理奠定基础。

Abstract: Topology optimization, using both physic-based approaches and deep learning surrogates, serves as a cornerstone for generative design agents in cyber-manufacturing systems. While deep learning surrogates have gained widespread adoption due to their speed in online design generation, this work demonstrates their vulnerability under input perturbations. In this work, we present a mechanics-grounded reliability evaluation framework that formulates an adversarial agent targeting the generative design models. We investigate a strictly non-intrusive threat model where bounded perturbations are introduced exclusively to the initial-density channel, while physical boundary conditions, compliance-gradient channels, network architectures, and solver routines remain intact. Evaluating surrogate models across U-Net, convolutional, and generative architectures with varying physics-gradient conditioning depths demonstrates that bounded initialization noise can cause catastrophic mechanical failure, increasing compliance by multiple orders of magnitude through severed load paths and disconnected supports. Furthermore, we discover that incorporating richer physics-gradient conditioning in the deep learning surrogates does not guarantee monotonic robustness across surrogate families. Finally, physics-in-the-loop recovery demonstrates that initializing the classical SIMP optimizer with perturbed topologies mitigates design performance degradation, having a high probability of restoring compliance to near-baseline levels across tested instances. These findings demonstrate that learned surrogates should serve as physics-verified initializers instead of replacing physics-based solvers entirely in a resilient cyber-manufacturing system. Moreover, the proposed adversarial agent provides a foundation for future training generative design agents robust against noise and targeted perturbations.

</details>


### [205] [Mitigating Explanation Leakage in Financial Fraud Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22607)
*Muhammad Waleed Gul,Elaheh Homayounvala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DP-FedSHAP，一种仅对联邦学习中后处理 TreeSHAP 解释向量施加客户端级差分隐私的架构，旨在平衡解释保真度、隐私保护与模型 AUPRC，并在 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集上优于权重级 DP 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融欺诈检测依赖中心化 ML 模型，存在数据隐私风险；联邦学习虽去中心化，但监管要求模型透明，需使用 TreeSHAP 等 XAI 工具。然而共享高保真 SHAP 解释会使联邦网络暴露于成员推理攻击 (MIA)，因此需要在保护隐私的同时保持解释质量和模型性能。

Method: 提出 DP-FedSHAP 架构，仅在客户端对后处理 TreeSHAP 向量施加差分隐私（客户端级 DP），而不扰动模型权重；对比基线为权重级 DP（直接扰动训练模型）。在高度不平衡的 IEEE-CIS 欺诈检测数据集上实验，评估解释保真度、隐私预算 (ε) 与 AUPRC 的权衡。

Result: 实验表明 DP-FedSHAP 在相同隐私预算下能保持更高的解释保真度和模型 AUPRC，优于权重级 DP 基线；随着隐私预算增加，解释保真度和 AUPRC 均提升，验证了仅对解释向量加噪的有效性。

Conclusion: DP-FedSHAP 通过仅对 SHAP 解释向量施加客户端级差分隐私，有效缓解了联邦学习中解释共享带来的成员推理攻击风险，同时在隐私-效用权衡上优于直接扰动模型权重的方法，为金融欺诈检测等受监管场景提供了可行的隐私保护可解释联邦学习方案。

Abstract: Financial fraud detection relies heavily on centralized machine learning models. This creates serious data privacy risks. Federated Learning (FL) decentralizes data processing, but financial regulations still require models to be transparent. This means using Explainable AI (XAI) tools such as TreeSHAP. Recent cybersecurity research shows a problem with this approach. Sharing high-fidelity SHAP explanations exposes the federated network to Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs). This dissertation proposes and evaluates DP-FedSHAP. It is a new architecture that applies client-level differential privacy only to post-hoc TreeSHAP vectors. It is compared against a Weight-Level DP baseline, which perturbs the trained model directly instead. Using the highly imbalanced IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection dataset, this study measures the trade-off between explanation fidelity, privacy preservation, and the model's Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC).

</details>


### [206] [What AstroPT knows about galaxies, and what that can teach us about LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22614)
*UniverseTBD,:,Kshitij Duraphe,Aman Kumar,Michael J. Smith,Shashwat Sourav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出利用天文学领域的已知物理规律作为地面真值，通过在星系图像上训练的类LLM模型AstroPT，校准机制性可解释性方法在语言模型中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究难以在语言模型中验证概念涌现顺序与线性探针是否恢复真实结构，因为语言缺乏概念难度的地面真值排序。天文学提供已知概念难度层级与物理关系的受控环境。

Method: 在数百万星系图像上训练类Transformer模型AstroPT，冻结其表征，跨检查点、层、模型规模及训练目标进行线性探针实验，考察星系属性（如波段星等、红移、恒星形成率）的可解码性与涌现顺序。

Result: 星系属性按已知物理难度固定顺序涌现：直接写入像素的量（波段星等）早期且浅层可解码；依赖多波段/光谱推断的量（红移、特定恒星形成率）晚期且深层涌现。该顺序对训练目标不变，随模型容量幅度缩放但序列不变。线性探针方向恢复了星系属性间已知的物理结构关系。

Conclusion: 天文学为机制性可解释性方法提供了受控的校准沙盒，使原本盲目应用于LLM的方法可被验证与校准。

Abstract: Interpretability research increasingly asks when concepts emerge during training and whether linear probes recover real structure, but in language models these claims are hard to validate because language offers little ground-truth ordering of concepts or relationships among them. We propose the use of astronomical ground truth through AstroPT, a transformer trained on millions of galaxy images, as a calibration testbed. AstroPT is an LLM-like model trained within a domain where the difficulty ordering of concepts and the relations among them are known in advance. Probing frozen representations across checkpoints, layers, model sizes, and objective choices, we find that galaxy properties emerge in a fixed order that tracks their known difficulty---quantities written almost directly into the pixels (band magnitude) become decodable early in training and shallow in the network, while multiband/spectra based and inferred quantities (such as redshift and specific star formation rate) emerge later and deeper. This order is invariant to our tested training objectives, and scales in magnitude but not in sequence with capacity. Our linear probe directions further recover the known physical structure among galaxy properties. Our findings suggest that astronomy offers a controlled sandbox for calibrating mechanistic interpretability methods we otherwise apply to LLMs blind.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [AIREP: A Protocol for Per-Decision Evidence in AI Runtime Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21363)
*Ali Toygar Abak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIREP协议，用于记录自动化AI运行时的治理决策（发布、阻塞、延迟、编辑、升级），将决策封装为可离线验证的签名对象，通过SHA-256哈希链确保防篡改和缺口检测，采用中立格式隔离厂商/模型/领域特定内容，提供参考实现和一致性测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI运行时治理决策缺乏统一、可验证、防篡改记录格式的问题，使任何方均可独立离线校验决策，增强透明度与问责制。

Method: 定义封闭动词集合描述决策，引用输入/输出/证据的哈希而非原值，声明证据覆盖范围；记录形成SHA-256哈希链绑定位置；将特定内容限制在可选命名空间，通过机械中立性测试保持共享格式纯净；提供参考实现与双语言一致性测试套件。

Result: AIREP格式实现了决策记录的离线可验证性、防篡改性、可审计性，并暴露了规范形式跨实现对齐、新鲜度见证、多运行时链等实现挑战。

Conclusion: AIREP作为通用治理决策记录格式，可被任何AI运行时采用，为AI治理提供标准化、中立、可信的审计基础设施。

Abstract: A protocol is presented for recording the governance decisions of automated AI runtimes. When a runtime releases, blocks, defers, redacts, or escalates an individual output, AIREP records that decision as a single signed object that any party can check offline, independent of the runtime that produced it. A record carries the decision as one of a closed set of verbs under a stated policy basis, references its input, output, and evidence by hash rather than by value, and declares both what its evidence covers and what it does not. Records form a SHA-256 hash chain that binds each record to its position, so that tampering and gaps are detectable by recomputation. Vendor-, model-, and domain-specific content is confined to a single optional namespace, and a mechanical neutrality test keeps the shared format free of it. A reference implementation and a two-language conformance kit are described. Some implementation issues are considered, and problems such as alignment of the canonical form across implementations, freshness witnesses, and multi-runtime chains are exposed. The format is offered for adoption by any AI runtime that records governance decisions.

</details>


### [208] [Reviewing Model Collapse and Countermeasures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21366)
*Xihao Xie,Beichen Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述论文，系统梳理了生成式AI中模型崩溃（Model Collapse, MC）现象的研究进展，涵盖不同应用场景下的MC表现、缓解对策，并指出了当前挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI发展，合成数据被广泛用于训练新一代模型，但模型与数据的自我消费循环导致模型崩溃，引发可信性担忧。尽管相关研究日益增多，但缺乏对MC现象的系统性综述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，全面收集并分析近期关于模型崩溃现象的研究，按应用场景和缓解策略分类整理，构建MC研究的全景图谱。

Result: 梳理了模型崩溃在不同应用场景（如语言建模、图像生成等）中的表现机制，总结了数据筛选、正则化、课程学习、人类反馈等多类缓解对策，识别了评估指标不统一、理论解释不足、长期动态建模缺失等核心挑战。

Conclusion: 模型崩溃是生成式AI可信发展的关键瓶颈，需建立统一评估基准、深化理论分析、探索长期演化机制，并推动合成数据质量控制与人机协作范式的协同创新，以保障模型迭代的可持续性。

Abstract: Driven by massive amounts of web-scale data, generative AI (GenAI) has achieved remarkable progress, enabling various applications in diverse sectors. The advances of GenAI have actuated practitioners to use AI-synthesized data for training next-generation AI models. Undeniably, using synthetic data has alleviated the increasing stringent demand for data supply. Unfortunately, it also introduces a new critical issue: in a self-consuming cycle between model and data, the model ultimately collapse, raising more trustworthiness concerns to GenAI. In recent years, increasingly more studies have investigated the phenomenon of model collapse (MC) and explored potential solutions to mitigate it. However, the review of the phenomenon of MC still remains blank. To fill this gap, this paper provides an up-to-date overview of these studies for consolidating and reviewing the progress of MC in different application scenarios and countermeasures for mitigating MC. We also highlight challenges and future research opportunities.

</details>


### [209] [AI Learning and Conceptual Transfer in the Game of Hidden Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21372)
*Christo Mathew,Wentian Wang,Jacob Feldman,Lazaros K. Gallos,Paul B. Kantor,Vladimir Menkov,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文总结了隐藏规则游戏(GOHR)的研究工作，重点探讨了基于Transformer的A2C框架下，强化学习智能体如何通过试错反馈推断隐藏规则，涵盖表征设计(特征中心/对象中心)、规则难度分析、迁移学习、泛化能力及伪机器人辅助人类学习分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解智能体如何在部分可观测、规则隐藏的环境中通过交互学习推断潜在规则，同时探索不同表征方式对学习效率的影响，以及AI辅助人类学习隐藏规则的可能性。

Method: 采用基于Transformer的Actor-Critic(A2C)架构，对比特征中心(Feature-Centric)和对象中心(Object-Centric)两种表征设计；通过实验分析规则难度、迁移学习与泛化性能；引入伪机器人模拟人类学习行为，对人类学习数据进行分类分析。

Result: 实验表明对象中心表征在规则推断上优于特征中心表征；规则难度显著影响学习收敛速度；迁移学习能加速新规则适应；伪机器人辅助揭示了人类学习策略的多样性分类。

Conclusion: GOHR为研究隐藏规则推断提供了有效测试平台，Transformer-based A2C结合对象中心表征能有效解决此类问题，且AI辅助分析人类学习行为具有应用潜力。

Abstract: This report summarizes the work conducted on the Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR), focusing on reinforcement learning agents trained to infer hidden rules from trial-and-error feedback, representation design, rule difficulty analysis, transfer learning, generalization, and pseudo-bot-assisted human learning analysis. The report focuses on the Transformer-based A2C framework, Feature-Centric and Object-Centric representations, experimental findings, and classification of human learning data.

</details>


### [210] [LitReview Arena: Evaluating Literature Review Agents with Battle-Style Peer Review Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21374)
*Ruotong Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Xurui Liu,Haidong Xue,Dong Liang,Jigao Fu,Wu YanBiao,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LitReview Arena，一个用于评估自动生成文献综述的对战式专家评测平台，收集约 3000 条专家判断，并训练出专家校准的评估器 LitJudge，显著提升了 LLM-as-a-judge 与人类专家的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献综述对科学进步至关重要，但现有自动评估指标（如参考文献重叠度）无法捕捉研究实用性，需依赖专家判断，因此需要一种结构化、专家驱动的评测协议。

Method: 构建 LitReview Arena 平台：领域专家（具 AI 论文写作经验）在匿名、按专业匹配的条件下对比综述草稿，针对五个文献综述专用维度给出逐维胜负判断，共收集约 3k 条含五维结果的专家偏好数据。

Result: 最强现有系统在整体实用性上仅以 23.0% 的胜率击败人类草稿；Agentic LLM（如 Sonar Deep Research）比基座模型高出 60% 以上；现有 LLM-as-a-judge 与人类专家一致性低（Spearman ρ=0.467），尤其在综合型维度上偏差大；基于收集数据训练的 LitJudge 将一致性提升至 ρ=0.78，接近专家间一致性。

Conclusion: LitReview Arena 提供了首个大规模、专家校准的文献综述评测基准，LitJudge 实现了与人类专家高度对齐的自动评估，代码与数据已开源，为后续综述生成模型的开发与评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Literature reviews are essential to scientific progress, but rigorously evaluating automatically generated reviews remains difficult because many aspects of research utility depend on expert judgment rather than reference-overlap metrics. We introduce LitReview Arena, a battle-style evaluation platform with a structured protocol tailored to literature review quality: domain experts with AI paper-writing experience compare anonymized drafts, are matched to topics within their expertise, and provide dimension-wise outcomes over five literature-review-specific criteria. From this protocol, we collect approximately 3k expert judgments, each containing five dimension-wise outcomes, and show that even the strongest current systems win only 23.0% of decisive matches against human drafts on overall utility, while agentic LLMs such as Sonar Deep Research substantially outperform base language models by over 60%. We further find that existing LLM-as-a-judge methods are substantially misaligned with human experts (Spearman's rho=0.467), especially on synthesis-heavy criteria such as paper structure and research suggestions. Using the collected preference data, we provide an expert-calibrated evaluator, LitJudge, which improves alignment to Spearman's rho=0.78, comparable to inter-expert consistency; code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/VanellopeAsher/LitReview-Arena.

</details>


### [211] [SchemaRouter: Field-Aware Tool Routing for Efficient Heterogeneous Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21375)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SchemaRouter，一种轻量级路由层，用于异构代理检索增强生成 (RAG) 系统。它将工具、端点、参数、响应字段、领域概念、单位、来源和许可策略表示为模式图，给定查询后输出可执行工具计划，指定调用哪些工具及检索哪些字段。实验表明，在材料科学基准上，SchemaRouter 以 227 个检索上下文 token（对比 fetch-everything 的 2,066）和 2.7 倍更低延迟，达到与 fetch-everything 相当的回答准确率 (0.71)，超越 prompt-all (0.66)，且在工具精确率 (0.93) 和参数有效性 (1.0) 上表现最佳，还能在 62% 的回答中提供可溯源和许可信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构代理 RAG 系统在编排外部 API、内部数据库、向量存储和图存储时，若将所有工具描述暴露给 LLM 或仅靠向量相似度选择工具，会导致两种代价高昂的失败：过度获取（增加载荷大小、token 使用和延迟）和获取不足（遗漏回答所需字段）。需要一种能同时解决效率、准确性和可验证溯源/许可的路由机制。

Method: SchemaRouter 构建模式图，节点包含工具、端点、参数、响应字段、领域概念、单位、来源、许可策略。小型 LLM 从查询中抽取意图、概念和来源约束；字段选择则通过意图组投影和概念-字段匹配（含别名层）在图上确性完成，输出可执行工具计划。

Result: 在 110 个材料科学查询基准上：回答准确率 0.71，与 fetch-everything 置信区间重叠，高于 prompt-all 的 0.66（区间亦重叠）；检索上下文 token 仅 227 对比 2,066；端到端延迟比 prompt-all 低 2.7 倍；工具精确率 0.93、参数有效性 1.0 冇最优；62% 回答包含溯源和许可信息，基线约为 0%。消融实验显示最小化字段数会降低准确率至 0.56，而保持召回的投影能恢复最高准确率。

Conclusion: SchemaRouter 通过模式图确定性路由，在保持竞争性准确率的同时，大幅提升效率、实现与模式规模无关的扩展，并提供可验证的溯源/许可落地回答，解决了异构代理 RAG 中的过度获取与获取不足问题。

Abstract: Heterogeneous agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems increasingly orchestrate external APIs, internal databases, vector stores, and graph stores. Exposing all tool descriptions to an LLM agent, or selecting tools only by vector similarity, causes two costly failures: over-fetching, which increases payload size, token use, and latency, and under-fetching, which omits fields needed to answer the query.
  We present SchemaRouter, a lightweight routing layer that represents tools, endpoints, parameters, response fields, domain concepts, units, provenance, and license policies as a schema graph. Given a query, SchemaRouter emits an executable tool plan specifying which tools to call and which fields to retrieve. A small LLM extracts intent, concepts, and source constraints, while field selection is deterministic over the graph through intent-group projection and concept-field matching with an alias layer.
  On a materials-science benchmark of 110 queries, SchemaRouter achieves answer accuracy of 0.71, matching fetch-everything within overlapping confidence intervals and exceeding prompt-all's 0.66, though their intervals overlap. It uses 227 retrieved-context tokens versus 2,066 for fetch-everything and achieves 2.7x lower end-to-end latency than prompt-all. It also obtains the best tool-exact rate of 0.93 and parameter validity of 1.0. SchemaRouter grounds provenance and license information in 62 percent of answers, compared with approximately 0 percent for all baselines.
  We also find that minimizing selected-field count is counterproductive: it reduces answer accuracy to 0.56 with negligible token savings, while recall-preserving projection restores top accuracy. SchemaRouter improves efficiency, schema-size-independent scaling, and verifiable provenance/license-grounded answering at competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [212] [RIACT: A Responsible AI System for Personalized Study Habit Tracking and Early Burnout Signal Detection in University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21379)
*Ria Sidhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RIACT系统，一种基于Web的应用程序，结合结构化学习会话记录与混合AI架构，为大学生提供个性化洞察和职业倦怠早期预警信号。系统通过确定性规则检测倦怠信号，利用受限LLM生成建议，强调负责任AI原则：预警基于可审计规则而非模型判断，输出定性为观察而非诊断，数据收集仅限自报行为字段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中学生职业倦怠高发（12%-70%以上），且通常仅在学业下滑后被事后识别。现有生产力工具仅记录活动而不解释，学生缺乏对自身学习行为的结构化可见性，亟需一种能提供早期预警和个性化洞察的工具。

Method: RIACT系统包含四个核心组件：1）学生按地点和时间记录学习会话；2）系统扣除休息时间计算净专注时长；3）基于周度行为对比的透明确定性规则检测倦怠信号；4）受固定输出模式约束的大语言模型情境化模式并生成个性化建议。设计遵循负责任AI原则：预警由可审计规则治理，输出框架为观察非诊断，数据收集限于自报行为字段。

Result: 系统设计理据完整，定位于学生倦怠文献与教育领域可解释AI研究中，并提出评估框架以验证行为信号与既定倦怠量表的一致性。

Conclusion: RIACT通过结构化日志与混合AI架构，实现了早期倦怠信号的透明检测与个性化干预建议，嵌入负责任AI原则，为解决学生倦怠事后识别难题提供了可落地的技术方案，并为后续实证验证奠定了基础。

Abstract: Student burnout is highly prevalent in higher education, with reported rates ranging from 12% to over 70% and consistently exceeding those of the working population - yet it is typically identified only retrospectively, after academic decline has already occurred. A contributing factor is that students have little structured visibility into their own study behaviour, and existing productivity tools record activity without interpreting it. This paper presents RIACT (Record, Insight, Analyze, Coach, Track), a web-based application that combines structured study session logging with a hybrid AI architecture to surface personalized insights and early burnout signals. Students log sessions by location and time; the system computes net focus time by accounting for breaks, detects burnout signals through transparent, deterministic rules operating on week-over-week behavioural comparisons, and uses a large language model - constrained to a fixed output schema - to contextualize patterns and generate personalized recommendations. The design embeds responsible AI principles throughout: warnings are governed by auditable rules rather than model judgement, all output is framed as an observation rather than a diagnosis and data collection is limited to self-logged behavioural fields. We describe the system's design rationale, situate it within the literature on student burnout and explainable AI in education and propose an evaluation framework for validating its behavioural signals against established burnout instruments.

</details>


### [213] [There Is No Neutral Harness: Modern LLM Leaderboards Are Manufactured by Config-Fragile Items](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21382)
*V. S. Raghu Parupudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出'脆弱性网格'框架，系统研究大语言模型评估工具链（选项顺序、提示措辞、答案提取方式）对多项选择基准测试结果的影响。通过12个模型在4个基准上3,679道题目的26种工具链配置下的匹配比较，发现模型得分是区间而非点值，工具链配置决定排名胜负，且基准压缩方法保留的高区分度题目反而更脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多项选择基准固定题目和答案，但评估工具链（选项顺序、提示词、答案读取方式）未标准化。先前工作仅报告聚合分数方差，未深入分析方差落在哪些题目上，以及这些题目是否正是区分模型能力的关键题目。

Method: 构建'脆弱性网格'：12个开源指令微调模型（4个家族）在4个基准（ARC, HellaSwag, MMLU, TruthfulQA）的3,679道题目上，采用26种同等合理的工具链配置进行评估，固定题目、模型权重和贪婪解码，仅变化工具链，记录每个模型、题目、配置下的正确性二值。

Result: 1) gemma4-31b得分随工具链在31%-89%区间波动；2) 相邻模型在稳定回答的题目上打平，配置脆弱题目平均贡献95.7%的分差；3) 12个模型中有4个在某些配置下排名第一，工具链决定胜者；4) 题目区分度与脆弱性相关性为0.28（95% CI 0.25-0.30），基准压缩保留了脆弱题目而非移除；5) 评分方式而非选项顺序是核心影响因子。

Conclusion: 评估工具链是模型排名的关键混杂变量，应将脆弱性网格作为排行榜发布前的必要检查。论文发布逐题记录和分析脚本，所有数字可在CPU上秒级复现，推动评估标准化。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks fix the questions and the correct answers, but not the harness: the order of the options, the wording of the prompt, and whether a language model's answer is read from generated text or from per-option likelihoods. Work on this harness sensitivity reports it as aggregate score variance, leaving unexamined which items the variance falls on and whether they are the items that separate one model from the next. We treat the evaluation harness of large language models (LLMs) as an independent variable and resolve its effect to single items. We introduce the \textit{fragility grid}: 12 open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs from 4 families answer the same 3{,}679 items from 4 benchmarks (ARC, HellaSwag, MMLU, TruthfulQA) under 26 equally defensible harness configurations, recording one correctness bit for every model, item, and configuration. The comparison is matched, since the items, the weights, and the greedy decoding stay fixed while only the harness varies. Under the grid a model's score is a band rather than a point: gemma4-31b scores between 31 and 89 percent depending only on the harness. Three results follow. On the items that two adjacent models both answer stably the pair is tied, and config-fragile items carry 95.7 percent of a pair's gap on average. Four of the 12 models reach rank one under some configuration, so the harness selects the winner. Item discrimination, the property that benchmark-compression methods maximize, correlates with fragility at 0.28 (95 percent CI 0.25 to 0.30), so compression keeps the fragile items rather than removing them. The scoring choice, not the option order that protocols usually fix, is the load-bearing axis. We release the per-item records and the analysis script, from which every number regenerates on a CPU in seconds, and we position the fragility grid as a check a leaderboard can run before it reports an order.

</details>


### [214] [Spyre-Accelerated Retrieval-Augmented Generation on IBM LinuxONE: A Cloud-Native Architecture for Secure, High-Throughput Enterprise AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21393)
*Sandeep Bokkasam,Pankaj D*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于 IBM LinuxONE 和 Spyre 加速器的六子系统 RAG 架构，实现敏感数据不出硬件边界的企业级生成式推理，端到端延迟低于 2 秒，较云端/离平台推理降低高达 20 倍，同时满足强加密与审计合规需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业环境中大模型部署面临数据与算力分离、敏感数据跨境传输导致的延迟、安全性及合规风险，需在保证数据不离开硬件边界的前提下实现低延迟、可审计的 RAG 推理。

Method: 构建六子系统 RAG 流水线：查询接入、向量检索、提示组装、LLM 推理、合规过滤、响应交付，全部运行于单台 IBM LinuxONE 系统内。利用 Spyre PCIe 推理卡承担生成式推理，Telum II 片上加速器处理轻量分类任务，Red Hat OpenShift 负责容器编排，配合 LinuxONE Secure Execution 技术将机密计算保障延伸至 AI 工作负载。

Result: 早期基准测试显示端到端 RAG 延迟 < 2 秒，较云端 GPU 及本地替代方案降低高达 20 倍延迟，同时保持强加密与审计态势，满足受监管行业的合规要求。

Conclusion: 该架构证明了在 IBM LinuxONE 上利用专用加速器与机密计算技术，可构建高性能、强安全、合规就绪的企业级 RAG 系统，为敏感数据场景提供了不出硬件边界的生成式 AI 部署范式。

Abstract: Running large language models inside enterprise environments has always bumped up against a practical wall: the data lives in one place, the AI horsepower sits somewhere else, and moving sensitive records between the two creates real headaches around latency, security, and regulatory exposure. IBM's Spyre accelerator PCIe inference card built for LinuxONE and the broader IBM Z family changes that equation.
  In this paper we lay out a six-subsystem RAG architecture that runs entirely on IBM LinuxONE, using Spyre for generative inference, the Telum II on-chip accelerator for lightweight classification tasks, and Red Hat OpenShift for container orchestration. Every piece of the pipeline from query intake through vector retrieval, prompt assembly, LLM inference, compliance filtering, and response delivery stays within a single LinuxONE system, so sensitive data never has to leave the hardware perimeter.
  We walk through the design choices behind each subsystem, dig into the Spyre compilation and serving stack, explain how LinuxONE's Secure Execution technology extends confidential-computing guarantees to AI workloads, and benchmark the architecture against cloud-GPU and on-premises alternatives. Early analysis points to end-to-end RAG latencies under two seconds and up to a 20x reduction compared to off-platform inference, all while keeping the strong encryption and auditability posture that regulated industries actually need.

</details>


### [215] [Hate Speech Classification In Roman Urdu: A Comparative Study On Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning And Prompt Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21408)
*Toneema Zubair*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言罗马乌尔都语的仇恨言论分类任务，对比了零样本推理、LoRA参数高效微调、提示词调优和提示词工程四种方法，旨在寻找数据稀缺场景下的最有效技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于互联网和社交媒体的普及，有毒和仇恨内容呈指数级增长，造成严重社会危害。罗马乌尔都语作为巴基斯坦及全球乌尔都语社区使用的低资源语言，因非正式语法、句式不一致、拼写变体多等特点，带来额外挑战。研究旨在探索在有限数据条件下最有效的仇恨言论分类技术。

Method: 设计四个实验：1) 直接使用大语言模型进行零样本推理，评估其对罗马乌尔都语的理解能力；2) 采用LoRA参数高效微调（PEFT），仅更新少量参数以降低计算成本；3) 探索提示词调优，使用混合和手工设计的提示词，仅需极少量训练样本；4) 应用提示词工程，通过零样本和少样本学习，仅依靠精心设计的指令提示词进行分类，无需进一步训练。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验结果，但表明研究比较了上述方法在低资源环境下的有效性，为后续选择最优技术提供实证依据。

Conclusion: 研究为低资源语言仇恨言论分类提供了多种技术路线的对比评估框架，有助于在数据稀缺场景下选择计算高效且效果较好的方法。

Abstract: Due to the widespread accessibility of the internet and social media, toxic and hateful con-tent has grown exponentially, causing significant distress and negative societal impacts. Ro-man Urdu, a low-resource language used in Pakistan and among Urdu-speaking communities worldwide, presents additional challenges because of its informal grammar, inconsistent sen-tence structures, and multiple variations in word spellings. This research aims to identify the most effective techniques for hate speech classification in such low-resource settings with limited data. To address this, the study investigates and compares the latest approaches, in-cluding prompt tuning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) using LoRA, and prompt en-gineering, under various experimental configurations. To achieve this objective, four exper-iments were designed. The first experiment involved direct inferencing with LLMs without any fine-tuning, to evaluate how well these models understand Roman Urdu in a zero-shot setting, especially given limited data. The second experiment utilized parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA, which updates only a small subset of parameters, thereby reducing computational cost. The third experiment explored prompt tuning with both mixed and manually crafted prompts, using very small sets of training examples relative to the entire dataset, making it computationally efficient as well. Finally, the fourth experiment applied prompt engineering through zero-shot and few-shot learning, relying solely on care-fully designed instruction prompts for classification without further training.

</details>


### [216] [The Abstention Protocol: RCA for Clos Fabrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21412)
*Madhava Gaikwad,Deepak Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoreSec是一种生产级根因分析（RCA）系统，采用基于PAM的弃权代数替代加权融合，通过控制标志组合遥测代理，实现确定性决策和显式弃权，结合拓扑感知配置捕捉Clos网络故障面，随证据积累单调收敛，在超大规模部署中提供稳定、可解释的RCA行为，无需重新调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型数据中心网络的遥测数据具有噪声大、部分缺失、异步等特性，传统基于评分的融合方法在这些条件下性能退化，产生不稳定或不正确的归因，亟需一种更鲁棒、可解释的自动化RCA方法。

Method: 提出CoreSec系统：1) 采用PAM风格的弃权代数替代加权融合，利用控制标志组合遥测代理，实现确定性决策和显式弃权（证据模糊时弃权）；2) 引入拓扑感知配置，捕捉Clos架构中的故障面，随证据积累单调收敛；3) 系统在超大规模生产环境部署并验证。

Result: CoreSec在超大规模生产环境中部署，能够跨多样化环境提供稳定且可解释的RCA行为，无需针对不同环境重新调优参数，验证了结构化组合与弃权机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化组合与弃权机制为实际云网络中的自动化根因分析提供了实用基础，证明了在噪声、部分、异步遥测条件下，基于弃权代数的确定性方法优于传统加权评分融合。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) in large datacenter networks is challenging because telemetry is noisy, partial, and asynchronous. Score-based approaches degrade under these conditions, often yielding unstable or incorrect attributions.
  We present \textsc{CoreSec}, a production RCA system that replaces weighted fusion with a PAM-style abstention algebra. Telemetry agents are composed using control flags that yield deterministic decisions and explicit abstention when evidence is ambiguous. CoreSec combines this algebra with topology-aware configurations that capture failure surfaces across Clos fabrics and converge monotonically as evidence accumulates.
  Deployed at hyperscale, CoreSec provides stable and explainable RCA behavior across diverse environments without retuning. Our experience shows that structured composition with abstention forms a practical foundation for automated RCA in real-world cloud networks.

</details>


### [217] [Retrieval-grounded robot program generation and simulation-based correction via Model Context Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21417)
*Zhichao Zhou,Siyuan Chen,Omkar Salunkhe,Ebru Turanoglu Bekar,Johan Stahre,Anders Skoogh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语言模型的工作流，从自然语言任务描述生成、验证并迭代修正 ABB RAPID 机器人程序。通过双流检索增强生成（RAG）管线结合技术文档与生产模板，以及自定义模型上下文协议（MCP）服务器连接 RobotStudio 实现自动上传、仿真执行与诊断反馈，解决了非接地语言模型的领域错误问题，并在拾取放置仿真单元中验证了仿真循环能捕获静态/语义检查遗漏的执行故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性制造要求工业机器人随产品变体快速重新编程，现有方法依赖专家手工编写代码，效率低且易出错；未接地的语言模型生成代码存在领域特定错误，且缺乏与工业仿真环境的闭环反馈。

Method: 1. 双流 RAG 管线：并行检索验证过的技术文档与生产模板，为代码生成提供接地上下文。2. 自定义 MCP 服务器：将语言模型客户端直连 ABB RobotStudio，支持代码自动上传、仿真执行、诊断反馈获取。3. 迭代修正循环：基于仿真执行失败（如吸盘释放高度错误、不可达放置目标、构型相关恢复动作）自动修正代码。

Result: 在 30 查询检索基准、代码生成范围检查及 RobotStudio 拾取放置仿真案例研究中表明：RAG 显著降低领域错误；MCP 实现了从生成到可执行反馈的闭环；仿真循环能暴露静态/语义检查无法捕获的运行时故障；但专家初始配置与最终监督仍不可完全消除。

Conclusion: RAG 与 MCP 的结合成功将接地代码生成与工业机器人仿真软件的可执行反馈连接起来，为柔性制造中的快速重编程提供了可行路径，虽减少但未消除专家介入需求。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires industrial robots to be reprogrammed rapidly as product variants change. This paper presents a language-model-based workflow that generates, validates, and iteratively corrects ABB RAPID robot programs from natural language task descriptions. A dual-stream retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounds code generation in verified technical documentation and production templates, reducing domain-specific errors produced by ungrounded language models. A custom Model Context Protocol (MCP) server connects the language-model client directly to ABB RobotStudio for automated code upload, simulation execution, and diagnostic feedback. The evaluation combines a 30-query retrieval benchmark, scoped code-generation checks, and RobotStudio case studies in a simulated pickand- place manufacturing cell. The simulation loop exposes execution failures that static and semantic checks alone cannot catch, including suction release-height errors, unreachable placement targets, and configuration-dependent recovery motions. The results show how RAG and MCP can connect grounded code generation with executable feedback from industrial robot simulation software, while reducing but not eliminating expert setup and final supervision.

</details>


### [218] [Composable Trust Infrastructure for Manufacturing Knowledge Graphs: Cross-System Provenance, Temporal Reasoning, and Decision Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21418)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种可组合的信任基础设施，集成SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、领域感知双时态版本控制和图原生决策对象四大能力，通过共享相关性标识符实现多维复合查询，消融实验证明四者缺一不可，并在包含11个工业数据源的测试平台上验证了全链路可审计性等涌现属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造知识图谱整合异构工业系统数据面临信任赤字：消费者无法判断查询数据的有效性、决策时刻的有效性、数据来源及处理过程，亟需解决多维信任能力的组合与涌现问题。

Method: 构建统一RDF架构，集成SHACL验证、PROV-O溯源、领域感知双时态版本控制和图原生决策对象四大能力；通过共享实体URI、摄入活动标识符和时态相关键实现能力组合，支持跨四维度的复合查询；基于89类ISA-95对齐本体，整合11个工业数据源（OPC UA、TIA Portal等），构建含8,743三元组、5个命名图、81条owl:sameAs同一性边的统一图谱；采用模拟但结构真实的数据进行实验评估。

Result: 消融实验显示移除任一能力恰好导致6个组合查询中3个失败，证明四大能力同等关键；高阶组合分析揭示4个三向涌现属性和1个不可约的四向属性（全链路可审计性，执行时间31ms）；测试平台验证了基础设施在真实工业场景下的可行性。

Conclusion: 该可组合信任基础设施通过四大核心能力的深度集成，实现了制造知识图谱的全维度信任保障，四大能力缺一不可且产生不可约的四向涌现属性，为异构工业系统数据集成提供了可验证、可追溯、可审计的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Manufacturing knowledge graphs that integrate data from heterogeneous industrial systems face a trust deficit: consumers cannot determine whether queried data is valid, whether it was valid when a decision was made, where it originated, or how it was acted upon. We argue that four trust capabilities -- SHACL validation, PROV-O provenance, domain-aware bi-temporal versioning, and graph-native decision objects -- compose through shared correlation identifiers to produce emergent trust properties that no single capability delivers alone. We present a composable trust infrastructure that integrates these four capabilities into a unified RDF architecture. Capabilities compose through shared entity URIs, ingestion activity identifiers, and temporal correlation keys, enabling compound queries spanning all four dimensions. An experimental ablation confirms that removing any single capability causes exactly three of six composition queries to fail, demonstrating that all four are equally load-bearing. Analysis of higher-order compositions reveals four emergent three-way properties and one irreducible four-way property (full-chain auditability, 31ms execution). The infrastructure is validated on a testbed integrating eleven industrial sources -- OPC UA, TIA Portal, eClass, AAS, ISA-95, ISA-18.2, SAP S/4HANA, Teamcenter, Opcenter EX, Insights Hub, and SCM -- under an 89-class ISA-95-aligned ontology. The unified graph contains 8,743 triples across five named graphs, stitched by 81 owl:sameAs identity edges. Evaluation uses simulated but structurally realistic data from purpose-built emulators; data structures and cross-system linkage patterns are representative of real industrial installations.

</details>


### [219] [Evaluating Multimodal Narrative Understanding of Popular Hollywood Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21430)
*David Bamman,Kent K. Chang,Allison Cooper,Juishan Hsu,Reina Kushihashi,Madison Mar,Arnav Podichetty,Rachael Samberg,Ipek Nil Sancak,Yuhan Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于票房流行度和公共领域状态的好莱坞电影集合，发布了1922-1979年的周票房数据，并创建了一个聚焦叙事元素的多模态选择题基准测试。实验显示视觉-语言模型表现接近随机猜测，音频-视觉模型最高仅达61.1%准确率，远低于人类水平，凸显了当前模型在电影叙事理解上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态语言模型在电影大规模计算分析方面展现潜力，但受版权保护限制，难以建立基于好莱坞电影的稳定基准，阻碍了电影历史和叙事技巧演变的研究。

Method: 基于两个标准构建新电影集合：1) 票房流行度——发布首个大规模开放的《综艺》杂志1922-1979年周票房数据；2) 可能的公共领域状态——通过研究美国版权登记目录中的版权注册和续展情况。在此基础上建立聚焦叙事元素的多模态选择题基准，直接评估模型对电影叙事研究的支撑能力。

Result: 视觉-语言模型在该任务上表现挣扎，许多接近随机猜测水平；音频-视觉模型（包括利用音频进行场景字幕生成的模型）最高准确率仅为61.1%，显著低于人类表现。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个合规的版权安全电影基准，揭示了当前多模态模型在电影叙事理解上的显著不足，为未来改进模型以支持电影学研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal language models increasingly show promise for enabling the large-scale computational analysis of film, opening up new avenues for learning about film history and the evolution of narrative techniques. But the creation of stable benchmarks built around Hollywood films is complicated by copyright protections. In this work, we address these concerns directly, by building a new collection of Hollywood films defined by two criteria: box office popularity (where we publish the first large-scale, open collection of weekly box office earnings reported by Variety magazine from 1922-1979); and likely public domain status (by researching copyright registrations and renewals in the US Catalog of Copyright Entries). We build a new multimodal MCQ benchmark on top of this collection that focuses on narrative elements that directly evaluate the abilities of models to inform meaningful research on film narrative; we find that many vision-language models struggle on this task (with many performing at near-chance levels of accuracy), while audio-visual models (including those that use audio in captioning scenes) reach a maximum accuracy of 61.1%, well below human-level performance.

</details>


### [220] [Agentic AI for Safety-critical Multi-drone Systems: Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21444)
*Timothy Merritt,Alejandro Jarabo-Peñas,Juan Bravo-Arrabal,Maria-Theresa Bahodi,Anders Lyhne Christensen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为一篇立场论文，综合了NAMUR（LLM支持的搜救/消防机器人控制）和PERSIST（关键基础设施持久无人机监控）两个项目的经验，主张将Agentic AI视为社会技术设计问题，强调界面、监督机制和评估实践与算法同等重要，提出以人为本、参与式、迭代的研究路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机系统在搜救（SAR）和关键基础设施监控等安全关键任务中应用前景广阔，但实际落地受限于自主性能不足以及将智能体行为融入专业工作流的困难：操作员需在不确定性、时间压力和问责制下理解、信任并治理自动化。

Method: 采用社会技术设计视角，结合NAMUR和PERSIST两个正在进行的项目经验，提出以人为本、参与式、迭代的研究方法：发掘利益相关者需求，通过连续原型塑造智能体能力，产出可迁移的概念验证系统和评估策略。

Result: 论证了智能体AI应作为社会技术设计问题处理，界面、监督机制、评估实践与算法同等关键；梳理出面向安全关键场景的可迁移概念验证系统与评估策略框架。

Conclusion: 未来多无人机系统在安全关键领域的落地需超越纯算法优化，转向社会技术协同设计，通过参与式迭代开发构建操作员可理解、可信、可治理的智能体系统，并形成可复用的评估范式。

Abstract: Multi-drone systems are increasingly positioned for safety-critical missions such as search and rescue (SAR) and critical infrastructure monitoring. Yet, real-world adoption remains constrained not only by autonomy performance, but by the difficulty of integrating agentic behavior into professional work: operators must understand, trust, and govern automation under uncertainty, time pressure, and accountability. This position paper synthesizes the ambitions and lessons from two ongoing efforts: NAMUR, which explores LLM-supported robot control in SAR and firefighting contexts, and PERSIST, which explores persistent drone operations for monitoring and security at critical infrastructure sites. We argue that agentic AI should be approached as a socio-technical design problem, where interfaces, oversight mechanisms, and evaluation practices are as critical as algorithms. We outline a human-centered, participatory, and iterative research approach aimed at uncovering stakeholder needs, shaping agent capabilities through successive prototypes, and producing transferable proof-of-concept systems and evaluation strategies for other safety-critical contexts.

</details>


### [221] [Software Frameworks for Explainable AI in Time Series Classification: A Systematic Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21449)
*Louis Peter,Nils Gumpfer,Jana Fischer,Christin Seifert,Jennifer Hannig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性调查了时间序列分类（TSC）领域的可解释人工智能（XAI）软件框架，对比了六个显式支持时间序列的框架，揭示了频域解释支持缺失、时间序列专用评估指标稀缺、跨框架解释一致性差等共性局限，呼吁构建统一、时间序列感知的XAI框架以实现忠实、可复现的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列分类广泛应用于决策关键场景，模型透明度和可信度要求推动了XAI方法的应用。然而现有研究碎片化，缺乏对解释生成软件框架、评估实践及实际局限的系统性理解，跨框架一致性、时间序列特定评估和可复现性受关注不足。

Method: 通过调查分析现有支持时间序列的解释生成与评估软件框架，从支持的XAI方法、评估指标、易用性、基准测试支持、可复现性等多维度进行对比，并实施实现层面的比较与频域支持分析。

Result: 识别出六个显式支持时间序列的框架，发现共同局限：仅一种方法支持频域解释（尽管频域对时间序列极其相关）；仅两个评估指标专为时间序列开发；相同XAI方法在不同框架中产生的解释存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于调查发现，讨论了开放挑战并规划未来研究方向，强调亟需统一的、时间序列专用的XAI框架，以实现忠实、可复现且具备时间序列感知能力的解释生成。

Abstract: Time series arise in a wide range of application domains and are analyzed using machine learning in decision-critical settings. Time series classification (TSC) is one of the most widely studied and relevant tasks. In this context, ensuring the transparency and trustworthiness of TSC models has become an important requirement, motivating the use of explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) methods. Despite growing interest, research on XAI for TSC remains fragmented, and a systematic understanding of the available software frameworks for explanation generation, their evaluation practices, and practical limitations is still lacking. Prior work largely focused on individual explanation methods, while cross-framework consistency, time-series-specific evaluation, and reproducibility have received little attention. In this survey, we analyze existing software frameworks for explanation generation and evaluation in TSC. We compare them along multiple dimensions, including supported XAI methods, evaluation metrics, usability, benchmarking support, and reproducibility, providing the first time-series-specific survey of frameworks with implementation comparisons and an analysis of frequency-domain support. We identify six frameworks that explicitly support time series and reveal common limitations: only one method supports frequency-domain explanations despite their relevance; only two evaluation metrics have been developed specifically for time series; and identical XAI methods can yield substantially different explanations across frameworks. Based on these findings, we discuss open challenges and outline directions for future research, highlighting the need for unified, time-series-specific XAI frameworks that enable faithful, reproducible, and time-series-aware explanations.

</details>


### [222] [Enhanced Artificial Neural Networks Using QHAdamW in Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21463)
*Mary Joy Daniel Vinas*
