In this paper, we term this phenomenon situational illusions and investigate: (1) how MLLMs perform under such illusions, and (2) how to mitigate the limitations. We first develop a comprehensive where-what-how taxonomy that characterizes where situational illusions occur, what targets they take, and how they arise. Building on this taxonomy, we introduce MSIBench, a benchmark designed to assess the discrimination, understanding, and reasoning capabilities of MLLMs under situational illusions. Evaluations of 27 model configurations reveal that current MLLMs are highly vulnerable to these illusions and exhibit 6 typical failure modes related to visual observation, grounding, and reasoning. To mitigate the limitations, we build on the core idea of systematically inspecting and reasoning over visual evidence for contextual understanding, developing prompting for closed-source models and supervised fine-tuning for open-source models, respectively. These two simple yet effective methods improve model performances by 20% at most, suggesting a practical path toward more reliable multimodal perception and reasoning in complex real-world environments.

</details>


### [288] [Read Less, Solve More: Token-Efficient Sparse Reading for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22237)
*Zedong Liu,Jiaan Wu,Xinyang Ma,Le Xu,Kai Wang,Yuanchao Hu,Dingwen Tao,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SparseRead 是一种无需训练、模型透明的阅读层，在内容进入模型上下文前控制准入，通过 regime-aware Read Gate、可扩展的 Reader Backends 和有状态协议实现稀疏证据获取，在六个前沿模型和五个工作负载场景中将 token 量减少高达 92.9%、壁钟时间减少高达 89.0%，同时保持或提升任务质量，且在三个智能体框架上表现一致，展现广泛可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期智能体需反复访问外部工件，但现有阅读接口常暴露整个对象，导致过度阅读，增加 token 和延迟成本，稀释任务相关证据；现有上下文缩减方法多在宽泛内容已进入轨迹后才介入，无法从源头控制内容准入。

Method: SparseRead 提出三大组件：1) regime-aware Read Gate（基于情境的读取门控），2) extensible Reader Backends（可扩展的读取后端），3) stateful protocol for bounded, source-anchored evidence acquisition with explicit refinement, verification, stopping, and fallback（用于有界、锚定源的证据获取的有状态协议，支持显式细化、验证、停止和回退）。该层在内容进入模型上下文前控制准入，实现训练自由、模型透明的稀疏读取。

Result: 在六个前沿模型（含 Claude Opus 5）和五个工作负载场景中，SparseRead 将 token 体积降低高达 92.9%，壁钟时间降低高达 89.0%，同时保持或提升任务质量；在三个智能体框架上增益一致，验证了广泛可移植性。

Conclusion: SparseRead 通过源头控制内容准入，有效解决了长周期智能体过度阅读问题，大幅降低 token 和延迟成本，且无需训练、模型无关，具备强泛化能力，为智能体高效交互外部工件提供了通用范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly rely on repeated access to external artifacts, yet current reading interfaces often expose entire objects even when only sparse evidence is needed. This over-reading increases token and latency costs and can dilute task-relevant evidence, while existing context-reduction methods mainly intervene after broad content has already entered the trajectory. We present SparseRead, a training-free, model-transparent reading layer that controls content admission before unnecessary evidence reaches the model context. SparseRead combines a regime-aware Read Gate, extensible Reader Backends, and a stateful protocol for bounded, source-anchored evidence acquisition with explicit refinement, verification, stopping, and fallback. Across six frontier models, including Claude Opus 5, and five workload scenarios, SparseRead reduces token volume by up to 92.9% and wall time by up to 89.0%, while preserving or improving task quality. Its consistent gains across three agent frameworks further demonstrate broad portability.

</details>


### [289] [Clarify User Expertise: Towards Proactive Conversational Agents Tailoring Responses to User Proficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22266)
*Zhihong Cao,Chen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PASSING框架，使对话代理能主动澄清用户专业水平，通过What-to-ask和How-to-ask策略（由LLM自博弈诱导）实现，实验证明其优越性，为更以人为中心的对话代理迈出关键一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话代理难以仅从查询中判断用户专业水平，无法动态调整回复以提升用户理解；现有研究多关注澄清查询歧义，而忽视了澄清用户专业水平以定制回复。

Method: 提出PASSING框架，包含What-to-ask（问什么）和How-to-ask（如何问）两个策略，通过LLM自博弈诱导，使代理能主动通过针对性询问澄清用户专业水平。

Result: 大量实验表明PASSING在主动澄清用户专业水平方面优于现有方法，能更好地定制回复以提升用户理解。

Conclusion: PASSING是迈向更以人为中心的对话代理的关键一步，使代理能根据用户专业水平动态调整交互策略。

Abstract: In the context of information seeking, conversational agents are undergoing an evolution from reactive tools to proactive, personalized assistants. A critical aspect of this evolution is the ability to tailor strategic interactions to a user's unique needs and expectations. Unlike existing studies that focus on proactively clarifying query ambiguities, we center on clarifying the user's expertise in order to tailor responses for better user comprehension. We find that existing agents struggle to determine user expertise from queries alone, a limitation that prevents them from dynamically adapting their responses. To address this gap, we introduce PASSING to empower the agent to proactively clarify a user's expertise through targeted inquiries. This is achieved by our What-to-ask and How-to-ask strategies, induced by LLM self-play. Our extensive experiments also show our superiority. We believe that PASSING represents a crucial step towards creating more human-centric conversational agents.

</details>


### [290] [HERO: Human-profile Enhanced Retrieval Optimization Framework for Long-term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22310)
*Yuanhua Lin,Yile Li,Zhiyuan Zhao,Jing Shang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HERO框架，通过异构记忆图保留原始对话文本，结合人类画像迭代图遍历检索，解决长时记忆中的信息丢失和语义漂移问题，在事实和个性化推理上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆方法依赖LLM压缩或重写对话历史，导致细粒度细节丢失和原始语气/语境侵蚀，需要一种保留原始证据并支持个性化检索的方法。

Method: HERO将对话历史转化为可追踪的异构记忆图，保留原始文本作为推理证据；检索时从当前查询提取初始锚点，结合人类画像通过迭代图遍历，自适应激活最信息量的图区域。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，HERO在事实推理和个性化推理任务上均优于强基线，且能更忠实地访问原始对话证据。

Conclusion: HERO通过异构图保留原始对话细节，结合人类画像指导的迭代检索，有效缓解了信息丢失和语义漂移，实现了更准确的长时记忆推理。

Abstract: Long-term memory is crucial for personalized responses and long-horizon agent interactions. Existing methods often rely on LLMs to compress or rewrite dialogue histories and use the transformed memories as retrieval evidence. Despite the progress in organizing fragmented contexts, two major drawbacks persist: (1) information loss from compression, which discards fine-grained but later useful details, and (2) semantic drift from rewriting, which erodes the original tone and situated context. In this work, we propose a novel Human-profile Enhanced Retrieval Optimization framework for long-term agent memory (HERO). Specifically, HERO converts the dialogue history into a traceable heterogeneous memory graph that preserves raw dialogue text as evidence for reasoning, thereby mitigating information loss. For retrieval, HERO extracts initial anchors from the current query and incorporates human profiles via an iterative graph traversal; these anchors and profiles provide guidance signals that adaptively activate the most informative regions of the graph. Experiments on two benchmark datasets show that HERO outperforms strong baselines on both factual and personalized reasoning, while providing more faithful access to raw dialogue evidence.

</details>


### [291] [Where Cognition Lives: Dissecting Emergent from Computed Function in a Minimal Complete Cognitive Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22347)
*Francisco M. Arrabal-Campos,Francisco G. Montoya,Alfredo Alcayde,Ignacio Fernández*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含循环推理器、自适应停止机制、稳态控制场和价值模块的最小完整认知架构，系统性地探究了各功能是否能从梯度下降中涌现，还是必须显式计算。实验表明：能力可以涌现；停止机制看似涌现实为测量伪影，平衡读出后优势消失；价值分配无法涌现，必须显式计算二阶决策；在冻结LLM上，自洽性投票提供有限增益，样本间一致性作为停止信号几乎无效；承诺下的价值在断崖成本族中比平滑成本族高出约7倍，源于可达范围扩大5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知架构不仅需要推理模块，还必须决定思考时长和分配精力的对象。作者旨在通过一个最小但完整的系统，严格区分“从梯度下降涌现”与“必须显式计算”的功能边界，并为每个空假设提供机制解释和阳性对照，建立可证伪的实验协议。

Method: 构建包含循环推理器、自适应停止、稳态控制场和价值模块的架构；在匹配平均计算量下对比均匀停止、难度停止、先验价值停止、后验自我观察停止；对比PonderNet风格停止（隐状态混合）与强制深度基线（单一隐状态），并通过平衡读出消除混合优势；训练耦合与显式分配器对比价值涌现；在冻结LLM执行器上测试自洽性投票与样本间一致性；在断崖成本族与平滑成本族中验证承诺下价值的可证伪预测。

Result: 1) 能力涌现；停止机制表观优势源于测量伪影：匹配平均计算量下收益从0.467(均匀)→0.546(难度)→0.698(先验价值)→0.921(后验自我观察)，但平衡读出后残差优势为+0.000 [0.000, 0.000]。2) 价值不涌现：训练耦合捕获零收益，显式分配器完全捕获(+0.151, 路由相关+0.79)。3) 冻结LLM上：自洽性投票增益+0.0236 [+0.0150, +0.0326]；样本间一致性作为停止信号几乎无效，质量集中在错误答案。4) 承诺下价值在断崖成本族+0.1312 [+0.1124, +0.1502]，约为平滑成本族7倍，因可达范围扩大5.1倍 [3.4, 8.2]。

Conclusion: 认知架构中的停止决策可通过梯度下降近似，但表观优势多为读出偏差；价值分配等二阶决策在价值与内容正交时无法涌现，必须显式计算；自洽性投票提供有限下界，样本间一致性不可靠；成本函数形状（断崖 vs 平滑）通过改变可达收益范围而非先验信息，显著放大承诺价值。实验协议本身（空假设+机制+阳性对照）是核心贡献。

Abstract: A cognitive architecture is more than the module that reasons: it must also decide how long to think and what deserves the effort. We built a minimal but complete system - a recurrent reasoner with adaptive halting, a homeostatic control field, and a value module - and asked of each part: does this function emerge from gradient descent, or must it be computed? Competence emerges. Stopping appears to emerge too, and to be worth more than everything decidable in advance, but that appearance is instrumentation: payoff at matched mean compute climbs from 0.467 (uniform) through 0.546 (difficulty) to 0.698 (ex-ante value), and the further climb to 0.921 (posterior self-observation) does not survive audit. PonderNet-style halting returns a halting-weighted mixture of hidden states while forced-depth baselines return one, and the language head is trained on the mixture alone; equalizing the readout annihilates the apparent advantage of native execution (residual +0.000 [0.000, 0.000]). Value does not emerge: trained couplings capture zero of a payoff an explicit allocator captures completely (+0.151, routing correlation +0.79), so the second-order decisions that pay must be computed, at least where value is orthogonal to content, as here by construction. On a frozen LLM actuator the same instruments show self-consistency voting to be a measured bound (+0.0236 [+0.0150, +0.0326]) and inter-sample agreement nearly worthless as a stopping signal, its mass concentrating on wrong answers. Every null we assert carries a mechanism and a positive control, and the protocol is part of the contribution. Executing our own falsifiable prediction, value under commitment pays +0.1312 [+0.1124, +0.1502] in a cliff-cost family, some seven times the smooth-family estimate - not because the cliff shifts information ex ante, but because it multiplies the attainable range fivefold (5.1x [3.4, 8.2]).

</details>


### [292] [Addressing the Selection Problem in Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22356)
*Claire Vlases,Katelyn Morrison*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有XAI技术虽多但用户难以选择合适技术，形式化了“选择问题”，并提出基于多智能体LLM编排工具的结构性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统XAI研究呈孤岛状态，用户需将自然语言不确定性自行转化为技术选择，这一前提条件难以满足，导致解释在实际应用中效果不佳。

Method: 采用逻辑前提-结论格式形式化“选择问题”，证明常规接口要求用户完成困难的翻译任务；提出多智能体LLM编排工具，自动将用户查询映射至恰当的XAI解释技术，并给出实例化示例。

Result: 论证了选择问题的普遍性与严重性，展示了结构性解决方案如何弥合用户不确定性与解释技术之间的鸿沟。

Conclusion: 研究揭示XAI有效性的核心瓶颈在于技术选择而非技术本身，基于LLM的自动化选择框架为提升XAI实用性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) research has produced a plethora of explanation techniques, yet user studies repeatedly show that available explanations are not effective in practice. We argue that, given the siloed nature of conventional XAI, users are struggling to select the appropriate XAI technique. Viewing XAI through a philosophical lens, we offer a formalization of what we call the selection problem: the systematic failure of XAI interfaces to bridge the gap between a user's natural-language uncertainty and the explanation technique that resolves it. Following a logical premise-conclusion format, we show that conventional interfaces require users to translate their uncertainty into a technique selection, a challenging prerequisite to meet. We also propose a structural solution: a multi-agent LLM orchestration tool that translates the user's query to the proper XAI explanation technique. We provide an example of how this structural solution could be instantiated to address the selection problem.

</details>


### [293] [Analyzing and Mitigating Cross-Lingual Degradation in Multilingual Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22363)
*Jingbo Wang,Sendong Zhao,Haochun Wang,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个覆盖八种语言的多语言医学视觉问答基准，揭示了跨语言性能下降具有场景依赖性，并提出了一种无需训练的场景感知表示工程方法 MedVL-XLRepE，利用模型在英语上的优势引导非英语表示，在三个大型视觉语言模型和八种语言上持续缓解跨语言退化，最高提升 6.33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学视觉问答评估几乎仅限于英语，限制了其在语言多样性患者和临床医生中的适用性。现有的多语言基准虽然显示大型视觉语言模型在非英语语言上性能下降，但缺乏对跨语言变异如何影响医学视觉问答所需不同能力的细粒度分析。

Method: 构建覆盖八种语言、按四大代表性场景组织的多语言医学视觉问答基准，隔离核心能力；评估五个开源和闭源 LVLM，发现跨语言退化高度依赖场景；提出 MedVL-XLRepE，一种无需训练的场景感知表示工程方法，在推理时利用模型优越的英语医学视觉问答能力，将非英语表示引导向其英语对应表示。

Result: 在三个 LVLM 和八种语言上，MedVL-XLRepE 持续缓解跨语言性能下降，最高带来 6.33% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: 跨语言性能下降并非均匀分布，而是高度场景依赖；通过场景感知的表示工程，可在无需额外训练的情况下有效利用英语能力提升多语言医学视觉问答性能。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (VQA) is a crucial task in clinical AI, yet its evaluation has so far centered almost exclusively on English, limiting its relevance to linguistically diverse patients and clinicians. Recent multilingual medical VQA benchmarks show that large vision-language models (LVLMs) degrade in non-English languages, but lack a fine-grained analysis of how cross-lingual variation affects the distinct capabilities that medical VQA requires. To this end, we construct a multilingual medical VQA benchmark over eight languages, organized into four representative scenarios that isolate the core capabilities medical VQA requires. Evaluating five open- and closed-source LVLMs, we find that cross-lingual degradation is not uniform but highly scenario-dependent. We therefore propose MedVL-XLRepE, a training-free scenario-aware representation engineering method, leveraging LVLMs' superior English medical VQA capability to steer non-English representations toward their English counterparts at inference time. Across three LVLMs and eight languages, MedVL-XLRepE consistently mitigates cross-lingual degradation, with gains of up to 6.33\%.

</details>


### [294] [WAM-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22364)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WAM-OPD（世界动作模型的在线策略蒸馏），一种面向视频优先世界动作模型的部署一致性后训练方法。学生模型在环境中自主交互生成历史分布，冻结的教师模型为其提供稠密的视频与动作监督，联合损失仅更新共享骨干网络中的轻量级适配器，并引入动作流匹配正则化。在RoboTwin 2.0两项任务上，Flash-WAM成功率从0%提升至58.3%（HANDOVER MIC）和从16.7%提升至33.3%（PUT OBJECT CABINET），验证了稠密教师监督在学生自诱导历史上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）将视觉未来预测与机器人动作生成耦合，但在蒸馏加速过程中，学生模型会丢失任务能力，且后续会遇到离线数据分布覆盖不足的状态。研究旨在探究：是否可以通过在线策略蒸馏（OPD）修复此类学生模型，而无需依赖稀疏奖励强化学习？

Method: WAM-OPD部署一致性后训练流程：1) 学生模型在环境中执行动作，自主决定历史轨迹分布；2) 冻结的教师模型对学生生成的历史提供连贯的视频与动作目标标签；3) 学生动作分支在自身生成的视频计划下训练（与部署时一致）；4) 联合视频与动作损失仅更新共享骨干网络中的轻量级适配器；5) 引入动作流匹配正则化增强训练稳定性。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准的两个任务上：HANDOVER MIC任务成功率从0.0%提升至58.3%，PUT OBJECT CABINET任务从16.7%提升至33.3%。作者强调这些是任务特定的能力验证，而非广泛泛化的证据。

Conclusion: 在学生自诱导的历史分布上施加稠密的教师监督，是视频优先世界动作模型极具前景的后训练接口。该方法避免了稀疏奖励RL的复杂性，通过部署一致的在线蒸馏有效修复了蒸馏导致的能力退化。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) couple visual future prediction with robot action generation, but accelerated students can lose task capabilities during distillation and later encounter states that are poorly represented by offline data. We study whether on-policy distillation (OPD) can repair such a student without requiring sparse-reward reinforcement learning. We introduce WAM-OPD, a deployment-consistent post-training recipe for a video-first WAM. The student acts in the environment and therefore determines the history distribution. A frozen teacher labels those student histories with coherent video and action targets, while the student action branch is trained under its own generated video plan, as it is at deployment. Joint video and action losses update lightweight adapters in the shared backbone, together with an action flow-matching regularizer. In preliminary RoboTwin 2.0 studies on two tasks, the released one-video/one-action-step Flash-WAM improves from 0.0% to 58.3% success on HANDOVER MIC, and from 16.7% to 33.3% on PUT OBJECT CABINET. These task-specific results are an initial capability proof rather than evidence of broad or uniform generalization. They nevertheless suggest that dense teacher supervision on student-induced histories is a promising post-training interface for video-first WAMs.

</details>


### [295] [LLMs for Survey Text Analysis - A Performance Comparison Between Humans and GPT-5 on Inductive Content Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22417)
*Leonardo Bergmann,Renata Gheorghiu,Ana Gvritishvili,Alex Mican,Chris Stewart,Topias Tolonen-Weckström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人类编码者与LLM（GPT-5.4）在归纳内容分析中的表现，发现两者在编码和主题生成上的ARI值接近人类内部一致性，表明LLM可作为定性分析的可扩展辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在定性研究文本分析中的应用日益增加，但其在归纳内容分析中的表现证据有限，需要实证比较。

Method: 从欧洲博士生调查中收集903个开放式回答，跨六个变量；五名人类编码者按标准化编码方案进行归纳内容分析；LLM（GPT-5.4）使用成熟提示程序执行相同任务；用调整兰德指数（ARI）评估人机输出一致性。

Result: 人机编码ARI为0.61，主题生成ARI为0.54，接近人类内部一致性（编码0.68，主题0.76）和LLM内部一致性（编码0.76）。不同变量间一致性差异大，低实体内一致性与低实体间一致性相关，数据特征和个体表现影响可靠性。

Conclusion: LLM在此特定设置下可近似人类编码，尤其在编码层面，可作为归纳定性分析的可扩展支持工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to support text analysis in qualitative research, yet evidence on their performance in inductive content analysis remains limited. This study compares human and LLM-based inductive coding of open-ended survey responses from 903 answers across six variables from a European PhD student survey. Five human coders performed inductive content analysis following a standardized coding scheme, while an LLM (GPT-5.4) conducted the same task using an established prompting procedure. Agreement between human and LLM outputs was assessed using the Adjusted Rand Index (ARI). Results showed an alignment between humans and the LLM, with ARI values of 0.61 for coding and 0.54 for theme generation. These values were close to the internal consistency of coding and theme results within humans (ARI = 0.68) and the LLM (ARI = 0.76). Agreement varied widely across variables, with low within-entity consistency consistently linked to low between-entity agreement, underscoring the role of data characteristics and individual performance in reliability. Overall, the findings suggest that LLMs can approximate human coding in this case-specific setting, particularly at the coding level, and may serve as a scalable support tool for inductive qualitative analysis.

</details>


### [296] [Where World Models Break: Natural-Input Failure Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22421)
*Zhanpeng Shi,Zi Liang,Rong Feng,Shiqin Tang,Xuyang Chen,Hongzong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BasinLens 方法，用于发现世界模型在自然有效输入下的灾难性预测失败模式，揭示了平均情况基准测试掩盖的关键漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估仅聚合普通查询下的平均误差，忽视了罕见或未观测条件-动作组合下可能发生的灾难性预测崩溃，这种系统性风险会通过控制管线传播，危及下游规划与决策。

Method: 形式化自然输入失败发现问题，提出 BasinLens：利用有效输入的底层结构（各坐标具有环境定义的语义类型与准许域），结合不确定性引导的全局搜索与类型化局部替换，在有限查询预算下高效挖掘可复现且局部持久的严重预测失败。

Result: 跨多基准与世界模型家族实验表明，BasinLens 能持续暴露常规评估无法揭示的可复现、局部持久的失败模式，证明平均情况基准会掩盖世界模型驱动控制中的重要脆弱性。

Conclusion: 世界模型的灾难性预测失败在自然有效输入中普遍存在且难以通过平均误差指标捕捉；需采用结构化、不确定性引导的主动测试方法（如 BasinLens）来系统性评估和缓解此类风险。

Abstract: World models predict action-conditioned futures and serve as critical internal simulators for downstream planning and control. However, catastrophic prediction failures of world models could dangerously propagate through the control pipeline, as subsequent agent or model training and decision-making depend heavily on the continuous environment evolution forecasted by these world models. Existing evaluations overlook this systemic risk: by aggregating average errors over benign generations from general queries, they fail to stress-test the model against catastrophic collapses under rare or unobserved condition-action combinations. To bridge this gap, we formalize the natural-input failure discovery problem: under a finite query budget, finding environment-valid conditions and action prefixes that induce severe prediction risk, verifying whether these failures reproduce on fresh seeds, and testing their persistence under nearby valid edits. Discovering such critical failures is computationally challenging, as valid condition-action combinations explode exponentially, rendering exhaustive search or standard sampling infeasible given the high cost of noisy rollouts. To tackle this, we propose BasinLens, which exploits the underlying structure of valid inputs, where each coordinate possesses environment-defined semantic types and admissible domains, by pairing uncertainty-guided global search with typed local replacements. Across diverse benchmarks and world-model families, BasinLens exposes reproducible and locally persistent failure modes that conventional evaluations fail to reveal, showing that average-case benchmarks can mask important vulnerabilities in world-model-driven control.

</details>


### [297] [Think with Structured Grounding: Perceptual Reinforcement Learning for Chart and Visual-Tabular Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22429)
*Changjiang Jiang,Qiannian Zhao,Lei Xin,Jinxiang Xie,Preslav Nakov,Zhuohan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Think with Structured Grounding (TwSG) 框架，通过蒸馏多步推理与微裁剪能力至单次前向传播，使多模态大语言模型 (MLLM) 内化细粒度图像感知能力，消除对外部工具的依赖，显著降低推理延迟并提升准确率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 依赖外部工具进行细粒度感知，引入高推理延迟，且难以解决文本密集、结构关系复杂视觉（如图表、可视化表格）中的空间结构鸿沟，导致无工具时难以完成精细视觉推理任务。

Method: TwSG 采用两阶段训练：(1) 冷启动监督微调 (SFT)，利用多轮带聚焦区域描述的数据培养复杂推理与错误恢复能力；(2) 强化微调 (RFT)，引入 TL-GRPO 过程奖励机制鼓励策略性推理。训练数据由教师模型根据真实答案引导的关键区域生成高质量 VQA 数据，将区域级监督信号蒸馏回全图表征。

Result: 跨多种 MLLM 架构的大量实验表明，TwSG 显著降低推理延迟，大幅提升准确率与鲁棒性，赋予模型原生细粒度区域描述与灵活推理能力。

Conclusion: TwSG 成功将工具辅助的细粒度感知能力内化到模型内部，实现了无需外部工具的高效、精准视觉推理，为文本密集型结构化视觉任务提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) capable of thinking with images often rely on external tools for fine-grained perception. However, this reliance introduces significant inference latency and fails to effectively resolve the spatial-structural gap-a fundamental challenge in text-dense and structurally relational visuals (e.g., charts and visual tables) where strict relative spatial arrangements bind textual elements. Without external tools, standard MLLMs struggle with such fine-grained visual reasoning tasks. To address these issues, we propose Think with Structured Grounding (TwSG), a novel fine-grained image perception framework designed to internalize complex images's tool-use capabilities within the model. TwSG distills the benefits of multi-step reasoning and micro-cropping into a single efficient forward pass during inference. Specifically, we use an MLLM to identify key regions guided by ground-truth answers, and then prompt a teacher model to generate high-quality visual question-answering (VQA) data. These fine-grained, region-based supervisory signals are subsequently distilled back into the full-image representation. Our training pipeline consists of two stages: (1) a cold-start supervised fine-tuning (SFT) phase using multi-turn data with focused area descriptions to foster complex reasoning and error recovery; and (2) a reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) phase driven by a novel process reward mechanism, TL-GRPO, which encourages strategic reasoning. Extensive experiments across various MLLM architectures demonstrate that TwSG reduces inference latency while substantially improving accuracy and robustness, endowing models with native fine-grained region description and flexible reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [298] [When Persona Simulations Are Informative: Graph-Structured Signals for Pluralistic Opinion Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22438)
*Taehyeon An,Jaehyeong Park,Donghyuk Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无监督诊断指标Persona-Conditioned Informativeness (PCI)，用于评估基于人设条件的大语言模型在模拟调查响应时，语义相似人设是否表现出一致的响应偏移。通过将人设建模为相似度图并应用Local Moran's I量化局部空间相干性，PCI能够在不使用构念标签的情况下提取紧凑的人设子集。实验表明，在57项修订版画像价值问卷(PVQ-RR)上，PCI选择的10%人设子集显著优于基于响应稳定性和随机选择的基线，验证了其作为合成受访者筛选内部诊断工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于人设条件的大语言模型被广泛用于模拟调查响应，但观测到的响应变异可能源于模型的无条件先验或token采样噪声，而非系统性的人设条件作用。缺乏一种不依赖外部人工基准的内部诊断指标来判断人设条件是否真正产生了有意义、系统性的响应变异。

Method: 1. 将人设表示为语义相似度图；2. 引入Persona-Conditioned Informativeness (PCI)指标，利用Local Moran's I统计量量化图上局部空间自相关，即测度语义相似人设是否在相同方向上偏离项目层面的采样基线；3. 基于PCI分数选取高分人设子集；4. 在PVQ-RR数据集上进行验证性因子分析(CFA)，对比PCI选择、响应稳定性选择和随机选择的构念恢复效果。

Result: 在57项PVQ-RR问卷上，PCI选择的10%人设子集在验证性因子分析中显著提升了整体构念恢复质量，优于基于响应稳定性选择的子集和随机选择的子集。PCI能够无监督地识别出对潜在价值结构贡献最大的人设。

Conclusion: PCI作为一种无需外部人工标注的无监督内部诊断指标，能够有效筛选出在调查流程中产生系统性、语义一致响应的人设，为合成受访者的质量控制提供了 principled 方法。

Abstract: Persona-conditioned large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate survey responses across diverse domains. However, apparent response variation can reflect unconditioned model priors or token sampling noise rather than systematic persona conditioning. We argue that persona-conditioned variation is informative when semantically similar personas exhibit concordant response shifts. To operationalize this principle, we introduce Persona-Conditioned Informativeness (PCI), an unsupervised diagnostic metric that measures whether semantically similar personas deviate in concordant directions relative to item-level sample baselines. By modeling personas as a similarity graph, PCI uses Local Moran's I to quantify local spatial coherence and extract compact persona subsets without using construct labels. To evaluate PCI without external human benchmarks, we test its ability to recover established latent value structure using the 57-item Portrait Values Questionnaire-Revised (PVQ-RR). Confirmatory factor analysis (CFA) shows that a PCI-selected 10% subset substantially improves overall construct recovery relative to response-stability and random selection. These findings support PCI as a principled internal diagnostic for screening synthetic respondents in survey pipelines.

</details>


### [299] [Small Reasoning Models are Instruction Followers in Function Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22472)
*Yalda Taheri,Mohammad Hassan Heydari,Erfan Naaman,Afsaneh Fatemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出指令跟随函数调用(IFFC)框架，将函数调用逻辑从主LLM解耦并委托给专用小模型，在指令跟随范式下实现更高准确率和边缘部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要通过微调、强化学习、多智能体框架提升原生函数调用LLM的准确性，但忽略了LLM在指令跟随语境下（标准用户-助手交互）比工具调用语境表现更优的特性。

Method: 提出IFFC框架，将函数调用逻辑从主LLM解耦，委托给专用的小模型，在指令跟随范式下运行；该框架支持激进量化，适合边缘设备部署。

Result: IFFC持续优于原生函数调用(NFC)和基于提示的函数调用(PFC)基线，在推理导向的LLM上增益尤为显著；在激进量化下仍保持鲁棒性能，实现高效设备端部署且无显著精度损失。

Conclusion: 建立了边缘计算场景下可靠、资源高效的函数调用新范式，证明指令跟随语境优于工具调用语境，解耦架构结合小模型可兼顾准确性与部署效率。

Abstract: Function calling represents the core capability of agentic large language models (LLMs). Existing research has focused on enhancing LLMs function-calling accuracy through fine-tuning, reinforcement learning (RL), and multi-agent frameworks, particularly for native function-calling LLMs. This work demonstrates that LLMs achieve superior accuracy in function calling in instruction-following contexts (i.e., standard user-assistant interactions) rather than a tool calling context. We introduce Instruction-Followed Function Calling (IFFC), a novel framework that decouples function-calling logic from the primary LLM and delegates it to a dedicated smaller model operating within the instruction-following paradigm. Our method consistently outperforms both native function calling (NFC) and prompt-based function calling (PFC) baselines, with particularly strong gains on reasoning-oriented LLMs. Furthermore, we demonstrate that IFFC maintains robust performance under aggressive quantization, enabling efficient on-device deployment without significant accuracy degradation. This work establishes a new paradigm for reliable, resource-efficient function calling in edge-computing scenarios.

</details>


### [300] [When Does AI for PDEs Yield Scientific Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22504)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文首次在AI-for-PDE基准测试中引入科学声明的证据支持评估，揭示了数值精度与证据支持可能对模型产生不同排名，并解释了现有基准测试可能偏向于为科学声明提供较弱证据支持的方法，形式化了AI-for-PDE中的评估-使用错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI-for-PDE基准测试主要评估模型的预测或近似精度，但在物理研究中，AI输出常作为科学声明的证据。这两个目标不等价：前者衡量输出与参考目标的一致性或对支配约束的满足度；后者询问在给定研究对象、科学声明、假设和证据标准的情况下，输出是否为该声明提供了充分证据。

Method: 扩展了一个广泛使用的PDE仿真基准测试和一个全面的PDE逆问题基准测试，首次在AI-for-PDE中实现对模型输出是否以及在多大程度上支持特定科学声明的评估。

Result: 数值精度和证据支持可以对模型产生不同的排名，解释了何时以及为何会出现这种差异，并揭示现有基准测试可能偏向于那些输出为感兴趣的科学声明提供较弱支持的方法。

Conclusion: 论文形式化、实证演示并解释了AI-for-PDE中的评估-使用错位问题，即基准测试评估的指标（数值精度）与实际科学使用所需的指标（证据支持）之间存在系统性偏差。

Abstract: Existing AI-for-PDE benchmarks primarily assess models in terms of predictive or approximation accuracy. In physics research, however, AI outputs often serve as evidence for scientific claims. These two objectives are not equivalent: the former measures an output's agreement with a reference target or satisfaction of governing constraints; the latter asks whether, given a specified object of study, scientific claim, assumptions, and evidence standard, the output provides sufficient evidence for that claim. To bridge this gap, we extend a widely used PDE-simulation benchmark and a comprehensive benchmark for PDE inverse problems to enable, for the first time in AI for PDEs, evaluation of whether and to what extent model outputs support specified scientific claims. Our results show that numerical accuracy and evidential support can rank models differently, explain when and why they do so, and reveal that existing benchmarks can favor methods whose outputs provide weaker support for the scientific claims of interest. Together, we formalize, empirically demonstrate, and explain this evaluation--use mismatch in AI for PDEs.

</details>


### [301] [ClawProBench: Trace-Aware Evaluation of AI Agents with Runtime Coverage and Frozen Workplace-Style Holdouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22510)
*YuanHang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了ClawProBench，一个面向运行时原生智能体的可追踪基准，包含全量档案（102场景）和保留集（68场景）两条赛道，通过执行轨迹评分揭示仅看最终答案的榜单会掩盖原生界面弱点、一次性成功及轨迹局部失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准常仅评估最终答案，忽略了有状态运行时中证据获取、运行时路由、安全边界、重复执行等环节的失效，导致评估单元欠规范。

Method: 构建OpenClaw运行时（含工作区工具、浏览、记忆、消息、调度、技能、子智能体等原生界面），设计ClawProBench基准：全量档案102个实时工作区与原生路由任务，保留集68个封闭世界JSON输出任务；采用安全门控评分公式，结合正确性、过程质量、效率，并保留失败证据供审计。

Result: 在68个配置上全量档案最高安全门控平均轨迹分0.7671；原生运行时任务（0.5238）显著低于实时工作区任务（0.6415）；保留集上pass@k-any（0.6638）远优于严格三次通过（0.2890）；全量档案与保留集排名弱相关；纯正确性排名与过程感知、安全门控、严格通过视角差异巨大。

Conclusion: 仅基于最终答案的排行榜会隐藏原生界面弱点、一次性成功及轨迹局部失效模式；需采用可追踪、过程感知、安全门控的评估体系才能全面刻画智能体在有状态运行时中的真实能力。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks often evaluate only final answers even when agents run on stateful runtimes. We argue this under-specifies what is being evaluated: the proper unit is a declared model-plus-runtime configuration whose failures can occur in evidence acquisition, runtime routing, safety boundaries, or repeated execution. We present ClawProBench, a trace-aware benchmark for runtime-native agent evaluation instantiated on OpenClaw, a live agent runtime with workspace tools and native surfaces for browsing, memory, messaging, scheduling, skills, and subagents. ClawProBench defines two tracks: a 102-scenario full profile with live workspace and native-runtime routing tasks, and a frozen 68-scenario holdout with closed-world JSON output contracts for robust ranking. Trials are scored from execution traces via a safety-gated formula combining correctness, process quality, and efficiency, preserving failure evidence for audit. Our anonymous artifact includes benchmark definitions, scoring code, manifests and sanitized traces. We evaluate 68 configurations on the full profile and 37 on holdout. The top safety-gated average trace score is 0.7671. Native-runtime tasks underperform workspace-live tasks (0.5238 vs. 0.6415). On holdout, pass@k-any outperforms strict three-trial pass (0.6638 vs. 0.2890), while full-profile and holdout rankings show weak alignment (Spearman 0.1300). Rankings based purely on correctness differ substantially from process-aware, safety-gated and strict-pass views. Final-answer leaderboards may hide native-surface weaknesses, one-off successes and trace-local agent failure modes.

</details>


### [302] [HANSARD: A Reference Architecture for Forensic Readiness, Runtime Witnessing, and Graded Attribution in Autonomous Multi-Agent AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22512)
*Christos Sardianos,Iliana Pla,Vasilis Efthymiou,Iraklis Varlamis,Thomas Lagkas,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HANSARD，一种面向自主多智能体系统的问责参考架构，将问责视为生命周期属性，通过就绪概况、五个扼杀点捕获、PROV-DM 对齐的因果图、事后重演与修正版 Halpern-Pearl 因果判定、协同残差量化，分离因果、责任与问责三层报告，并以证据分级上限加以约束，使归因洗白（attribution laundering）变得可检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统在金融、软件供应链、安全运营中已大规模部署，并出现首批 AI 编排的入侵活动。但当系统造成危害时，无法稳健确立发生了什么、什么导致了它、谁应负责：溯源取证工作在错误抽象层、形式因果假设已知因果模型、智能体审计信任自我记录。核心失效模式是归因洗白——将行为分散到冗余智能体，使无一成为反事实必要原因；更糟的是记录由嫌疑主体生成，智能体可预判调查、日志基础设施可能串谋。

Method: HANSARD 架构包含五大支柱：1) 运行前密封的就绪概况，界定后续发现的主张边界；2) 在智能体触达范围之外的五个扼杀点捕获证据，使遗漏而非仅篡改可被检测；3) 运行期增量构建类型化的 PROV-DM 对齐因果图，三个实时指标读取图以门控监督而非裁决；4) 事后重演基于修正版 Halpern-Pearl 定义给出或然效应与补偿集大小；5) 协同残差度量组合而非个体导致的危害，使洗白可见。最终分别输出因果、责任、问责报告，各自受证据分级上限约束。

Result: 架构实现了因果、责任、问责的分层报告，每层由证据分级封顶；协同残差量化揭示归因洗白；五个扼杀点捕获使遗漏可检测；修正版 Halpern-Pearl 定义支持事后或然效应与补偿集计算；就绪概况提供事前约束。整体形成完整的生命周期问责链路。

Conclusion: HANSARD 将问责确立为多智能体系统的生命周期属性，通过事前约束、运行期可验证捕获、因果图实时监督、事后反事实重演与协同残差量化，系统性解决归因洗白问题，给出分层证据分级的问责报告框架，并规划了未来研究议程。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems nowadays act in finance, software supply chains, and security operations. Already, the first largely AI-orchestrated intrusion campaigns have been reported. Yet, when such a system causes harm, no method can robustly establish what happened, what caused it, or who is accountable. This is because provenance forensics works at the wrong abstraction, formal causality assumes the causal model, and agent auditing trusts self-recording. The target failure mode is, thus, attribution laundering, i.e., spreading an act across redundant agents until none is a but-for cause. Worse, the record is produced by the suspects, which comprises the assumption adopted throughout this work. Agents may therefore anticipate the investigation and the part of logging infrastructure may itself collude. In this paper, HANSARD is proposed, a reference architecture treating accountability as a life-cycle property. First, a readiness profile sealed before operation bounds what later findings may claim. Second, capturing at five choke points beyond the agents' reach makes omissions detectable, not only tampering. Third, a typed PROV-DM-aligned causal graph accrues as the system runs, and three indicators read it live to gate oversight without adjudicating. Fourth, post-incident replay yields contingent effects under the modified Halpern-Pearl definition, together with a compensation-set size. Finally, a synergy residual measures harm due to the combination rather than to individuals, making laundering visible. Cause, responsibility and accountability are then reported separately, each capped by an evidentiary tier, while a future research agenda is also provided.

</details>


### [303] [CONTRAMEM: Learning Self-Evolving Procedural Memory from Contrasting Multi-Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22533)
*Zheyuan Deng,Binghang Lu,Hanqi Feng,Shirley Huang,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuanda Xu,Zhiwei Zhang,Yige Sun,Changhong Mou,Runyu Zhang,Yuexing Hao,Barnabas Poczos,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CONTRAMEM，一种无需训练、源模型灵活的自进化程序性记忆框架，利用同任务结果差异作为监督，将应用级 Function Cards 和任务级 Skill Cards 组织成紧凑记忆库，通过局部整理而非追加或全量重写实现演化。在 GAIA2/ARE 任务上，三种源模型成功率均提升一倍以上（26.2%→55.3%），且同一记忆库迁移至未见 Qwen3.7 Plus（18.5%→35.5%）及 AppWorld 均显著优于无记忆与单源自记忆基线。异构多模型轨迹构建的记忆优于单模型多轮采样，核心优势源于对比行为多样性而非更强模型或更多采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主计算机使用代理在长程任务中易出现程序性失败（误读状态、工具语义、任务进度）。程序性记忆虽能提升决策一致性并减少冗余探索，但无模型训练条件下构建高质量记忆极具挑战。现有方法多依赖追加式积累或全库重写，难以高效提炼可迁移的程序知识。

Method: CONTRAMEM 将同任务不同轨迹的结果差异（正确性、效率、恢复、失败模式）作为监督信号，提取结果相关的程序性区分，蒸馏为应用级 Function Cards 和任务级 Skill Cards。记忆库通过局部整理（Localized Curation）演化：仅针对冲突或冗余卡片进行精准更新，避免全量重写。支持异构多模型轨迹作为来源，利用对比行为多样性增强记忆泛化能力。

Result: 在 GAIA2/ARE 基准上，三种源模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Sonnet 4.6、DeepSeek V4 Pro）成功率均从 ~27% 提升至 ~52-61%，整体从 26.2% 升至 55.3%。同一记忆库零样本迁移至 Qwen3.7 Plus 提升 18.5%→35.5%。在 AppWorld 公开测试集上，三种中等模型均超越无记忆与单源自记忆变体。同等轨迹预算下，异构多模型轨迹构建的记忆显著优于单模型多轮采样。

Conclusion: CONTRAMEM 证实了无需训练即可通过对比同任务结果差异构建高质量、可迁移的程序性记忆。局部整理机制有效平衡了记忆紧凑性与演化灵活性。异构行为多样性是记忆泛化的关键，而非单纯增加采样量或依赖更强模型。该框架为自主代理的长程任务鲁棒性提供了通用、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous computer-use agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks requiring coordinated application calls, persistent state tracking, and verifier-sensitive writes, yet they remain prone to procedural failures: misreading application state, tool semantics, or task progress. Procedural memory promises more consistent decisions and less redundant exploration, but constructing high-quality memory without model training remains challenging. We introduce CONTRAMEM, a source-flexible, training-free framework for self-evolving procedural memory that treats same-task outcome variation as supervision: differences in correctness, efficiency, recovery, and failure modes expose outcome-relevant procedural distinctions, distilled into a compact bank of app-level Function Cards and task-level Skill Cards that evolves through localized curation rather than append-only accumulation or whole-bank rewriting. On held-out GAIA2/ARE computer-use tasks, CONTRAMEM more than doubles the success rate across the three source-model targets (26.2% to 55.3%), with consistent per-model gains (GPT-5.5: 27.5 to 61.0; Claude Sonnet 4.6: 28.0 to 52.5; DeepSeek V4 Pro: 23.0 to 52.5). The same bank transfers unchanged to the unseen Qwen3.7 Plus (18.5 to 35.5), indicating transferable procedural knowledge rather than model-specific behavior. The same construction carries over unchanged to AppWorld, beating both no memory and its own single-source self-memory variant for all three mid-tier agents on both public test splits. Under a matched trajectory budget, heterogeneous multi-model trajectories yield stronger memory than self- or same-model multi-rollout memory: the margin comes from contrastive behavioral diversity, not stronger source agents or more sampling.

</details>


### [304] [STAGE: Stateful Translation to Agentic Graph Execution with Policy-Scoped Context and Deterministic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22538)
*Mengxi Luo,Changjia Chen,An Cao,Zirong Huang,Wanyi Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAGE 是一个可执行图框架，将模型判断限制在策略范围节点，程序控制由确定性代码处理，从而提高策略执行的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略管辖代理需要在遵循授权程序的同时解读案件证据，现有方法将策略判断与程序控制混合，导致执行可靠性不足。

Method: 提出 STAGE 框架：采用可执行图，将模型判断约束在策略范围节点，过程控制交由确定性协调器强制执行；每个节点模型接收任务相关策略上下文并返回类型化结果。

Result: 在 SOP-Bench Referral Abuse、两个 τ²-bench 领域和专有银行基准 Smart Dispute 上评估。相比单体全策略执行，STAGE 普遍提升任务成功率与重复运行可靠性；在更深的 Telecom 和 Smart Dispute 工作流上增益最大，Pass³ 分别提升 7.5–55.0 和 57.2–65.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 将策略范围上下文与确定性程序控制相结合，能显著提高策略执行的可靠性。

Abstract: Policy-governed agents must interpret case evidence while following an authorized procedure. We present \textsc{Stage}, an executable-graph framework that confines model judgment to policy-scoped nodes while placing procedural control in deterministic code. At each node, the model receives task-relevant policy context and returns a typed result, while the coordinator enforces the reviewed execution contract. We evaluate \textsc{Stage} on SOP-Bench Referral Abuse, two $τ^2$-bench domains, and Smart Dispute, a proprietary banking benchmark. Compared with monolithic full-policy execution, \textsc{Stage} generally improves task success and repeated-run reliability across workflows of varying procedural complexity. The largest gains occur on the deeper Telecom and Smart Dispute workflows, where $\mathrm{Pass}^3$ increases by 7.5--55.0 and 57.2--65.7 percentage points, respectively, depending on the model. These results show that combining policy-scoped context with deterministic procedural control can improve the reliability of policy execution.

</details>


### [305] [Scaling Curriculum Learning For Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22549)
*Cevahir Koprulu,David Paz,Feng Tao,Yuliang Guo,Xinyu Huang,Ufuk Topcu,Liu Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CL4AD，首个将课程学习集成到批量自动驾驶模拟器（GPUDRIVE）中的框架，将场景选择建模为无监督环境设计问题。引入基于成功率、行为真实度及遗憾估计的效用函数，大规模实验表明：课程学习比领域随机化提前 10 亿步达到 99% 成功率，墙钟时间缩短 77%，样本效率提升 67%，并优于启发式课程（仅最大规模下有一例外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批量自动驾驶模拟器虽能在数天内完成数十亿次交互，但标准训练方案——领域随机化——均匀采样场景，导致大量交互用于对策略改进贡献微小的案例，样本效率未随吞吐提升而跟上。

Method: 提出 CL4AD 框架，将场景选择形式化为无监督环境设计问题；设计三类效用函数：基于成功率、基于智能体行为真实度、基于遗憾估计；在 GPUDRIVE 中实现并行课程学习，自适应优先采样高价值场景。

Result: 大规模实验显示：课程学习比领域随机化提前 10 亿步达到 99% 成功率，墙钟时间减少 77%；受限算力消融实验中样本效率提升 67%；优于静态/动态属性的启发式课程（仅最大规模下一例外）；分析了效用函数随规模的行为及优先场景的演化。

Conclusion: CL4AD 验证了课程学习在批量自动驾驶模拟器中的显著加速作用，通过无监督环境设计视角统一多种效用函数，实现了样本效率与训练速度的双重提升，并开源实现以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Batched simulators for autonomous driving have recently enabled training reinforcement learning (RL) agents at scale, encompassing thousands of traffic scenarios and billions of interactions within a matter of days. Although such high-throughput feeds RL algorithms faster than ever, their sample-efficiency has not kept pace: As the standard training scheme, domain randomization uniformly samples scenarios, thereby consuming a vast number of interactions on cases that contribute little to learning. Curriculum learning offers a remedy by adaptively prioritizing scenarios that matter most to policy improvement. We present CL4AD, the first integration of curriculum learning into batched autonomous driving simulators by framing scenario selection as an unsupervised environment design problem. We introduce utility functions that shape curricula based on success rates and the realism of the agent's behavior, in addition to existing regret-estimation functions. Large-scale experiments in GPUDRIVE demonstrate that curriculum learning achieves a 99% success rate a billion steps earlier than domain randomization, reducing wall-clock time by 77%, and outperforms heuristic curricula with static and dynamic attributes, with only one exception at the largest scale. An ablation under limited compute shows that curriculum learning improves sample efficiency by 67%. We also investigate how utility functions behave at scale, and how prioritized scenarios evolve during training. We release an implementation of CLForAD in GPUDRIVE.

</details>


### [306] [CausalCache: Conditional High-Fidelity Restoration for Long-Horizon GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22577)
*Jiaxuan Luo,Zhanfeng Liao,Jiayao Teng,Yuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CausalCache，一种用于长时程 GUI 智能体的可视化历史选择机制，在固定视觉预算下根据预测效用动态恢复历史截图，而非单纯依赖最近事件，显著提升跨应用任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 GUI 智能体面临上下文窗口限制：动作历史可用文本完整保留，但历史截图只能存入少量。现有方法（Recent-B）仅保留最近截图，忽略更早但可能关键的历史视觉信息。需在固定预算下智能选择恢复哪些历史截图以最大化任务成功率。

Method: 1. 将问题建模为预算保真度恢复：每个事件保留文本摘要，预算 B 决定恢复哪些事件的截图。2. CausalCache 对完整历史打分，仅当旧事件预测效用超过近期事件时才替换。3. 引入历史门控键/值适配器，仅修改被恢复的历史图像 token，无历史图像时完全旁路，保持当前屏幕处理不变。4. 在桌面轨迹上用匹配预算干预训练适配器与选择器，零样本迁移至移动端评估。

Result: 在 OSWorld-Verified 上，启用历史截图比仅摘要记忆提升约 13 个百分点。官方 15 步限制下 CausalCache 与 Recent-4 无显著差异；30 步诊断中 CausalCache 46.7% 对 42.4%（+4.3）。117 个 MobileWorld 任务零样本测试中，CausalCache 36.8% 对 Recent-4 30.2%。增益集中在预定义的跨应用记忆候任务（30.6% 对 19.4%，+11.2），单应用控制任务无显著差异（43.6% 对 42.4%）。

Conclusion: 在固定视觉预算下，基于预测效用选择性恢复历史截图比单纯分配给最近事件更有效，尤其利于需要跨应用长程记忆的任务。

Abstract: Long-horizon GUI agents can retain complete action histories as compact text, but only a few historical screenshots fit in active context. We formulate this as budgeted fidelity restoration: every event remains summarized, while a fixed budget $B$ determines which events regain their archived screenshots. Recent-$B$ assigns all visual slots to the latest events. CausalCache instead scores the complete history and swaps in an older event only when its predicted utility exceeds that of a recent event. A history-gated key/value adapter modifies only restored history-image tokens and is exactly bypassed when no history image is active, preserving current-screen processing. The adapter and selector are trained with matched-budget interventions on desktop trajectories and evaluated zero-shot on mobile. On OSWorld-Verified, activating historical screenshots improves success by about $13$ percentage points over summary-only memory. Under the official $15$-step limit, CausalCache and Recent-$4$ are statistically indistinguishable; in a $30$-step diagnostic, CausalCache achieves $46.7\%$ success versus $42.4\%$ ($+4.3$ points). Zero-shot on $117$ MobileWorld tasks, CausalCache improves over Recent-$4$ from $30.2\%$ to $36.8\%$. The gain is concentrated on a pre-defined cross-app memory-candidate split ($30.6\%$ vs. $19.4\%$, $+11.2$ points), while single-app controls show no detectable difference ($43.6\%$ vs. $42.4\%$). These results show that selecting which past events regain pixels is more effective than spending a fixed visual budget entirely on recency.

</details>


### [307] [Weakly supervised concept Bottleneck Learning for Robust Two stage Object centric visual reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22584)
*Sparsh Tiwari,Gesina Schwalbe,Bettina Finzel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出动态正交概念瓶颈（D-OCB），一种面向对象的槽位-VAE框架，在极弱监督下提取人类对齐的符号谓词，通过动态学习超参数分配、惩罚概念子空间相关性以及自适应维度分配机制，防止表示崩溃，在极少标签预算下实现高概念对齐和下游视觉推理准确率，匹配或超越端到端范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段神经符号架构将连接主义感知与关系推理分离，但将高阶谓词锚定到视觉帧通常需要昂贵的标注。现有方法在极弱监督下难以稳定学习人类对齐的符号概念，且超参数平衡困难、概念子空间相关性导致表示崩溃。

Method: D-OCB基于对象中心的槽位-VAE，包含三大创新：1）动态学习最优超参数分配，消除手工调整损失平衡系数；2）在重建自监督基础上惩罚概念子空间间的相关性，注入概念类别独立性先验；3）动态维度分配机制，让表现良好的概念释放潜在维度给落后概念，防止表示崩溃。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，D-OCB在极少标签预算下达到高概念对齐和下游视觉推理准确率，匹配或超越端到端范式。

Conclusion: D-OCB通过动态正交概念瓶颈设计，解决了极弱监督下符号概念学习的不稳定性和超参数敏感性问题，为神经符号视觉推理提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Two-stage neuro-symbolic architectures provide an elegant paradigm for visual problem solving by cleanly separating connectionist perception of predefined symbols from possibly later defined relational reasoning thereon. However, anchoring high-level predicates into visual frames typically necessitates annotations that are expensive to acquire. In this work, we introduce the Dynamic Orthogonal Concept Bottleneck (D-OCB), an object-centric slot- VAE framework designed to extract human-aligned symbolic predicates under extremely weak supervision. D-OCB eliminates the arduous manual tuning of loss-balancing coef- ficients by dynamically learning optimal hyperparameter allocations during training. To infuse prior knowledge on independence of concept categories, in addition to standard re- construction self-supervision we penalize correlation across concept subspaces. Crucially, to combat the instability of very low supervision regimes, D-OCB incorporates a dynamic di- mensionality allocation mechanism; this adaptive formulation allows well-represented con- cepts to yield latent dimensions to underperforming concepts that are lagging behind, effectively preventing representation collapse and significantly improving overall concept accuracy. Through an extensive empirical evaluation, we demonstrate that our framework achieves high concept alignment and downstream visual reasoning accuracy using minimal label budgets, matching or outperforming end-to-end paradigms.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [308] [Revisiting N2DCG: An Empirically Grounded Reformulation of Carousel Recommendation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21877)
*Jingwei Kang,Santiago de Leon-Martinez,Maarten de Rijke,Harrie Oosterhuis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文针对轮播界面中的推荐系统评估指标N2DCG存在的两个主要局限（理想排序违反轮播约束、折扣函数不符合用户浏览行为）提出改进版N2DCG，通过遵守约束的归一化和基于实证的折扣函数，在眼动追踪数据和模拟实验中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮播界面广泛应用于视频/音乐流媒体服务，但现有评估指标N2DCG沿用单列网页搜索的假设，未能适应二维轮播布局：其理想排序违反轮播约束，折扣函数不反映真实用户浏览行为，导致评估不准确。

Method: 重新设计N2DCG：1) 修正理想排序以满足轮播约束（如行/列容量限制）；2) 基于实证眼动数据构建符合用户浏览模式的折扣函数；3) 在真实眼动数据集和基于实证浏览模式的模拟轮播布局上验证指标性能。

Result: 改进后的指标在真实眼动数据上更好地反映用户实际浏览行为，且在模拟轮播布局的对比实验中能更准确预测不同布局的优劣。

Conclusion: 提出的改进版N2DCG通过约束感知的归一化和实证驱动的折扣函数，解决了原指标在二维轮播场景下的核心缺陷，为轮播推荐系统提供了更有效的离线评估工具。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces have been widely used in video and music streaming services, yet it remains unclear how to properly evaluate recommender systems in these two-dimensional layouts. N2DCG has been proposed to address this gap by adapting NDCG to carousel-based recommendation, but it relies on unverified assumptions borrowed from the single-list web-search setting that do not transfer well to two-dimensional carousel layouts.
  We identify two substantial limitations of N2DCG: its ideal ranking, used for normalization, violates carousel constraints, and its discount function does not reflect user browsing behavior observed in empirical data. To address both limitations, we propose a reformulation of N2DCG that normalizes appropriately by respecting constraints and uses an empirically grounded discount function. We validate the proposed metric, showing that it better reflects users' empirical behavior on real-world eye-tracking data and better predicts the comparison results of carousel layouts simulated based on empirical examination patterns.

</details>


### [309] [Enhancing Group Recommendation with Memory-Augmented Reasoning in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21939)
*Qimeng Niu,Bowen Hao,Zixuan Zhang,Shuyu Qu,Hongzhi Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AGR（基于大语言模型的智能体），通过记忆模块动态管理用户/群组交互历史，结合推理模块的多步推理过程，实现可解释的群组推荐，并采用强化微调（SFT+GRPO）训练，在LastFM和Douban数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的群组推荐方法将交互历史视为固定文本，忽略了用户/群组偏好的动态演化，且缺乏对复杂群体决策过程的显式建模，导致推荐准确性和可解释性受限。

Method: AGR包含两大模块：1) 记忆模块：基于token的哈希表，支持插入、更新、检索、遗忘无关记录及画像摘要，动态追踪偏好演化；2) 推理模块：基于检索到的动态画像，执行群体兴趣收集、共识细化、多维评估和可解释推荐生成四步推理。训练采用强化微调范式：先用监督微调（SFT）赋予调用模块的基础能力，再用组相对策略优化（GRPO）增强模块协调的自主性。

Result: 在LastFM和Douban两个真实数据集上，AGR在推荐准确率（如Recall@K, NDCG@K）和可解释性指标上均显著优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: AGR通过显式建模偏好动态演化与群体共识形成过程，结合强化微调实现模块自主协调，有效解决了群组推荐中的动态性与可解释性难题，代码已开源。

Abstract: The core challenge in group recommendation lies in modeling the dynamic evolution of user preferences and explain?ing the consensus formation process. Existing Large Language Model (LLM)-based methods, despite improved interpretability, treat interaction history as fixed text, ignoring the natural evolution of group/user preferences over time, and lacking explicit modeling of the complex group decision-making process. To address these issues, we propose AGR, a LLM-based agent, which consists of a Memory Module and a Reasoning Module. The Memory Module employs a token-based hash table to dynamically manage the historical interactions of groups and users. This design supports fundamental operations including insertion, updating, retrieval, forgetting of irrelevant records, and summarization of evolving group and user profiles for efficiently tracking. Based on these retrieved dynamic profiles, the Reason?ing Module then performs a multi-step reasoning process includ?ing Group Interests Collection, Group Consensus Refinement, Multi-dimensional Evaluation and Explainable Recommendation Generation, thereby moving beyond black-box inference to de?liver fully interpretable recommendations. In practice, we adopt the Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) paradigm, where we first use Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) to equip the model with basic capabilities for invoking the Memory and Reasoning modules, and then employ Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to enhance its autonomous ability to coordinate these modules. Experiments on LastFM and Douban datasets demonstrate that AGR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both recommendation accuracy and explainability. Our model is open-sourced at https://huggingface.co/niuqimeng/AGR.

</details>


### [310] [From Click Modeling to Offline and Off-Policy Evaluation in Carousel Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22022)
*Jingwei Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文摘要介绍了作者的博士研究，旨在解决轮播界面推荐系统中点击建模和离线/反事实评估的挑战，提出了一系列连贯的工作：从用户交互研究、点击模型设计框架、离散选择模型、轮播专用离线指标到反事实评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮播界面将多个排序列表水平堆叠呈现，导致排序与二维布局紧密耦合，用户行为受行组织、视口约束和项上下文影响，使得用户反馈难以解释，给推荐评估带来新挑战。

Method: 1. 研究用户在轮播界面的交互行为；2. 开发点击模型设计框架，优先考虑观测变量间的数学关系而非潜在行为假设；3. 使用离散选择模型将点击建模为选择；4. 开发轮播专用的离线评估指标；5. 计划开发反事实评估方法，从日志交互中估计推荐策略性能。

Result: 已完成用户交互研究和点击模型设计框架；正在进行离散选择模型建模点击和轮播专用离线指标开发；下一步将开发反事实评估方法。

Conclusion: 预期贡献是一个连贯的工作体系，将轮播点击建模与离线和反事实评估联系起来，使轮播推荐策略能够更可靠地改进。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces are widely used in modern recommendation systems. Unlike traditional interfaces that present a single ranked list, carousels simultaneously present several ranked lists to the user, as horizontally swipeable rows stacked on top of each other. In this design, the rankings are closely tied to the two-dimensional layout. Consequently, user behavior is shaped not only by item preference, but also by row organization, viewport constraints, and item context. This tight coupling between ranking and presentation complicates the interpretation of user feedback, introducing new challenges for recommendation evaluation.
  My PhD research aims to address these challenges by rethinking how carousel clicks are modeled and how carousel recommendation policies can be evaluated from logged interaction data. So far, I have studied how users interact with carousel interfaces and developed a click model design framework that prioritizes mathematical relationships between observed variables over latent behavioral assumptions. Building on these results, my ongoing work includes a project using discrete choice models to represent clicks as choices, alongside a project that develops carousel-specific offline metrics. As a next step, I plan to develop off-policy evaluation methods that estimate the performance of recommendation policies from logged interactions. Taken together, the expected contribution of my thesis is a connected body of work that links carousel click modeling with offline and off-policy evaluation, so that carousel recommendation policies can be improved more reliably.

</details>


### [311] [SARCLIP: A Scalable CLIP-Based Retrieval System for Seventeenth-Century Spanish American Notary Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22036)
*Chandrasekhar Syamala,Parshad Suthar,Hulayyil Alshammari,Viviana Grieco,Praveen Rao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SARCLIP 是一个面向阿根廷国家档案馆 17 世纪西班牙美洲公证记录（超 1360 万词级图像块、100 多卷胶卷）的已部署检索系统。基于 CLIP ViT-B/16 并经古文书专家标注数据对比微调，系统通过 FAISS 近似最近邻索引实现全语料级检索，再用 Rocchio 伪相关反馈优化 Top-k 结果，并构建“检索-浏览-标注-再训练”的人机闭环。演示展示了其在近全量语料上的完整交互工作流，超越了早期仅在 5 卷子集上评估的原型阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史手稿档案因书写风格不一、古旧拼写规范及大规模可靠转录缺失，难以用标准文本搜索技术处理。现有研究原型多局限于小规模子集评估，缺乏面向近全量语料、支持人机协作迭代的完整部署系统。

Method: 1. 模型基座：CLIP ViT-B/16，在古文书专家标注数据上对比微调。2. 规模化检索：构建 FAISS 近似最近邻索引覆盖近全量语料。3. 结果优化：引入 Rocchio 伪相关反馈精排 Top-k。4. 人机闭环：提供可视化文档浏览、画布级补丁标注界面，并支持周期性模型再训练。

Result: 系统已作为完整交互工具部署运行，覆盖 1360 万词级图像块、100 多卷胶卷的近全量语料。演示现场验证了检索、浏览、标注、再训练的全流程闭环，标志着从小规模原型向生产级古文书检索系统的跨越。

Conclusion: SARCLIP 证明了将 CLIP 类视觉语言模型与高效近似检索、伪相关反馈及人机协作标注相结合，可构建面向大规模历史手稿档案的实用化、可迭代优化的检索系统，为数字人文与档案学提供了可复用的技术范式。

Abstract: Historical manuscript archives resist standard text search due to inconsistent handwriting, archaic orthography, and the absence of reliable transcriptions at scale. We present SARCLIP (Spanish American Notary Records Meets CLIP), a deployed retrieval system for the National Archives of Argentina's seventeenth-century Spanish American notary records, a corpus of more than 13.6 million word-image patches spanning over 100 microfilm rolls ("rollos"). SARCLIP is built on a CLIP ViT-B/16 model contrastively fine-tuned on paleography-expert-annotated data, and extends prior work by (1) scaling approximate nearest-neighbor retrieval to the near-complete corpus via a FAISS index, (2) refining top-k results through pseudo-relevance feedback (Rocchio), and (3) closing a human-in-the-loop cycle through visual document browsing, canvas-based patch annotation, and periodic model retraining. Unlike the system's initial research prototype, which evaluated retrieval on a small five-rollo subset, SARCLIP is demonstrated as a complete, interactive tool operating over the near-complete corpus. Attendees experience the full search, browse, annotate, and retrain workflow live during this demonstration.

</details>


### [312] [GrOIL: Graph-Grounded Domain Ontology Induction with Constrained LLM Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22135)
*Maruf Ahmed Mridul,Abid Talukder,Oshani Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个七阶段图基管道，将领域文档自动转换为完整、可审计的OWL TBox，无需无约束生成步骤，实现语料库定位、词汇一致性、公理级表达性和端到端溯源的同时满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动系统无法同时满足语料库定位、词汇一致性、公理级表达性和端到端溯源这四个构建形式本体的关键需求，因此需要一种新的方法来从领域文档构建高质量、可审计的本体。

Method: 1. 将文档编码为统一话语超图（UDH），捕捉实体参与和话语依赖；2. 通过七个阶段将图证据转换为类层次、类型化对象/数据属性和限制公理；3. 将大语言模型（LLM）的使用限制在狭窄的、图定位的中介任务中；4. 配对断言盒（ABox）填充过程将命名个体定位到诱导的TBox中，支持SPARQL功能评估；5. 每个发出的术语都携带从原始源段落通过每个管道阶段的完整决策链，确保可审计性。

Result: 在人寿保险领域评估：在两个基准和一个包含10个产品类型的100份合同语料库上，该管道在能力问题（CQ）覆盖率上显著优于直接和多智能体LLM基线（0.85 vs 0.63/0.62；0.77 vs 0.40/0.44）；关键词覆盖率与手工构建的参考本体相当；在结构化缺口与重叠推理上表现强劲；本体增长分析显示词汇在规模上趋于饱和，证明管道能从大规模文档语料库产生稳定、可复用的领域表示。

Conclusion: 该七阶段图基管道成功实现了从领域文档到完整、可审计OWL TBox的端到端自动化构建，无需人工TBox工程即可获得高质量本体，且具有强溯源性、稳定性和可复用性，为大规模领域本体自动化构建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Constructing formal ontologies from domain documents requires simultaneously enforcing corpus grounding, vocabulary consistency, axiom-level expressivity, and end-to-end provenance, a combination no existing automatic system delivers. We present a seven-stage graph-grounded pipeline that converts domain documents into a complete, auditable Web Ontology Language (OWL) Terminological Box (TBox) without any unconstrained generation step. Documents are first encoded as Unified Discourse-Hypergraphs (UDH) capturing entity participation and discourse dependencies; subsequent stages transform this graph evidence into a class hierarchy, typed object and datatype properties, and restriction axioms, with Large Language Model (LLM) usage restricted to narrow, graph-grounded mediation tasks. A paired Assertional Box (ABox) population procedure grounds named individuals in the induced TBox, enabling SPARQL-based functional evaluation. Every emitted term carries a full decision chain from raw source passages through each pipeline stage, making the TBox directly auditable and suitable for targeted human refinement. Evaluated on the life insurance domain using two established benchmarks and a new 100-contract corpus spanning ten product types, our pipeline achieves strong results across all evaluation dimensions, outperforming direct and multi-agent LLM baselines on competency-question (CQ) coverage (0.85 vs. 0.63 and 0.62 on one term-life contract; 0.77 vs. 0.40 and 0.44 on another contract), while also attaining high keyphrase coverage comparable to a manually-constructed reference ontology and strong performance on structured gap-and-overlap reasoning, all without any manual TBox engineering. Ontology growth analysis provides evidence consistent with vocabulary saturation at scale, demonstrating that the pipeline produces stable, reusable domain representations from large document corpora.

</details>


### [313] [Prompt-Based Abstention Fails Under Misleading Context: A Controlled Study of Small Frozen RAG Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22228)
*Yohanes Andre Setiawan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 GRAB-RAG 基准，评估 RAG 中模型在四种上下文条件（支持性、退化、缺失、误导性）下的弃答行为，发现模型在证据缺失时能可靠弃答，但在误导性证据下仍有 41.6% 回答错误，且 63% 直接复述植入的错误实体；思维链帮助有限，冲突检查虽降低错误率但牺牲覆盖率，NLI 验证器恢复覆盖但当参数记忆与误导段落一致时失效。提示式弃答仅判断上下文是否充分而非正确，无法在不权衡覆盖率的情况下解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的弃答机制将缺失证据和误导性证据视为同一问题，但模型在证据缺失时会弃答，而在证据误导时却倾向于回答。需要系统评估模型在不同证据质量下的弃答行为，揭示提示式弃答的局限性。

Method: 构建 GRAB-RAG 基准，包含 Natural Questions 和 HotpotQA 两个数据集，每个问题配四种上下文条件：支持性（金标准段落）、退化（噪声段落）、缺失（无相关段落）、误导性（编辑金标准段落支持错误答案并混入其他检索段落）。在三个小型冻结模型（3.8B-8B）上测试五种弃答策略，包括显式弃答提示、思维链、生成器侧冲突检查、NLI 验证器等。

Result: 模型在缺失证据时可靠弃答，但在误导性条件下显式弃答提示仍有 41.6% 回答错误，其中 63% 直接复述植入错误实体。思维链改善有限。生成器侧冲突检查将错误率降至 13.3% 但大量丢弃正确答案；NLI 验证器恢复覆盖率，但当模型参数记忆与误导段落一致时失效。提示式弃答仅评估上下文充分性而非正确性，无验证器能在不牺牲覆盖率的情况下弥补这一差距。

Conclusion: RAG 系统需区分证据缺失与证据误导，当前基于提示的弃答机制不足以应对误导性证据。需开发能同时检测上下文正确性与参数记忆冲突的验证机制，在保持覆盖率的前提下提高安全性。GRAB-RAG 为该研究方向提供了标准化评估基准。

Abstract: Missing and misleading evidence are not the same problem in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but prompt-based abstention treats them alike. Models abstain when context is absent, not when it is misleading. We introduce GRAB-RAG (Graded Abstention Benchmark for Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a paired benchmark that tests the same questions across four context conditions (supportive, degraded, missing, and misleading) in Natural Questions and HotpotQA. In the misleading condition, we edit a gold passage to support a wrong answer and place it among other retrieved passages. We test five abstention policies on three small frozen models (3.8B--8B) across two QA benchmarks. Models abstain reliably when evidence is missing, but under explicit abstention prompting still answer 41.6% of misleading questions, with 63% of those answers echoing the planted wrong entity verbatim. Chain-of-thought provides little additional benefit. A generator-side conflict check cuts the rate to 13.3% but discards many correct answers, while an NLI verifier recovers that coverage but fails when parametric memory and the misleading passage agree on the same wrong answer. Prompt-based abstention asks whether context is sufficient, not whether it is correct. Neither verifier closes this gap without trading coverage for safety.

</details>


### [314] [GRAFT: Graph-Distilled Generative Retrieval for Facet-Aware Scientific Literature Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22381)
*Italo Luis da Silva,Hanqi Yan,Yujing Wang,Jiangnan Ye,Lin Gui,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Graft：一种基于图蒸馏的生成式检索器，通过四类面向（问题、方法、结果、贡献）构建有类型边的论文图，解决覆盖率不足与生成标识符与查询图连接不一致的问题，在 LitWeave 语料上实现 91% 教师图 Recall@20 恢复率，且能为每篇检索结果输出 0.922 精度的面向标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档级检索器将多种论文关联类型压缩为单一相似度分数，无法解释关联原因；引用/相似度检索仅限于已知邻域，而生成式检索可直接生成文档标识符，支持科学发现所需的探索性检索。

Method: 1. 构建四面向类型边的论文图（面向项+引用信号）；2. 将图蒸馏为生成式检索器，标识符即论文自身的面向文本；3. 覆盖感知蒸馏：反向邻居回退、最小覆盖阈值、边重要性加权，解决朴素枚举仅覆盖 84% 语料的问题；4. 图加权倒数秩融合：按查询-候选边权重缩放秩项，丢弃无图支持候选，保证生成标识符与查询在图中相连。

Result: 在 11,359 篇 NLP 论文的 LitWeave 语料上，Graft 无需最近邻索引或编码器即恢复教师图 91% 的 Recall@20，在语料外查询论文上超越教师图；面向标签复现精度达 0.922，使每篇返回论文均附带解释性面向标签而非不透明分数。

Conclusion: Graft 通过图蒸馏与覆盖感知训练、图加权融合，实现了可解释、高覆盖、探索性强的生成式学术论文检索，为科学发现提供了更透明的检索范式。

Abstract: Scientific papers may relate by problem, method, result, or contribution, but document-level retrievers collapse these into a single similarity score without saying why they are related. Citation- and similarity-based retrieval alone also confines search to the neighbourhood of what is already known, whereas generative retrieval generates document identifiers directly, enabling the exploratory retrieval that scientific discovery depends on. We connect papers in a graph whose edges are typed by these four facets, derived from facet items and citation signals, and distil it into a generative retriever whose identifiers are the papers' own facet text. Two graph properties do not survive naive distillation. First, because every training pair is an edge, naive enumeration indexes just 84% of the corpus. Coverage-aware distillation makes every paper learnable through a reverse-neighbour fallback, a minimum-coverage threshold, and edge-importance weighting. Second, constrained decoding guarantees that every generated identifier is a valid paper, but not that the graph connects it to the query. Graph-weighted reciprocal rank fusion scales each candidate's rank term by its query-candidate edge weight, dropping unsupported ones. On LitWeave, our constructed corpus of 11,359 NLP papers, Graft recovers 91% of its graph teacher's Recall@20 with no nearest-neighbour index or encoder at inference, and outperforms the graph teacher on query papers outside the corpus. It reproduces the graph's own facet labels at 0.922 precision, so every returned paper arrives labelled with the facet that surfaced it rather than an opaque score.

</details>


### [315] [FashionKG-RAG: Knowledge Graph-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Fashion Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22688)
*Yujuan Ding,Linyin Luo,Shijie Wang,Xu Yuan,Yunshan Ma,Yi Bin,Wenqi Fan,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 FashionEcoKG（全面的时尚领域知识图谱）和 PG-RAG（无训练的剪枝-落地 RAG 框架），通过双粒度路径重排序模块（PSR 与 GAR）解决时尚问答中的概念稠密与语言噪声问题，实验表明其检索与回答准确率优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚领域知识密集且决策依赖多类知识融合，现有 LLM 受限于幻觉与领域专业化不足；现有时尚知识图谱多局限于产品属性或物品关系，难以捕捉完整时尚生态，故需构建领域全覆盖的高保真知识图谱并设计适配的 RAG 机制。

Method: 1. FashionEcoKG：三阶段 Agentic 流水线，从权威教材抽取高保真知识核心，经跨域增强与生成式扩展强化结构连通性。2. PG-RAG：免训练框架，含双粒度路径重排序（DGPR）— PSR 将查询蒸馏为骨架形式提升召回；GAR 对候选路径逐点审视原始完整查询以保全局相关性。

Result: 在精心构建的时尚问答数据集上，PG-RAG 结合 FashionEcoKG 显著提升检索召回与回答准确率，优于无 RAG 与现有 KG-RAG 基线。

Conclusion: FashionEcoKG 与 PG-RAG 协同解决了时尚领域知识覆盖不足与 RAG 噪声敏感问题，为知识密集型垂直领域提供了可复用的图增强 LLM 方案。

Abstract: Fashion is a knowledge-intensive domain in which effective decision-making depends on integrating multiple types of knowledge. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed many areas, their application in fashion remains limited by hallucinations and weak domain specialization. Knowledge Graph (KG)-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offers a promising way to add structured knowledge to LLMs. However, existing fashion KGs are typically restricted to product-level attributes or item relations, and fail to capture the broader fashion ecosystem. To bridge these gaps, we propose \textbf{FashionEcoKG}, a comprehensive, domain-wide knowledge graph built with expert-level precision and professionalism. It is constructed through a three-stage agentic pipeline that extracts high-fidelity knowledge cores from authoritative textbooks and strengthens structural connectivity through cross-domain augmentation and generative expansion. To leverage this resource, we further develop \textbf{PG-RAG} (Pruning-Grounding RAG), a training-free framework designed to handle the conceptual density and linguistic noise of fashion queries. Specifically, we introduce a Dual-Granularity Path Re-Ranking (DGPR) module of two stages. The Pruning-based Semantic Ranking (PSR) module distills each query into a skeleton form to improve retrieval recall, while the Grounding-based Agentic Ranking (GAR) performs point-wise scrutiny of candidate paths against the original full query to ensure global relevance. Experiments on a curated fashion QA dataset show that PG-RAG effectively leverages FashionEcoKG to improve retrieval and answer accuracy, outperforming both non-RAG and existing KG-RAG baselines.

</details>


### [316] [Rethinking Item Tokenization in Generative Recommenders: From Fixed Atoms to Semantic Subwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22734)
*Xinrui Miao,Mingjia Yin,Jiaqing Zhang,Wei Guo,Yong Liu,Yuyang Ye,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出语义子词标记化（SST）解决生成式推荐系统中固定长度语义ID导致的“项内注意力过载”问题，通过项级子词标记化（IST）合并稳定原子token为语义子词，再引入行为诱导共现增强（BCA）注入粗粒度语义前缀转移信号，提升模型对项间行为转移的建模能力，在多个数据集和骨干网络上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐系统通常将物品标记化为固定长度的语义ID序列用于自回归预测，但这种细粒度表示导致用户上下文建模中出现“项内注意力过载”：模型过度关注低层级的项内依赖，而忽视高层级的项间行为转移模式。

Method: 提出语义子词标记化（SST）：1）项级子词标记化（IST），将稳定相邻的原子token合并为紧凑的语义子词token，减少编码器中的项内重组；2）行为诱导共现增强（BCA），注入粗粒度语义前缀转移信号，引导释放的建模容量关注项间行为规律性。保持目标解码为固定长度。

Result: 在三个公开数据集和三个生成式推荐骨干网络上的大量实验表明，SST相对于固定长度和可迁移变长语义ID基线均取得实证性能提升。

Conclusion: SST通过变长语义子词表示历史物品并保持固定长度目标解码，有效缓解了项内注意力过载，将建模能力重新导向项间行为转移，显著提升生成式推荐性能。

Abstract: In generative recommender systems, items are typically tokenized into fixed-length semantic ID sequences for autoregressive next-item prediction. However, for user-context modeling, this fine-grained representation triggers Intra-item Attention Overload: excessive attention is spent on low-level intra-item dependencies rather than high-level inter-item behavioral transitions.
  To address this, we propose Semantic Subword Tokenization (SST), which represents historical items as variable-length semantic subwords while preserving fixed-length target decoding. SST first applies Item-level Subword Tokenization (IST) to merge stable adjacent atom tokens into compact semantic subword tokens, thereby reducing intra-item reassembly in the encoder. It then introduces Behavior-induced Co-occurrence Augmentation (BCA) to inject coarse-grained semantic prefix transition signals, guiding the freed modeling capacity toward inter-item behavioral regularities. Extensive experiments on three public datasets and three generative recommender backbones show empirical improvements of SST over fixed-length and transferable variable-length SID baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/mxrcandy/Semantic-Subword-Tokenization.

</details>


### [317] [Risk-Aware Reranking for Agentic Tool Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22751)
*Qinfei Li,Xiaoxuan Dong,Jin Zhang,Dexu Yu,Wenhao Deng,Junchen Fu,Youhua Li,Hanwen Du,Chunxiao Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种风险感知的工具检索框架，通过分别建模查询条件下的相关性和工具级别的暴露风险，引入显式权衡参数平衡安全性与实用性，在ToolGraph上平滑分数并可选应用基于规则的安全约束，从而在检索阶段减少高风险工具的暴露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具检索方法主要优化语义相关性，安全评估往往关注执行后的失败而非检索阶段引入的风险。工具检索会暴露可执行动作，对某任务有用的工具可能对另一任务来说是不必要或有风险的，因此需要在检索阶段进行过滤以减少暴露给智能体的候选动作空间。

Method: 在冻结的一阶段检索器之上构建轻量级重排序框架：分别建模查询条件相关性与工具级暴露风险，通过显式参数控制安全-效用权衡，在ToolGraph上平滑分数，并可选应用基于规则的安全约束。同时标注了UltraTool和Seal-Tools中6,108个工具的五级序数风险等级，并定义了衡量top-k结果中高风险工具暴露的指标。

Result: 在UltraTool和Seal-Tools上的实验表明，该方法在相关性-安全性权衡上优于仅优化相关性的检索器和重排序基线，带规则过滤的变体为安全关键部署提供了保守的操作点。

Conclusion: 检索阶段的过滤可以在执行前减少暴露给智能体的候选动作空间，补充了下游工具使用的安全防护措施。代码和补充材料已开源。

Abstract: Tool retrieval determines which external tools are exposed to an LLM agent for a user query or task, making retrieval a critical pre-execution safety boundary. Unlike document retrieval, tool retrieval exposes executable actions: a tool that is useful for one task may be unnecessary or risky for another. However, existing tool-retrieval methods primarily optimize semantic relevance, and safety evaluations often focus on failures after tool execution rather than risks introduced during retrieval. We study risk-aware tool retrieval, where the goal is to retrieve useful tools while reducing exposure to higher-risk tools. We propose a lightweight reranking framework on top of a frozen first-stage retriever. The framework models query-conditioned relevance and tool-level exposure risk separately, combines them through an explicit parameter controlling the tradeoff between safety and utility, smooths scores over a ToolGraph, and optionally applies rule-based safety constraints. To support retrieval-time safety evaluation, we annotate 6,108 tools across UltraTool and Seal-Tools with five ordinal risk levels and define metrics that measure risky-tool exposure in the top-$k$ results. Experiments on UltraTool and Seal-Tools show that our approach improves the relevance--safety tradeoff over relevance-only retrievers and reranking baselines, with the rule-filtered variant providing a conservative operating point for safety-critical deployments. These findings indicate that retrieval-stage filtering can reduce the candidate action space exposed to agents before execution, complementing downstream tool-use safeguards. The code and supplementary materials are available at: https://github.com/qli447/risk-aware-tool-retrieval-release.

</details>


### [318] [Same Agent, Different Answers: A Repeat-Aware Audit of Corpus-Induced Answer Churn in Retrieval-Augmented QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22856)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出“准确率盲区答案波动”现象，即检索增强问答系统在索引扩展后答案发生变化但整体准确率变化不大，并引入“快照兼容性审计”方法量化该波动，实验表明兼容性失效可能在无明显效用变化时发生，建议发布时同步审计兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注聚合准确率，忽略了索引更新导致的个别答案变更（答案波动），且单次对比受生成随机性干扰，需一种能区分真实更新效应与随机波动的审计方法。

Method: 提出快照兼容性审计，通过跨快照分歧减去同快照重复分歧来估计超额答案波动；在冻结的FineWeb前缀上从1个分片扩展到7个分片，在Natural Questions（400题）和TriviaQA（200题）上进行预注册实验，并用DeepSeek生成器进行盲测复现。

Result: Natural Questions上归一化精确匹配和语义超额波动分别为6.44%和10.25%，而精确匹配准确率仅下降1.50个百分点；40/400题出现可重复的语义翻转。TriviaQA上超额波动较小但方向一致，准确率反向变化。DeepSeek复现实验中语义超额波动8.75%，精确匹配却上升3.00个百分点。

Conclusion: 答案级兼容性可能在无显著或方向一致的效用变化下失效，检索增强系统发布时应同时审计兼容性与效用。

Abstract: A retrieval-augmented QA system can return different answers after an index expansion even when its requested model identifier, prompt, retrieval policy, evidence depth, rendering, and exposed generation controls are held fixed. Aggregate accuracy may hide these changes when gains and losses cancel, while ordinary generation variability makes one-shot comparisons overstate update effects. We call the hidden phenomenon accuracy-blind answer churn and introduce the \emph{Snapshot Compatibility Audit}, which estimates excess answer churn by subtracting same-snapshot repeat disagreement from cross-snapshot disagreement. We instantiate it by expanding one frozen FineWeb prefix from one to seven shards. In a preregistered 400-question Natural Questions study, normalized-exact and blinded-semantic excess churn are 6.44 and 10.25 percentage points while exact-match accuracy changes by only $-1.50$ points. A post-hoc analysis finds repeat-stable semantic flips on 40/400 questions. A separately preregistered 200-question TriviaQA study yields smaller, directionally consistent excess churn while exact-match accuracy moves in the opposite direction. An outcome-blind post-hoc 100-question subset replication with a second DeepSeek generator and serving configuration finds 8.75 pp of semantic excess churn even as exact match rises by 3.00 percentage points. Answer-level compatibility can therefore fail without a conspicuous or consistently directed utility shift. Retrieval-augmented releases should audit compatibility alongside utility.

</details>


### [319] [WARP: Wasserstein-Aligned RAG for Population Opinions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22859)
*Aman Singh Thakur,Aditya Agrawal,Alwarappan Nakkiran,Alex Karlsson*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出WARP，一系列后检索算法，利用Wasserstein-1距离将检索证据校准至人群观点分布，解决标准Top-k检索忽略少数派观点及现有校准方法忽略情感序数结构的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Top-k检索按查询相似度排序，导致少数派观点消失；现有多样性重排序（MMR、DPP）缺乏目标分布，基于KL/JS散度的校准方法将观点桶视为无序，无法区分强正与强负情感的序数差异。

Method: WARP先找回余弦排序可能掩埋的少数派观点，再用Wasserstein-1距离选择文档，使其情感强度分布匹配人群目标分布，捕捉序数结构。提供三个变体分别适配稠密、稀疏、可变候选池，平衡校准质量与速度。

Result: 在三个评论领域（3.5万文档、156查询、26实体）上，领域适配变体将分布误差降低至少43%，亚秒级延迟；五位LLM裁判在k≤5时有86%有决断对比中偏好WARP生成答案。

Conclusion: WARP有效将检索证据校准至人群观点分布，同时提升检索校准质量与下游生成答案的偏好度，为RAG系统提供可落地的观点代表性解决方案。

Abstract: RAG systems are increasingly used to summarize what large collections of documents say. A user asks "What do people think about X?" and receives an answer that reads as consensus. But standard top-k retrieval ranks documents by query similarity, not by how faithfully they represent the population, so minority views quietly disappear. Existing fixes fall short. Diversity re-rankers like MMR and DPP spread retrieved documents apart, but with no target distribution to aim for. Calibration methods based on KL or JS divergence do target one, yet treat opinion bins as unordered: confusing strong positive with strong negative costs no more than an adjacent-bin miss.
  We introduce WARP, a family of post-retrieval algorithms that calibrate retrieved evidence to the population's opinion distribution. WARP first recovers underrepresented opinions that cosine ranking may bury, then uses Wasserstein-1 distance to select documents whose sentiment-intensity distribution matches the population target, capturing the ordinal structure ignored by KL and JS divergence. We develop three variants for dense, sparse, and variable candidate pools, trading off calibration quality and speed. Across three review domains spanning 35K documents, 156 queries, and 26 entities, WARP's domain-matched variants reduce distributional error by at least 43% with sub-second latency. These gains carry through to generation: a five-judge LLM panel prefers WARP-generated answers in 86% of decided comparisons at k <= 5.

</details>


### [320] [Cascading Relevance-driven Recommendation Network for CTR Prediction in Trigger-Introduced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22973)
*Kaixuan Chen,Wenwen Wang,Xing Fang,Yang Huang,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的推荐场景——触发式推荐，并设计了级联相关性驱动推荐网络，通过触发-目标交互层、级联兴趣融合模块和类别辅助成对损失三个核心组件，显著提升了触发项与目标项的相关性建模能力，在工业和公开数据集上均优于现有最先进方法，并在线上A/B测试中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台中存在一种新的推荐场景：用户点击感兴趣的商品作为触发项，表达即时兴趣，随后在承接页推荐相关目标项。触发项包含强但模糊的即时兴趣，区别于搜索词的显式意图。现有方法依赖大量标注数据，缺乏对触发相关性的深度探索，影响用户沉浸式体验。

Method: 提出级联相关性驱动推荐网络，包含三个核心组件：1) 触发-目标交互层，基于个性化门控提取触发项与目标项的交互特征；2) 级联兴趣融合模块，通过级联注意力块显式估计用户触发意图，自适应融合即时兴趣与个性化兴趣；3) 类别辅助成对损失，利用触发项与目标项的类别关联指导，增强触发相关性建模。

Result: 在工业数据集和公开数据集上的大量实验表明，CRRN在各项指标上均优于当前最先进的推荐方法。线上A/B测试进一步验证了该方法在实际业务场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: CRRN通过显式建模触发项与目标项的交互与相关性，有效解决了触发式推荐场景中即时兴趣模糊、相关性建模不足的问题，为电商推荐系统提供了新的技术范式，且已开源代码供复现与应用。

Abstract: E-commerce has emerged as crucial platforms for people's daily consumption and shopping interests. There is a new recommendation scenario, Trigger-Introduced Recommendation (TIR), where users click interested product, which is defined as the trigger item, containing their instant interest, and in the undertaking page following the relevant target items. Distinguished from traditional search and recommendation scenarios, trigger contains relatively strong instant interest, which is more vague and implicit compared to search terms. Relying on large amounts of labeled data, existing methods lack the exploration of trigger relevance, which affects users' immersive experience. To alleviate this problem, we propose the Cascading Relevance-driven Recommendation Network (CRRN) to emphasize the interaction and relevance between trigger and target, comprising three essential components: 1) the Trigger-Target Interaction layer extracts interaction features of trigger and target based on personalized gating. 2) Cascading Interest Fusion module explicitly estimates users' trigger intention and fuses instant and personalized interests adaptively with cascading attention blocks. 3) Category-assisted Pairwise Loss enhances trigger relevance with the guidance of category association between trigger and target. Extensive experiment results show that CRRN outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on both industrial and public datasets. Online A/B tests further validate the effectiveness of our method. Our code is available at https://github.com/a-little-cabbage/CRRN.

</details>


### [321] [Hypergraph Embedding Indexing for Efficient Dense Vector Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22980)
*Kishore Konda*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出超图嵌入索引（HEI），利用高激活潜在维度的组合组织文档，实现倒排索引式候选生成并保持稠密嵌入的语义排序能力；多互补超图提升召回率且避免维度爆炸；引入“激活多样性”作为坐标倒排索引效率的诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻（ANN）索引将嵌入视为不可分的高维点，无法利用倒排索引的高效候选生成机制，且难以在保持语义排序的同时实现可扩展的检索覆盖。

Method: 构建超图嵌入索引（HEI），将文档按高激活潜在维度的组合映射到超边；生成多个互补超图以覆盖不同语义子空间；提出激活多样性指标量化嵌入在坐标倒排框架下的可索引性。

Result: HEI 在保持稠密嵌入语义排序能力的同时，实现倒排索引式候选生成；多超图策略显著提升检索覆盖率且避免单一超图维度增长带来的组合爆炸；激活多样性与索引效率强相关，可作为嵌入可索引性的诊断指标。

Conclusion: 超图嵌入索引框架将稠密向量检索与倒排索引机制融合，通过激活维度组合和多超图互补实现高效、可扩展的语义检索；激活多样性为嵌入模型选择与索引设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Dense vector retrieval has become the foundation of modern semantic search, yet existing approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) indexes treat an embedding as an indivisible point in a high-dimensional space. In this work, we propose the Hypergraph Embedding Index (HEI), a framework that instead organizes documents according to combinations of highly activated latent embedding dimensions. This formulation enables inverted-index style candidate generation while preserving the semantic ranking capabilities of dense embeddings. We further demonstrate that constructing multiple complementary hypergraphs substantially improves retrieval coverage without the combinatorial growth associated with increasing the dimensionality of a single hypergraph.
